# TDP — Functionality Checklist

Complete functional inventory of the TDP field-service platform, generated by reading the full source of both repositories:

- **Frontend** — `tdp-frontend/` (React 18, CRA, JavaScript)
- **Backend** — `tdp-backend/` (NestJS 11, Prisma 5, PostgreSQL; REST prefix `/api`)

Snapshot: 2026-09-25, branch `release/v1.1.8` in both repos. Every item is a `- [ ]` checkbox so the document can be used directly as a QA / regression checklist. Exact validation limits, error messages, toast and confirmation texts are quoted from the code. File references are relative to each repo (`FE/` = `tdp-frontend/src`, `BE/` = `tdp-backend/src` where abbreviated).

**Roles:** `ADMIN`, `CLIENT` (company posting work), `FREELANCER` (engineer), `CUSTOMER` (client's customer), `MODERATOR` (backend enum only — not used by the frontend).

**Markers:** items tagged **[BUG?]**, **[GAP]**, **Bug:**, *not rendered*, *dead code* or *does nothing* describe current behaviour that looks unintended — verify before treating it as a requirement. A consolidated list is in [Appendix — Known issues](#appendix--known-issues-found-while-building-this-checklist).

## Contents

1. [Notifications, Emails, Real-time, Scheduled Jobs & Roles](#notifications-emails-real-time-scheduled-jobs--roles) — roles × permissions matrix, 27 notification types, 25 email templates, socket events, chat rules, 7 cron jobs
2. [Frontend — Authentication, Onboarding & App Shell](#frontend--authentication-onboarding--app-shell)
3. [Frontend — Work Orders, Jobs & Dashboards](#frontend--work-orders-jobs--dashboards)
4. [Frontend — Work Order Details, Offers & Chat](#frontend--work-order-details-offers--chat)
5. [Frontend — Invoices, Company, Customers & Permissions](#frontend--invoices-company-customers--permissions)
6. [Frontend — Profiles, Workforce, Admin & Task Board](#frontend--profiles-workforce-admin--task-board)
7. [Backend — REST API Endpoints & Validations](#backend--rest-api-endpoints--validations)
8. [Backend — Business Rules](#backend--business-rules)
9. [Appendix — Known issues](#appendix--known-issues-found-while-building-this-checklist)

# Notifications, Emails, Real-time, Scheduled Jobs & Roles

Paths: `BE` = `tdp-backend/src`, `FE` = `tdp-frontend/src`. Line numbers are approximate anchors (the start of the call or block).
Legend: **[BUG?]** = a likely defect or mismatch that needs a test to confirm; **[GAP]** = defined but never used.

---

## 1. In-app & push notifications

### 1.1 Core delivery pipeline (every notification below goes through this)

- [ ] `NotificationService.sendNotification` removes the sender from `recipientIds` before it creates anything, so an actor never gets a notification about their own action. `BE/modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:588`
- [ ] One `Notification` row is created per recipient, with `receiverId, senderId|null, type, title, content, url, workOrderId|null, chatRoomId|null`. `notifications.service.ts:19-52`
- [ ] Socket: every recipient gets the event `notification` in room `user_<id>`, with payload `{id, content, url, type, title, senderId, createdAt, isRead, sender{id,firstName,lastName}, receiver{deviceToken, role{role}}, workOrderId, chatRoomId, referenceId, companyId}`. `notifications.service.ts:606-621`, `notifications.gateway.ts:104-110`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** The socket payload includes `receiver.deviceToken`, so the recipient's FCM token is sent to the browser. `notifications.service.ts:44,616`
- [ ] FCM push goes out only when the recipient is **not** online (no socket has called `subscribeUser`) **and** has a `deviceToken`. `notifications.service.ts:623-651`
- [ ] The FCM push body is `content` with HTML stripped (`<br>` and `</p>` become newlines, other tags removed). The FCM title is `title`. `notifications.service.ts:570-576,627`
- [ ] The FCM data payload holds `id, content, url, type, title, senderId, createdAt, isRead, unreadCount, workOrderId, referenceId, companyId`, all as strings. `unreadCount` is the count of **all** unread notifications, MESSAGE included. `notifications.service.ts:628-649`
- [ ] The iOS badge is set to `unreadCount` (`apns.payload.aps.badge`) and the sound is `default` on Android and iOS. `BE/modules/notifications/firebase-admin.service.ts:27-44`
- [ ] FCM errors are logged but not re-thrown, so the triggering action never fails because of a push. `firebase-admin.service.ts:45-47`
- [ ] **[GAP]** `pushSilentBadgeUpdate` is never called, and it hard-codes `badge: 5`. `firebase-admin.service.ts:50-77`
- [ ] The device token is set through `PATCH /users/deviceToken` with body `{deviceToken}`. `BE/modules/users/users.controller.ts:315`
- [ ] When the socket event goes to a user who is online, `markMessagesAsDelivered` also runs: chat read-status rows become `isDelivered=true` and `messageDelivered` is emitted to `user_<id>`. `notifications.gateway.ts:104-108,29-90`

### 1.2 NotificationType enum (`BE/../prisma/schema.prisma:303-331`) and every place each type is created

Unless a line says otherwise, the title is `` `WO-${referenceId}: ${title}` ``, the URL is `/workOrder/details/:id`, and FCM follows §1.1. "Team" means `getTeamMemberIds([wo.teamId, wo.secondaryTeamId])`: every member of the work order's primary and secondary teams, with deleted teams skipped. `BE/utils/helpers/getTeamMemberstoNotify.ts:3-29`
"Customer copy" means `notifyCustomerOfWorkOrderEvent`. It goes to every CUSTOMER-role user whose `customerId` is the work order's `clientId` or that client's `parentClientId`, minus the actor, with URL `/workOrder/details/:id?fromCustomerBoard=true`, and it is best-effort (wrapped in try/catch). `BE/modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6675-6690,6707-6735`

**MESSAGE**
- [ ] Chat send (socket `sendMessage` or REST inbox send). The recipient is each authorised room participant who is **not** the sender and **not** currently viewing the room. Title: `chatRoom.workOrder.title` (no WO- prefix). Content: the message HTML, prefixed with a reply quote `<span …>↩ {replyTo.sender.firstName}: {first 60 chars}</span>` when it is a reply to a message that is not deleted. URL: `` `/workOrder/messages/${workOrderId}?engineerId=${chatRoom.engineerId ?? engineerId}` ``. `chatRoomId` is set. `BE/modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:379-388,450-494`
- [ ] The client company owner gets a MESSAGE notification **even while viewing the room**. `chat.gateway.ts:519-538`
- [ ] A replayed send (same `clientMessageId`) creates **no** new notification. `chat.gateway.ts:253`

**COUNTER**
- [ ] Engineer updates an existing counter offer (`PATCH /offers/engineer/counter`). Recipients: the team. Content: `` `${sender.firstName} ${sender.lastName} has updated a counter offer.` `` URL `/workOrder/providers/:id?tab=pending`. `BE/modules/offers/offers.service.ts:963,973`
- [ ] Engineer makes a new counter offer. Content: `` `… has made a counter offer.` ``, same recipients and URL. `offers.service.ts:~980`

**REQUESTED**
- [ ] Engineer requests a work order (`POST /offers/engineer/request`, when status is not ROUTED). Recipients: the team. Content: `` `${firstName} ${lastName} has sent an offer.` `` URL `/workOrder/providers/:id?tab=pending`. `offers.service.ts:402`

**ROUTED**
- [ ] Client routes a work order to an engineer (`POST /offers/route`). Recipient: that engineer (`sendRoutedNotification`). Sender: `workOrder.createdById`. Content: `A new work order has been routed to you.` `offers.service.ts:484-493`
- [ ] Freelance company owner routes to a member (`POST /freelanceCompany/route`). Recipient: the member. Content: `A new work order has been routed to you.` `BE/modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:443`
- [ ] Freelance company owner assigns to a member (`POST /freelanceCompany/assign`). Recipient: the member. The type is **ROUTED** (not ASSIGNED) and the content is `A work order has been assigned to you.` `freelanceCompany.service.ts:560`

**ACCEPT**
- [ ] Assignment of an ACCEPTED offer (`POST /offers/assign`). Recipients: the team. Content: `` `${firstName} ${lastName} has accepted.` `` URL `?tab=assigned`. `offers.service.ts:1569`
- [ ] Engineer accepts a routed offer (`POST /offers/route/accept/:id`). Recipients: the team. Content: `` `${firstName} ${lastName} has accepted the routed offer.` `` URL `?tab=pending`. `offers.service.ts:1839`

**WITHDRAW**
- [ ] Engineer withdraws an offer (`DELETE /offers/engineer/withdraw/:id`). Recipients: the team. There is **no title**. Content: `` `${first} ${last} has withdrawn their offer for Work Order #${referenceId}.` `` URL `/workOrder/details/:id?tab=pending`. `offers.service.ts:2345`

**WORK_ORDER_PUBLISHED**
- [ ] Marketplace publish goes through `sendWorkOrderPublishedNotification(wo, 50)`: at create (non-customer, non-draft), at client approve-and-publish of a customer ticket, and at draft→publish when the next status is PUBLISHED. `work-orders.service.ts:849,1184,6612`
- [ ] Recipients: FREELANCER users whose `personalAddress` has lat/lng within **50 km** (Haversine) and who have **no** ACCEPTED freelance-company membership, **plus** the owners of freelance companies that have an ACCEPTED member within 50 km. `notifications.service.ts:655-723`
- [ ] Skipped entirely when the work order has no address lat/lng (a remote work order or a draft without an address). `notifications.service.ts:659-660`
- [ ] Content: `` `New work order available in ${city, state, country}. Request now to get assigned.` ``. With no area it reads `…available in your area…`. Title: `WO-{ref}: {title}`. Sender: `createdById`. `notifications.service.ts:725-739`
- [ ] Client approves a customer ticket (`POST /workOrders/:id/approvePublish`). The customer creator gets `Your work order has been accepted.` (only when creator ≠ approver; best-effort). `work-orders.service.ts:1191`
- [ ] Draft published by someone other than its author (`POST /workOrders/publish/:id`). The author gets `Your work order has been published.`, or `Your work order has been submitted for approval.` when the next status is not PUBLISHED. `work-orders.service.ts:6646`

**WORK_ORDER_ASSIGNED**
- [ ] `POST /offers/assign` (both branches). Recipients: the assigned engineer, plus the assigned freelance company owner when that is someone else. Sender: `createdById`. Content: `A work order has been assigned to you.` `offers.service.ts:1587,1607`
- [ ] Customer copy: `An engineer has been assigned to your work order.` `offers.service.ts:1650-1657` (called at 1597, 1617)

**WORK_ORDER_UNASSIGNED**
- [ ] Offer status set back to PENDING (`POST /offers/status` → `unAssignWorkOrder`). Recipient: the engineer. **No title.** Content: `` `Work Order #${referenceId} has been unassigned from you.` `` `offers.service.ts:2165`

**WORK_ORDER_CONFIRMED**
- [ ] Sub-status becomes CONFIRMED (`PATCH /workOrders/:id/subStatus`). Recipients: the team plus the freelance company owner. Content: `` `${engineerName|'The engineer'} has confirmed the work order.` `` `work-orders.service.ts:1539`
- [ ] Customer copy: `The engineer has confirmed your work order.` `work-orders.service.ts:1550`

**WORK_ORDER_UPDATED**
- [ ] A payment update removes pending offers (`updateWorkOrderOffers`). Recipients: pending offer users. Content: `Your offer has been removed due to payment update.` URL `/workOrder/payment/:id`. `work-orders.service.ts:5459`
- [ ] A schedule change removes offers (`PATCH` update). Recipients: `scheduleAffectedUserIds`. Content: `The work order schedule has been updated. Your offer has been removed.` `work-orders.service.ts:6102`
- [ ] Move to draft (`PATCH /workOrders/draft/:id`), team audience (minus the actor). Content: `` `${actorName} has moved this work order to draft.` `` when the actor is a CUSTOMER, otherwise `This work order has been moved to draft.` `work-orders.service.ts:6450`
- [ ] Move to draft, customer audience. Content: `Your work order has been moved to draft.` URL `?fromCustomerBoard=true`. A warning is logged when there are no customer recipients. `work-orders.service.ts:6483`

**WORK_ORDER_CHECK_IN**
- [ ] Engineer checks in (`POST /timeLogs/checkIn`). Recipients: the team plus the freelance company owner. Content: `` `${first} ${last} has checked in.` `` `BE/modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:224`. Customer copy: `The engineer has checked in.` `time-logs.service.ts:234`
- [ ] Check-in reminder cron (see §5). Title `Check-In Reminder`. Content: `` `WO-${ref}: ${title}. You have not checked in yet. Please check in.` `` `BE/modules/work-orders/work-orders.scheduler.ts:138`

**WORK_ORDER_CHECK_OUT**
- [ ] `POST /timeLogs/checkOut`. Recipients: the team plus the freelance company owner. Content: `` `… has checked out.` `` `time-logs.service.ts:437`. Customer copy: `The engineer has checked out.` `time-logs.service.ts:447`

**WORK_ORDER_READY_TO_GO**
- [ ] Engineer completes the START_TIME task (`PATCH /tasks/:id` → `startTimeTask`). Recipients: the team plus the freelance company owner. Content: `` `… is ready to start work order.` `` `BE/modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:215`. Customer copy: `The engineer is ready to start your work order.` `tasks.service.ts:225`

**WORK_ORDER_ON_THE_WAY**
- [ ] ON_THE_WAY task. Content: `` `… is on the way.` `` `tasks.service.ts:379`. Customer copy: `The engineer is on the way.` `tasks.service.ts:389`

**WORK_ORDER_RUNNING_LATE**
- [ ] RUNNING_LATE task. Content: `` `… is running late.` `` `tasks.service.ts:488`. Customer copy: `The engineer is running late.` `tasks.service.ts:498`

**WORK_ORDER_WORK_DONE**
- [ ] MARK_COMPLETE task. Recipients: the team only (no freelance company owner). Content: `` `… has completed.` `` `tasks.service.ts:569`. Customer copy: `The engineer has completed your work order.` `tasks.service.ts:579`

**WORK_ORDER_APPROVED**
- [ ] Customer copy only, sent after the approve transaction (`POST /workOrders/:id/approve`): `Your work order has been approved.` `work-orders.service.ts:8751`

**WORK_ORDER_INCOMPLETE**
- [ ] Customer copy only (`POST /workOrders/incomplete`): `Your work order has been marked incomplete.` `work-orders.service.ts:8302`

**WORK_ORDER_CANCELLED**
- [ ] Engineer or freelance company owner cancels (`POST /workOrders/engineer/cancel/:id`). Recipient: the work order `createdById`. Content: `Cancelled` or `` `Cancelled: ${cancellationReason||reason}` `` (the raw enum code, not title-cased). `work-orders.service.ts:10015`
- [ ] Client cancels (`POST /workOrders/client/cancel/:id`). Recipient: the assigned engineer. Content: `Cancelled[: reason]`. `work-orders.service.ts:10283`

**WORK_ORDER_FEEDBACK**
- [ ] Client leaves feedback for the engineer (`POST /feedback/engineer`), only when the work order status is APPROVED, INVOICED, DISPUTED or PAID. Recipients: the engineer plus the assigned freelance company owner. Content: `` `${createdBy name|'The client'} has left feedback for you.` `` URL `/workOrder/feedback/:id`. `BE/modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:36-41,176-177,218-249`
- [ ] Deferred: feedback left before approval is announced when the work order is approved (`notifyEngineerOfClientFeedback`). `feedback.service.ts:252-275`, called at `work-orders.service.ts:~8758`
- [ ] Engineer leaves feedback for the client (`POST /feedback/client`). Recipients: the work order `createdById` plus the freelance company owner. Content: `` `${engineer name} has left feedback for you.` `` `feedback.service.ts:383`

**PROFILE_APPROVED**
- [ ] Admin approves an engineer (`GET /admin/approveProfile/:id`). Title `` `🎉 Welcome Aboard, ${firstName}!` ``, content `Congratulations! Your account has been approved.`, URL `/jobs`. Not awaited. `BE/modules/admin/admin.service.ts:62`
- [ ] Admin activates an engineer (`GET /admin/activateEngineer/:id`): same text. `admin.service.ts:411`
- [ ] Admin approves a customer (`POST /admin/customers/:id/approve`). The owner gets `` `🎉 Welcome Aboard, ${firstName}!` `` / `Congratulations! Your profile has been approved.` URL `/workOrders`. `admin.service.ts:646`
- [ ] Company user reactivated (`PATCH /companies/users/:id/activate`) when that user owns a customer entity and the status is APPROVED: `` `Welcome back, ${firstName}!` `` / `Your account has been reactivated.` URL `/workOrders`. `BE/modules/companies/companies.service.ts:785`
- [ ] Same endpoint, non-customer (company) user set to APPROVED: `🎉 Welcome Aboard…` / `Congratulations! Your company profile has been approved.` URL `/jobs`. `companies.service.ts:809`

**SYSTEM**
- [ ] Customer submits their profile (`GET /clients/customer/submitProfile`). Recipient: the **first** ADMIN found. Title `🧾 Customer Profile Submitted`. Content `` `Customer "${clientName}" has been submitted by ${first} ${last}.` `` URL `/admin/review/customers`. `BE/modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1655`
- [ ] Engineer submits their profile (`GET /users/engineer/submitProfile`). Recipient: the first ADMIN. Title `Profile Submitted`. Content `` `${first} ${last} has submitted their profile.` `` URL `/user/details/profileInformation/:id`. `BE/modules/users/users.service.ts:3808`
- [ ] Company submits its profile (`GET /companies/submitProfile`). Recipient: the first ADMIN. Title `🏢 Company Profile Submitted`. Content `` `Company "${name}" has been submitted by ${first} ${last}.` `` URL `/admin/review/companies`. `companies.service.ts:1066`
- [ ] Engineer updates a generic task (`updateTaskToCompelte`). Recipients: the team. Content `` `${engineer} has updated the task (${parentTask.title})` `` `tasks.service.ts:113`
- [ ] Customer invoice verified (released), marked paid, or overdue. Recipients: CUSTOMER users of the invoice client or its parent (the client and its users must not be deleted), and only for invoices with `role === CLIENT`. Title `` `INV-#${id}` ``. URL `/invoices/edit/:id?role=CLIENT`. `BE/modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2152-2176,2178-2205`
  - [ ] Verified (`PATCH /invoices/verify/:id`): `` `A new invoice of ${currency} ${grandTotal} has been generated for you.` `` `invoices.service.ts:2086`
  - [ ] Overdue (nightly cron): `` `Your invoice of ${cur} ${total} is overdue. It was due on ${DD MMM, YYYY}.` `` `invoices.service.ts:2138`
  - [ ] Paid (`PATCH /invoices/paid/:id`): `` `Your invoice of ${cur} ${total} has been marked as paid.` `` `invoices.service.ts:2524`
- [ ] Team task created with assignees (`POST /teamTasks`). Title `📝 New Task Assigned`, content `` `You have been assigned to task "${title}".` `` URL `/taskBoard/:referenceId`. `BE/modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:176`
- [ ] Team task assignees change (`PATCH /teamTasks/:taskId`). Removed assignees get `📝 Task Unassigned` / `You have been unassigned from task "…".` Added assignees get `📝 Task Assigned` / `You have been assigned to task "…".` `team-tasks-management.service.ts:570,581`
- [ ] Team task comment (`POST /teamTasks/:taskId/comments`). Recipients: assignees plus previous commenters, minus the author. Title `` `Task: ${title}` ``. Content `` `${first} ${last} commented: "${comment with <p>→<span>}"` `` `team-tasks-management.service.ts:809`

**OTHER**
- [ ] Customer raises a ticket (non-draft create) or a customer draft is published into PENDING_APPROVAL (`notifyClientTeamOfPendingTicket`). Recipients: the client company owner plus `workOrderManagerId`, minus the sender. Title `New ticket pending review`. Content `` `${parentCustomer.clientName || senderName || 'A customer'} submitted "${title}" for review.` `` An email to the team inbox follows (§2). `work-orders.service.ts:857-900`
- [ ] Schedule change (date or time) on an assigned work order. The engineer gets `` `Schedule has been updated to ${DD/MM/YYYY, hh:mm a}. Please confirm.` `` `work-orders.service.ts:6090`
- [ ] Mark incomplete. The engineer gets `` `${reasonType}: ${reason}` `` `work-orders.service.ts:8292`
- [ ] Approve. The engineer gets `Congratualations! your worked is approved.` (typos are in the source). `work-orders.service.ts:8730`
- [ ] 12h/24h start reminder cron. Title `Reminder`, sender user `1` (§5). `work-orders.scheduler.ts:79`

**Never created ([GAP])**
- [ ] `WORK_ORDER_PAID`, `WORK_ORDER_REMINDER` and `FREELANCE_COMPANY_INVITE` have no creator anywhere in BE. The frontend still handles `FREELANCE_COMPANY_INVITE` (navigates to `/user/settings/linkedCompanies/:id`). `FE/layouts/TopBar/components/Notifications.js:67`
- [ ] Reminders use type `OTHER`, not `WORK_ORDER_REMINDER`. Freelance company assign uses `ROUTED`, not `WORK_ORDER_ASSIGNED`.

### 1.3 Read / read-all / delete / cleanup / unread counts

- [ ] `GET /notifications` returns MESSAGE notifications (title swapped for the WO title, plus referenceId/companyId) followed by all non-MESSAGE notifications, not paginated, with sender profile pictures. `notifications.controller.ts:19`, `notifications.service.ts:97-181`
- [ ] `GET /notifications/messages?cursor&limit=10` returns MESSAGE notifications grouped by chat room and paginated by chatRoomId cursor. The response is `{data, nextCursor, unreadNotificationCount}`, where `unreadNotificationCount` is the number of **rooms** that have an unread MESSAGE. `notifications.service.ts:214-379`
  - [ ] A CLIENT who is not the company owner and belongs to no team gets an empty result. `:242-248`
  - [ ] A non-owner CLIENT is scoped to work orders of their teams. The company owner (checked through both `ownedCompany` and `company.ownerId`) sees everything. `:249-262`
  - [ ] The sender avatar falls back to the company logo when the sender has no profile picture. `:398-422`
- [ ] `GET /notifications/others?cursor&limit=10` returns non-MESSAGE notifications, cursor = notification id. `unreadNotificationCount` is the total unread non-MESSAGE count. `notifications.service.ts:438-467`
- [ ] Linked-company filter: an engineer with an ACCEPTED freelance-company membership does not see notifications for direct-client work orders (only those with `workOrderId=null` or `assignedFreelanceCompanyId != null`). Applied to all, others and chat. It is **not** applied to CLIENTs on `/messages`. `notifications.service.ts:82-95,265`
- [ ] `GET /notifications/chat` returns unread MESSAGE notifications for the user. `notifications.controller.ts:66`
- [ ] `PATCH /notifications/read/:id` marks one notification read, scoped by `receiverId = me`. **[BUG?]** Another user's id makes Prisma `update` throw (500) instead of returning 404. `notifications.service.ts:53-65`
- [ ] `PATCH /notifications/readAll` marks all **non-MESSAGE** notifications read and returns `{message, count}`. `notifications.service.ts:521-535`
- [ ] `PATCH /notifications/messages/readAll` marks all MESSAGE notifications read. `notifications.service.ts:537-551`
- [ ] `PATCH /notifications/messages/readAll/:chatroomId` marks one room's MESSAGE notifications read. It also runs automatically on `joinGroupChat` and on an inbox mark-read. `notifications.service.ts:553-568`, `chat.gateway.ts:92`, `mobile-inbox.service.ts:230`
- [ ] There is **no delete endpoint** for notifications. The only deletion is the nightly cleanup below.
- [ ] Cleanup: every night at midnight, all notifications with `createdAt` older than **1 month** are deleted, whatever their read state or type. `BE/modules/notifications/notifications.scheduler.ts:12-26`
- [ ] FE: the bell shows `others.unreadNotificationCount` and the message icon shows `messages.unreadNotificationCount`. A live `notification` event is prepended to the matching cache (deduplicated by id). For a MESSAGE, the unread count goes up only when that work order has no other unread MESSAGE. `FE/hooks/useNotificationsSocket.js:88-166,233,241`
- [ ] FE: a SYSTEM notification shows a toast `You have a new notification` and invalidates the freelance-company queries. `useNotificationsSocket.js:91-113`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** FE groups message notifications by `notification.conversationId`, but BE notifications only carry `chatRoomId`, so every item lands in the one group `"undefined"`. `useNotificationsSocket.js:269`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** Clicking a message notification calls `PATCH notifications/messages/readAll/${notification.conversationId}`. That value is undefined, so the server receives `+undefined` = NaN and fails. `FE/layouts/TopBar/components/MessageNotifications.js:100`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** The FE title map labels `ACCEPT` as "Work Order Routed:" and uses lowercase `"system"`, which never matches `SYSTEM`. `FE/layouts/TopBar/components/Notifications.js:168-212`
- [ ] FE: clicking a notification marks it read and navigates to `url`. A CUSTOMER gets `withCustomerBoardParam` applied. `Notifications.js:65-76`

## 2. Emails (`BE/templates/*.hbs`, `MailService.sendMail`)

Transport and config:
- [ ] SMTP through SES using `SES_HOST/PORT/SMTP_USERNAME/SMTP_PASSWORD`. Default from is `"SES_FROM_NAME" <SES_FROM_MAIL>`. Templates are loaded from `dist/templates` by the Handlebars adapter with `strict: true`, and `nest-cli.json` copies `templates/**` as assets. `BE/app.module.ts:67-85`
- [ ] `sendMail(to[], subject, template, context, attachments?, cc?, fromEmail?, fromName?)` catches and logs every error, so a failed email never fails the request. `BE/modules/mail/mail.service.ts:14-42`
- [ ] `CUSTOMER_NOTIFICATIONS_EMAIL` comes from env, with a hard-coded default in `BE/utils/variables.ts:26`. It receives the internal customer-registration and customer-profile-submitted emails.
- [ ] `CANCELLED_EMAIL_RECIPIENTS` is an env JSON array, used only on **engineer**-initiated cancellation for the `work-order-cancelled-client` email. `work-orders.service.ts:9950`
- [ ] Invoice emails send from `accounts@techdomain.com.au` as "Tech Domain Accounts". `invoices.service.ts:2110,2432`

| Template | Trigger (endpoint → method) | Recipient | Subject (exact) | Key context | Attach. | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `verify-otp` | Email signup `POST users` → `UsersService.create` (skipped for social signup) | New user | `Tech Domain - Verify OTP` | firstName, otp | – | users.service.ts:396 |
| `verify-otp` | `GET /otps/resend/:email` → `resendOtp` | User | ` Tech Domain - Verify OTP` (**leading space**) | firstName, otp | – | otps.service.ts:54 |
| `reset-password` | `POST /auth/forgotPassword` | User | `Tech Domain - Reset Password` | firstName, resetLink (`/resetPassword?token=` with a 64-char nanoid, **15-min** expiry, role param) | – | auth.service.ts:524 |

Email checks:
- [ ] Invoice emails go out **one per recipient** (SES rejects a whole message if any address is unverified), with duplicate addresses removed. cc goes on the first copy only. `invoices.service.ts:2413-2440`

- [ ] Sending without `toEmail` and without skipEmail returns 400 `Invalid Email`. `invoices.service.ts:2218`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** The `work-order-submitted` template uses `recipientName`, `scheduledDateTime` and `siteLocation`, but the caller never passes them. With Handlebars `strict: true` a missing field may throw, and the error is swallowed, so the email silently fails. Needs verifying. `templates/work-order-submitted.hbs`, `work-orders.service.ts:921`
- [ ] `welcome-company-user` is passed `supportEmail`, but the template does not render it.
- [ ] Cancellation reason in the email is title-cased (`CUSTOMER_RESCHEDULED` becomes "Customer Rescheduled"). OTHER uses the free text. `work-orders.service.ts:9750-9758`
- [ ] Cancellation email subjects say `Cancelled` while the in-app content uses the raw reason code.

## 3. WebSocket events

All three gateways are `@WebSocketGateway` on the **default namespace and same server**: notifications and work-orders use `{cors:true}`, chat uses `{cors:{origin:'*',credentials:true}}`. The FE opens one socket (`io(REACT_APP_SOCKET_URL, {transports:['websocket'], auth:{userId, role}})`) and on connect emits `subscribeUser {userId}` and `joinRoom "user_<id>"`. `FE/context/SocketContext.js:19-36`

- [ ] **Connection gate:** `WorkOrdersGateway.handleConnection` disconnects any socket whose handshake `auth` lacks `userId` or `role`. Because the gateways share the server, this affects every client, mobile included. `BE/modules/work-orders/work-orders.gateway.ts:31-42`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** Security: every handler trusts client-supplied `userId`, `senderId`, `clientId` and `engineerId`. The handshake carries no JWT verification. Worth a pen test: an impersonated `subscribeUser {userId:X}` would receive X's notifications.

### 3.1 NotificationGateway (`BE/modules/notifications/notifications.gateway.ts`)
| Dir | Event | Payload | Room / recipients | FE listener |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| in | `joinRoom` | `"user_<id>"` (string) | joins that room, then marks chat messages delivered for the handshake user (CLIENT: rooms of their company; others: rooms where they are the engineer) | emitted in SocketContext.js:35 |
| in | `subscribeUser` | `{userId}` | joins `user_<id>` and adds the socket to `onlineUsers` (this drives the FCM "offline" check) | SocketContext.js:33, useWorkOrderSocket.js:198, useAnalyticsLiveRefresh.js:30 |
| out | `notification` | §1.1 payload | `user_<receiverId>` | `FE/hooks/useNotificationsSocket.js:165` |
| out | `messageDelivered` | `{message, userId}` | `user_<id>` | **no FE listener** (the handler is commented out, useChatSocket.js:145) |
| – | disconnect | – | removes the socket id and deletes the user from `onlineUsers` when none are left | – |

### 3.2 WorkOrdersGateway (`BE/modules/work-orders/work-orders.gateway.ts`)
| Dir | Event | Payload | Room / recipients | FE listener |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| in | `subscribeUser` | `{userId}` | joins `user:<id>` (**colon**; notifications use an underscore). Both gateways handle the same event name, so both rooms are joined | – |
| in | `subscribeWorkOrder` | `{engineerId, workOrderId}` | joins `workOrder:<id>` with **no authorisation check** despite the comment; records engineerId against the socket | FE/hooks/useWorkOrderSocket.js:118 |
| out | `workOrder:update:<workOrderId>` | FREELANCER assigned: `{accessRevoked:false, updatedWorkOrder, updatedUserAllowedAction}`. Other engineers who made offers: `{accessRevoked:true, forceUpdate, …their state, message:'🚫 Work Order X was assigned to someone else'}` and they are removed from the room. CLIENT **creator only**: `{updatedWorkOrder}` | direct `client.emit` per tracked socket | useWorkOrderSocket.js:123 (`forceUpdate` refetches allowed actions, payment and jobDetail) |
| out | `workOrder:published` | the full work order | `user:<id>` for **all users** (any role) with a personal address within 50 km, plus owners of freelance companies with a member in range | useWorkOrderSocket.js:221 (prepend to jobs), useAnalyticsLiveRefresh.js |
| out | `workOrder:list:update` | formatted FREELANCER work order | `user:<id>` | useWorkOrderSocket.js:201 (removes it on the AVAILABLE filter, otherwise merges), analytics |
| out | `workOrder:list:remove` | work order `{id,…}` | `user:<id>` | useWorkOrderSocket.js:215, analytics |

Emit sites:
- [ ] `pushRealTimeChanges(woId, userId)` drives `workOrder:update:<id>`. It is called across offers, tasks, time-logs and work orders. `BE/modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1920-1995`
- [ ] `broadcastWorkOrderPublished` fires on create (non-customer), approvePublish and draft→publish. `work-orders.service.ts:851,1185,6613`
- [ ] `workOrderListUpdate` fires on request offer, assignment, mark incomplete and approve. `offers.service.ts:413,1626`, `work-orders.service.ts:8309,8740`
- [ ] `removeWorkOrderFromList` fires on unassign, offer delete (`DELETE /offers/:id`) and moving a PUBLISHED work order to draft (sent to every engineer who made an offer). `offers.service.ts:2176,2823`, `work-orders.service.ts:6429`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** The `workOrder:published` socket reaches all roles and company-linked engineers, but the matching notification is limited to FREELANCERs not linked to a company.
- [ ] **[BUG?]** `workOrder:update` for clients goes only to the work order **creator**, never to team members, CUSTOMERs or ADMINs.
- [ ] **[BUG?]** `handleDisconnect` deletes every `engineerSubscriptions` entry for that userId, even when the user still has other tabs open.

### 3.3 ChatGateway (`BE/modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts`)
| Dir | Event | Payload | Room / recipients | FE listener |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| in | `joinGroupChat` | `{workOrderId, companyId, engineerId, clientId}` | runs `fetchOrCreateChatRoom` (authorisation), sets `socket.data.{userId, chatRoomId, chatAuthedAt}`, joins room `"<chatRoomId>"`, upserts ChatRoomUser with unread 0, marks messages read, and marks MESSAGE notifications read | FE/hooks/useChatSocket.js:327 (clientId = auth user for CLIENT, CUSTOMER and FREELANCER; otherwise workOrderClientId) |
| out | `joinSuccess` | the chatRoom record | the caller | useChatSocket.js:344 |
| out | `joinError` | `{message}` | the caller | useChatSocket.js:357 (console only) |
| out | `newParticipant` | `{userId, message:"User X has joined the chat for engineer Y."}` | the room | useChatSocket.js:362 (no-op) |
| out | `unreadCountUpdated` | `{conversationId, chatRoomId, unreadCount:0}` | the caller | useChatSocket.js:156, FE/hooks/useInbox.js:117 |
| out | `allMessagesReadByUser` | `{message, userId, readerUserId, chatRoomId, user{firstName,lastName}, lastUnreadMessageId}` | `user_<id>` of each socket in the room | useChatSocket.js:214 |
| in | `sendMessage` | `{conversationId\|chatRoomId, senderId, engineerId, message, workOrderId, digitalAssetIds[], messageType?, replyToId?, clientMessageId?}` | see §4; the return value is the Socket.io ack | useChatSocket.js:440, FE/views/WorkJobDetails/ProvidersTab/BulkMessageModal.js:77 |
| out | `sendError` | `{chatRoomId, message:'You are not authorized to send messages in this chat.'}` | the caller | useChatSocket.js:255 (toast) |
| out | `receiveMessage` | saved message (+sender.profilePicture, files, reactions, conversationId) | room `"<chatRoomId>"` | useChatSocket.js:199 |
| out | `messageDelivered` | `{userId, deliveredTo[], message}` | `user_<participant>` | **no FE listener** |
| out | `messageReadByUser` | `{messageId, readerUserId, conversationId, chatRoomId, user}` | room (for viewers who are active) | useChatSocket.js:146 |
| out | `unreadCountUpdated` | `{conversationId, chatRoomId, unreadCount, recentMessage{message,createdAt}}` | `user_<participant>` (a count for non-viewers, 0 for the sender and viewers) | useChatSocket.js:156, useInbox.js:117 |
| in | `editMessage` | `{messageId, senderId, newContent}` | – | useChatSocket.js:461 |
| out | `messageEdited` | `{conversationId, messageId, message, isEdited:true, editedAt}` | the room of `socket.data.chatRoomId` | useChatSocket.js:241 |
| in | `deleteMessage` | `{messageId, senderId}` | – | useChatSocket.js:465 |
| out | `messageDeleted` | `{conversationId, messageId}` | room | useChatSocket.js:247 |
| in | `toggleReaction` | `{messageId, userId, emoji, conversationId?\|chatRoomId?}` | – | useChatSocket.js:469 |
| out | `reactionUpdated` | `{conversationId, messageId, reactions[] (with user.profilePicture)}` | room | useChatSocket.js:251 |
| out | `error` | `{message:'conversationId is required to toggle a reaction'}` | the caller | none |
| in | `chatRoomVisibility` | `{visible, conversationId?\|chatRoomId?}` | sets `socket.data.viewingConversationId` (null when hidden). The FE re-sends it on reconnect | useChatSocket.js:391,402 |
| in | `leaveChatRoom` | `{clientId}` | leaves the room, emits `userLeft`, deletes the room if it is empty and unprotected (emitting `chatRoomDeleted`), clears `socket.data.chatRoomId` | useChatSocket.js:370 |
| out | `userLeft` | `{userId, message}` | room | **no FE listener** |
| out | `chatRoomDeleted` | `{chatRoomId}` | room | **no FE listener** |

Chat socket mismatches:
- [ ] **[BUG?]** `editMessage` and `deleteMessage` throw on `chatRoomId.toString()` when the socket never joined (`socket.data.chatRoomId` is undefined). The error is caught and logged, so the edit is saved but never broadcast. `chat.gateway.ts:108-114,132-134`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** Mobile `PATCH /inbox/readAll` emits `unreadCountUpdated {conversationIds[], unreadCount:0}` with **no `conversationId`**. `useInbox` ignores it (`conversationId == null` returns early) and `useChatSocket` writes `unreadCounts[undefined]`. `mobile-inbox.service.ts:286`, `useInbox.js:75-76`
- [ ] **[GAP]** `ChatGateway.markMessagesAsDelivered` (emits to room `"<userId>"` without the `user_` prefix) and `handleSingleMarkMessagesAsRead` are never called. `chat.gateway.ts:607-744`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** The web FE never sends `clientMessageId`, so web sends are not idempotent. `useChatSocket.js:440-449`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** `leaveChatRoom` sends `clientId = workOrderClientId` for non-CLIENT roles, which is inconsistent with join. The gateway ignores it for room lookup but echoes it in `userLeft`. `useChatSocket.js:372`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** The `receiveMessage` and `allMessagesReadByUser` FE handlers are removed with `socket.off(event)` and no handler reference, which also detaches other components' listeners. `useChatSocket.js:265-270`

## 4. Chat & inbox rules (`BE/modules/chat/*`)

Room creation and participants:
- [ ] A room is keyed by `(workOrderId, companyId, engineerId)`. The customer↔team room is keyed by `(workOrderId, companyId, userId=customer)` with `engineerId=null`. Room name: `Group Chat for Work Order <id>`. `chat.service.ts:303-363`
- [ ] New engineer room participants: the work order's two team members, the company owner, the engineer, and the assigned freelance company owner (only when the engineer is an ACCEPTED member of that company). `chat.service.ts:151-155,226-243`
- [ ] Customer room participants: the customer, both teams' members and the company owner. Missing participants are topped up every time the room is fetched. `chat.service.ts:368-420`
- [ ] The customer room is created at customer ticket create (non-draft), at customer draft publish, and backfilled on `GET /chats/users/:wo/:company` for customer-raised, non-DRAFT work orders when the caller is the customer or a team member. `work-orders.service.ts:837-846,6628`, `chat.service.ts:432-474,742-754`
- [ ] Assignment of a freelance company member creates the group room proactively. `offers.service.ts:1622`
- [ ] Opening an existing room adds the opener as a participant when they are missing, and marks messages read for them. `chat.service.ts:319-335`

Who may join, create or send (`fetchOrCreateChatRoom`, `canUserSendMessageInRoom`):
- [ ] Allowed: a work order team member (primary or secondary), the client company owner, the assigned engineer, the assigned freelance company owner, an ACCEPTED member of the assigned freelance company, an engineer with an offer in ROUTED/ACCEPTED/PENDING (matched on `userId` or `referredEngineerId`) opening **their own** room, or the existing room's engineer. `chat.service.ts:157-224,944-1020`
- [ ] A FREELANCER may never open or create the customer room (403). `chat.service.ts:90-98`
- [ ] The CUSTOMER who created a work order may not open engineer rooms (403). `chat.service.ts:143-146`
- [ ] The customer (`room.userId`) may always send in their own room. `chat.service.ts:970`
- [ ] Socket send authorisation is cached on the socket for **5 min** (`CHAT_SEND_AUTH_TTL_MS`) after a successful join or check. After that it is re-checked against the DB. `chat.gateway.ts:24,211-234`
- [ ] Read gate (`GET /chats/:roomId`, inbox): the user needs a ChatRoomUser row **and** a live relationship: engineer or customer of the room, company owner, member of either team, assigned freelance company owner, **or anyone who ever sent a message in the room** (they keep history but cannot reply). `chat.service.ts:882-936`
- [ ] `GET /chats/users/:workOrderId/:companyId` returns an empty list for a non-owner FREELANCER. A freelance company owner sees only their own room plus their members' rooms, and only when the company is assigned. The assigned member is listed even before a room exists. CLIENT and others see only rooms they participate in. Non-integer params return `[]`. Rooms of deleted users (or `SYSTEM_USER_ID`) are flagged `isDeletedUser`. `chat.service.ts:657-880`
- [ ] Customer rooms are marked `isCustomerChat: true` in the list. `chat.service.ts:838`

Messages, unread counts and read receipts:
- [ ] Save: the message is created with `isRead=false`, the files are linked, and then **every other participant's** `ChatRoomUser.unreadCount` is incremented with one raw UPDATE. `chat.service.ts:530-593,1250-1258`
- [ ] Viewing: a participant is "active" when their socket's `viewingConversationId === roomId`. When the socket never reported visibility, room membership is used instead. `chat.gateway.ts:271-279`
- [ ] Active non-senders get a read-status row (`isDelivered & isRead = true`) and `messageReadByUser`, and their unread count is reset to 0. `chat.gateway.ts:422-448,495-517`
- [ ] Inactive non-senders get a read-status row (unread), `unreadCountUpdated` with the current count, and a MESSAGE notification. `chat.gateway.ts:450-494`
- [ ] A FREELANCER participant is included only when they are the room engineer, the assigned engineer or the freelance company owner. Other stale engineer rows are skipped. `chat.gateway.ts:336-351`
- [ ] The freelance company owner and the client company owner are auto-added as participants when they are missing. `chat.gateway.ts:355-377`
- [ ] Each participant is processed in its own try/catch, so one failure does not block the others. `chat.gateway.ts:409-545`
- [ ] Join or inbox read: `ChatMessage.isRead=true` for others' messages, `unreadCount=0`, the user's read-status rows become `isRead`, `allMessagesReadByUser` is broadcast, and MESSAGE notifications for the room are cleared. `chat.service.ts:1260-1283`, `chat.gateway.ts:617-683`

Edits, deletes and reactions:
- [ ] Only the original sender can edit (`message` replaced, `isEdited=true`, `editedAt=now`). There is no time limit and no validation of the content. `chat.service.ts:476-483`
- [ ] Only the sender can delete. It is a soft delete (`isDeleted=true`) and the content is kept in the DB. The inbox preview shows `This message was deleted`. `chat.service.ts:485-492`, `mobile-inbox.service.ts:539`
- [ ] Reaction toggle is unique per `(messageId, userId, emoji)`; toggling again removes it. **No membership check** on the reacting user. `chat.service.ts:494-512`
- [ ] A reply quote is omitted from the notification when the replied-to message is deleted. `chat.gateway.ts:379-388`

Idempotency:
- [ ] `clientMessageId` (max 64 chars) is unique per sender (`senderId_clientMessageId`). A resend returns the original row with `isReplay`: no broadcast, no unread increment, no notification. A concurrent duplicate is handled through P2002 → the winner's row is read back. `chat.service.ts:541-578,595-625`, `chat.gateway.ts:253`

Attachments:
- [ ] `POST /chats/files` (multipart `files`, `workOrderId`) uploads to S3 at `<userId>/workOrders/<woId>/messages` and creates `CHAT_MESSAGE` digital assets, returning their ids. `chat.controller.ts:163-182`, `BE/modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1518-1548`
- [ ] **[GAP]** No file-size, count or type limits on the server (`FilesInterceptor('files')` has no limits, and upload errors are swallowed and return undefined). The web FE only removes duplicates by name+size. `FE/views/WorkJobDetails/Tabs/ChatTab/index.js:293-320`
- [ ] The inbox send DTO allows at most **20** `digitalAssetIds`. `BE/modules/chat/dto/send-inbox-message.dto.ts:17-22`
- [ ] **[BUG?]** Attached `digitalAssetIds` are not checked for ownership, so any asset id can be linked.

History:
- [ ] `GET /chats/:chatRoomId?cursor&limit` paginates by message id. Default page is 10, maximum 100. The response is `{messages (ascending), nextCursor (oldest id), hasMore}`. `chat.service.ts:1022-1131`, `chat.controller.ts:66-92`

Mobile inbox (`@Controller(['mobile/inbox','inbox'])`, JWT) `BE/modules/chat/mobile-inbox.controller.ts`:
- [ ] `GET /inbox?limit&cursor`: default 20, maximum 50. Keyset cursor is base64url `createdAt|id`, and a malformed cursor falls back to page 1. Only accessible rooms are listed, and only rooms with at least one message. Returns `{data, nextCursor, totalUnreadCount (sum of counts), unreadConversationCount}`. `mobile-inbox.service.ts:48-134`
- [ ] Conversation row: `conversationId, chatRoomId, workOrder{id,title,referenceId,status}, engineerId, company, participant` (counterpart user, or the COMPANY view when the viewer is the engineer or customer), `participantCount, latestMessage{preview, text, messageType, isDeleted, hasAttachments, createdAt, sender{…, isMine}}, latestMessageAt, unreadCount`. `mobile-inbox.service.ts:350-512`
- [ ] Preview text: `You: …` or `{senderFirstName}: …`, clipped at 200 chars. Special cases: voice becomes `🎤 Voice message`, location becomes `📍 Location`, a file-only message becomes `📎 Attachment`, a deleted one becomes `This message was deleted`. `mobile-inbox.service.ts:523-549`
- [ ] `GET /inbox/:id?limit&before&after&markAsRead` returns 403 for non-participants. Messages are marked read unless `markAsRead=false`. `limit=0` (the default) returns the whole thread. `before` gives `previousCursor` and `after` gives `nextCursor` (the catch-up read, ascending). The response includes the full participant list. `mobile-inbox.service.ts:136-174`
- [ ] `POST /inbox/:id/message` checks `{message (required), digitalAssetIds≤20, messageType?, replyToId?, clientMessageId≤64}` with ValidationPipe. Missing room returns 404; not allowed returns 403. It sends through the same gateway path as web (broadcast, counts, notifications) and returns `{sentMessage, conversation}`. `mobile-inbox.service.ts:184-217`
- [ ] `PATCH /inbox/:id/read` returns `{unreadConversationCount}`. `mobile-inbox.controller.ts:78-83`
- [ ] `PATCH /inbox/readAll` clears every accessible unread room (messages, counts and MESSAGE notifications) and returns `{conversationsCleared, totalUnreadCount, unreadConversationCount}`. `mobile-inbox.service.ts:248-298`
- [ ] FE top-bar inbox (`useInbox`) applies `unreadCountUpdated` locally and falls back to invalidating when the conversation is not on the page. `FE/hooks/useInbox.js:65-118`, `FE/layouts/TopBar/components/Inbox.js:35`

Room cleanup:
- [ ] On `leaveChatRoom`, a room with zero messages is deleted **unless** it is protected. Protected rooms are assignment rooms (the room engineer is the assigned engineer or the freelance company owner) and customer rooms. `chat.service.ts:1383-1436`
- [ ] Deleting a room removes its ChatRoomUser and ChatMessageReadStatus rows explicitly. Messages, files and reactions cascade. `chat.service.ts:1349-1363`

## 5. Scheduled jobs (`ScheduleModule.forRoot()` at `BE/app.module.ts:65`)

| Job | Schedule | Selection | Action | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WorkOrderScheduler.sendWorkOrderStartReminders` | `EVERY_HOUR` | status ASSIGNED, subStatus READY_TO_GO or CONFIRMED, engineerId not null, not deleted, with start date, time and timezone (otherwise a warning is logged and it is skipped). The start is built as `orderStartDate` + `orderStartTime` (HH:mm) in `wo.timeZone` | When the start is **23.5–24.5 h** or **11.5–12.5 h** away, the engineer gets an OTHER notification titled `Reminder`, sender `1`. READY_TO_GO text: `` `WO-${ref}: ${title} starts at ${DD/MM/YYYY, hh:mm a}.` `` CONFIRMED text adds `Please mark your work order “Ready to Go” to confirm your readiness before the scheduled start time.` | work-orders.scheduler.ts:29-90 |
| `WorkOrderScheduler.sendCheckInReminders` | `EVERY_MINUTE` | ASSIGNED, engineer set, not deleted, subStatus in NOT_CONFIRMED, CONFIRMED, NOT_READY, READY_TO_GO, ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE | When the rounded minutes to start are **5, 0 or −10**, the engineer gets WORK_ORDER_CHECK_IN `Check-In Reminder`: `… You have not checked in yet. Please check in.` | work-orders.scheduler.ts:13-20,92-148 |
| `NotificationScheduler.deleteOldNotifications` | `EVERY_DAY_AT_MIDNIGHT` | every notification with `createdAt < now − 1 month` | hard delete, and logs the count | notifications.scheduler.ts:12-26 |
| `ChatScheduler.cleanupChatRooms` step 1 `deleteEmptyChatRooms` | `EVERY_DAY_AT_MIDNIGHT` | rooms with no messages that are not protected (assignment or customer rooms) | delete the rooms and their participant and read rows | chat.scheduler.ts:14-18, chat.service.ts:1439-1450 |
| step 2 `deleteUnreadClientChatRooms` | same | work order status in ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, WORKDONE, VERIFIED, APPROVED, INVOICED or PAID, **and** at least one message, **and** every message unread and sent by a CLIENT, **and** `orderStartDate` more than **15 days** ago, **excluding** the assigned engineer's room and customer rooms | delete | chat.service.ts:1454-1502 |
| `InvoiceScheduler.automarkOverdueInvoices` | `EVERY_DAY_AT_MIDNIGHT` | `dueDate < now` and status GENERATED, VERIFIED or UNPAID (no `isDelete` filter) | In one transaction: set OVERDUE and write status history (changedBy `1`). Then send the SYSTEM "overdue" notification (§1.2) to customers, **only** for invoices that were VERIFIED or UNPAID and have CLIENT role. **No email.** | invoice.scheduler.ts:15-74 |
| `TeamTaskScheduler.deleteOldCompletedTasks` | `EVERY_DAY_AT_MIDNIGHT` | team tasks with status COMPLETED and `createdAt` more than **3 months** ago | hard delete | team-tasks-management.scheduler.ts:13-28 |

- [ ] The midnight crons run on server time (no timezone given), and the reminder crons use each work order's timezone.
- [ ] **[BUG?]** Reminders have no "already sent" flag. Two server instances, or overlapping hourly windows (the ±0.5 h tolerance on an hourly cron), can send duplicates. Check-in reminders rely on exact minute rounding, so a cron tick that fires late can miss one.
- [ ] Reminders and overdue changes are attributed to system user id `1` (hard-coded).

## 6. Roles & permissions

Mechanics:
- [ ] Permissions are rows of `(module ModuleType, isRead, isWrite, isUpdate, isDelete)` on a **Designation**. At login `attachPermissions` puts them on `user.role.permissions`, and they are signed into the JWT (`jwt.strategy.ts:61`), so changes take effect only after the next login or refresh. `BE/modules/auth/auth.service.ts:333-347`
- [ ] `PermissionsGuard` allows access when the user has **any** of the required `{module, access}` pairs. A handler with no `@RequiredPermissions` is **denied** when this guard is applied. `BE/common/guards/permissions.guard.ts:8-39`
- [ ] If a user has no designation at login, one is auto-created ("auto generated on login") with role-specific defaults (below). `auth.service.ts:610-1360`
- [ ] A CLIENT **owner** is topped up at every login: any `ClientPermissionModules` value that is missing is added with full CRUD. `auth.service.ts:1095-1133`, `BE/utils/enums.ts:247-276`
- [ ] A CUSTOMER is reconciled at every login (when a designation exists): required modules are added or upgraded (INVOICE R, CUSTOMER_INVOICE R, ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE RW, CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS RWUD, plus CUSTOMER_USERS RWUD when the user owns a customer entity). `ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE` is always removed, and CUSTOMER_USERS is removed when they do not own an entity. `auth.service.ts:1235-1357`
- [ ] MODERATOR gets **no** permissions (no branch in `attachPermissions`), so no modules or screens.
- [ ] **[BUG?]** On a CUSTOMER's first login without a designation, default rows are created only when `user.company.ownerId === user.id`. Otherwise the designation is empty until the next login's top-up. `auth.service.ts:1148`

### 6.2 Frontend screen access (`FE/routes.js`, `FE/App.js:108-165`, `FE/hooks/useAuth.js:38-49`, `FE/layouts/SidebarNav/SidebarItem.js:71-79`)

Rules:
- [ ] A route is registered when the user has **read** on its `module` (or any entry in `modules`) **and** meets `requiredAccess`. A route with `module:""` and no children is always registered, except `completeYourProfile`, which is hidden once the user is APPROVED. `App.js:108-161`
- [ ] Sidebar visibility (`isShow`) by role:
  - CLIENT and ADMIN see items with `isShowClient:true`.
  - FREELANCER sees items with `isShowClient:false`.
  - CUSTOMER sees items with `isShowCustomer:true`, or `isShowClient:false` without `isShowCustomer:false`.
  - Child items: CUSTOMER uses `isShowCustomer`, everyone else uses `isShowClient`.
  - A CUSTOMER with a single visible child navigates straight to that child.
- [ ] `isFreelanceCompanyRoute` (Workers) is shown only to a FREELANCER who owns the company. `useAuth.js:43-46`
- [ ] Company settings tabs are filtered by `roles.includes(authRole)` and `ownerOnly` (company owner). `FE/views/CompanySettings/index.js:109`

Screens per role (with default permissions):
- [ ] **ADMIN** sidebar:
  - Accounts `/admin/review` (Vendors `companies`, Customers `customers`, Engineers `engineers`; detail pages `companies/:id`, `customers/:id`, `freelanceCompanies/:id`). Needs ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT with W.
  - Manage `/manage` (Work Experience: Technology Stacks/category, Category/vendors, Skills/products; Qualifications: Qualifications, Sub-Qualifications).
  - Hidden: `/user/details/*` (Personal Info, Profile, Permissions, Company Details, Bank Accounts, Workers), `admin/customers/entity/:id`.
  - Invoices only if an INVOICE row is granted.
- [ ] **CLIENT (owner, defaults)** sidebar:
  - Dashboard `workBoard/dashboard`.
  - Work Board: Work Orders, Customer Work Orders, Customers (Client Details / Account), Projects (Deliverables and Client Request are commented out).
  - Task Board, Work Force (Coverage Map, All Engineers, Vendors, Talent Pools), Invoices (Finance Dashboard, Customer Invoices, Engineer Invoices, edit/:id).
  - Hidden pages: `workOrder/create|edit/*` (needs W), project and client create/edit/view, parent customer pages, `/company/*` (Company Details, Users, Role Management, Branches, Bank Accounts, Teams, Departments [ownerOnly]), `/company/:role/permissions/:id` (Workboard, Invoices, Work Force, Task Board, General), `/user/settings/*` (Profile, Change Password).
  - Job detail tabs: Details, Payment, Providers, Messages, Job Sign-Off, Feedback, History.
- [ ] **CLIENT staff:** the same set, limited to their designation's modules.
- [ ] **FREELANCER** sidebar:
  - Analytics `engineer/analytics` and Job `jobs` (JOBS read).
  - Workers `workers` (company owner only).
  - Hidden: `completeYourProfile` (until APPROVED), `/user/settings/*` (Personal Info, Profile, Change Password, Bank Accounts, Linked Company, Notification placeholder), `/company/settings` (FREELANCE_COMPANY_WORKERS, ownerOnly).
  - Job tabs: Details, Payment, Messages, Job Sign-Off, Feedback.
- [ ] **CUSTOMER** sidebar:
  - Dashboard `customerDashboard` and Work Orders `workOrders` (CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS).
  - Work Force → All Engineers only (Vendors is `isShowCustomer:false`).
  - Invoices → Customer Invoices, plus `edit/:id`.
  - Create/edit work order through `modules:[WORK_ORDERS, CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS]` (needs W).
  - Hidden: `/customerSettings` (Company Details, Entities, Users) and `/user/settings` (Profile, Change Password).
  - Job tabs: Details, Payment, Messages, Job Sign-Off (no Feedback, Providers or History).
- [ ] **MODERATOR:** only the empty-module routes (for example `/workOrder/*`, `completeYourProfile`, `/customerSettings`, `/company/:role/permissions`). No sidebar items.
- [ ] **[BUG?]** `/customerSettings` and `/company/:role/permissions/:id` have `module:""` and children, so App.js does not register them through the empty-module shortcut. Check that they resolve for their intended roles.
- [ ] Backend `@RequiredPermissions` coverage (101 uses): work-orders (WORK_ORDERS, CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS, ACTION_*), invoices (CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY/SEND/PAID_*), projects (CLIENT_PROJECT), talent-pool (WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL, WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS), workforce (WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS, WORK_FORCE_COVERAGE_MAP), teams (COMPANIES), qualifications (ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT), customer-dashboard (CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS read), users (USERS, ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT), companies `POST /users` (USERS W), clients (CLIENT_CUSTOMER). Test each with a role that is missing the module and expect 403.

# Frontend — Authentication, Onboarding & App Shell

## Routing, Guards & Session

### Route table (public vs protected)
- Source: `src/App.js`, `src/RequireAuth.js`, `src/routes.js`, `src/layouts/Layout.js`
- [ ] Public routes (inside `Layout`, no shell when logged out): `/login`, `/forgotPassword`, `/resetPassword`, `/otp`, `/createNewPassword`, `/clientSignup`, `/customerSignup`, `/getStarted`, `/techSignup`, `/requestDemo` (renders Technician signup form), `/socialCallback`, `/not-found`, `/unauthorized`
- [ ] `/` (index, logged out) renders `FlightboardPage` placeholder; logged in, `RequireAuth` redirects `/` by role (see below)
- [ ] `/unauthorized` renders plain `<h1>Unauthorized</h1>` (inside shell when logged in)
- [ ] `/not-found` renders `NoUserPage` "No User Linked :("
- [ ] Catch-all `*`: logged in shows centred "Unauthorized" heading; logged out redirects (replace) to `/getStarted`
- [ ] Logged-in user visiting `/login`, `/otp`, `/forgotPassword`, `/resetPassword`, `/clientSignup`, `/customerSignup`, `/getStarted`, `/techSignup` (prefix match) is redirected to `/` (NOT `/createNewPassword`, `/socialCallback`, `/requestDemo`)
- [ ] `/completeYourProfile` is a "custom full page" (no sidebar/topbar/notification bar), protected by `RequireAuth`; route removed entirely once `user.status === "APPROVED"` (then shows catch-all "Unauthorized")
- [ ] A submitted (`SUBMITTED`) profile can still open `/completeYourProfile`

### Route filtering by module/permission (App.js)
- [ ] Route with `module: ""` and no `childs` is always allowed
- [ ] Route allowed when user has `isRead` on any of `route.modules` (or `route.module`) AND every `requiredAccess` flag (`isRead`/`isWrite`) matches the user's permission on one of those modules
- [ ] Child routes filtered the same way (module + requiredAccess) — this mutates `route.childs`, so sidebar sub-menus shrink too
- [ ] Child/tab routes additionally filtered by `roles` containing `authRole` (only for route registration, not sidebar)
- [ ] Unmatched protected URL for allowed module set → `/unauthorized` (RequireAuth) with `state.from`

### RequireAuth
- Source: `src/RequireAuth.js`
- [ ] Not authenticated → full reload to `/getStarted` (renders nothing meanwhile)
- [ ] "Authenticated" = `user` + `accessToken` in localStorage AND `user.isFirstLogin === false` (first-login users are treated as logged out)
- [ ] At `/`: FREELANCER → `jobs`; CLIENT → `/workBoard/workOrders`; CUSTOMER → `/workOrders`; ADMIN → `/admin/review/engineers`; other role (e.g. MODERATOR) → empty target
- [ ] User lacking any allowed module → `Navigate /unauthorized` (replace)

### useAuth hook
- Source: `src/hooks/useAuth.js`
- [ ] Reads `user`, `accessToken` from localStorage (JSON)
- [ ] `userModules` = modules with `isRead` true; `userPermissions` = full permission list
- [ ] `authRoutes` (sidebar source) = routes with `isShow` and (no module or module in `userModules`); `isFreelanceCompanyRoute` routes only when role FREELANCER and `user.company.ownerId === user.id`
- [ ] `getPermissionForModule(module)` returns `hasReadAccess/hasWriteAccess/hasUpdateAccess/hasDeleteAccess` (default false)
- [ ] `logout()` → `POST auth/logout {refreshToken}` → clear auth storage → full reload `/login`; on API failure nothing happens (error swallowed, user stays logged in)

### Login-success redirect (authUserSlice.setLoginSuccessful)
- Source: `src/store/authUser/authUserSlice.js`
- [ ] Stores `user`, `accessToken`, `refreshToken` in localStorage
- [ ] Redirect (full reload): `isFirstLogin` → `/createNewPassword`; CLIENT → `/workBoard/workOrders`; CUSTOMER → `/workOrders`; FREELANCER → `/jobs`; ADMIN → `/admin/review/engineers`

### Token refresh & forced logout (axiosInstance)
- Source: `src/lib/axiosInstance.js`, `src/utils/helpers.js`
- [ ] Every request sends `Authorization: Bearer <accessToken>` when present; base URL `REACT_APP_BASE_URL`
- [ ] On 401 (first time per request): `POST auth/refreshToken {oldRefreshToken}` via plain axios; stores new `accessToken` + `refreshToken`; retries original request
- [ ] Concurrent 401s while refreshing are queued and retried with the new token after refresh
- [ ] No refresh token stored → `logout()` + reject
- [ ] Refresh fails → queued requests rejected, `logout()` called
- [ ] `helpers.logout()`: `POST auth/logout {refreshToken}` → on success clear storage + reload `/login`; on failure does nothing (verify user is not stuck on a broken session)
- [ ] `clearAuthStorage()` removes all localStorage keys EXCEPT keys matching `ColumnSettings:` (table column prefs persist across logout)
- [ ] Stored `user` object is not refreshed by the token refresh (permissions changes need re-login)

### Socket connection
- Source: `src/context/SocketContext.js`
- [ ] Connects only when `auth.user.id` exists; URL `REACT_APP_SOCKET_URL`, websocket transport, auto-reconnect every 2000ms, `auth: {userId, role}`
- [ ] On `connect`: emits `subscribeUser {userId}` and `joinRoom "user_<id>"`
- [ ] Disconnects on unmount; logs `connect_error`

### Global toasts & sound
- Source: `src/index.js`, `src/store/toast/toastSlice.js`, `src/store/toast/NotificationToast.js`, `src/utils/playSound.js`
- [ ] ToastContainer: top-right, auto-close 5000ms, progress bar, newest on top, close on click, draggable, pause on hover/focus loss
- [ ] `showToast` types: success / error / info / warning / default / `notification` (custom card)
- [ ] Notification toast: avatar (sender picture or coloured initials), title + message rendered as HTML (message clamped to 2 lines); clickable when `url` → dismisses all toasts and navigates to url
- [ ] `sound: true` plays `/sounds/notification.mp3`; audio is unlocked on the first click anywhere in the app (`initAudio` in App.js) — no sound before first user click

## Authentication Screens

### Login
- Route: `/login` — public (logged-in users redirected to `/`)
- Source: `src/pages/LoginPage.js`, `src/views/Login/index.js`, `src/views/Login/LoginForm.js`
- [ ] Header: brand logo, "Welcome to Trusted Platform"
- [ ] Email field — text, required, placeholder "William123@gmail.com", envelope icon
- [ ] Password field — password, required, placeholder "*****************", eye icon toggles show/hide
- [ ] Validation runs on submit only (not on change/blur)
- [ ] Email empty → "Email is required"; invalid → "Enter a valid email"
- [ ] Password empty → "Password is required" (no min length enforced)
- [ ] "Remember Me" checkbox — toggles label colour only; no functional effect
- [ ] "Forgot Password?" link → `/forgotPassword`
- [ ] "Login" button — full width, shows spinner while loading → `POST auth/login {email, password}`
- [ ] Success → toast "Login Successfully" → `setLoginSuccessful` role redirect (full reload)
- [ ] Any API error → red banner "Invalid Email/Password" + both fields red, plus error toast with server message (fallback "An error occurred")
- [ ] Wrong credentials (404) → toast "Invalid email or password"
- [ ] Unverified email (HTTP 307) → backend resends OTP → navigate `/otp?email=<encoded>` + toast "Please verify email"
- [ ] Deactivated account, customer sub-user (`deactivated && isCustomerUser`) → modal "Account Deactivated": "Your access to your company's Tech Domain account has been deactivated by your organisation's administrator." / "If you need access restored, please contact your company administrator." — single "OK" button (no cancel)
- [ ] Deactivated account, others → modal "Account Deactivated": "Your account is not active. Please contact support to reactivate your account." — "Cancel" closes; "Contact Support" opens `mailto:helpdesk@techdomain.com.au` subject "Account Activation Request", body includes entered email
- [ ] `?socialError=<reason>&email=` in URL → red banner, URL cleaned via `history.replaceState`:
  - [ ] `NO_ACCOUNT` → "No account found for <email|that account>. Please sign up or use a different account." + "SignUp" link highlighted/animated
  - [ ] `NO_EMAIL` → "We couldn’t get an email address from that account. Please sign in with your email instead."
  - [ ] `UNVERIFIED_EMAIL` → "That account’s email isn’t verified. Please sign in with your password first."
  - [ ] `ACCOUNT_DELETED` → "This account has been deleted. Please contact support."
  - [ ] `ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED` → "Your account is not active. Please contact support."
  - [ ] `PROVIDER_ERROR` / unknown reason → "We couldn’t complete sign-in. Please try again."
- [ ] "Don’t have Login ? SignUp here" — "SignUp" link → `/getStarted`
- [ ] "Never Miss a Job Update" separator + "Engineers & Providers — Get the Tech Domain App"
- [ ] Apple App Store badge → opens `https://apps.apple.com/us/app/tech-domain/id6742741677` in new tab
- [ ] Google Play badge → opens `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.techdomain.tdm&pli=1` in new tab
- [ ] Google / Apple sign-in buttons and "or" separator are commented out (DISABLED) — must not render

- Source: `src/components/ui/GoogleSignInButton.js`, `src/components/ui/AppleSignInButton.js`

### Wizard variants by role
- [ ] FREELANCER individual (not owner, not member): steps Verify your Identity, Tax Information, Billing rates, Resume, Insurance (optional), Profile Details (optional)
- [ ] FREELANCER company member: Verify your Identity, Tax Information, Insurance (optional), Profile Details (optional)

- [ ] CLIENT: single nav step "Profile Details" but accordion header reads "Verify your Identity" (label mismatch to verify) — company form
- [ ] CUSTOMER: steps "Company Details" and "Accounts"

### Missing-field rules (tooltip + completion)
- [ ] Identity: "Nationality", "ID document", "Address", "Country", "City", "State"
- [ ] Tax: "ABN or Foreign Contractor declaration"
- [ ] Billing: "Hourly, Half Day & Full Day rates" (all three > 0)
- [ ] Resume: "Resume"
- [ ] Insurance: "Insurance policy"
- [ ] Profile Details: "Profile picture", "About me", "At least one skill"
- [ ] Company: "Registration number", "Company address", "Registration certificate", "Annual revenue" (freelance company only; "N/A" counts as missing)
- [ ] Customer Company Details: "Company Name", "Address", "City", "Zip / Post Code", "Country", "Phone", "Email", "Annual Revenue", "Contact Person", "Registration Number", "Registration Certificate"
- [ ] Customer Accounts: "Accounts Person", "Accounts Email", "Accounts Phone"

### Submit / Resubmit button (Header)
- [ ] Individual engineer: "Submit" enabled only when Identity + Tax + Billing + Resume complete → `GET users/engineer/submitProfile` → navigate `/jobs`
- [ ] Company member engineer: enabled when Identity + Tax complete → same endpoint → `/jobs`
- [ ] CLIENT / vendor owner: enabled when company registration number, address, registration certificate (+ annual revenue for vendor) saved → `GET companies/submitProfile` → vendor owner `/jobs`, client `/workBoard/workOrders`
- [ ] CUSTOMER: enabled when both customer steps have no missing fields → `GET clients/customer/submitProfile` → refetch me → `/workOrders`
- [ ] No success toast on submit; errors swallowed (customer logs to console)
- [ ] Status SUBMITTED and unchanged → disabled "Submitted" button
- [ ] After submission, any successful save on the page → `PATCH users/profileEdited` → button becomes "Resubmit" with attention animation + amber text "You've updated your profile since your last submission. Please resubmit it for verification."
- [ ] `profileNeedsResubmit` from server also restores "Resubmit" state on reload

Any time worked beyond the minimum is billed at the additional hourly rate."; HALF_DAY "A flat rate covering up to 4 hours on site. Any time worked beyond 4 hours is billed at the additional hourly rate."; FULL_DAY "A flat rate covering up to 8 hours on site. Any time worked beyond 8 hours is billed at the additional hourly rate."; FIXED "A single agreed amount for the whole job, regardless of how long it takes. The approximate hours are used for scheduling only."; FTH "A flat rate covering the first two hours on site. Any time worked beyond 2 hours is billed at the additional hourly rate."; CUSTOM_HOURS "A flat rate covering a fixed block of hours that you define. Any time worked beyond that block is billed at the additional hourly rate."

## Hooks — Live updates

### useWorkOrderSocket
- Source: `src/hooks/useWorkOrderSocket.js`; used by `views/FindJob/index.js`, `views/FindJob/JobDetails/index.js`
- [ ] Default jobsFilters {status "AVAILABLE", isCounter false}

- [ ] Non-AVAILABLE status → POST `workOrders/engineer` (status, isCounter + same filters)
- [ ] Per-WO: emits `subscribeWorkOrder` {engineerId, workOrderId}; listens `workOrder:update:{id}`
- [ ] `forceUpdate` payload → refetch allowed actions + payment details, invalidate ["jobDetail"]
- [ ] `updatedWorkOrder` / `updatedUserAllowedAction` payloads merged into state
- [ ] Emits `subscribeUser` {userId}; listens `workOrder:list:update` (AVAILABLE tab: removes row; other tabs: merges payload into row), `workOrder:list:remove` (removes row), `workOrder:published` (prepends new job)
- [ ] Listeners cleaned up on unmount (no duplicate handlers after navigating away/back)
