- [ ] Only its own handler detached on unmount

## Create / Edit Work Order Wizard

### Wizard shell (header + stepper)
- Routes: `/workOrder/create/{summary|scheduling|payment|assessment}`; `/workOrder/edit/{summary|scheduling|payment|assessment}/:id`
- Access: module WORK_ORDERS or CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS with isWrite (ADMIN/CLIENT with WORK_ORDERS; CUSTOMER via CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS); Assessment step WORK_ORDERS only
- Source: `src/views/WorkOrder/CreateWorkOrder/index.js`, `CreateWorkOrderHeader.js`, `WorkOrderStepper.js`
- [ ] Header title "New Work Order" (create) / "Edit Work Order" (edit); sticky, offset when banner visible
- [ ] Steps for CLIENT/ADMIN: summary → scheduling → payment → assessment; CUSTOMER: summary → scheduling → payment (no Assessment)
- [ ] 4th step label shows "(Coming Soon)"
- [ ] Step labels NOT clickable for navigation (handler is a no-op)
- [ ] Edit mode loads WO via GET `workOrders/{id}` and hydrates every step (status, overview, SLA, teams, description, work experience, customer files + baseline, qualifications grouped by type, location, schedule (scheduleType falls back to HARD_START), pay incl. engineer pay; `enableCustomRates` derived from any engineer rate present)
- [ ] Create mode clears createdByRole and customer files left over from a previous edit
- [ ] "Back" button: hidden on first step; goes to previous step (keeps `/edit/.../:id` in edit mode)
- [ ] "Next" button: hidden on last step; validates current step first; on failure toast = single error text, or "Please complete {n} required fields" when >1, then scrolls/focuses first invalid field; on success navigates to next step
- [ ] Page scrolls to top on each step change (Next, Back, browser nav, direct URL)
- [ ] Full-page loader while publishing/saving

#### Header — "Save As Draft" button
- [ ] Shown on create, and on edit of a DRAFT WO; hidden when editing any non-draft WO
- [ ] Disabled until Title is non-blank (and while saving); outlined primary when enabled
- [ ] Create: POST `workOrders` with isDraft=true; blank "" fields stripped from payload
- [ ] Draft edit: PATCH `workOrders/{id}` then sync files/POs/expense attachments
- [ ] Blocks if a qualification type has no sub-type: toast "Select at least one sub-type for each qualification, or remove it — a qualification can't be saved without one."
- [ ] Success toast create: "Work Order Drafted" (info); draft edit: "Draft Saved" (success)
- [ ] Redirects to list with Draft tab: `/workBoard/workOrders?filter=DRAFT` (customer: `/workOrders?filter=DRAFT`) and sets status filter [DRAFT]

#### Header — primary button (Publish / Submit / Update)
- [ ] Label: "Update" when editing a live (non-draft, non-pending) WO; "Submit" for CUSTOMER; otherwise "Publish"
- [ ] CUSTOMER: disabled until title, client (entity), location (lat/lng or saved addressId; remote ok), start date+time, currency and payment-type rate > 0 set; HOURLY also needs minimumHours ≥ 1 and opted-in additional/NBH rates > 0; HALF_DAY/FULL_DAY/FTH need hourlyRate > 0; CUSTOM_HOURS needs customHours
- [ ] CLIENT create / draft publish / pending-approval review: disabled until title, location, paymentTerms, currency, pay-type fields (HOURLY rate>0 + min hrs ≥1 + opted-in extra rates>0; HALF_DAY halfDayRate + hourlyRate>0; FULL_DAY fullDayRate + hourlyRate>0; FIXED fixedAmount>0; FTH fthRate + hourlyRate>0; CUSTOM_HOURS customHours + customHoursRate>0), engineer pay (when custom rates on: HOURLY engineerHourlyRate>0, opted-in additional>0, min hrs>0 requires additional>0; HALF/FULL day rate + engineerHourlyRate>0; FIXED amount>0), start date+time, technology stack + category + ≥1 skill, and no task left as type CUSTOM
- [ ] Plain Update of a live WO is never disabled
- [ ] Spinner while create/upload/approve/publish in flight
- [ ] Create publish (client): full validation (below) → POST `workOrders` (isDraft false) → upload customer files POST `digitalAssets/workOrder/{id}` → POs POST `workOrders/{id}/purchaseOrder` → expense attachments POST `workOrders/{id}/expenseAttachments` (customer + engineer expenses)
- [ ] Create publish toast: "Work Order Published" (client) / "Work order has been submitted for acceptance." (customer)
- [ ] Customer submit redirects to `/workOrders?filter=PENDING_APPROVAL` (Submitted tab); client publish redirects to `/workBoard/workOrders`
- [ ] Draft edit → "Publish": full validation → PATCH `workOrders/{id}` → sync files/POs → POST `workOrders/publish/{id}`; toast "Work Order Published" (customer: "Work order has been submitted for acceptance."); redirect to `?filter=PUBLISHED` (customer `?filter=PENDING_APPROVAL`)
- [ ] Draft publish failure with `missingFields`: multi-line toast "Cannot publish. Please complete:\n• field…" (8s); else API message / "Failed to publish work order"
- [ ] Client editing customer-raised PENDING_APPROVAL WO → "Publish" = Review & Publish: full validation → PATCH `workOrders/{id}` → sync files/POs → POST `workOrders/{id}/approvePublish`; toast "Work Order Published"; redirect `/workBoard/workOrders`; failure toast API message / error.message / "Failed to publish work order"
- [ ] Live edit "Update": PATCH `workOrders/{id}` → delete removed customer files DELETE `digitalAssets/{id}` → upload new files → POs → expense attachments; toast "Work Order Updated"; redirect to list; failure toast = API message
- [ ] 401 on create → auth storage cleared, reload to `/getStarted`
- [ ] Create errors: string message → single toast; array message → one toast per entry
- [ ] Form state reset after every successful save
- [ ] Work Order Manager falls back to current user on edit/review when unset; customers always send null

#### Header — full publish validation (client), each jumps to the offending step
- [ ] Title empty → toast "Title is required" (→ summary)
- [ ] No client → "Client is required" (→ summary)
- [ ] No project (non-customer) → "Project is required"
- [ ] No Service Delivery team (non-customer) → "Service Delivery Team is required. Link one to the selected client."
- [ ] No Operations team (non-customer) → "Operations Team is required. Link one to the selected client."
- [ ] Qualification row without type → "Please select a qualification type" (→ scheduling)
- [ ] Qualification row without sub-types → "Please select at least one sub-type for qualification" (→ scheduling)
- [ ] No qualifications at all → "Qualification Type is required" (→ summary)
- [ ] No technology stack (non-customer) → "Technology Stacks are required"; no category → "Category is required"; no skills → "Skills are required"
- [ ] Exact location without lat/lng → "Address is required" (→ scheduling); saved location without addressId → "Address is required"
- [ ] PO with file but no number → "Each attached file must have a Purchase Order number" (inline "Purchase Order number is required if a file is attached") (→ payment)
- [ ] FULL_DAY/HALF_DAY/HOURLY/FTH with empty hourlyRate → "Hourly rate is required" (→ payment)
- [ ] Custom engineer rates on: engineer min hours > 0 with additional ≤ 0 → "Additional Hourly rate is required"
- [ ] Engineer currency set but type rate missing → "Engineer rates are required"
- [ ] Engineer rate set but no engineer currency → "Engineer Currency is required"
- [ ] Engineer rate set but engineerHourlyRate empty → "Engineer Hourly rate is required" (→ payment)
- [ ] Engineer FIXED with amount empty/≤0 → "Fixed Amount rate cannot be less than $1" (→ payment)
- [ ] No start date → "Schedule is required." (→ scheduling)

### Step 1 — Summary
- Route: `/workOrder/create/summary`, `/workOrder/edit/summary/:id`
- Source: `src/views/WorkOrder/Steps/Summary.js` (+ Forms Overview, Description, Qualification, WorkExperience, Documents)
- [ ] Step validation combines Overview + Work Order Eligibility (+ Work Experience for non-customers); all errors reported at once

#### Overview card (`Forms/OverviewForm.js`)
- [ ] Work Order Title — text, required, max 100 chars, counter "{n}/100", hint "(A clear title helps providers understand your project)"; inline error "Title is required"; step error "Work Order Title is required"
- [ ] Incident ID — text, optional
- [ ] Client (label "Entity" for CUSTOMER) — searchable single select, required; options GET `clients?search&companyId&isParentClient=false` (customer: +parentClientId = own customer id); search debounced 500ms; loading "Loading clients…"; no options "No clients match your search" / "No clients available"; placeholder "No clients"/"No entities"; error "Client is required"/"Entity is required"
- [ ] Client field disabled for a client reviewing a customer-raised ticket
- [ ] Changing client clears project, auto-fills both teams from client, clears team errors when supplied
- [ ] Project (hidden for CUSTOMER) — searchable, required; GET `projects?search&companyId&clientId`; placeholder "Search or select a project"; no-options "No projects for this client" / "No projects match your search" / "No projects available"; error "Project is required"
- [ ] Picking a project first back-fills its client and both teams
- [ ] Service Delivery Team (hidden for CUSTOMER) — read-only, required, hint "(Set automatically from the client)"; placeholder "Select a client first" / "This client has no team linked"; error "Service Delivery Team is required"
- [ ] Operations Team (hidden for CUSTOMER) — same behaviour; error "Operations Team is required"
- [ ] "Link a team" pill (opens `/workBoard/clients/edit/{clientId}` in new tab) shown when selected client has no team
- [ ] Step errors when client chosen but team missing: "Service Delivery Team is required"/"Operations Team is required" (customer: "The selected entity has no Service Delivery Team assigned"/"The selected entity has no Operations Team assigned")
- [ ] SLA — optional select "Select SLA": 2H, 4H, SBD, NBD, 2BD, Planned (customer: beside Entity; client: below teams)
- [ ] Work order options (hidden for CUSTOMER) checkboxes: "Disable export button", "Disable counter offers", "Require GPS during check in and check out"

#### Service Description card (`Forms/DescriptionForm.js`)
- [ ] Work Description — rich-text editor, optional, hint "(Provide precise instructions to guarantee your work order is completed correctly)"
- [ ] "Confidential Information – Optional" switch (off by default; on in edit if data exists); note "Critical information will be confidential to you and will not display to providers"
- [ ] Switch on → second rich-text editor for confidential info

#### Work Order Eligibility card (`Forms/QualificationForm.js`)
- [ ] Intro text about licenses/certifications/insurance…
- [ ] Qualification Type — single select, required, options GET `qualifications`; error "Qualification Type is required"
- [ ] Sub-type multi-select (label = selected type name, default "License"), required, placeholder "Search...", options GET `qualifications/subQualificationsByQualificationId/{typeId}`; error "Sub-Qualification Type is required"
- [ ] "Add Qualification" (+ icon) adds a row
- [ ] Remove (–) icon removes row; last row → toast "At least one qualification is required."
- [ ] Step errors: "At least one qualification is required" / "Select at least one sub-type for each qualification"
- [ ] Loader while types/sub-types load; edit mode pre-fetches sub-type options for each row

#### Work Experience card (`Forms/WorkExperienceForm.js`) — hidden for CUSTOMER
- [ ] Technology Stacks — single select, required, GET `categories`; placeholder "Select type of work..."; error "Technology Stacks are required"
- [ ] Category — single select, required, GET `categories/findAllVendorsByCategoryId/{categoryId}`; placeholder "Add additional types of work..."; error "Category is required"
- [ ] Skills — multi select, required, GET `categories/subCategoriesByCategoryId/{vendorId}`; placeholder "Add services..."; error "Please select at least 1 skill"; step error "Skills are required"
- [ ] Changing stack or category clears category + skills

#### Customer Files card (`Forms/DocumentsForm.js`)
- [ ] Title "Customer Files (Optional)" with section switch (on by default) to show/hide card body
- [ ] "Add File" drop zone ("or drag and drop here to upload"), any type, no size limit client-side; "(Max 5 at a time!)"
- [ ] Dropping >5 files keeps first 5 and shows red "Only first 5 files uploaded"
- [ ] Switch "supplier can view these files even before the work assignement" (isCustomerFilesVisible)
- [ ] "Document library" button opens library modal ("find a file you already uploaded")
- [ ] File tiles: image thumbnail or file-type icon, name (truncated, tooltip), eye icon opens in new tab ("View File"), trash icon removes
- [ ] Library files GET `workOrders/documents`

#### Document Library modal (`CreateWorkOrder/DocumentLibraryModal.js`)
- [ ] Title "Select from Library"; grid 2/3/4 columns by breakpoint
- [ ] Loading skeleton tiles; error text "Failed to load documents."; empty state "No documents found in the library."
- [ ] Click tile toggles selection (check badge, dimmed); hover shows file name
- [ ] "Cancel" closes; "Add {n} File(s)" (hidden when library empty, disabled when none selected) adds selected files to card and closes

### Step 2 — Scheduling

- [ ] Validates Location + Schedule; Tasks and Contacts cards shown on create only (hidden in edit mode)

The saved location will be permanently removed." / "Delete" → DELETE `locations/{id}`; toast "Location deleted successfully" / error message or "Failed to delete location"; clears selection if deleted one was selected; list refetched

- [ ] Gap check: selecting WORK_BETWEEN / WORK_ANYTIME renders no date/time fields (their forms are commented out) — Next then fails on start date/time
- [ ] HARD_START info banner: "Supplier for this type of work order must reach 15 minutes before the work order schedule time."
- [ ] Date — required, min = today in site time zone; inline "Date is required"
- [ ] Time — required, disabled until date chosen; past hours/minutes disabled when date is today (site TZ); inline "Time is required"
- [ ] Time Zone — read-only abbreviation; note "Time zone is set according to the work location."
- [ ] Step errors: "Start date is required", "Start time is required"
- [ ] (WorkBetween form, parked) fields: Any day between [date] and [date]; Only between the times [time] and [time]; Time Zone
- [ ] (WorkAnytime form, parked) fields: Any day between [date] at [time]; Only between the times [date] at [time]; Time Zone

- [ ] "Add Task" adds custom task (COMPLETE_CUSTOM_TASK) at top under "On Site"

- [ ] (Other task types supported by code: CALL → Phone (number) + Reason; COLLECT_SIGNATURE → "From (e.g. on-site manager)"; DOWNLOAD_FILE) — type selector commented out
- [ ] Trash icon deletes task
- [ ] "Add Notifications" adds email row; each "Email address for notification" (placeholder "Email...") validated: invalid → "Enter a valid email"; duplicate in task → "Email already added"; X removes row

- [ ] Non-customer: "Work Order Manager" checkbox enables select; options GET `/teams/teamMembers?teamIds={serviceDelivery,operations}`
- [ ] "Add Contact" adds contact block
- [ ] Contact fields: Name or Description (required marker, max 50), Title select (Project Manager, Location Contact, Resource Coordinator, Emergency Contact, "Techinal Help", Check-in / Check-out), Phone (required marker, default country AU), Email (optional, type email), Note (max 100)
- [ ] Trash icon removes contact
- [ ] Only contacts with a phone number are sent on save (no inline validation shown)

This action cannot be undone." / Cancel / Delete → DELETE `workOrders/{id}/purchaseOrder/{poId}`; toast "Purchase Order deleted successfully" / "Failed to delete Purchase Order"

## Client Work Board — Work Order List

### Work Orders listing
- Route: `/workBoard/workOrders`
- Access: module WORK_ORDERS isRead (ADMIN, CLIENT; sidebar "Work Board › Work Orders")
- Source: `src/views/WorkOrder/WorkOrderList/index.js`, `WorkOrderListingHeader.js`, `WorkOrderTable.js`, `WorkOrderFilters.js`

#### Header
- [ ] Title "Work Orders"; action area hidden for FREELANCER
- [ ] "Create New Work Order" — shown with WORK_ORDERS write access; if user status ≠ APPROVED opens Complete Profile modal; else resets form and goes to `/workOrder/create/summary`
- [ ] "Generate Invoice" — shown only when rows selected AND status filter includes APPROVED AND an Engineer (provider) filter AND engineer currency filter set AND ENGINEER_INVOICE write access
- [ ] Generate Invoice with any selected WO whose engineer currency ≠ filter → modal "Currency Mismatch": "The following selected work order(s) has/have an engineer currency that does not match the selected {CUR} filter:" + "#ref, …" + "Please deselect it/them before generating the invoice." (OK / Close)
- [ ] No mismatch → opens Generate Engineer Invoice modal (role FREELANCER, selected IDs, engineer, currency)

#### Toolbar
- [ ] "Add Filter" opens Search Criteria drawer
- [ ] Applied-filter chips "{Prefix}: {value}" with × to remove: Country, City, Provider, Team, Client (per id), Team (per id), Project, Currency, Engineer Currency, Status (only when applied from drawer; "Approved"-style casing), Created By, ID, Technology Stack, Category, Skill (per id), Rate "$min - $max", Date (schedule range), Date (created range), Payment, Requests, Cancelled By, Cancel Reason
- [ ] Removing chip resets page to 0; Country removal also clears City; Technology Stack removal clears Category + Skills; Category removal clears Skills; Client removal clears project if it belonged to that client; Cancelled By removal clears reason; Engineer Currency removal clears selected currency; Provider removal clears selected engineer
- [ ] "Customize View" button opens Customize Columns drawer
- [ ] Search box placeholder "Search by WO title or reference ID"; debounced 500ms; resets to page 1

#### Status tabs (FilterChip row)
- [ ] Draft (DRAFT), Submitted (PENDING_APPROVAL), Published (PUBLISHED, default), Assigned, Inprogress (INPROGRESS), Work Done (WORKDONE), Verified, Approved, Invoiced, Paid, Cancelled, Issue (INCOMPLETE), All
- [ ] Counts from GET `workOrders/statusCounts` (drawer filters + search applied); while searching, active tab shows search total
- [ ] All chip always shows grand total; active when no status or multiple statuses
- [ ] Clicking tab updates `?filter=` URL param, redux status, clears appliedFromDrawer
- [ ] `?filter=A,B` on load preselects statuses
- [ ] "INFLIGHT" status (URL) filters client-side to WOs starting today or tomorrow

#### Table (BaseDataTableV2)
- [ ] API GET `workOrders?page&limit&status&search&sortBy=orderStartDate,orderStartTime&sortOrder&columns={visible fields}&…filters`
- [ ] Only visible columns' data requested
- [ ] Checkbox selection (feeds Generate Invoice); checkbox + ID columns sticky
- [ ] Server-side pagination, page sizes 10/20/50/100 (default 50); page persisted in redux
- [ ] Server-side sort only on Schedule (asc default / desc)
- [ ] Row count: search or All → total; drawer single status → that status count; APPROVED → total; else status count
- [ ] Full-page loader while list loading

- [ ] Engineer Currency

## Engineer — Find Job

### Jobs listing
- Route: `/jobs`

- [ ] On load asks browser geolocation; coordinates reverse-geocoded via Google Geocode API; one retry after 3s on failure; loader while locating
- [ ] Coverage coordinates default to browser location unless a coverage search was applied
- [ ] Full-page loader while AVAILABLE jobs (socket hook) or other-tab jobs load
- [ ] 401 on job fetch → logout
- [ ] Company member engineers (`isCompanyMember`): Coverage Area control hidden; tabs Available, Routed, Countered, Requested, Paid hidden; default tab Assigned

#### Header
- [ ] "Coverage Area ({radius} Km)" pill (default 100) toggles dropdown (does not close on outside click)
- [ ] Dropdown: radio "On Site Job" (only option), address search (Google Places, helper "Country, City, state or zip"), range slider 0–500 km with "Coverage Area ({radius} km)", "Cancel" closes, "Apply" refetches jobs + counts and closes
- [ ] Selecting an address sets coverage coordinates + `coverageAreaSearchText` (then `isCoverageApplied=true` sent)
- [ ] Applied-filter chips with ×: Technology Stack, Category, Skill (per id), Rate "$min - $max", Date "start - end" (Any when blank), Payment, Requests; removing resets to page 0; Technology Stack removal clears Category + Skills; Category removal clears Skills
- [ ] Filters icon button (tooltip "Filters") highlighted when filters applied; opens Filters drawer
- [ ] Search "Search by WO title or reference ID" — debounced 500ms, resets to page 0

#### Status tabs
- [ ] Available (AVAILABLE, default for independent engineers), Routed, Countered (PENDING + isCounter true), Requested (PENDING + isCounter false), Assigned, Inprogress, Work Done, Incomplete, Approved, Paid, All ("")
- [ ] `?filter=` URL param kept in sync (COUNTER / REQUESTED / ALL mapped to API values)
- [ ] Counts GET `workOrders/jobsCount` (lat, lng, radius, filters, search, isCoverageApplied); keys AVAILABLE, ROUTED, COUNTERED, REQUESTED, ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, WORKDONE, INCOMPLETE, APPROVED, PAID, ALL; while searching active tab shows row count
- [ ] Changing tab resets to page 0
- [ ] AVAILABLE tab data GET `workOrders/findJobs`; other tabs POST `workOrders/engineer` (page, limit, search, coverage, filters, status, isCounter) → `workOrdersWithDistance`, `totalCount`
- [ ] Live: new published job prepended; job taken/updated removed from Available or merged in other tabs; removed job disappears (socket events above)

#### Bulk action buttons (above table)
- [ ] Routed tab: "Declined", "Accept" buttons
- [ ] "Withdraw" (only if status COUNTER/REQUESTED — never true since API status is PENDING; verify)
- [ ] "Export" always shown
- [ ] Assigned tab: "Cancel Assignment"
- [ ] Gap check: none of these buttons has an onClick handler (no action performed)

#### Table
- [ ] Checkbox selection; server pagination 10/20/50/100 (default 10); page stored in `filters.pagination`
- [ ] Columns: ID (reference ID → `/workOrder/details/{id}`), Incident ID, Job Title (link; right-click → "Open" / "Open in New Tab"), Location ("addressLine1, country"), Status (derived: offer DECLINED/ROUTED; ACCEPTED→Routed; PENDING+counter→Countered; PENDING→Requested; VERIFIED→Work Done; + sub-status badge except PO_PENDING/REVIEWED), Type of Work (or "not specified"), Schedule ("MM/DD/YYYY at hh:mm A (TZ)"), Pay (engineer pay type: "Hourly @ CUR rate (min n hrs)", "Half Day @ … & Additional hrs @ …", "Full Day @ …", "Fixed @ …"; currency defaults "USD"; value priority assigned offer → own offer → WO engineer rate → engineer's billing rates), Distance ("n.nn km"), Company (creator's company), Buyer Rating (static rating widget), Requests (totalOffers)

#### Filters drawer (`FindJobFilters.js`)
- [ ] Title "Filters"; "Apply Filters" (disabled until a filter set / while loading), Cancel, "Reset Filters" link (blue when filters applied)
- [ ] Start Date / End Date (range constrained)
- [ ] Technology Stacks (GET `categories`) → Category (GET `categories/findAllVendorsByCategoryId/{id}`) → Skills (multi, GET `categories/subCategoriesByCategoryId/{id}`); parent change clears children

# Frontend — Work Order Details, Offers & Chat

## Work Order Details — Page Shell

### Job Details container
- Route: `/workOrder/<tab>/:id` (parent route `/workOrder`, `module: ""` = any authenticated user; child tabs filtered by role + WO status, see Tabs strip)
- Access: CLIENT, FREELANCER, CUSTOMER (ADMIN is not in any tab's `roles` list, so ADMIN sees header + action bar but no tabs)
- Source: `src/views/FindJob/JobDetails/index.js` (page), `src/pages/FindJobPages/JobDetailsPage.js`
- [ ] On load calls `GET workOrders/:id` (react-query key `jobDetail`, staleTime 0) and `GET workOrders/actions/allowed/:id` (userAllowedAction flags)
- [ ] Both queries refetch whenever `:id` or the pathname (tab) changes
- [ ] Full-page `BaseLoader` shown while WO detail or allowed-actions loading
- [ ] Error or loading shows `BaseNotFound` with API `response.data.message`
- [ ] WO detail stored in Redux `workOrder.workOrderDetails`; cleared on unmount (`clearWorkOrderDetail`)
- [ ] Header + ActionBar are sticky on ≥sm screens (top offset shifts when the global banner is visible)
- [ ] Socket: emits `subscribeWorkOrder` `{engineerId, workOrderId}`; listens `workOrder:update:<id>` (src/hooks/useWorkOrderSocket.js)
- [ ] Socket payload `forceUpdate: true` → refetch allowed actions + `GET workOrders/estimatedPayment/:id` + invalidate `jobDetail`
- [ ] Socket payload `updatedWorkOrder` / `updatedUserAllowedAction` merged into Redux detail / allowed-action state live
- [ ] Socket: emits `subscribeUser` `{userId}`; listens `workOrder:list:update`, `workOrder:list:remove`, `workOrder:published`
- [ ] Access revoked → `navigateWithReload('/workOrder/details/:id')` (hard reload)

## Work Order Details — Header

### Header (title / status / cards)
- Source: `src/views/WorkJobDetails/WorkJobDetailsHeader.js`, `TitleCard.js`, `ScheduleCard.js`, `PaymentCard.js`, `LocationCard.js`, `WorkingHoursCard.js`, `UserDetailsCard.js`, `src/components/CurrentStatusCard`
- [ ] Title card: status badge, sub-status badge (arrow →), `#<referenceId>`, `On MM/DD/YYYY` (createdAt), WO title
- [ ] Query `?fromCustomerBoard=true` → badge shows `customerStatus` (falls back to status) and `customerSubStatus`
- [ ] Non-CLIENT title status overrides (priority): isDeclined → "DECLINED"; isRouted/isAccepted → "ROUTED"; isCounter → "COUNTER"; isRequested → "REQUESTED"
- [ ] Any role: cancelled by client/engineer → "CANCELLED"; FREELANCER company owner of WO + status INPROGRESS → "ASSIGNED"
- [ ] FREELANCER/CUSTOMER: VERIFIED displays as WORKDONE
- [ ] FREELANCER (and company owner): sub-status REVIEWED hidden (treated as null)
- [ ] Status CANCELLED → sub-status badge shows `customerStatus`
- [ ] Sub-status PO_PENDING → plain PO Pending badge (no tooltip)
- [ ] Sub-status tooltip text: customerSubStatus CANCELLED → "Cancelled, but payment is still due to the customer"
- [ ] Tooltip ON_THE_WAY: FREELANCER "Marked at: dddd, DD/MM/YYYY at hh:mm A (TZ)" else "Provider is on the way to the work order location"
- [ ] Tooltip RUNNING_LATE: FREELANCER "Marked at: …" else "Provider is running late"
- [ ] Tooltip CHECK_IN/CHECK_OUT: completed-task timestamp "dddd, DD/MM/YYYY at hh:mm A (TZ)" in WO timezone
- [ ] Card 1 — status WORKDONE/VERIFIED/APPROVED/PAID → Working Hours card (`totalHours` formatted)
- [ ] Card 1 — cancelled by engineer/client (and not assigned-to-another-engineer) → Cancelled card; tagline "The work order has been cancelled by engineer." / "The work order has been cancelled by client." (when `showCancellationReason`) else "The work order has been cancelled."
- [ ] Card 1 — otherwise Schedule card: "Schedule: Work Between" or "Schedule: Fixed Start", `MM/DD/YYYY h:mm A (TZ)` (UTC formatted)
- [ ] Schedule card INCOMPLETE → "Status: INCOMPLETE" + "Working Hours: X.XX Hours" (sum of timeLogs)
- [ ] Schedule card WORKDONE+REVIEWED → "Status: REVIEWED"; CLIENT sees "Provider has reviewed the work order"
- [ ] Card 2 — status CANCELLED(by client)/INCOMPLETE/VERIFIED/APPROVED/PAID/WORKDONE → Payment card "Payment: <label>" + `<currency> <amount>`
- [ ] Payment label: REVIEWED+CLIENT "Reviewed"; WORKDONE/INCOMPLETE "Estimated"; VERIFIED CLIENT "Verified"; APPROVED "Approved"; CANCELLED "Pending"; VERIFIED FREELANCER "Estimated"
- [ ] Payment amount: CUSTOMER sees `clientRates`, others `engineerRates`; "Pending" shows grandTotal else totalWorkOrderCost (2 dp); from `GET workOrders/estimatedPayment/:id`
- [ ] Payment amount hidden for freelance-company engineer (FREELANCER, not company owner, assigned member / WO assigned to a company)
- [ ] Card 2 — WORKDONE+REVIEWED for CLIENT → Schedule card; cancelled by engineer → Schedule card; else Location card
- [ ] Location card: "Location" + addressLine1 link → opens `https://maps.google.com?q=<lat>,<lng>` in new tab
- [ ] User card FREELANCER: "Project Manager" + WO manager (fallback createdBy) name → profile link; avatar
- [ ] User card CLIENT/CUSTOMER with engineer: company name + "ID: <companyId>" link to owner profile (not CUSTOMER), engineer name + "ID: <id>" → profile `?direct=true`
- [ ] Engineer phone shown as WhatsApp link `https://wa.me/<digits>` (hidden for CUSTOMER in non-company branch, hidden if engineer deleted)
- [ ] Deleted engineer: name grey, not a link, no ID, "deleted user" pill (unless name already "Deleted User")
- [ ] No engineer: "Engineer: Unassigned"
- [ ] CLIENT (not CUSTOMER), no engineer/company: offers summary link → `/workOrder/providers/:id?tab=pending`; text "No Request" or "<n> Requests, <n> Counters, <n> Routed"
- [ ] Routed member (company-owner-created ROUTED/ACCEPTED/ASSIGNED offer to another member): extra row "Assigned Engineer" (FREELANCER owner) / "Field Engineer" (others) + profile link; hidden for non-owner freelancers and for the routed engineer themself

## Work Order Details — Tabs strip & alerts

### Tabs strip
- Source: `src/views/WorkJobDetails/WorkJobTabs.js`, `src/routes.js` (`/workOrder` childs)
- [ ] Details — `/workOrder/details/:id` — CLIENT, FREELANCER, CUSTOMER — all statuses (DRAFT…CANCELLED)
- [ ] Payment — `/workOrder/payment/:id` — CLIENT, FREELANCER, CUSTOMER — all statuses; hidden for FREELANCER who is `isAssignedMember`
- [ ] Providers — `/workOrder/providers/:id` — CLIENT only — all statuses
- [ ] Messages — `/workOrder/messages/:id` — CLIENT, FREELANCER, CUSTOMER — every status except DRAFT
- [ ] Messages hidden for FREELANCER unless isWorkOrderAlreadyAssigned / isRouted / isAccepted / isChatInitiated / isAssignedMember
- [ ] Job Sign-Off — `/workOrder/signoffSheet/:id` — CLIENT, FREELANCER, CUSTOMER — WORKDONE, VERIFIED, APPROVED, INVOICED, DISPUTED, PAID
- [ ] Feedback — `/workOrder/feedback/:id` — CLIENT, FREELANCER — WORKDONE, APPROVED, VERIFIED, INVOICED, DISPUTED, PAID; FREELANCER needs `isEngineerFeedbackVisible`
- [ ] History — `/workOrder/history/:id` — CLIENT only — all statuses
- [ ] Freelancer whose WO went to another engineer: Job Sign-Off and Feedback tabs hidden
- [ ] Active tab derived from URL path on load
- [ ] Switching tab saves chat draft (ChatTab ref `saveDraft`) before navigating
- [ ] Switching away from Messages strips `?engineerId=`; switching to Providers adds `?tab=nearby` if absent; other tabs strip `?tab=`
- [ ] Tabs scrollable with scroll buttons (also on mobile); Messages tab panel stays mounted (hidden) to keep chat state

### Alerts above tabs
- [ ] CLIENT + WO has notes → "Note:" alert with notes (multi-line preserved, not closable)
- [ ] CANCELLED + `showCancellationReason` → "Cancellation Reason:" error alert: "<Label>: <reason>" / "<Label>" / "No reason provided" (labels from `CANCELLATION_REASON_LABELS`)
- [ ] isAccepted && not yet assigned → info "Work Order Accepted" / "Waiting for the client’s confirmation…"
- [ ] FREELANCER assigned-to-another-engineer (had requested/countered/routed/accepted, not declined) → info "Work Order Assigned" / "This work order has been assigned to another engineer. You can remove it from your listing." + "Remove from List" button
- [ ] Remove from List confirm modal: title "Remove from List", body "Are you sure you want to remove this work order from your list?", buttons "Cancel" / "Remove from List" (loading state)
- [ ] Confirm → `POST offers/engineer/notInterested` `{workOrderId, status:"DECLINED", offerId?: routedOfferId}` → navigate `/jobs?filter=ROUTED|COUNTER|REQUESTED` (per prior state) or `/jobs`
- [ ] Remove from List error toast: API message or "Unable to remove from list"
- [ ] INCOMPLETE → error alert: title = latest incomplete reason label (`IncompleteWorkOrderReasonType`), message "Details: <reason>" (if reason)

## Work Order Details — Action Bar

### Action bar (all roles)
- Source: `src/views/WorkJobDetails/ActionBar.js`
- Flags from `GET workOrders/actions/allowed/:id`; permission modules: `ACTION_REVIEW_WORK_ORDER`, `ACTION_VERIFY_WORK_ORDER`, `ACTION_APPROVE_WORK_ORDER`, `ACTION_CANCEL_WORK_ORDER` (write access)
- [ ] CLIENT: "Copy" button (no handler — placeholder)
- [ ] CLIENT: "Save as Template" button (no handler — placeholder)
- [ ] CLIENT: "Tags" button (no handler — placeholder)
- [ ] All roles: "Print" button (no handler — placeholder)
- [ ] All roles, status ASSIGNED/INPROGRESS/INCOMPLETE: "Report problem" (no handler — placeholder)
- [ ] CLIENT: "Add Note" → opens Add Note modal
- [ ] CLIENT: "Create New Task" → opens Task Board AddEditTaskModal pre-linked to this WO (`src/views/TaskBoard/components/AddEditTaskModal`)
- [ ] CLIENT: "Mark PO Pending" when status VERIFIED (or customerStatus between VERIFIED…APPROVED) and subStatus ≠ PO_PENDING → `POST workOrders/:id/poPending`; toast "Action Completed Successfully"; error toast = API message; button loading
- [ ] CLIENT + status ASSIGNED + ACTION_CANCEL_WORK_ORDER write: "Cancel" → Cancel by Client modal
- [ ] "Undo Approval" button is commented out (not rendered)

### Action bar — FREELANCER, status PUBLISHED
- [ ] "Not Interested" (FREELANCER, isRouted or isAccepted, not already assigned; any status) → `POST offers/engineer/notInterested` `{workOrderId, status:"DECLINED", offerId?}`; toast "Job Updated Successfully"; refetch WO + allowed actions; error toast = API message
- [ ] "Counter Offer" shown only when: not (counter-offers enabled && isRequested), not (counter-offers enabled && cancelled by engineer), not isDeclined, not already assigned, not isCounter, not isAccepted
- [ ] Counter Offer: user status ≠ APPROVED → Complete Profile modal instead; else opens counter-offer modal in "add" mode
- [ ] Counter modal chosen by `engineerPaymentType`: HOURLY/FTH → CounterOfferModal; FIXED → Fixed; HALF_DAY → HalfDay; FULL_DAY → FullDay
- [ ] "Accept" (isRouted; for company owner/member only when WO has engineerPaymentType + an engineer rate) → profile check → Request Offer modal (accept mode)
- [ ] "Request" shown when not declined/routed/counter/cancelled-by-engineer/requested/accepted (company owner additionally needs engineer payment type + rate) → profile check → Request Offer modal
- [ ] "Withdraw Request" (isRequested, not assigned) → WO Confirmation modal "Withdraw Request"
- [ ] "Edit Counter Offer" (isCounter, not assigned) → counter modal in "edit" mode
- [ ] "Withdraw Counter Offer" (isCounter, not assigned) → Withdraw Counter Offer modal

### Action bar — FREELANCER, status ASSIGNED
- [ ] "Cancel Assignment" (FREELANCER, subStatus NOT_CONFIRMED/CONFIRMED/READY_TO_GO, status ≠ CANCELLED) → Cancel Assignment modal
- [ ] Company owner who is WO's company assignee: if an ASSIGNED member offer exists → read-only chip "Assigned to: <First Last>" (profile link)
- [ ] Company owner, confirmed (isWorkOrderConfirmByEngineer) and no member → "Assign to Member" → Assign to Member modal
- [ ] Company owner, not confirmed/cancelled/started → "Confirm" → WO Confirmation modal "Confirm Work Order"
- [ ] Company owner not the company assignee → no buttons
- [ ] Individual engineer / assigned member, not confirmed/cancelled/started → "Confirm" → WO Confirmation modal
- [ ] Confirmed && not started → "Ready to go" → WO Confirmation modal "Ready to go"; disabled until within window
- [ ] Allowed window (WO timezone): now > start − 2h default; − 4h if subStatus READY_TO_GO; − 36h if CONFIRMED; false if no start date/time

### Action bar — FREELANCER, started (isStartTime)
- [ ] "Mark Complete" (status INCOMPLETE/INPROGRESS + isCheckOut) → Mark Complete modal
- [ ] "On the Way" (subStatus READY_TO_GO, not CANCELLED) → On The Way modal; disabled outside window
- [ ] "Running Late" (subStatus READY_TO_GO or ON_THE_WAY, not CANCELLED) → Running Late modal; disabled outside window
- [ ] "Check In" (status ASSIGNED and subStatus ≠ READY_TO_GO) → Check In modal
- [ ] "Check In" (isCheckOut + status INCOMPLETE/INPROGRESS — re-check-in after checkout) → Check In modal
- [ ] "Check out" (isCheckIn + status INCOMPLETE/INPROGRESS) → Check Out modal

### Action bar — FREELANCER feedback
- [ ] "Leave Feedback" (assigned engineer on WO, not yet given, `canEngineerGiveFeedback`) → opens Feedback modal
- [ ] "Modify Feedback" (assigned engineer, already given, within window) → Feedback modal in edit mode
- [ ] Auto-open feedback modal for FREELANCER on APPROVED if not given and user is not company member/owner (once per WO, Redux `feedbackModalOpenByWorkOrder`)

### Action bar — CLIENT / CUSTOMER
- [ ] CLIENT, status CANCELLED, customerStatus null: "Mark Payable" → confirm modal (renders only with ACTION_VERIFY_WORK_ORDER write)
- [ ] Mark Payable confirm: title "Confirm Payable", body "Are you sure you want to mark this work order as Payable?" / "This action cannot be undone.", buttons "Cancel"/"Mark Payable" → `POST workOrders/:id/markPayable` → navigate `/workOrder/details/:id?fromCustomerBoard=true`, toast "Work Order Payable"
- [ ] CLIENT, status APPROVED, ACTION_APPROVE write: "Mark Dispute" → confirm title "Confirm Dispute", body "Are you sure you want to mark this work order as Disputed?" / "This action cannot be undone.", confirm "Confirm Dispute" → `PATCH workOrders/:id/status` `{status:"DISPUTED"}`; toast "Work Order Disputed"
- [ ] CLIENT/CUSTOMER, status PUBLISHED or PENDING_APPROVAL: "Unpublish" (CLIENT on PUBLISHED) / "Move to Draft" (CUSTOMER, or PENDING_APPROVAL) → Unpublish modal
- [ ] CLIENT, not given feedback, status WORKDONE/APPROVED/VERIFIED/INVOICED/DISPUTED/PAID: "Leave Feedback"
- [ ] CLIENT/CUSTOMER with feedback given (same statuses): "Modify Feedback" → edit mode
- [ ] Auto-open feedback modal for non-FREELANCER on WORKDONE if actor hasn't given feedback (not for CUSTOMER — modal never renders for CUSTOMER)
- [ ] Status DRAFT or subStatus CANCELLED: "Publish" (CUSTOMER on DRAFT: "Submit") → DRAFT: `POST workOrders/publish/:id`; subStatus CANCELLED: `GET workOrders/republish/:id`
- [ ] Publish success toast: CUSTOMER on DRAFT "Work order has been submitted for acceptance." else "Work Order Published"
- [ ] Publish error with `missingFields`: toast "Cannot publish. Please complete:\n• <field>…" (8s autoClose, multi-line); else API message or "Could not publish this work order."
- [ ] CLIENT, status PENDING_APPROVAL: "Review & Publish" → navigate `/workOrder/edit/summary/:id`
- [ ] Status INCOMPLETE: "Mark Complete" → Mark Complete modal
- [ ] Status WORKDONE: "Mark Incomplete" → Mark Incomplete modal
- [ ] WORKDONE + subStatus null + ACTION_REVIEW write: "Review" → Approve modal (review stage)
- [ ] WORKDONE + subStatus REVIEWED + ACTION_VERIFY write: "Verify" → Approve modal (verify stage)
- [ ] VERIFIED + ACTION_APPROVE write: "Approve" → Approve modal (approve stage)
- [ ] Note: `handleMarkCompleteButton` guard (outstanding COMPLETE_CUSTOM_TASK → toast "Complete the remaining task(s) first: <titles>") is defined but NOT wired — Mark Complete buttons open the modal directly (verify server-side rule instead)

## Work Order Details — Messages Tab (Chat)

### Messages tab — access & conversation selection
- Route: `/workOrder/messages/:id[?engineerId=<id>]` — CLIENT, CUSTOMER, FREELANCER (freelancer only when assigned / routed / accepted / chat initiated / assigned member); every status except DRAFT
- Source: `src/views/WorkJobDetails/Tabs/ChatTab/index.js`, `components/Chat.js`, `components/ChatUsers.js`, `ChatFileUploadModal.js`, `src/hooks/useChatSocket.js`
- [ ] Tab panel stays mounted when hidden; draft saved per user when switching tabs
- [ ] Sidebar (conversation list) shown for CLIENT, and for freelance company owner when list non-empty; hidden for individual engineer / CUSTOMER (single-thread view)
- [ ] Chat users `GET chats/users/:workOrderId/:companyId` (CLIENT or company owner only); refetched when tab becomes visible / engineer or company assignment changes
- [ ] CLIENT default conversation: assigned engineer / vendor / routed member if assigned, else first active (non-deleted) user; `?engineerId=` overrides (loads `GET users/:id?direct=true` then opens)
- [ ] FREELANCER (individual): auto-opens own thread with client team
- [ ] Company owner: opens cached room immediately; if no chat users, auto-opens own thread
- [ ] CUSTOMER: single engineer-less room with the team; joins on socket `connect` (and immediately if connected), re-joins on reconnect
- [ ] Opening a conversation emits `leaveChatRoom` then `joinGroupChat` `{workOrderId, companyId, engineerId, clientId (acting user), authUserId}`; `joinSuccess` sets room, zeroes its unread count, refreshes message notifications and chat users; `joinError` logged
- [ ] Deleted user selected → panel "This account has been deleted" / "<Name> is no longer available to chat with." (no join)
- [ ] No conversation selected (desktop) → "No Conversation Selected" / "Choose a message from the list to get started."
- [ ] Socket emits `chatRoomVisibility` `{visible, conversationId}` when tab visible + document visible; re-sent on reconnect; false on unmount (controls server unread/notification behaviour)

### Conversation list (ChatUsers)
- [ ] Header "Chat" + search icon (decorative — no search implemented)
- [ ] Loading: 6 skeleton rows; empty: "No messages yet."
- [ ] Row: avatar, name (company owner's own entry shows client company name/logo), time (today → clock "h:mm am", older → "DD/MM")
- [ ] Preview: "You: …" or "<sender first name>: …"; voice → "Voice message"; location → "Location"
- [ ] Draft present → "Draft: <text>" in primary colour
- [ ] Unread: bold name; 1 unread → dot; >1 → count pill (from `unreadCountUpdated` socket + initial API counts)
- [ ] Active row highlighted; hover card effect
- [ ] Deleted user row: "deleted user" pill, 50% opacity, not clickable
- [ ] CLIENT: rows for users outside the assignment (engineer, vendor owner, routed member) are blurred once WO is assigned; customer chat never blurred
- [ ] New messages move that conversation to top (`receiveMessage`, own sends)
- [ ] Mobile: list hidden while a conversation is open; back arrow in chat header returns to list

### Chat thread
- [ ] Header: CLIENT / owner viewing others → counterpart avatar + name (link to profile, new-tab capable); engineer view → client company logo + name (link to counterpart profile)
- [ ] History `GET chats/:roomId?cursor&limit=10` (infinite, newest page first; no refetch on focus; stale 60 s); loading skeleton bubbles
- [ ] Scroll up (wheel/touch within 150 px of top) loads one older page, 500 ms cooldown; "Load older messages" button when more exist; spinner while fetching; scroll position anchored
- [ ] Messages grouped by date with pill "Today" / "Yesterday" / "DD/MM/YYYY"
- [ ] Consecutive messages from same sender collapse (only first shows avatar + name + time)
- [ ] Meta line: sender name (link) + time ("h:mm AM" today, "Yesterday h:mm", "DD/MM/YYYY h:mm") + "Edited"
- [ ] "Last Read" divider before first unread message; auto-scrolls to it on open; otherwise scrolls to bottom on first load / new message
- [ ] "↓" scroll-to-bottom button when > 100 px from bottom
- [ ] Incoming bubbles grey (left, avatar); own bubbles blue (right, no avatar)
- [ ] Message body rendered as sanitized markdown/HTML (bold, italic, underline, strike, lists); bare URLs auto-linked (open new tab)
- [ ] Location message ("lat,lng") → embedded map card; click opens `https://www.google.com/maps?q=<lat>,<lng>&z=18`; invalid → "Invalid location" (no web UI to send a location)
- [ ] Deleted message → italic "This message was deleted" (no actions)
- [ ] Read ticks (own messages): double-check grey → primary once any recipient has read; tooltip "Seen by:" + reader names (profile links)
- [ ] Socket `messageReadByUser` / `allMessagesReadByUser` update ticks live
- [ ] No typing indicator implemented
- [ ] Socket `sendError` → toast API message or "You are not authorized to send messages in this chat."

### Composer — send, format, attachments, voice
- [ ] Quill editor; Enter sends, Shift+Enter newline (Enter inside a list continues the list)
- [ ] Pencil icon "Show Formatting Options" toggles toolbar (bold, italic, underline, strike, ordered/bullet list); state saved per WO
- [ ] Leading/trailing blank paragraphs trimmed; blank message with no files not sent
- [ ] Send icon (tooltip "Send") shown when text or files present; otherwise microphone (tooltip "Voice message")
- [ ] Send → (if files) `POST chats/files` multipart `files` + `workOrderId` → then socket `sendMessage` `{conversationId, senderId, message, workOrderId, engineerId, digitalAssetIds, messageType, replyToId?}`; clears text, reply and draft
- [ ] Upload in progress → spinner replaces send button; paperclip hidden
- [ ] Paperclip (tooltip "Add files") → "Upload Files" modal: drag & drop zone "Drag & Drop files here or click below", "Choose Files" button (clicking anywhere in the dropzone opens the picker — the button itself has a no-op handler), multi-file; duplicates (same name+size) ignored; list shows name + image preview or type icon (pdf/word/excel/powerpoint/zip/other); "Cancel" / "Done"
- [ ] No client-side file type or size limits on chat attachments (server has no multer limit either)
- [ ] Pasting an image/screenshot into the editor adds it as an attachment instead of inline
- [ ] Attachment strip above editor: thumbnails or file icons; hover × removes; draft updated
- [ ] Sent attachments render via image grid / file tiles with download link
- [ ] Voice: mic starts recording (asks mic permission; denial cancels), live waveform, mm:ss timer; × cancels; paper-plane sends → uploads `voice_<ts>.webm|ogg|m4a` via `POST chats/files` then `sendMessage` with `messageType:"voice"`
- [ ] Voice playback: play/pause, waveform (seek by click/drag/touch), duration/elapsed, speed cycle 1x → 1.5x → 2x
- [ ] Drafts: per work order + per conversation (text, files, toolbar state) kept in Redux across tab/conversation switches; cleared on send

### Message actions — reply, edit, delete, reactions, emoji
- [ ] Hover actions on any non-deleted message: 😊 react, reply; own messages also edit (not for voice) and delete
- [ ] Reply → reply card above composer (sender, date/time, 2-line preview or "Attachment", × "Cancel reply"); editor focused; sent message shows quoted preview that scrolls to and flashes the original on click
- [ ] Edit → inline editor with Cancel / Save; empty text blocked "Empty message can not be saved." (allowed if message has files) → socket `editMessage` `{messageId, senderId, newContent}`; `messageEdited` updates and shows "Edited"/"edited"
- [ ] Delete → confirm "Delete Message" / "Are you sure you want to delete this message? This cannot be undone." / "Cancel" / "Delete" → socket `deleteMessage` `{messageId, senderId}`; `messageDeleted` marks deleted
- [ ] React → quick bar 👍 ❤️ 😂 😮 😢 🙏 + "+" full emoji picker (search "Search emoji…", 310×400, flips above when no room below); closes on outside click
- [ ] Reaction pills grouped by emoji, count shown when > 1, tooltip lists reactor names; clicking a pill toggles own reaction; "Add reaction" pill button
- [ ] Socket `toggleReaction` `{messageId, userId, emoji, conversationId}`; `reactionUpdated` refreshes reactions live
- [ ] Live: `receiveMessage` appends to open room (deduped by id) and bumps conversation in list

### Chat hooks
- Source: `src/hooks/useChatSocket.js`, `src/hooks/useInbox.js`
- [ ] useChatSocket listens: `messageReadByUser`, `unreadCountUpdated`, `receiveMessage`, `allMessagesReadByUser`, `messageEdited`, `messageDeleted`, `reactionUpdated`, `sendError`, `joinSuccess`, `joinError`, `newParticipant`, `connect`
- [ ] useChatSocket emits: `joinGroupChat`, `leaveChatRoom`, `chatRoomVisibility`, `sendMessage`, `editMessage`, `deleteMessage`, `toggleReaction`
- [ ] Only its own `unreadCountUpdated` handler removed on unmount (top-bar inbox badge keeps working)
- [ ] useInbox (top-bar inbox): `GET inbox?cursor&limit=20` when dropdown opens; "load more" by cursor; `unreadCountUpdated` → refetch if count rose / conversation unknown, local decrement if fell, no-op if unchanged

# Frontend — Invoices, Company, Customers & Permissions

> Access model: `RequireAuth` redirects unauthenticated users to `/getStarted`; a user whose `userModules` don't include any module of the matched route set is redirected to `/unauthorized`. Per-button gating uses `getPermissionForModule(<MODULE>)` → `hasReadAccess / hasWriteAccess / hasUpdateAccess / hasDeleteAccess`.

## Invoices

Sidebar group "Invoices" (module `INVOICE`, icon `fa-file-invoice-dollar`), visible to CLIENT, CUSTOMER (isShowCustomer) and ADMIN. Children: "Finance Dashboard" (`/invoices/analytics`, CLIENT/ADMIN — covered elsewhere), "Customer Invoices", "Engineer Invoices" (CLIENT/ADMIN only), hidden `edit/:id`.

### Customer Invoices list
- Route: `/invoices/customer` — module `CUSTOMER_INVOICE`; CLIENT, ADMIN, CUSTOMER

- [ ] ("Invite Workforce" header button is commented out — confirm NOT rendered)

- [ ] API: `GET /invoices/statusCount?...same filters` feeds chip counts

# Frontend — Profiles, Workforce, Admin & Task Board

## Profile Details (viewing another user's profile)

### Profile Details shell & tabs
- Route: `/user/details/<tab>/:id` (optional `?direct=true`) — module `USERS` (isRead). Children: `profileInformation/:id` (ADMIN, FREELANCER), `profile/:id` (ADMIN, CLIENT, FREELANCER, CUSTOMER), `companyDetails/:id` (ADMIN, company-owner only), `bankAccounts/:id` (ADMIN), `workers/:id` (ADMIN, company-owner only), `notes/:id` / `recommendations/:id` / `privateFeedback/:id` (CLIENT, placeholder `<h1>` only), `messages/:id` (CLIENT, FREELANCER, placeholder). Availability tab commented out; Permissions tab commented out.
- Source: `src/views/ProfileDetails/index.js`, `ProfileTab.js`, page `src/pages/ProfilePages/ProfileDetailPage.js`
- [ ] Loads user via `GET users/:id` (`?direct=true` when query param set); no retry on 4xx, max 2 retries otherwise
- [ ] 403 response → centered "Unauthorized" heading
- [ ] FREELANCER viewing another FREELANCER who is not a member of their company and not their own company owner → "Unauthorized"
- [ ] Non-ADMIN viewer (any viewed role) OR any viewer of a CUSTOMER → single tab-less role page (CustomerProfile / ClientProfile / FreelancerProfile); no tabs rendered
- [ ] Role page: any URL other than `/user/details/profile/:id` redirects (replace) to `/user/details/profile/:id` preserving query string
- [ ] ADMIN viewing CLIENT/FREELANCER → tabbed view; tabs filtered by route `roles` including viewer role
- [ ] "Personal Info" tab hidden when viewer is FREELANCER and not self, or viewed user is company owner
- [ ] "Company Details" and "Workers" tabs only when viewed user `isCompanyOwner`
- [ ] Deep link to a tab not allowed for viewer → redirect (replace) to first allowed tab, preserving `?direct=true`
- [ ] Clicking tab navigates to `/user/details/<tab>/:id` (query string dropped)
- [ ] Qualification types fetched `GET qualifications` only for tabbed view (map name→id: equipment, background check, licences, certification, insurance)
- [ ] "Approve Profile" button (top-right of tabs) — ADMIN only, viewed status in PENDING / SUBMITTED / UNAPPROVE → opens ApproveEngineerModal (see Admin › Approve Engineer modal); refetches user on success
- [ ] Sticky header offset changes when announcement banner visible

# Backend — REST API Endpoints & Validations

## Cross-cutting behaviour (applies to every route)

- [ ] Global route prefix is `/api` (`app.setGlobalPrefix('api')` in `src/main.ts`); CORS `origin: '*'`; static assets served from `/public`.
- [ ] **No global `ValidationPipe`** is registered (`main.ts` only registers `SanitizeInputInterceptor`; no `APP_PIPE`). DTO class-validator rules are enforced **only** on routes that attach `@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe(...))` or `@Body(new ValidationPipe(...))` — each endpoint below says whether a pipe is applied. On routes without a pipe, DTO decorators are documentation only.
- [ ] Global `SanitizeInputInterceptor`: strips NUL bytes / disallowed control characters from every HTTP request body (via `stripControlChars`) before controllers run (prevents PostgreSQL error 22021).
- [ ] Global exception filter `AllExceptionsFilter` is commented out — Nest default error format applies.
- [ ] **DeviceMiddleware** (`src/common/middleware/device.middleware.ts`) is applied to all routes (`path: '*'`, all methods) and sets `req.deviceInfo`:
  - [ ] If header `x-device-id` is present (mobile app): reads `x-device-type`, `x-device-brand`, `x-device-model`, `x-os`, `x-os-version`, `x-platform`, `x-app-version`, `x-device-id`, `x-fcm-token` (optional); browser fields undefined.
  - [ ] Otherwise parses `User-Agent` with UAParser: deviceType = UA device type, else `mobile` for Android/iOS, `desktop` for Windows/Mac/Linux, else `unknown`; `platform: 'web'`; captures browserName/browserVersion; deviceId/fcmToken/appVersion undefined.
  - [ ] `ipAddress` = first entry of `x-forwarded-for`, else `req.socket.remoteAddress`.
- [ ] **JWT guard** (`JwtAuthGuard` = passport `'jwt'`): Bearer token from `Authorization` header, expiry enforced. `JwtStrategy.validate` throws `UnauthorizedException`:
  - [ ] `'Session invalid or logged out'` — no active session matching `payload.sessionId` + `payload.sub`.
  - [ ] `'Session expired'` — `session.expiresAt < now`.
  - [ ] `'User not found'`, `'User deleted'` (`isDeleted`), `'User deactivated'` (`status === DEACTIVATED`).
  - [ ] `req.user` = `{ id, sessionId, permissions }` (permissions come from the JWT payload, not DB).
- [ ] **PermissionsGuard** + `@RequiredPermissions({ module, access })`: returns `false` (→ 403 `Forbidden resource`) if the handler has no `requiredPermissions` metadata; passes if ANY user permission has matching `module` AND `permission[access] === true` (OR semantics across listed permissions). Written as `module:access` below.
- [ ] **ProfileCompletionGuard**: loads user and runs `checkUserProfileCompletion`; throws `ForbiddenException('Unauthorized')` if no user id, or `ForbiddenException('Please complete your profile to access this feature. Missing: <fields>')`.
- [ ] Root `AppController` → `GET /api` returns `appService.getHello()` (no guard).

Legend: **Guards** — `JWT` = JwtAuthGuard; `Perm(module:access)` = PermissionsGuard with required permission; `Profile` = ProfileCompletionGuard; `none` = public. **VP** = ValidationPipe applied (yes/no).

## Auth

Source: `src/modules/auth/*` (controller, `auth.service.ts`, strategies, `social/*`, `dto/*`). Signup DTO lives in `src/modules/users/dto/create-user.dto.ts`.

### Passport strategies and guards (cross-cutting behaviour)

- [ ] **JwtAuthGuard / JwtStrategy** (`AuthGuard('jwt')`): reads a `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header, verifies with `JWT_SECRET`, `ignoreExpiration: false`. `validate()` then:
  - [ ] looks up `Session` with `id = payload.sessionId`, `userId = payload.sub`, `isActive = true`; none found → `UnauthorizedException('Session invalid or logged out')`
  - [ ] `session.expiresAt < now` → `UnauthorizedException('Session expired')`
  - [ ] user not found → `UnauthorizedException('User not found')`
  - [ ] `user.isDeleted` → `UnauthorizedException('User deleted')`
  - [ ] `user.status === DEACTIVATED` → `UnauthorizedException('User deactivated')`
  - [ ] sets `req.user = { id, sessionId, permissions }`. `permissions` comes from the JWT payload, so it is frozen at login time.
- [ ] **PermissionsGuard** (`src/common/guards/permissions.guard.ts`): reads `@RequiredPermissions(...)` metadata. If none is set it returns `false`, which gives 403 "Forbidden resource". Otherwise access is granted when **any** of the user's `permissions[]` has `module === required.module && permission[required.access] === true`. With several required permissions this is OR logic.
- [ ] **LocalAuthGuard / LocalStrategy** (`usernameField: 'email'`, password field `password`):
  - [ ] `AuthService.validateUser`: case-insensitive, trimmed email lookup. The `isDeleted` filter is commented out in the query and handled explicitly below.
  - [ ] no user → `HttpException('Invalid email or password', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)` (404)
  - [ ] `user.isDeleted` → `HttpException('User not found', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)` (404)
  - [ ] bcrypt mismatch → `HttpException('Invalid email or password', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)` (404)
  - [ ] strategy: `!user || user.isDeleted` → `UnauthorizedException()` (no message)
  - [ ] on success, attaches role permissions (`attachPermissions`) and strips `password`
- [ ] **Permission auto-provisioning at login** (`attachPermissions`, which runs for every login path):
  - [ ] ADMIN with no `assignedDesignation`: creates designation "Admin" ("auto generated on login") with full CRUD on ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT, USERS, COMPANIES, MANAGE, WORK_EXPERIENCE, QUALIFICATIONS
  - [ ] FREELANCER with no designation: creates "Engineer" with full CRUD on JOBS and USERS
  - [ ] CLIENT with no designation:
    - [ ] creates "Client". Only when `company.ownerId === user.id` does it grant full CRUD on COMPANIES, WORK_ORDERS, ACTION_APPROVE/VERIFY/REVIEW/CANCEL_WORK_ORDER, USERS, CLIENT_CUSTOMER, CLIENT_PROJECT, JOBS, CLIENT_BANK_ACCOUNTS, COMPANY_BRANCHES, INVOICE, CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY/SEND/PAID_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY/SEND/PAID_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ENGINEER_INVOICE, WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS, WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL, ENGINEER_SKILLS_SET and USER_PERMISSIONS, plus WORK_FORCE_COVERAGE_MAP (read only)
    - [ ] when the designation already exists and the user is the company owner, any missing `ClientPermissionModules` rows are topped up with full CRUD
  - [ ] CUSTOMER with no designation:
    - [ ] creates "Customer". When the user is the company owner it grants USERS (full CRUD), CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS (full CRUD), and read-only CLIENT_PROJECT, CLIENT_CUSTOMER and WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS
    - [ ] when the designation already exists it ensures INVOICE (read), CUSTOMER_INVOICE (read), ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE (read+write) and CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS (full CRUD)
    - [ ] it also ensures CUSTOMER_USERS (full CRUD) when the user owns a non-deleted Client entity
    - [ ] it deletes ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, and deletes CUSTOMER_USERS when the user is not the owner
- [ ] **GoogleStrategy** (`'google'`):
  - [ ] scope `email, profile`, `prompt: 'select_account'`, callback `${BASE_URL}/api/auth/google/callback`
  - [ ] maps to a `SocialProfile {provider:'google', providerUserId, email, emailVerified, firstName, lastName}`
  - [ ] `emailVerified` is read from `emails[0].verified ?? _json.email_verified` and defaults to false
- [ ] **GoogleOAuthGuard**: wraps `AuthGuard('google')` and adds a signed OAuth `state` JWT (secret `${JWT_SECRET}:oauth-state`, 10m) carrying:
  - [ ] `intent`: `'signup'` when `?intent=signup`, else `'login'`
  - [ ] `role`: `?role=TECH|CLIENT`, anything else is dropped
- [ ] **MicrosoftStrategy** (`'microsoft'`):
  - [ ] scope `user.read`, tenant from env, callback `${BASE_URL}/api/auth/microsoft/callback`
  - [ ] returns `{email, firstName, lastName, accessToken}`
  - [ ] throws a TypeError when `emails` is missing (unguarded)
- [ ] **LinkedinStrategy** (`'linkedin'`):
  - [ ] scope `openid, profile, email`
  - [ ] callbackURL is hard-coded to `http://localhost:8080/auth/linkedin/callback`. It is not env-driven and not under `/api`, so this flow is effectively a dev stub.
- [ ] **FirebaseTokenService.verify(idToken)** (mobile/web Apple and Google via Firebase):
  - [ ] empty token → `UnauthorizedException('Token is required')`
  - [ ] Firebase app not initialised → `UnauthorizedException('Firebase is not configured on this server')`
  - [ ] `verifyIdToken` fails → `UnauthorizedException('Invalid or expired token')`
  - [ ] `sign_in_provider` not `google.com`/`apple.com` → `UnauthorizedException('Sign-in method "<method>" is not supported. Use Google or Apple.')`
  - [ ] no provider subject id in `firebase.identities` → `UnauthorizedException('Token is missing the provider account id')`
  - [ ] the display name is split on the first whitespace into firstName/lastName
- [ ] **SocialIdentityService.resolve(profile)**. Outcomes are return values, not exceptions:
  - [ ] no email → REJECT `NO_EMAIL`
  - [ ] linked `SocialAccount` (provider+providerUserId) → LOGIN, unless the user is deleted (`ACCOUNT_DELETED`) or DEACTIVATED (`ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED`)
  - [ ] no linked account and no user with that email (case-insensitive) → SIGNUP
  - [ ] email match but `emailVerified` false → REJECT `UNVERIFIED_EMAIL`
  - [ ] otherwise LINK, subject to the same deleted/deactivated checks
- [ ] **Social rejection messages** (`SOCIAL_REJECTION_MESSAGES`, `src/utils/variables.ts`):
  - [ ] `NO_ACCOUNT`: "No account found for ${email || 'that account'}. Please sign up or use a different account."
  - [ ] `NO_EMAIL`: "We couldn’t get an email address from that account. Please sign up with your email instead."
  - [ ] `UNVERIFIED_EMAIL`: "That account’s email isn’t verified. Please sign in with your password first."
  - [ ] `ACCOUNT_DELETED`: "This account has been deleted. Please contact support."
  - [ ] `ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED`: "Your account is not active. Please contact support."
- [ ] **Signup ticket** (`SocialFlowService`):
  - [ ] JWT signed with `${JWT_SECRET}:signup-ticket`, 15m, claims `{provider, providerUserId, email, firstName, lastName, role}`
  - [ ] an invalid or expired ticket → `BadRequestException('This signup link has expired. Please sign in again.')`
  - [ ] an invalid state JWT silently falls back to `{intent:'login'}`
- [ ] **`AuthService.login(req)`** (shared by password, Google, Firebase, Microsoft and social signup):
  - [ ] `req.deviceInfo.deviceType` missing → `HttpException('Please clear the app from background and Try again.', HttpStatus.BAD_GATEWAY)` (502)
  - [ ] `status === 'DEACTIVATED'` → `HttpException({ message, deactivated: true, isCustomerUser }, 403)`
    - [ ] message for a customer sub-user (`customer.ownerId !== user.id`): "Your access to your company's Tech Domain account has been deactivated by your organisation's administrator. If you need access restored, please contact your company administrator."
    - [ ] message otherwise: "Your account is not active. Please contact support"
  - [ ] `isVerified === false` → re-sends the OTP (`otpService.resendOtp(email)`), then `HttpException('Please verify email', HttpStatus.TEMPORARY_REDIRECT)` (307)
  - [ ] in a transaction:
    - [ ] creates a `Session` with the DeviceMiddleware info (ip, deviceType/brand/model, os/osVersion, platform, appVersion, deviceId, fcmToken, browserName)
    - [ ] signs a refresh token (`JWT_REFRESH_SECRET`, `JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN`) and stores it bcrypt-hashed, with `session.expiresAt` set to now + 7 days
    - [ ] signs an access token (`JWT_SECRET`, `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN`) with payload `{sub, sessionId, permissions}`
  - [ ] returns the user plus `profilePicture`, `teams[]`, `isCompanyOwner`, `isCompanyMember`, `hasPendingCompanyInvite` and `isCustomerOwner`
- [ ] **DeviceMiddleware** (applied to all routes, `path: '*'`), which sets `req.deviceInfo`:
  - [ ] if the `x-device-id` header is present, it reads the mobile headers `x-device-type`, `x-device-brand`, `x-device-model`, `x-os`, `x-os-version`, `x-platform`, `x-app-version`, `x-device-id` and `x-fcm-token`
  - [ ] otherwise it parses `User-Agent` with UAParser. deviceType becomes `mobile` for Android/iOS, `desktop` for windows/mac/linux, else `unknown`; platform is `'web'`
  - [ ] the IP comes from the first `x-forwarded-for` entry, falling back to `socket.remoteAddress`
  - [ ] a mobile client that sends `x-device-id` without `x-device-type` triggers the 502 "Please clear the app from background and Try again." at login

### /api/auth

- [ ] `GET /api/auth/google` — guard: GoogleOAuthGuard. No JWT, public (all roles).
  - [ ] Purpose: starts Google OAuth and redirects to Google.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `intent` (`signup` | anything else, which means `login`)
    - [ ] `role` (`TECH` | `CLIENT`)
  - [ ] Both params are packed into the signed `state`.
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/google/callback` — guard: GoogleOAuthGuard. Public.
  - [ ] Purpose: Google redirect target. It always responds with a redirect to `FRONTEND_BASE_URL`:
    - [ ] authenticated → `/socialCallback?accessToken=…&refreshToken=…`
    - [ ] SIGNUP when the state intent is not `signup` → `/login?socialError=NO_ACCOUNT&email=<masked relay>`
    - [ ] SIGNUP with signup intent → `/clientSignup?ticket=` for role CLIENT, `/techSignup?ticket=` for role TECH, `/getStarted?ticket=` otherwise
    - [ ] rejected → `/login?socialError=<REASON>`
    - [ ] any exception → `/login?socialError=PROVIDER_ERROR`
  - [ ] Service: `googleLogin` → no `req.user` → `NotFoundException('No profile from google')`. This is caught and becomes the PROVIDER_ERROR redirect.
  - [ ] On a LINK outcome it creates a `SocialAccount` row before issuing tokens.
- [ ] `POST /api/auth/socialLogin` and `POST /api/auth/mobile/socialLogin` (same handler) — no guard, public.
  - [ ] Purpose: exchanges a Firebase ID token (Google or Apple) for TDP tokens. Used by the mobile apps and by web Apple sign-in.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe: **applied** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`).
  - [ ] DTO `SocialTokenDto`:
    - [ ] `idToken`: string, required — `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - [ ] `role`: optional — `@IsOptional() @IsIn(['TECH','CLIENT'])`
  - [ ] Responses:
    - [ ] authenticated → `{status:'authenticated', data:user, accessToken, refreshToken, message:'Login successfully'}`
    - [ ] signup → `{status:'signup', reason:'NO_ACCOUNT', ticket, prefill:{email, emailMasked, firstName, lastName}, message: NO_ACCOUNT text}`
    - [ ] rejected → `HttpException({status:'rejected', reason, message: SOCIAL_REJECTION_MESSAGES[reason]()}, 403)`
  - [ ] Throws: every FirebaseTokenService `UnauthorizedException` listed above, plus all `login()` errors.
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/connectedAccounts` — guard: JwtAuthGuard. All authenticated roles.
  - [ ] Purpose: lists the social providers linked to the current user, as `{id, provider, email, createdAt}` sorted by createdAt asc.
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/session` — guard: JwtAuthGuard. All roles.
  - [ ] Purpose: returns the current user in the same shape as login. Used after the OAuth callback.
  - [ ] Throws `NotFoundException('User not found')`.
  - [ ] Re-runs `attachPermissions`, so it can provision designations and permissions.
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/signupTicket` — no guard, public.
  - [ ] Purpose: decodes a signup ticket for the signup form.
  - [ ] Query param: `ticket` (string).
  - [ ] Returns `{email, emailMasked, firstName, lastName, provider, role}`.
  - [ ] Throws `BadRequestException('This signup link has expired. Please sign in again.')`.
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/microsoft` — guard: `AuthGuard('microsoft')`. Public.
  - [ ] Purpose: starts Microsoft OAuth.
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/microsoft/callback` — guard: `AuthGuard('microsoft')`. Public.
  - [ ] Purpose: logs in an existing user by email.
  - [ ] Redirects:
    - [ ] success → `${FRONTEND_BASE_URL}?success=<accessToken>`. The access token is passed in the query string and no refresh token is returned.
    - [ ] any error → `${FRONTEND_BASE_URL}/not-found`
  - [ ] Service errors (all swallowed into the not-found redirect):
    - [ ] no `req.user` → `NotFoundException('No user from microsoft')`
    - [ ] no non-deleted user with that email → `NotFoundException('No user linked')`
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/linkedin` — guard: `AuthGuard('linkedin')`. Public. **Stub**: starts LinkedIn OAuth, but the callback URL is hard-coded to localhost.
- [ ] `GET /api/auth/linkedin/callback` — guard: `AuthGuard('linkedin')`. **Stub**: the handler body is empty (empty try/catch), sends no response and issues no token, so the request hangs.
- [ ] `POST /api/auth/login` — guard: LocalAuthGuard. Public, all roles.
  - [ ] Purpose: email and password login.
  - [ ] Body: `{ email, password }`. No DTO and no ValidationPipe.
  - [ ] Returns `{data:user, accessToken, refreshToken, message:'Login successfully'}`.
  - [ ] Throws: the LocalStrategy/validateUser errors (404 "Invalid email or password", 404 "User not found", 401), then the `login()` errors (502 device, 403 deactivated, 307 "Please verify email").
- [ ] `POST /api/auth/logout` — no guard, public. Body is untyped, no DTO or ValidationPipe.
  - [ ] Purpose: deactivates the session (`isActive=false`).
  - [ ] Body: `{ refreshToken }`.
  - [ ] Service:
    - [ ] verifies the refresh token with `JWT_REFRESH_SECRET`
    - [ ] bcrypt mismatch against the session → `HttpException('Something went wrong', HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)` (503)
    - [ ] all errors are re-thrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`; a JWT error has no status, which gives a 500
  - [ ] Response message: 'Logout successfully'.
- [ ] `POST /api/auth/refreshToken` — no guard, public. No DTO or ValidationPipe.
  - [ ] Purpose: rotates the refresh token and issues a new access token.
  - [ ] Body: `{ oldRefreshToken }`.
  - [ ] Service throws:
    - [ ] missing or blank → `BadRequestException('Refresh token is required')`
    - [ ] verify fails → `UnauthorizedException('Invalid or expired refresh token')`
    - [ ] hash mismatch → `UnauthorizedException('Refresh token does not match any active session')`
    - [ ] expired → `UnauthorizedException('Session expired')`, and the session is set inactive
  - [ ] It also extends `session.expiresAt` by 7 days.
  - [ ] Signs the new access token with `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET` (a **different** env var from the `JWT_SECRET` used at login and in JwtStrategy). Check that the two env values match.
  - [ ] If the session is not found, `session.refreshToken` is read from null and throws a TypeError (500).
  - [ ] Response message: 'Refreshed successfully'.
- [ ] `POST /api/auth/signup` — no guard, public. Used for FREELANCER, CLIENT and CUSTOMER self-signup.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe: **applied** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`), but not with `whitelist` or `transform` options.
  - [ ] DTO `CreateUserDto`:
    - [ ] `firstName`: string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, {message:'First name must only contain letters'})`
    - [ ] `lastName`: string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, {message:'Last name must only contain letters'})`
    - [ ] `email`: required — `@IsNotEmpty @IsEmail`, `@Transform` lowercase (not effective without `transform:true`)
    - [ ] `phoneNumber`: string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`
    - [ ] `roleId`: number, optional — `@IsNumber @IsOptional`
    - [ ] `ticket`: string, optional — social signup ticket
    - [ ] `password`: string, required only when there is no `ticket` (`@ValidateIf(!dto.ticket)`) — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @MinLength(8) @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*[0-9])(?=.*[!@#$%^&*]).{8,20}$/, {message:'Password must be 8-20 characters and include upper and lower case letters, a number and a symbol'})`
    - [ ] `areaCoverage`: string, optional
    - [ ] `skillset`: string[], optional — `@IsArray @IsString({each:true})`
    - [ ] `freelanceCompanyName`: string, optional — service-company owner, keeps the FREELANCER role
    - [ ] `companyId`: number, optional
    - [ ] Client-company fields, all string and optional:
      - [ ] `name`
      - [ ] `noOfEmployees`
      - [ ] `companyAnnualRevenue`
      - [ ] `technicianFrequency`
      - [ ] `technicianUrgency`
    - [ ] Public customer signup fields:
      - [ ] `customerCompanyName`: string, optional
      - [ ] `companyEmail`: optional — `@IsEmail`
      - [ ] `website`: string, optional
    - [ ] `referralSource`: string, optional
    - [ ] `permissionId`: number, optional (`@IsNumber @IsNotEmpty @IsOptional`)
    - [ ] `isVerified`: boolean, optional
    - [ ] `address`: optional object — `@Type(() => AddressTypeDto)`, but it has no `@ValidateNested`, so the nested rules below are **not enforced**:
      - [ ] `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`: all string
      - [ ] `type`: AddressType
      - [ ] `longitude`, `latitude`: `@IsDecimal`
  - [ ] Flow with a `ticket` (social signup):
    - [ ] `verifySignupTicket` → `BadRequestException('This signup link has expired. Please sign in again.')`
    - [ ] the email is taken from the ticket, not the body
    - [ ] resolve ≠ SIGNUP → `HttpException('This account already exists. Please sign in.', HttpStatus.CONFLICT)` (409)
    - [ ] creates the user with a random 96-hex placeholder password, links the social account and logs in (with the `login()` errors)
    - [ ] returns `{data, accessToken, refreshToken, message:'Signed up successfully'}`
  - [ ] Flow without a ticket: `usersService.create`:
    - [ ] existing user that is deleted → `HttpException('Account deleted. Please contact support.', 403)`
    - [ ] existing user that is verified → `HttpException('User already exists.', 409)`
    - [ ] existing user that is unverified → OTP re-sent, then `HttpException('User already exists with this email, OTP code sent', 403)`
    - [ ] **Note:** the whole `create()` is wrapped in a catch that re-throws `HttpException(error.message || 'Something went wrong', 500)`, so all three statuses above actually arrive as **500** with the same message text
    - [ ] returns `{message:'Verification email sent'}`
- [ ] `PUT /api/auth/createNewPassword` — guard: JwtAuthGuard. All roles; first-login users with an admin-generated password.
  - [ ] Body: `{ newPassword: string }`. Inline type, **no validation at all**, so there is no password-strength check.
  - [ ] Hashes with `SALT` rounds and sets `isFirstLogin=false`.
  - [ ] Throws `InternalServerErrorException('Please contact support')` when the user is not found.
  - [ ] Returns message 'Password created successfully'.
- [ ] `POST /api/auth/forgotPassword` — public.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe: **applied**.
  - [ ] DTO `ForgotPasswordDto`: `email`, string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`. There is no `@IsEmail`.
  - [ ] Service:
    - [ ] case-insensitive lookup of a non-deleted user
    - [ ] creates a `ResetPasswordToken` (nanoid(64), expires in 15 min)
    - [ ] emails the 'reset-password' template with the link `${FRONTEND_BASE_URL}/resetPassword?token=…&role=…`
    - [ ] errors are swallowed and the method returns undefined
  - [ ] Responses:
    - [ ] `{sent:true, message:'Password reset email sent'}`
    - [ ] `{sent:false|undefined, message:'This is not a registered email'}`
- [ ] `PUT /api/auth/changePassword` — guard: JwtAuthGuard. All roles.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe: **applied**.
  - [ ] DTO `ChangePasswordDto`:
    - [ ] `oldPassword`: string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`
    - [ ] `newPassword`: string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @MinLength(8)`. There is no complexity regex, unlike signup and reset.
  - [ ] Service throws:
    - [ ] `NotFoundException('User not found')`
    - [ ] bcrypt mismatch → `HttpException('Wrong password', HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)`
  - [ ] The update is not awaited.
  - [ ] Response message: 'Passsword changed successfully' (typo as in the code).
- [ ] `PUT /api/auth/resetPassword` — public.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe: **applied**.
  - [ ] DTO `ResetPasswordDto`:
    - [ ] `resetToken`: string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`
    - [ ] `newPassword`: string, required — `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @MinLength(8) @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*[0-9])(?=.*[!@#$%^&*]).{8,20}$/, {message:'Password must be 8-20 characters and include upper and lower case letters, a number and a symbol'})`
  - [ ] Service throws:
    - [ ] token not found → `HttpException('Invalid link', HttpStatus.AMBIGUOUS)` (300)
    - [ ] expired → `NotFoundException('Expired link')`
    - [ ] user missing → `InternalServerErrorException('Please contact support')`
  - [ ] Sets `isFirstLogin=false`. The token is **not** invalidated after use, so it can be reused until it expires.
  - [ ] Response message:
    - [ ] iOS mobile (`deviceInfo`): 'Please return to the mobile app and log in to continue.'
    - [ ] otherwise: 'Passsword reset successfully'


## Admin

Source: `src/modules/admin/admin.controller.ts`, `admin.service.ts`, `dto/*`. The class is decorated `@Controller('admin')`. No global ValidationPipe is applied; routes are noted individually below. `CreateAdminDto` and `UpdateAdminDto` are empty and unused (stubs).

### /api/admin

- [ ] `GET /api/admin/newUsers` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isRead`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Purpose: paginated list of verified, non-deleted users other than the caller, sorted `createdAt desc`.
  - [ ] Returns `{totalCount, statusCounts, data[]}`. Each row includes profilePicture, identityDocuments (IDENTITY_PROOF signed URLs), freelanceCompany, isFreelanceCompanyOwner and isFreelanceCompanyEngineer.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `role`: UserType
    - [ ] `limit`: default 10
    - [ ] `page`: default 1
    - [ ] `search`
    - [ ] `fullName`: matched exactly on "first last" (ILIKE), with a fallback filter
    - [ ] `email`
    - [ ] `country`
    - [ ] `city`
    - [ ] `status`
    - [ ] `startDate` and `endDate`: `createdAt` range, invalid dates are ignored
    - [ ] `excludeCompanyMembers='true'`
    - [ ] `excludeCompanyOwners='true'`
  - [ ] No validation.
  - [ ] Throws `HttpException('Failed to fetch filtered users', 500)` on any error.
- [ ] `GET /api/admin/approveProfile/:id` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Purpose: sets the user's status to APPROVED. This is a state change on a GET.
  - [ ] Sends a PROFILE_APPROVED notification ("🎉 Welcome Aboard, {firstName}!").
  - [ ] Sends the email 'profile-approved-company-engineer' if the user is an accepted freelance-company member, otherwise 'profile-approved'.
  - [ ] Throws `HttpException('Access denied', 403)` if the caller's role is not ADMIN.
  - [ ] An unknown id surfaces as a Prisma error (500).
- [ ] `POST /api/admin/inviteEngineer` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Purpose: emails the 'invite-engineer' template with the link `${FRONTEND_BASE_URL}/techSignup?role=FREELANCER`.
  - [ ] DTO `InviteEngineerDto`: `email`, `@IsEmail()`.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe: **not applied**, so the `@IsEmail` rule is not enforced.
  - [ ] Throws `HttpException('Access denied', 403)` for a non-ADMIN caller.
  - [ ] Returns `{success:true, message:'Invitation sent to <email>'}`.
- [ ] `GET /api/admin/activateEngineer/:id` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Purpose: sets a FREELANCER's status to APPROVED, sends a notification and the 'profile-approved' email.
  - [ ] Throws:
    - [ ] `ForbiddenException('Only admins can activate engineers')`
    - [ ] `ForbiddenException('Engineer not found')` (a 403, not a 404)
  - [ ] Response message: 'Engineer activated successfully'.
- [ ] `GET /api/admin/deactivateEngineer/:id` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Purpose: sets a FREELANCER's status to DEACTIVATED. That user's JWT calls then return 401 "User deactivated".
  - [ ] Throws:
    - [ ] `ForbiddenException('Only admins can deactivate engineers')`
    - [ ] `ForbiddenException('Engineer not found')`
  - [ ] Response message: 'Engineer deactivated successfully'.
- [ ] `POST /api/admin/request/resubmitMissingDocuments` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe: **applied**.
  - [ ] DTO `SendMissingDocEmailDto`:
