    - [ ] `emailTemplate`: array, required — `@IsArray @IsEnum(EmailTemplateType,{each:true})`. Allowed values: CUSTOM_EMAIL, ID, RATES, SKILL_SET, EQUIPMENT, EMPLOYMENT, LANGUAGE, EDUCATION, PROFILE_PICTURE, IDENTITY_PROOF, WORKING_RIGHTS, ADDRESS.
    - [ ] `engineerId`: required — `@IsInt`
    - [ ] `emailContent`: string, required only when `emailTemplate` includes CUSTOM_EMAIL (`@ValidateIf`) — `@IsString`
  - [ ] Service:
    - [ ] the engineer must have the FREELANCER role
    - [ ] a PENDING engineer keeps their status; any other status becomes UNAPPROVE
    - [ ] `reminderCount` is incremented
    - [ ] with CUSTOM_EMAIL it sends the 'custom-email' template ('Important: Action Required'); otherwise 'request-missing-documents' with an upload link to `/completeYourProfile`
  - [ ] Errors: engineer not found → `Error('Engineer not found')`. All errors are re-thrown as a plain `Error('Failed to send email. Please try again later.')`, which gives a 500 "Internal server error" and the message is not exposed.
  - [ ] Returns `{success, message:'Email sent successfully', status, reminderCount}`.
- [ ] `PATCH /api/admin/permissions` — **no guards** (JWT and Permissions are commented out). **Stub**: the service `createOrUpdateUserPermissions(body){}` is empty.
  - [ ] ValidationPipe is applied to `body: any`, so it is a no-op.
  - [ ] It is publicly reachable and returns `{data: undefined, message:'SUCCESS'}`.
- [ ] `POST /api/admin/skill/verify` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite` OR `ENGINEER_SKILLS_SET:isWrite`). Roles: ADMIN, CLIENT.
  - [ ] Body (untyped, no validation):
    - [ ] `userSkillId`: number
    - [ ] `isVerified`: boolean
  - [ ] Throws:
    - [ ] `NotFoundException(' user not found')` (leading space as in the code)
    - [ ] role not ADMIN or CLIENT → `HttpException('Only admin can verify/unverify skills', 403)`
    - [ ] `NotFoundException('User skill not found')`
  - [ ] Everything is re-wrapped as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.
  - [ ] Returns `data: undefined`.
- [ ] `POST /api/admin/equipment/verify` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite` OR `ENGINEER_SKILLS_SET:isWrite`). Roles: ADMIN, CLIENT.
  - [ ] Body (untyped, no validation):
    - [ ] `userQualificationId`: number
    - [ ] `isVerified`: boolean
  - [ ] Throws:
    - [ ] `NotFoundException(' user not found')`
    - [ ] `HttpException('Only admin can verify/unverify equipment', 403)`
    - [ ] `NotFoundException('User Equipment not found')`
  - [ ] Returns `{id, isVerified}`.
- [ ] `GET /api/admin/customers` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isRead`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Purpose: lists parent customers. These are Client rows with `ownerId` not null; when `parentClientId` is given it lists that parent's child entities instead.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `limit`: default 20
    - [ ] `page`: default 1
    - [ ] `search`
    - [ ] `country`
    - [ ] `city`
    - [ ] `status`: owner status
    - [ ] `customerName`
    - [ ] `email`: owner email
    - [ ] `parentClientId`
    - [ ] `sortBy`: one of id, clientName, createdAt, userCount, entityCount, status, city, state, country. Anything else sorts by createdAt desc.
    - [ ] `sortOrder`: `asc`, else desc
  - [ ] Each returned customer includes a signed logo URL (the parent's logo is used for child entities), isSelfSignup, userCount (live CUSTOMER logins), entityCount, totalWorkOrders, projectCount and workOrdersByStatus.
- [ ] `GET /api/admin/customers/:id/approvalOptions` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isRead`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Purpose: returns the customer's company `branches`, plus `serviceDeliveryTeams` and `operationsTeams`. Teams are filtered by department name containing "service delivery" or "operations", falling back to all teams.
  - [ ] Throws `NotFoundException('Customer not found')`.
- [ ] `POST /api/admin/customers/:id/approve` — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`). Role: ADMIN.
  - [ ] Body (inline type, no validation), all optional:
    - [ ] `branchId`: number
    - [ ] `teamId`: string
    - [ ] `secondaryTeamId`: string
  - [ ] Purpose:
    - [ ] applies branchId, teamId and secondaryTeamId to all child entities
    - [ ] sets the owner's status to APPROVED
    - [ ] sets the parent's `teamEmail` to the service-delivery team's email
    - [ ] sends a PROFILE_APPROVED notification and the 'customer-profile-approved' email
  - [ ] Throws:
    - [ ] `NotFoundException('Customer not found')`
    - [ ] customer has no owner → `HttpException('Customer has no account', 400)`
    - [ ] `NotFoundException('Branch not found')`
    - [ ] `NotFoundException('Team not found')`
  - [ ] Response message: 'Customer approved successfully'.


## Analytics

Source: `src/modules/analytics/analytics.controller.ts`, `analytics.service.ts`. There are no DTOs and no ValidationPipe; all query params are raw strings, numeric ones are converted with `+x`, and empty values become `undefined`.

- [ ] **Guard**: class-level `@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)` on every route, with no PermissionsGuard.
- [ ] **Roles**:
  - [ ] CLIENT: company dashboards, scoped to `user.companyId`
  - [ ] FREELANCER: the `engineer/*` routes
- [ ] **Common company-scoped behaviour**:
  - [ ] `getUserCompanyId` → `NotFoundException('User not found')`
  - [ ] a `clientId` that is a parent client expands to the parent plus its non-deleted children
  - [ ] DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL and CANCELLED work orders are excluded from all metrics, except cards that opt CANCELLED back in
  - [ ] the date filter is on `orderStartDate`

### /api/analytics

- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/filterOptions` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Returns the company's parent `clients` `{id, clientName}` and `teams` (non-deleted, excluding names that contain "-").
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/kpi` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Purpose: KPI cards with sparklines.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`: string
    - [ ] `tz`: IANA zone for the "Today" card. Invalid values fall back to `APP_TIMEZONE`, then 'Australia/Sydney'.
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/trends` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `granularity`: `weekly` | `monthly` | `yearly`, default monthly, not validated
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/target` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Purpose: progress against the hard-coded annual target `ANNUAL_TARGET = 5000`.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `year`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/workOrders` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Purpose: work-order analytics.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/monthly` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `year`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/teamTrends` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/advanced` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/breakdown` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/topEngineers` — **commented out, not routed**. The service method `getTopEngineers` still exists.
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/worldMap` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Purpose: work orders grouped by country.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `teamId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/clientTrends` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Purpose: yearly trends per parent client.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startYear`: default current year − 1
    - [ ] `endYear`: default current year
  - [ ] Returns `{categories, series: []}` when there are no parent clients.
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/clientMonthlyRace` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `year`
    - [ ] `clientId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/engineer/kpi` — JWT. Role: FREELANCER.
  - [ ] Purpose: engineer KPIs.
  - [ ] Scope: a freelance-company owner sees themselves plus all ACCEPTED members; anyone else sees only themselves.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
  - [ ] No thrown errors.
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/invoices` — JWT. Role: CLIENT (finance dashboard).
  - [ ] Purpose: invoice KPIs converted to USD.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `role`: `CLIENT` means revenue measured on `subTotal`; anything else means FREELANCER expenses measured on `grandTotal`
  - [ ] FX rates are fetched from `https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/USD` and cached for 1h. On failure it keeps the stale cache, or falls back to `{USD:1}` and leaves amounts unconverted.
  - [ ] Throws `NotFoundException('User not found')`.
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/invoices/yearlyTrend` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Purpose: monthly trend per client line for a calendar year.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `year`
    - [ ] `clientId`
    - [ ] `role`: CLIENT or FREELANCER, as above
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/invoices/profitTrend` — JWT. Role: CLIENT.
  - [ ] Purpose: monthly profit (revenue − expenses) in USD.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `year`
    - [ ] `clientId`
- [ ] `GET /api/analytics/engineer/invoiceStats` — JWT. Role: FREELANCER.
  - [ ] Purpose: the engineer's FREELANCER invoice stats. Uses the same engineer scope as `engineer/kpi`.
  - [ ] Query params:
    - [ ] `startDate`
    - [ ] `endDate`

## Assessments

### `/assessments`
- [ ] POST /api/assessments — guards: JWT. Roles: intended ADMIN/CLIENT (any authenticated user can call it). Purpose: create an assessment with nested questions and answers, owned by `req.user.id`. ValidationPipe: **yes** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default options, so no whitelist/transform). Response message: `Assessment created successfully`.
  - Body `CreateAssessmentDto`:
    - `assessmentTitle`: string, required. Rules: `@IsString`, `@IsNotEmpty`.
    - `passingScore`: number, required. Rules: `@IsInt`, `@IsNotEmpty`.
    - `questions`: array, required. Rules: `@IsArray`, `@IsNotEmpty`, `@ValidateNested({each:true})`, `@Type(CreateAssessmentQuestionDto)`.
      - `questions[].question`: string, required. Rules: `@IsString`, `@IsNotEmpty`.
      - `questions[].answers`: array, required. Rules: `@IsNotEmpty`, `@IsArray`, `@ValidateNested({each:true})`.
        - `answers[].answer`: string, required. Rule: `@IsString`.
        - `answers[].isCorrect`: boolean, optional. Rules: `@IsBoolean`, `@IsOptional`.
    - `jobCountExemption`: integer, optional. Rules: `@IsOptional`, `@IsInt`.
    - `talentPoolExemption`: string, optional. Rules: `@IsOptional`, `@IsString`.
  - Service: no explicit throws. Prisma errors surface as 500.
- [ ] GET /api/assessments — guards: JWT. Purpose: list the caller's assessments, including questions and answers (`isCorrect` is exposed). Message: `Assessments fetched successfully`.
- [ ] GET /api/assessments/:id — **stub**. Guards: none. Returns the string `This action returns a #<id> assessment`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/assessments/:id — **stub**. Guards: none. Body `UpdateAssessmentDto` (PartialType of Create), no ValidationPipe. Returns `This action updates a #<id> assessment`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/assessments/:id — **stub**. Guards: none. Returns `This action removes a #<id> assessment`.


## bank-details

### /api/bankDetails
Controller: `BankDetailsController` (`src/modules/bank-details/bank-details.controller.ts`). All routes use `JwtAuthGuard` only; `PermissionsGuard` / `RequiredPermissions` are imported but **not applied**. Every service method wraps its body in `try/catch` and rethrows `new HttpException(error.message, error.status)`. For create, `getAllBankDetailsByBranchId`, and remove there is no `|| 500` fallback, so a Prisma error with no status becomes an HttpException with an undefined status.

- [ ] POST /api/bankDetails. Guard: JWT. Roles: CLIENT (company owner), FREELANCER (own account), ADMIN (on behalf of a user). Purpose: create a bank account record. Sensitive fields are AES-encrypted at rest: accountNumber, iban, swiftCode, routingNumber, bsb, institutionNumber, transitNumber.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default options, so no transform and no whitelist).
  - Body `CreateBankDetailDto`:
    - `accountHolderName`: string, required, `@IsString()`
    - `bankName`: string, required, `@IsString()`
    - `bankAddress`: string, optional, `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
    - `accountNumber`: string, required, `@IsString()`
    - `iban`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
    - `swiftCode`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
    - `accountType`: required, `@IsEnum(AccountType)`. Values: SAVINGS, CHECKING, CURRENT, BUSINESS, SALARY, FIXED_DEPOSIT, OTHER.
    - `currency`: required, `@IsEnum(CurrencyType)`. Values are ISO-4217 codes such as AED, AUD, and so on (full list in prisma `enum CurrencyType`).
    - `routingNumber`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
    - `sortCode`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
    - `branchId`: number, optional, `@IsNumber()`
    - `userId`: number, optional, `@IsNumber()`. Used only when the caller is ADMIN; it replaces the owner id.
    - `bsb`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
    - `institutionNumber`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
    - `transitNumber`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
    - `bankCode`: string, optional, `@IsString()`
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 404 NOT_FOUND)` when the caller user is missing or deleted.
    - `HttpException('Only company owner can add bank details', 404)` for a CLIENT who is not the owner. **Note:** the `company.findUnique` call is not awaited, so this check never fires.
  - Row `companyId` is taken from the caller.
- [ ] GET /api/bankDetails/user/:id. Guard: JWT. Roles: ADMIN (any user id), others (their own only; `:id` is ignored). Purpose: list a user's bank accounts, decrypted, with an in-memory search and pagination.
  - ValidationPipe: **NO**. The DTO validators are declarative only.
  - Query `UserBankDetailsDto`:
    - `page`: `@IsOptional() @IsNumberString()`, default '1'
    - `limit`: `@IsOptional() @IsNumberString()`, default '20'
    - `search`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`. Case-insensitive match on decrypted accountNumber, iban, accountHolderName, or bankName.
  - Throws: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 404)` when the caller is not found.
- [ ] GET /api/bankDetails/:id. Guard: JWT. Roles: CLIENT company users. Purpose: list the bank accounts attached to branch `:id` inside the caller's company, decrypted.
  - ValidationPipe: n/a (no DTO).
  - Throws: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 404)` when the caller is not found.
- [ ] PATCH /api/bankDetails/:id. Guard: JWT. Roles: owner of the record, or ADMIN (any record). Purpose: partially update a bank record. `:id` is a string (uuid).
  - ValidationPipe: **NO**. Body `UpdateBankDetailDto` = `PartialType(CreateBankDetailDto)` (all fields optional, same validators, not enforced).
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 401)` when the caller is not found.
    - `HttpException('Bank detail not found', 404)` when no record exists (non-admins are scoped to `userId` = caller).
  - Bug to verify: sending `sortCode` writes into the **`bankCode`** column. `userId` and `companyId` cannot be changed.
- [ ] DELETE /api/bankDetails/:id/:userId. Guard: JWT. Roles: owner, or ADMIN (for the `:userId` given). Purpose: soft-delete (`isDeleted=true`).
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 404)` when the caller is not found.
    - `HttpException("this account doesn't exist", 404)` when the record is not found for that user.


## Branches

### `/branches`
All routes use `JwtAuthGuard` + `PermissionsGuard` with module `COMPANY_BRANCHES`. **No ValidationPipe** on any route, so DTO decorators are not enforced. Roles: CLIENT (company owner or staff). The service requires `role = CLIENT`.
- [ ] POST /api/branches — permission `COMPANY_BRANCHES:isWrite`. Purpose: create a branch plus a `BRANCH_ADDRESS` address on the company owned by the caller. Message `SUCCESS`.
  - Body `CreateBranchDto` (decorators are declared but not enforced):
    - `name`: string, required (`@IsString`, `@IsNotEmpty`).
    - `email`: string, optional (`@IsString`, `@IsOptional`).
    - `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `country`, `state`, `zipCode`, `phone`: string, optional (`@IsOptional`, `@IsString`).
    - `taxType`: `TaxType` enum (`GST` | `VAT` | `OTHERS`), `@IsNotEmpty` only (there is no `@IsEnum`).
    - `taxNumber`: string, required (`@IsNotEmpty`, `@IsString`).
  - Throws: `HttpException('Not Found', 404)` when the caller is not a non-deleted CLIENT. If the caller owns no company, `company.id` raises a TypeError. The catch block rethrows `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`, so the status is undefined for non-HTTP errors.
- [ ] GET /api/branches — permission `COMPANY_BRANCHES:isRead`. Purpose: list non-deleted branches of the caller's company, searching `name`/`email` case-insensitively.
  - Query `BranchDto` (not validated): `page` (numeric string, optional, default `'1'`), `limit` (numeric string, optional; without it, all rows are returned), `search` (string, optional).
  - Returns `{data, totalCount}`.
  - Throws: `HttpException('Not Found', 404)` when the user is not a CLIENT. Other errors → `HttpException(message, status||500)`.
- [ ] GET /api/branches/:id — permission `COMPANY_BRANCHES:isRead`. **Stub**: returns `This action returns a #<id> branch`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/branches/:id — permission `COMPANY_BRANCHES:isUpdate`. `@UseGuards` is duplicated (JWT, Permissions, JWT again). Purpose: update branch fields and the nested address. Body `UpdateBranchDto` (PartialType of Create, not validated).
  - Throws: `HttpException('User not found', 404)`, `HttpException('Branch not found', 404)` (branch not in the caller's company). Other errors are rethrown with `status||500`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/branches/:id — permission `COMPANY_BRANCHES:isDelete`. Purpose: soft-delete (`isDeleted=true`) a branch of the caller's company.
  - Throws: `HttpException('User not found', 404)`. A missing or already-deleted branch → Prisma P2025, rethrown as `HttpException(msg, 500)`. The `'Branch not found'` branch is unreachable.


## Categories

### `/categories`
- [ ] POST /api/categories — guards: JWT. Roles: ADMIN (not enforced). Purpose: create a category, stamped with `userId`. ValidationPipe: **yes**.
  - Body `CreateCategoryDto`: `category` (required, `@IsNotEmpty`; `@IsString` is commented out).
- [ ] POST /api/categories/subCategories — guards: JWT. Purpose: create a sub-category under a vendor. ValidationPipe: **yes**.
  - Body `CreateSubCategoryDto`: `subCategory` (required, `@IsNotEmpty`), `vendorId` (string, required, `@IsNotEmpty` `@IsString`).
- [ ] POST /api/categories/bulk — guards: JWT. Purpose: bulk-create categories (`skipDuplicates`). ValidationPipe is declared, but the param type is an array (`CreateCategoryDto[]`), so Nest skips validation.
  - Actual body shape: `{ category: string[] }`.
- [ ] POST /api/categories/subCategories/bulk — guards: JWT. Purpose: bulk-create sub-categories for one vendor. Validation is effectively skipped (array type).
  - Actual body: `{ subCategory: string[], vendorId: string }`.
- [ ] POST /api/categories/vendors/bulk — guards: JWT. Purpose: bulk-create vendors under one category. Validation is effectively skipped.
  - Actual body: `{ vendors: string[], categoryId: string }`. The declared `CreateVendorDto` would require `name` and `categoryId`, both `@IsNotEmpty` `@IsString`.
- [ ] POST /api/categories/subCategories/services — guards: JWT. Purpose: create a service under a sub-category. ValidationPipe: **yes**.
  - Body `CreateServiceDto`: `service` (string, required, `@IsNotEmpty` `@IsString`), `subCategoryId` (string, required, `@IsNotEmpty` `@IsString`).
- [ ] GET /api/categories/vendors — guards: JWT. Purpose: paginated vendor list, searching vendor name or category name, sorted by name.
  - Query `ListQueryDto` (not validated, so `@Transform` does not run): `search` (string, optional), `page` (int ≥1, `@Min(1)`), `limit` (int, `@IsPositive`). The service coerces with `Number()`/`getPagination`: no limit → all rows.
  - Returns `{data,total,page,limit}`.
- [ ] GET /api/categories/subCategories — guards: **none** (public). Purpose: paginated sub-category list, searching sub-category, vendor name and category name. Query `ListQueryDto` (not validated).
- [ ] GET /api/categories — guards: JWT. Purpose: paginated category list (non-deleted, sorted asc). Query `ListQueryDto` (not validated). Returns `{data,total,page,limit}`.
- [ ] GET /api/categories/findAllVendorsByCategoryId/:id — guards: **none**. Purpose: vendors for a category id (string UUID). Message `SUCCESS`.
- [ ] GET /api/categories/subCategoriesByCategoryId/:id — guards: **none**. Purpose: sub-categories whose `vendorId = :id` (the name says category, but it filters by vendor). Message `SUCCESS`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/categories/:id — **stub**, guards none. Returns `This action updates a #<id> category`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/categories/:id — **stub**, guards none. Returns `This action removes a #<id> category`.
- Service throws: none. Bulk endpoints crash with a TypeError (500) if the body shape is wrong.


## Chat

Files: `src/modules/chat/chat.controller.ts`, `mobile-inbox.controller.ts`, `chat.service.ts`, `mobile-inbox.service.ts`, `chat.gateway.ts`, `chat.scheduler.ts`, `dto/*`.

No global ValidationPipe exists. Only `POST /api/(mobile/)inbox/:conversationId/message` applies one (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default options: no `transform`, no `whitelist`).

### /api/chats (ChatsController)

- [ ] POST /api/chats/room/group
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT and team members, FREELANCER (the assigned engineer, an engineer with an offer, a freelance-company owner or member), CUSTOMER.
  - Purpose: fetch or create the work-order chat room (engineer↔team room, or the customer↔team room when the target is a CUSTOMER).
  - Body `JoinChatDto` (ValidationPipe **NOT applied**, so decorators are not enforced):
    - `workOrderId`: number, required. `@Type(() => Number) @IsInt() @IsNotEmpty()`.
    - `companyId`: number, required. `@Type(() => Number) @IsInt() @IsNotEmpty()`.
    - `engineerId`: number, required. `@Type(() => Number) @IsInt() @IsNotEmpty()`. This is the target user: the engineer, or a customer.
  - Service throws (`ChatService.fetchOrCreateChatRoom`):
    - `ForbiddenException('Work order not found')`: customer branch, work order missing.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not authorized to access this chat')`: a FREELANCER opener tries to open the customer↔team room.
    - `ForbiddenException('Work order not found')`: the work order is not found for `{id, companyId}`.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not authorized to access this chat')`: the caller is the CUSTOMER who created the work order and uses the engineer-room path.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not authorized to access this chat')`: the caller is none of these: a team member (teamId/secondaryTeamId plus the company owner), the freelance-company owner, the assigned engineer, an engineer with an active offer (ROUTED/ACCEPTED/PENDING, matched on userId or referredEngineerId, and only when caller == engineerId), an accepted freelance-company member, or the existing room engineer.
  - Participants are the team members plus engineerId. The FC owner is added only if the engineer is an ACCEPTED member of the assigned FC.
- [ ] GET /api/chats/users/:workOrderId/:companyId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT/team, FREELANCER company owner.
  - Purpose: list the users/rooms that have chatted on a work order (sidebar).
  - Params: `workOrderId`, `companyId`.
  - Controller throw: `HttpException('Invalid companyId provided', HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST)` when companyId is missing or NaN.
  - Service behaviour:
    - A non-integer workOrderId, companyId or userId returns `[]`.
    - A FREELANCER who owns no company gets `[]`.
    - An FC owner sees results only if their company is assigned.
  - Response: `{ data, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.
- [ ] GET /api/chats/:chatRoomId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any room participant.
  - Purpose: cursor-paginated group-chat messages.
  - Query `GetChatMessagesDto` (ValidationPipe **NOT applied**, so the service coerces the values):
    - `cursor?`: number. `@IsOptional() @Type(() => Number) @IsInt() @Min(1)`. This is the oldest message id the client already holds.
    - `limit?`: number. `@IsOptional() @Type(() => Number) @IsInt() @Min(1) @Max(100)`. The service default is 10 (`DEFAULT_MESSAGES_PAGE_SIZE`) and the value is clamped to 1–100 (`MAX_MESSAGES_PAGE_SIZE`).
  - Controller throw: `HttpException('You are not allowed to view these messages', HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN)` when the caller is not in the accessible rooms (`verifyChatParticipant`).
  - Returns `{ messages (oldest first), nextCursor, hasMore }`. A NaN chatRoomId returns an empty page.
- [ ] POST /api/chats/files
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any chat user.
  - Purpose: upload chat attachments and return their digital-asset ids.
  - Multipart: `FilesInterceptor('files')`.
    - **No maxCount, no fileSize limit and no mimetype filter.**
    - Body `workOrderId` is untyped (`any`) and has no validation.
  - No files returns `{ fileIds: [] }`. Otherwise it returns `{ data: number[] digitalAssetIds, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.
  - Files are stored in S3 under `<userId>/workOrders/<workOrderId>/messages`, category `CHAT_MESSAGE`.
  - The service (`DigitalAssetService.uploadChatMessageFiles`) catches and logs errors, returning `undefined` on failure with no exception.
- [ ] (commented out, not routed) `POST /api/chats/:chatRoomId/send`, `POST /api/chats/:chatRoomId/addParticipants`.
- Unused DTOs: `SendMessageDto` (`chatRoomId`, `senderId`, `workOrderId` each `@IsInt() @IsNotEmpty()`; `message` `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`) and `UpdateChatDto` (PartialType plus `id`) are not bound to any route.

### /api/mobile/inbox and /api/inbox (MobileInboxController, both prefixes are aliases; class-level JWT guard)

- [ ] GET /api/mobile/inbox (also /api/inbox)
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: all chat users (mobile and web).
  - Purpose: list conversations with previews and unread counts.
  - Query:
    - `limit?`: string, converted to a number. Default 20, clamped to 1–50.
    - `cursor?`: opaque encoded string.
  - Response: `{ data, nextCursor, totalUnreadCount, unreadConversationCount, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.
- [ ] GET /api/mobile/inbox/:conversationId
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: conversation summary plus messages. By default it also marks the conversation read.
  - Query:
    - `limit?`: default 0, meaning the whole conversation.
    - `before?`: message id, for older history.
    - `after?`: message id, for a forward catch-up after reconnect.
    - `markAsRead?`: every value except the string `'false'` marks the conversation read.
  - Throws:
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to view this conversation')` when the caller is not a participant.
    - `NotFoundException('Conversation not found')` from the summary lookup.
  - Returns `{ conversation, messages, previousCursor, nextCursor }`. `previousCursor` is set only on a non-backfill full page. `nextCursor` is set only on an `after` backfill with a full page.
- [ ] POST /api/mobile/inbox/:conversationId/message
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: send a message. It is delegated to `ChatGateway.handleSendMessage`, so it has the same broadcast, unread, notification and push effects as a web send.
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`).
  - Body `SendInboxMessageDto`:
    - `message`: string, required. `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`.
    - `digitalAssetIds?`: number[]. `@IsOptional() @IsArray() @ArrayMaxSize(20) @Type(() => Number) @IsInt({ each: true })`.
    - `messageType?`: string. `@IsOptional() @IsString()`.
    - `replyToId?`: number. `@IsOptional() @Type(() => Number) @IsInt()`.
    - `clientMessageId?`: string. `@IsOptional() @IsString() @MaxLength(64)`. This is an idempotency key: resending the same key returns the original message with no new row and no second broadcast. A P2002 race returns the winning row.
  - Throws:
    - `NotFoundException('Conversation not found')`.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to send messages in this conversation')` when `canUserSendMessageInRoom` is false.
  - Returns `{ data: { sentMessage, conversation }, message: 'Message sent successfully' }`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/mobile/inbox/readAll
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: mark every accessible conversation as read.
  - Returns `{ data: { conversationsCleared, totalUnreadCount, unreadConversationCount }, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/mobile/inbox/:conversationId/read
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: mark one conversation read without loading it.
  - Throws `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to view this conversation')` for a non-participant.
  - Returns `{ data: { unreadConversationCount }, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.

### Chat WebSocket gateway (Socket.IO, `@WebSocketGateway({ cors: { origin: '*', credentials: true } })`, default namespace)

The socket has **no JWT auth**. Identity comes from payload fields (`clientId`, `senderId`, `userId`). Messages from the client:

- [ ] `joinGroupChat` `{ workOrderId, clientId, companyId, engineerId }`
  - Runs `fetchOrCreateChatRoom` with `clientId` as the acting user.
  - On failure the client receives `joinError { message }`. The default message is `'You are not authorized to access this chat'`.
  - On success:
    - Sets `client.data.userId`, `chatRoomId` and `chatAuthedAt`, and joins the room.
    - Sends `joinSuccess` to the client and broadcasts `newParticipant` to the room.
    - Resets unread and marks messages and notifications read.
    - Sends `unreadCountUpdated { conversationId, chatRoomId, unreadCount: 0 }`.
- [ ] `sendMessage` `{ conversationId | chatRoomId, senderId, engineerId, message, workOrderId, digitalAssetIds, messageType?, replyToId?, clientMessageId? }`
  - Authorization is cached per socket for 5 minutes (`CHAT_SEND_AUTH_TTL_MS`). After that it is re-checked with `canUserSendMessageInRoom`.
  - Denied: the client receives `sendError { chatRoomId, message: 'You are not authorized to send messages in this chat.' }`.
  - Success: the room receives `receiveMessage`. The server also emits `messageDelivered`, `messageReadByUser` and `unreadCountUpdated` (to `user_<id>` rooms) and triggers notifications.
  - A replay (same `clientMessageId`) is not re-broadcast.
- [ ] `editMessage` `{ messageId, senderId, newContent }`
  - Throws `Error('Unauthorized')` when the sender is not the author. The error is caught and only logged.
  - Broadcasts `messageEdited`.
- [ ] `deleteMessage` `{ messageId, senderId }`
  - Soft delete. The same `Error('Unauthorized')` check applies and is logged only.
  - Broadcasts `messageDeleted`.
- [ ] `toggleReaction` `{ messageId, userId, emoji, conversationId?, chatRoomId? }`
  - Without a room, the client receives `error { message: 'conversationId is required to toggle a reaction' }`.
  - Broadcasts `reactionUpdated`.
- [ ] `chatRoomVisibility` `{ visible, conversationId?, chatRoomId? }`: tracks whether the conversation is currently being viewed.
- [ ] `leaveChatRoom` `{ clientId }`
  - Broadcasts `userLeft`.
  - If the room is empty it may be deleted, and `chatRoomDeleted { chatRoomId }` is emitted.
- Server-to-client events also include `allMessagesReadByUser`.
- Scheduler: `ChatScheduler.cleanupChatRooms` runs at `EVERY_DAY_AT_MIDNIGHT`. It deletes rooms with no messages, and rooms on active work orders where a client messaged a non-assigned engineer who has not read them in 15 days (`deleteUnreadClientChatRooms`).
- `addParticipantToChatRoom` throws a plain `Error('❌ User <id> does not exist.')` or `Error('❌ Chat room <id> does not exist.')`. It is an internal helper with no HTTP route.


## Clients

Source: `src/modules/clients/clients.controller.ts`, `clients.service.ts`, `dto/*`. Permission module: `CLIENT_CUSTOMER` (some routes also accept `ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT`; PermissionsGuard passes if the user has ANY of the listed module/access pairs).

Shared helpers:
- `isCompanyUser(userId)` finds user `isDeleted:false`; if no user or no `companyId` it throws `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')`.
- `ensureNoDuplicateClient`: same `clientName` + `email` + `companyId`, not deleted (and not the same id on update). If one exists it throws `BadRequestException('Client with same name and email already exists')`.
- File uploads on client routes use `FileFieldsInterceptor([{ name: 'clientLogoFile', maxCount: 1 }, { name: 'registrationCertificateFile', maxCount: 1 }])`. No multer `limits`, no fileFilter, and no mimetype/size validation at the controller. `DigitalAssetService.uploadClientDigitalAsset` / `uploadClientRegistrationCertificate` throw `BadRequestException('File is required')` only when the file is missing, and they are only called when a file is present.

### /api/clients

- [ ] POST /api/clients: JwtAuthGuard + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isWrite`). Roles: CLIENT (company user). Creates a client/customer record, its address (type `CLIENT_CUSTOMER`, locationType `COMMERCIAL`) and a ClientAccount, with an optional logo and registration certificate. multipart/form-data.
  - ValidationPipe: YES (`@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({ transform: true }))`, no whitelist, so extra fields are NOT stripped). Because it is method-level, it also runs on `@Request`/`@UploadedFiles`.
  - Body `CreateClientDto`:
    - `clientName`: string, REQUIRED, `@IsString()`
    - `managerName`: string, effectively REQUIRED (`@IsString()` with no `@IsOptional`, even though it is typed `?`)
    - `website`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `taxRegistrationNumber`: optional `@IsString()` (GST/VAT)
    - `registrationNumber`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `email`: optional `@IsString()` (NOT IsEmail)
    - `teamEmail`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `policies`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `instructions`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `phoneNumber`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `locationType`: optional `@IsString()` (typed LocationType, no IsEnum; the service ignores it and hard-codes `COMMERCIAL`)
    - `country`, `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `branchId`: optional `@IsInt()`. Transform: `undefined`/`''`/`'null'` → undefined, else `parseInt`
    - `teamId`: optional `@IsString()` (service-delivery team)
    - `secondaryTeamId`: optional `@IsString()` (operations team)
    - `accountsPerson`, `accountsEmail`, `accountsPhone`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `paymentTerms`: optional `@IsNumber()` + `@Type(() => Number)`
    - `taxType`: optional, NO validator (typed Prisma `TaxType`)
    - `taxPercentage`: optional `@IsNumber()` + `@Type(() => Number)`
    - `isParentClient`: optional `@IsBoolean()`. Transform: `value === 'true' || value === true`
    - `parentClientId`: optional `@IsInt()`. Transform: parseInt when not undefined/''
  - Files: `clientLogoFile` (max 1), `registrationCertificateFile` (max 1). No size or type limits.
  - Service throws:
    - `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')` if the user has no company
    - `BadRequestException('Client with same name and email already exists')`
    - `Error('Branch not found')` if `branchId` is not in the company. This is a plain Error, so it returns a 500.
    - `BadRequestException('Service delivery team is required')` if not a parent and no `teamId`
    - `BadRequestException('Operations team is required')` if not a parent and no `secondaryTeamId`
    - `Error('Team not found')` (plain Error, 500)
    - `BadRequestException('Secondary team must be different from the primary team')`
    - `Error('Secondary team not found')` (plain Error, 500)
    - `BadRequestException('Parent client not found or is not a parent client')` (in the transaction)
    - Also inherits `createClientAccount` throws (see POST /clients/account). Here the full user object is passed, so the company check works.
  - Side effect: if the primary team has an email, it is copied to `teamEmail` on the client, or on the parent client when `parentClientId` is set.
  - Response `{ data: { clientName, website, id }, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.

- [ ] GET /api/clients: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isRead`). Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER (scoped). Paginated list of the company's clients with WO status counts (PUBLISHED/ASSIGNED/WORKDONE/APPROVED/PAID). Parent clients aggregate their children's counts.
  - ValidationPipe: NO. The `FilterClientDto` decorators are inert and query values arrive as strings.
  - Query `FilterClientDto` (all optional):
    - `search` (IsString)
    - `manager` (IsString, managerName contains, case-insensitive)
    - `limit` (IsNumberString)
    - `page` (IsNumber, default 1)
    - `projectsCount`, `totalWorkOrdersCount`, `publishedCount`, `assignedCount`, `workDoneCount`, `approvedCount`, `paidCount` (IsNumber, declared but not used as filters)
    - `companyId` (IsString)
    - `clientId` (IsString)
    - `country` (IsString)
    - `branchId` (`@Type(()=>Number) @IsNumber`)
    - `teamId` (IsString, matches primary or secondary team)
    - `taxType` (IsString, clientAccount.taxType)
    - `teamIds` (IsString, CSV)
    - `clientIds` (IsString, CSV)
    - `isParentClient` (`'true'|'false'` string)
    - `parentClientId` (IsString)
  - CUSTOMER role is scoped to its own client id OR the children of its customerId.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')` if the user is not found.

- [ ] GET /api/clients/accounts: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isRead`). Lists ClientAccounts for clients created by the current user (`createdById = userId`).
  - No DTO, no ValidationPipe.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('User ID is required.')` and `BadRequestException('Invalid user ID.')`.
  - Returns `{ totalCount, accounts }`.

- [ ] GET /api/clients/availableToLink: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isRead`). Lists company clients that are not parents and have no parent, so they can be linked to a parent.
  - Query: `page` (default 1), `limit` (default 20). Manual `+` casts, no pipe.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')`.

- [ ] GET /api/clients/:parentClientId/children: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isRead`). Lists the child clients of a parent client, with the parent summary and a signed logo URL.
  - Query `FilterClientDto` (limit, page, search, clientId, branchId, teamIds, manager, country, taxType used). No ValidationPipe.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')` and `NotFoundException('Parent client not found')` (parent must be `isParentClient`, not deleted, same company).

- [ ] PATCH /api/clients/linkToParent: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isUpdate`). Links child clients to a parent client.
  - Body is an untyped inline type `{ childIds: number[]; parentClientId: number }`. No DTO or validation.
  - Throws:
    - `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')`
    - `NotFoundException('Parent client not found or is not a parent client')`
    - `NotFoundException('One or more clients not found')`
    - `BadRequestException('Client "<clientName>" is a parent client and cannot be linked as a child')`
    - `BadRequestException('Client "<clientName>" is already linked to a parent')`
  - Returns `{ message: '<n> client(s) linked to parent successfully' }`.

- [ ] GET /api/clients/:id: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isRead` OR `ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isRead`). Returns full client details, including address, branch, teams, owner, non-deleted childClients with signed certificate URLs, and signed logo/certificate URLs.
  - There is no company-ownership check, so any holder of the permission can read any client id.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Client not found')`.
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Client fetched successfully' }`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/clients/unlinkFromParent: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isUpdate`). Unlinks a child from its parent and clears `defaultEntityId` references to the child.
  - Body `{ childId: number }` (untyped, no validation).
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')`, `NotFoundException('Client not found')` and `BadRequestException('Client is not linked to any parent')`.
  - Returns `{ message: 'Client unlinked from parent successfully' }`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/clients/:id: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isUpdate` OR `ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isUpdate`). Updates a client (partial, field-by-field). Files as on POST.
  - ValidationPipe: YES (`transform: true`).
  - Body `UpdateClientDto` = `PartialType(CreateClientDto)`. All fields are optional, with the same validators and transforms.
  - For a self-signup customer with `defaultEntityId`, the ENTITY_ONLY_FIELDS (`managerName`, `registrationNumber`, `taxRegistrationNumber`, `accountsPerson`, `accountsEmail`, `accountsPhone`, `paymentTerms`, `taxType`, `taxPercentage`) are written to the default entity. The certificate goes to the entity too.
  - Service throws:
    - `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')` (editor missing, or a non-admin without a company)
    - `NotFoundException('Client not found')`
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to update this client')` (non-admin, different company)
    - `BadRequestException('Client with same name and email already exists')` (only when name or email changes)
    - `BadRequestException('Service delivery team is required')` and `BadRequestException('Operations team is required')` (only when a non-parent's teams change)
    - `NotFoundException('Team not found')`
    - `BadRequestException('Secondary team must be different from the primary team')`
    - `NotFoundException('Secondary team not found')`
  - Sending an empty `secondaryTeamId` disconnects it.
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Client updated successfully' }`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/clients/customer/profile: JwtAuthGuard only. Roles: CUSTOMER. The customer edits their own customer profile. Files as on POST.
  - ValidationPipe: YES (`transform: true`). Body `UpdateClientDto` (as above).
  - Throws `ForbiddenException('Only a customer account can manage a customer profile')` (role is not CUSTOMER or there is no customerId).
  - Throws `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to update this customer')` (the client is not owned by the user).
  - Then all the `PATCH /:id` throws apply, with the duplicate check skipped.
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Profile saved' }`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/clients/:id/customerProfile: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isUpdate`). Roles: ADMIN. The admin edits a customer profile (any client with `ownerId` not null). Files as on POST.
  - ValidationPipe: YES. Body `UpdateClientDto`.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Customer not found')`, then the `PATCH /:id` throws (duplicate check skipped).
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Profile saved' }`.

- [ ] GET /api/clients/customer/submitProfile: JwtAuthGuard only. Roles: CUSTOMER. Submits the customer profile for admin approval: sets user status `SUBMITTED` and `profileNeedsResubmit=false`, sends the first ADMIN a SYSTEM notification and emails the internal notifications address.
  - This state-changing action uses GET.
  - Throws the two `ForbiddenException`s of `resolveOwnCustomer` (see customer/profile).
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Profile submitted for approval' }`.

- [ ] POST /api/clients/owner/:id: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isWrite`). Roles: CLIENT company owner. Creates the CUSTOMER login user for client `:id`: a random password (`crypto.randomBytes(8)` base64), a 'Customer' designation, customer owner permissions and a copied address. Sends a welcome email containing the password.
  - Body `any` (`email`, `firstName`, `lastName`, `phone`). No validation. Values fall back to the client's email/clientName/phoneNumber.
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('Customer is required')` (id missing or NaN)
    - `ForbiddenException('Only company owners can create users under a company')`
    - `ForbiddenException('Customer does not exist')`
    - `HttpException('User already exists.', 409 CONFLICT)` (verified user with the email)
    - `HttpException('User already exists with this email, but not verified', 403 FORBIDDEN)`
    - `BadRequestException(err)` on Prisma P2002 involving `clientId`
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Company user created successfully' }`.

- [ ] DELETE /api/clients/owner/:id: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isDelete`). Soft-deletes the owner user of client `:id` and clears `ownerId`.
  - There is no company-ownership check.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Client not found')` and `HttpException('No associated account found for this client', 404)`.
  - Returns `{ message: 'Associated account deleted successfully' }`.

- [ ] GET /api/clients/account/:id: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isRead` OR `ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isRead`). Fetches a ClientAccount by account id, or by client id when `?byClientId=true`.
  - Query: `byClientId` (string `'true'`).
  - No throws. Returns `{ data: account|null, message: 'SUCCESS' }`. No company scoping.

- [ ] POST /api/clients/account: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isWrite`). Creates a ClientAccount (accounts contact, payment terms and tax).
  - ValidationPipe: NO. The validators are inert.
  - Body `CreateClientAccountDto`:
    - `clientId`: `@IsInt()`, required
    - `accountsPerson`: optional `@IsString`
    - `email`: optional `@IsString`
    - `phone`: optional `@IsString`
    - `paymentTerms`: optional `@IsNumber`
    - `taxType`: optional, no validator
    - `taxPercentage`: optional `@IsNumber`
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Client not found')`, `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to add accounts for this client')` and `BadRequestException('Client Account already exists.')` (P2002 on clientId).
  - BUG: the controller passes `+req.user.id` (a number) as `user`, so `user.companyId` is undefined. The company check then always fails with the Forbidden error for any existing client.

- [ ] PATCH /api/clients/account/:accountId: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isUpdate`). Updates a ClientAccount.
  - ValidationPipe: NO. Body `UpdateClientAccountDto` = `PartialType(CreateClientAccountDto)`. `clientId` is needed for the company lookup.
  - Empty or NaN `taxPercentage` is stored as null. `paymentTerms` is cast with `+` (NaN when missing).
  - Throws:
    - `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')`
    - `NotFoundException('Client not found')`
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to update this client account')`
    - `NotFoundException('Client account not found')`

- [ ] DELETE /api/clients/:id: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`CLIENT_CUSTOMER:isDelete`). Soft-deletes a client. It also soft-deletes the client's customer users, unlinks its children, clears `defaultEntityId` references, deletes the logo from S3 and soft-deletes the ClientAccount.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')`, `NotFoundException('Client not found')` and `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to delete this client')`.
  - Returns `{ message: 'Client deleted successfully' }`.

- [ ] (commented out) POST /api/clients/set-permissions: disabled and not routed. `SetClientPermissionsDto` has `module` IsEnum(ModuleType), `userId` IsNumber, and `isRead`/`isWrite`/`isDelete`/`isUpdate` IsBoolean.


## Companies

Source: `src/modules/companies/companies.controller.ts`, `companies.service.ts`, `dto/*`.

### /api/companies

- [ ] POST /api/companies: NO guards (public). Creates a Company row directly from the DTO (`company.create({ data: dto })`).
  - ValidationPipe: YES (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default options, no transform).
  - Body `CreateCompanyDto`:
    - `name`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `noOfEmployees`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `companyAnnualRevenue`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `technicianFrequency`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `technicianUrgency`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `ownerId`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()` but typed number. The validator requires a string while Prisma expects an Int, so a valid request will likely fail at the DB.
  - Returns `{ data: { company, message: 'Company created successfully' } }`.
  - Security: unauthenticated company creation.

- [ ] POST /api/companies/users: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`USERS:isWrite` OR `ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`). Roles: CLIENT company owner. Creates a CLIENT-role user under the owner's company, with an address and `isFirstLogin=true`.
  - ValidationPipe: YES (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`).
  - Body `CreateCompanyUser`:
    - `firstName`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `lastName`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `designation`: number, `@IsNumber() @IsNotEmpty()` (designation id)
    - `password`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty() @MinLength(8)`
    - `email`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsEmail()`
    - `phoneNumber`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
    - `country`, `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`, `timeZone`: optional `@IsString()`
    - `longitude`, `latitude`: optional `@IsNumber()`
  - Throws:
    - `ForbiddenException('Only company owners can create users under a company')`
    - `HttpException('User already exists.', 409)`
    - `HttpException('User already exists with this email, but not verified', 403)`
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Company user created successfully' }`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/companies/users/:id: JWT + PermissionsGuard (`USERS:isWrite`). Updates a company user (name, phone, designation, address). `:id` uses ParseIntPipe.
  - ValidationPipe: YES.
  - Body `UpdateCompanyUser` = `PartialType(CreateCompanyUser)` with `password` and `email` marked `@Exclude()`. Exclude has no effect without transform, so `email` is passed to the update. It adds `status`: optional `@IsString()`.
  - Throws `ForbiddenException('Only company owners can update users under a company')` (when editing someone else without owning a company) and `ForbiddenException('something is wrong')` (target not in the same company).
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'Company user updated successfully' }`.

- [ ] GET /api/companies/users: JwtAuthGuard only. Roles: CLIENT/CUSTOMER company users, ADMIN. Lists the CLIENT/CUSTOMER users of a company that have `customerId=null`.
  - Query `FilterCompanyDto` (no ValidationPipe, inert):
    - `page` (IsNumber)
    - `limit` (IsNumberString)
    - `search`, `companyName`, `fullName`, `country`, `city`, `email`, `manager`, `status`, `role` (IsString)
    - `companyType` (`@IsIn(['FREELANCE','CLIENT'])`)
    - `filter` (IsBoolean)
    - `id` (IsNumber, the company id; when given it overrides the caller's company and is NOT authorization-checked)
  - Throws `HttpException('Access denied', 403)` if the user is not found.

- [ ] GET /api/companies: JwtAuthGuard only. It carries `@RequiredPermissions(ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isRead)`, but PermissionsGuard is NOT applied, so the permission is not enforced. Roles: ADMIN. Lists all companies with stats.
  - Query `FilterCompanyDto` (search, country, city, companyName, email, manager, status, companyType FREELANCE|CLIENT, limit, page). No pipe.
  - Throws `Error('Failed to fetch companies')` (plain, 500) on any error.
  - Returns `{ companies, totalCount, currentPage, totalPages }`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/companies/users/:companyUserId/deactivate: JwtAuthGuard only. `:companyUserId` uses ParseIntPipe. Deactivates a user.
  - A customer owner cascades to all of that customer's CUSTOMER users and ends their sessions ('Customer and all its users have been deactivated').
  - A company owner cascades to all CLIENT/CUSTOMER/FREELANCER users of the company ('Owner and all users under the company have been deactivated').
  - Otherwise it sets `DEACTIVATED` and deactivates sessions (filtered by `req.deviceInfo.deviceId` from DeviceMiddleware when present).
  - Throws `ForbiddenException('Only company owners, admins, or the user themselves can deactivate this account')`, and `ForbiddenException('User not found')` (admin) or `ForbiddenException('User not found or not under your company')`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/companies/users/:companyUserId/activate: JwtAuthGuard only. ParseIntPipe. Reactivates a user: status `APPROVED` if verified, else `PENDING`. The owner only, with no cascade.
  - Throws `ForbiddenException('Only company owners or admins can activate users under a company')`, and `ForbiddenException('User not found')` or `ForbiddenException('User not found or not under your company')`.

- [ ] GET /api/companies/submitProfile: JwtAuthGuard only. Roles: CLIENT owner (also freelance-company owners). Submits the company profile: owner status `SUBMITTED`, `profileNeedsResubmit=false`, and a SYSTEM notification to the first ADMIN.
  - This state-changing action uses GET.
  - Throws `HttpException('User does not own a company', 400)` internally, but the catch-all rethrows it as `HttpException('Failed to submit company profile', 500 INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)`. The 400 is therefore never seen.
  - Returns `{ data: { company, owner }, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.

- [ ] GET /api/companies/:id: JwtAuthGuard only. ParseIntPipe (a non-integer id gives 400 'Validation failed (numeric string is expected)'). Returns the company with owner, non-deleted users (profile pictures), work-order count, address and digital assets grouped by category with signed URLs.
  - There is no ownership check.
  - Throws `HttpException('Company not found', 404)`.

- [ ] PATCH /api/companies/:id: JwtAuthGuard only. Roles: company owner or ADMIN. Updates company details, address and documents. multipart/form-data via `AnyFilesInterceptor()`, which accepts any field names with no limits.
  - Recognised file fields: `companyLogo` → COMPANY_LOGO, `registrationCertificate` → REGISTRATION_CERTIFICATE, `vatCertificate` → VAT_GST_CERTIFICATE, `publicLiabilityInsurance` → PUBLIC_LIABILITY_INSURANCE, `workerCompensationInsurance` → WORKER_COMPENSATION_INSURANCE. There is no size or mimetype validation.
  - Omitting files crashes: `files.forEach` runs on undefined when the request is not multipart.
  - ValidationPipe: NO. Body `UpdateCompanyDto` = `PartialType(CreateCompanyDto)` plus these optional fields (inert):
    - `email` (IsEmail)
    - `website`, `fax`, `mobileNumber`, `country` (IsString)
    - `address` (IsString, maps to addressLine1)
    - `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`, `timeZone` (IsString)
    - `longitude`, `latitude` (IsString, parseFloat'd)
    - `logo` (IsString)
    - `digitalAssetId` (IsInt)
    - `vatNumber`, `registrationNumber` (IsString)
  - Throws `ForbiddenException('Only the company owner can update this company')`, `ForbiddenException('Company not found')` and `BadRequestException('File is required')` (from uploadCompanyDigitalAsset, unreachable).

- [ ] DELETE /api/companies/:id: NO guards. Stub: returns the string `This action removes a #<id> company` and does nothing.


## customer-dashboard

### /api/customerDashboard
Controller-level guards: `JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard`. Scope is resolved from the JWT only (`resolveScope`). Errors raised by `resolveScope` apply to both routes:
- `ForbiddenException('Customer dashboard is only available to customer accounts')` when the caller is not role CUSTOMER or is not found.
- `ForbiddenException('This account is not linked to a customer entity')` when `companyId` or `customerId` is missing, or the linked client is not found or is deleted in the same company.

- [ ] GET /api/customerDashboard/filterOptions. Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS:isRead`. Roles: CUSTOMER. Purpose: return `{ isParentEntity, entities[{id,name}] }`. A parent entity gets its child entities; a child entity gets only itself.
  - ValidationPipe: n/a.
- [ ] GET /api/customerDashboard. Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS:isRead`. Roles: CUSTOMER. Purpose: a single aggregated dashboard covering summary counts, monthly trends, status distribution, entity breakdown, invoices (CLIENT-role invoices only), and country breakdown.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES** (`@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({ transform: true, whitelist: true }))`).
  - Query `CustomerDashboardFilterDto`:
    - `startDate`: optional, `@IsDateString()`. Default is the start of the previous month (UTC). It is clamped so the range is at most `MAX_TREND_MONTHS = 24` months before the end.
    - `endDate`: optional, `@IsDateString()`. Default is the end of next month (UTC).
    - `entityId`: optional, `@Type(() => Number) @IsInt()`. It is intersected with the authorised entities; a foreign id returns an empty dashboard, not an error.
  - Work orders are filtered on `orderStartDate` within the range. Invoices are filtered on `createdAt`.


## customer-users

### /api/customerUsers
Controller-level guard: `JwtAuthGuard` only (no PermissionsGuard). Owner-only checks come from `resolveOwner`, which is used by createUser, status, remove, and delete:
- `ForbiddenException('Customer users are only managed from a customer account')` when the caller is not CUSTOMER.
- `ForbiddenException('This account is not linked to a customer entity')` when `companyId` or `customerId` is missing, or the client is not found.
- `ForbiddenException('Only the customer account owner can manage users')` when the caller is not `client.ownerId`.

- [ ] POST /api/customerUsers/createUser. Guard: JWT. Roles: CUSTOMER (entity owner). Purpose: create a sub-user under the owner's customer entity. It generates a random password (`crypto.randomBytes(8).toString('base64')`, hashed with bcrypt using `SALT`) and creates a new "Customer" designation. Response message: 'User account created under your customer account'.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default, so `transform: false`). The email lowercase `@Transform` affects only validation, not the value passed on; the service compares case-insensitively.
  - Body `CreateCustomerUserDto`:
    - `firstName`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty() @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, { message: 'First name must only contain letters' })`
    - `lastName`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty() @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, { message: 'Last name must only contain letters' })`
    - `email`: `@IsEmail() @IsNotEmpty()`, plus `@Transform` to lowercase and trim
    - `phoneNumber`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('You cannot add yourself as a user')` when the email equals the owner's email.
    - `ConflictException('An account with this email already exists.')` when the email matches a non-deleted user (case-insensitive).
- [ ] GET /api/customerUsers/members. Guard: JWT. Roles: CUSTOMER owner (own entity); ADMIN or a CLIENT company user whose company services the customer (with `customerId`). Purpose: paginated list of customer users.
  - ValidationPipe: **NO** (inline query type).
  - Query parameters:
    - `page`: default 1
    - `limit`: default 20
    - `search`
    - `customerId`: numeric. Omit it for the caller's own entity.
    - `sortBy`: `id | name | status | createdAt`. Anything else sorts by `createdAt desc`.
    - `sortOrder`: `asc`; anything else means `desc`.
    - `fullName`
    - `email`
    - `country`
    - `status`
  - The owner is excluded when a customer views its own list, and included (with `isOwner: true`) for outside viewers.
  - Throws:
    - `NotFoundException('Customer not found')` when `customerId` is unknown or deleted.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to view this customer’s users')` for a non-admin who is not in the servicing company, or a CUSTOMER viewer.
    - The `resolveOwner` errors above apply when `customerId` is omitted.
- [ ] PATCH /api/customerUsers/status. Guard: JWT. Roles: CUSTOMER owner. Purpose: bulk activate or deactivate sub-users. Deactivating also ends their active sessions. Reactivated users go to APPROVED if `isVerified`, otherwise PENDING. Response message: 'Users activated successfully' or 'Users deactivated successfully'.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES** (default).
  - Body `UpdateCustomerUsersStatusDto`:
    - `userIds`: `@IsArray() @ArrayNotEmpty({ message: 'Select at least one user.' }) @IsInt({ each: true }) @Type(() => Number)`
    - `active`: `@IsBoolean()`
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('Select at least one user')` when the deduplicated list is empty.
    - `BadRequestException('You cannot change the status of your own account')`.
    - `NotFoundException('Some users were not found under your customer account')`.
    - `BadRequestException('The selected users are already deactivated')`.
    - `BadRequestException('Only a deactivated account can be activated')`.
- [ ] POST /api/customerUsers/remove. Guard: JWT. Roles: CUSTOMER owner. Purpose: bulk soft-delete, looping over `removeUser` for each id. Response message: 'Users removed successfully'.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES**.
  - Body `RemoveCustomerUsersDto`:
    - `userIds`: `@IsArray() @ArrayNotEmpty({ message: 'Select at least one user.' }) @IsInt({ each: true }) @Type(() => Number)`
    - `reason`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty({ message: 'Please provide a reason before deleting the users.' }) @MaxLength(500)`, trimmed
  - Throws: `BadRequestException('Select at least one user')`, plus every error listed for DELETE below.
  - Not transactional across users: a failure part-way leaves earlier users deleted.
- [ ] DELETE /api/customerUsers/:id. Guard: JWT. Roles: CUSTOMER owner. Purpose: soft-delete one sub-user. It creates a `deletedAccount` record, anonymises the email to `deleted-<id>@deleted.invalid`, blanks the phone and password, and deactivates sessions. Response message: 'User removed successfully'.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES**.
  - Body `RemoveCustomerUserDto`:
    - `reason`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty({ message: 'Please provide a reason before deleting the user.' }) @MaxLength(500)`, trimmed
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('You cannot remove your own account')`.
    - `NotFoundException('User not found under your customer account')`.


## Departments

### `/departments`
All routes use JWT. Roles: CLIENT company owner for writes; any company member for reads.
- [ ] POST /api/departments — ValidationPipe: **yes** (`@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({transform:true, whitelist:true}))`). Purpose: create a department in the owner's company and optionally link teams. Message: `Department created successfully`.
  - Body `CreateDepartmentDto`:
    - `name`: string, required. Rules: `@IsString`; `@IsNotEmpty({message:'Department name is required'})`; `@MaxLength(100,{message:'Department name must be at most 100 characters'})`; trimmed.
    - `description`: string, optional. Rules: `@IsString`; `@MaxLength(500,{message:'Description must be at most 500 characters'})`; trimmed.
    - `teamIds`: string[], optional. Rules: `@IsArray`; `@ArrayUnique({message:'Team IDs must be unique'})`; `@IsUUID('all',{each:true,message:'Each team ID must be a valid UUID'})`.
  - Throws:
    - `ForbiddenException('Only the company owner can manage departments')`
    - `ConflictException('A department with this name already exists')` (case-insensitive per company; also on P2002)
    - `BadRequestException('One or more selected teams are invalid')` (a team is not in the company or is deleted)
- [ ] PATCH /api/departments/:id — `ParseIntPipe` on id. ValidationPipe: **yes** (transform+whitelist). Purpose: rename/describe a department and re-sync its teams (teams no longer listed are detached). Message `Department updated successfully`.
  - Body `UpdateDepartmentDto`: PartialType of Create, plus an overridden `name`: optional, `@IsString`, `@IsNotEmpty({message:'Department name cannot be empty'})`, `@MaxLength(100,{message:'Department name must be at most 100 characters'})`, trimmed.
  - Throws: `ForbiddenException('Only the company owner can manage departments')`, `NotFoundException('Department not found')`, `ConflictException('A department with this name already exists')`, `BadRequestException('One or more selected teams are invalid')`.
- [ ] GET /api/departments — query params: `page`, `limit`, `search` (raw strings, coerced with `+`; blank search is ignored). Purpose: list the company's departments with teams and creator. No company → `{data:[], totalCount:0}`. Without a limit, all rows are returned.
- [ ] GET /api/departments/:id — `ParseIntPipe`. Throws `NotFoundException('Department not found')`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/departments/:id — `ParseIntPipe`. Purpose: detach teams, then **hard-delete** the department. Message `Department deleted successfully`.
  - Throws: `ForbiddenException('Only the company owner can manage departments')`, `NotFoundException('Department not found')`.


## Designations

### `/designations`
All routes use JWT (no PermissionsGuard). Roles: CLIENT (company).
- [ ] POST /api/designations — ValidationPipe: **yes**. Purpose: create a designation in the caller's company (roleId = CLIENT role), then seed all `ClientPermissionModules` permissions set to false inside a transaction.
  - Body `CreateDesignationDto`: `designation` (string, required, `@IsString` `@IsNotEmpty`), `description` (string, optional, `@IsOptional` `@IsString`).
  - Throws: `HttpException('User not found', 404)`, `HttpException('Client role not found', 404)`. The catch-all is `HttpException(error.message || 'Something went wrong', error.status || 500)`.
- [ ] GET /api/designations — Purpose: list the designations of the caller's company.
  - Throws: `HttpException('Not found', 404)`. Rethrows `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.
- [ ] GET /api/designations/:id — Purpose: get the permissions for a designation, auto-creating any missing `ClientPermissionModules` rows with all flags false.
  - Throws: `HttpException('Not found', 404)`, `HttpException('Designation Not found', 404)`. The catch block is **empty**, so errors are swallowed and the endpoint returns `data: undefined` with 200.
- [ ] PATCH /api/designations/:id — no ValidationPipe. Body `UpdateDesignationDto` (PartialType: `designation?`, `description?`). Returns the raw updated designation (not wrapped).
  - Throws: `HttpException('Not found', 404)`. The catch is **empty**, so failures return 200 with an empty body.
- [ ] POST /api/designations/permissions — ValidationPipe: **yes**. Purpose: update one permission row (read/write/update/delete flags) of a designation. Returns `data: undefined` (the service returns nothing).
  - Body `UpdateDesignationPermissionsDto`:
    - `designationId`: number, required (`@IsNumber`, `@IsNotEmpty`).
    - `permissionId`: number, required (`@IsNumber`, `@IsNotEmpty`).
    - `module`: `ModuleType`, required (`@IsNotEmpty` only, no `@IsEnum`).
    - `isRead`, `isWrite`, `isUpdate`, `isDelete`: boolean, required (`@IsNotEmpty`, `@IsBoolean`).
  - Throws: `HttpException('Not found', 404)`, `HttpException('Designation not exist', 404)`. Rethrows `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.


## Digital Assets

Source: `src/modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.controller.ts`, `digital-assets.service.ts`. DTOs `CreateDigitalAssetDto` and `UpdateDigitalAssetDto` are empty and unused.

Upload size rule: `UploadSizeValidator` (`src/utils/helpers/uploadSizeValidator.ts`).
- Convertible images (mime `image/*` except `image/svg+xml` and `image/gif`, or extension jpg/jpeg/png/tif/tiff/bmp/webp/avif/heic/heif) have a maximum of 10 MB (`IMAGE_MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE`).
- All other files have a maximum of 2 MB (`FILE_MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE = COMPRESS_THRESHOLD_BYTES`).
- Error text: `Images must be 10 MB or smaller.` or `Files of this type must be 2 MB or smaller.`
- ParseFilePipe returns a 400. A missing file also fails ParseFilePipe (`fileIsRequired` defaults to true) with 'File is required'.
- `FileTypeValidator` checks the mimetype against the regex.
- Routes without ParseFilePipe have no size or type checks.

### /api/digitalAssets

- [ ] POST /api/digitalAssets/profile: JwtAuthGuard. Any role. Uploads or replaces the profile picture (the previous one is soft-deleted). The file is resized to 500x500 JPEG at quality 75 and stored at `<userId>/profile`.
  - Field `file` (FileInterceptor, single). ParseFilePipe: UploadSizeValidator + `FileTypeValidator({ fileType: /(jpeg|jpg|png|webp)$/ })`.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('File is required')`.
  - Returns `{ data: { url }, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.

- [ ] POST /api/digitalAssets/identity: JwtAuthGuard. Roles: FREELANCER. Uploads ID documents (category IDENTITY_PROOF).
  - Field `documents`, `FilesInterceptor('documents', 2)` (max 2 files; a third gives multer 'Unexpected field'). ParseFilePipe: UploadSizeValidator + `FileTypeValidator({ fileType: /(jpeg|jpg|png|webp|pdf)$/ })`.
  - Throws `HttpException('Please upload the front side of your document (ID, Driving License, or Passport).', 400)` when the array is empty.
  - OCR validation exists in the service (`validateIDDocument`) but is NOT called.
  - BUG: `uploadIDDocuments` does not return the transaction result, so `data` is undefined.
  - Message: `Document verified and saved successfully as.`

- [ ] POST /api/digitalAssets/workingRights: JwtAuthGuard. Roles: FREELANCER. Uploads or replaces the WORKING_RIGHTS document (the previous one is soft-deleted).
  - Field `file`. ParseFilePipe: UploadSizeValidator + `/(jpeg|jpg|png|webp|pdf)$/`.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('File is required')`.
  - Returns `{ data: { url }, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.

- [ ] POST /api/digitalAssets/education: JwtAuthGuard. Roles: FREELANCER. Uploads or replaces EDUCATION_CERTIFICATE.
  - Field `file`. ParseFilePipe: UploadSizeValidator + `/(jpeg|jpg|png|webp|pdf)$/`.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('File is required')`.

- [ ] POST /api/digitalAssets/resume: JwtAuthGuard. Roles: FREELANCER (and admin/owner on behalf of a user). Uploads or replaces the RESUME.
  - Field `file`. ParseFilePipe: UploadSizeValidator + `FileTypeValidator({ fileType: /(jpeg|jpg|png|webp|pdf|msword|vnd\.openxmlformats-officedocument\.wordprocessingml\.document)$/i })` (images, PDF, DOC, DOCX).
  - Query `targetUserId` (optional; uploads for that user instead). NO authorization check on `targetUserId`, so any authenticated user can replace another user's resume.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('File is required')`.

- [ ] POST /api/digitalAssets/workOrder/:id: JwtAuthGuard. Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER. Uploads work-order customer files (WORK_ORDER_CUSTOMER_FILE) to `<userId>/workOrders/<id>/customerFiles` and logs `CUSTOMER_FILE_UPLOADED` on the work order.
  - Field `files`, `FilesInterceptor('files', 5)` (max 5). NO size or type validation. There is no check that the user may access the work order.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('work order id is missing.')` and `BadRequestException('No files uploaded.')`.
  - Service errors are swallowed (console.log), so the response may be empty on failure.
  - Returns `{ message: 'Files uploaded successfully', uploadedCount, files }`.

- [ ] POST /api/digitalAssets/workOrder/:id/deliverables: JwtAuthGuard. Roles: FREELANCER. Uploads deliverables (WORK_ORDER_DELIVERABLE_FILE) to `<userId>/workOrders/<id>/deliverables`.
  - Field `files`, max 5. NO size or type validation and no work-order access check.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('work order id is missing.')` and `BadRequestException('No files uploaded.')`. Service errors are swallowed.
  - Returns `{ message: 'Files uploaded successfully', uploadedCount, files }`.

- [ ] DELETE /api/digitalAssets/resume: JwtAuthGuard. Soft-deletes the RESUME.
  - Query `targetUserId` (optional). No authorization check, so it can delete any user's resume.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('No resume found for this user')`.
  - Returns `{ data: { id }, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.

- [ ] DELETE /api/digitalAssets/:id: JwtAuthGuard. Soft-deletes a digital asset.
  - Allowed if it is the caller's own asset. Also allowed for a WORK_ORDER_CUSTOMER_FILE when the caller is a CLIENT of the WO's company, or a CUSTOMER who created the WO or whose client (or its children, if a parent) owns it. The deletion logs `CUSTOMER_FILE_DELETED`.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('Digital asset not found')` (both when not found and when not permitted).
  - Returns `{ id }`.

- [ ] DELETE /api/digitalAssets/workOrder/deliverables/:assetId: JwtAuthGuard. Roles: FREELANCER. Soft-deletes the caller's own WORK_ORDER_DELIVERABLE_FILE.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('Digital asset not found')`.
  - Returns `{ id }`.

## Education

### `/education`
**No ValidationPipe** on any route. Roles: FREELANCER (own profile); ADMIN via `targetUserId`.
- [ ] POST /api/education — guards: JWT. Query: `targetUserId` (optional). If given, the record is created for that user; there is **no ownership/role check**. Purpose: add an education record.
  - Body `CreateEducationDto` (not enforced):
    - `institutionName`: string, required (`@IsString`).
    - `educationType`: enum, required (`@IsEnum(EducationType)`). Values: PHD_DEGREE, MASTERS_DEGREE, BACHELORS_DEGREE, HIGH_SCHOOL, ASSOCIATE_DEGREE, DOCTORATE, DIPLOMA, CERTIFICATION, OTHER.
    - `startDate`: Date, required (`@IsDate`).
    - `endDate`: Date, optional (`@IsOptional`, `@IsDate`).
  - The body is spread straight into Prisma, so unknown fields cause a Prisma 500. Returns the raw record.
- [ ] GET /api/education/all/:id — guards: JWT; `ParseIntPipe`. Purpose: list all education records of user `:id` (any authenticated user can read any user's list). Message `SUCCESS`.
- [ ] GET /api/education/:id — guards: JWT; `ParseIntPipe`. Purpose: get one record owned by the caller. Throws `NotFoundException('Education with ID <id> not found')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/education/:id — guards: **none** (unauthenticated); `ParseIntPipe`. Body: `any`, passed straight to `prisma.education.update`. No ownership check. A missing record → Prisma P2025 → 500.
- [ ] DELETE /api/education/:id — guards: JWT; `ParseIntPipe`. Query `targetUserId` (optional). Purpose: hard-delete the record where `{id, userId}`. A missing record → Prisma P2025 → 500. Returns `data: undefined`, message `SUCCESS`.


## Employment

### `/employment`
**No ValidationPipe** on any route. Roles: FREELANCER; ADMIN via `targetUserId`.
- [ ] POST /api/employment — guards: JWT. Query `targetUserId` (optional; no ownership check). Purpose: add an employment record. Returns the raw record.
  - Body `CreateEmploymentDto` (not enforced):
    - `companyName`: string, required (`@IsString`).
    - `designation`: string, required (`@IsString`).
    - `country`: string, required (`@IsString`).
    - `city`: string, required (`@IsString`).
    - `isPresent`: boolean, required (`@IsBoolean`).
    - `startDate`: Date, required (`@IsDate`).
    - `endDate`: Date, optional (`@IsOptional` `@IsDate`).
    - `description`: string, optional (`@IsOptional` `@IsString`).
- [ ] GET /api/employment/:id — guards: JWT; `ParseIntPipe`. Purpose: list all employments of **user** `:id`. Message `SUCCESS`.
- [ ] GET /api/employment/:id (second handler `findOne`, no guard) — **unreachable/shadowed**: the first `GET :id` handler wins. It would throw `NotFoundException('Employment with ID <id> not found')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/employment/:id — guards: **none**; `ParseIntPipe`. Body `UpdateEmploymentDto` (PartialType, not validated). Updates by id with no ownership check. A missing record → P2025 → 500.
- [ ] DELETE /api/employment/:id — guards: JWT; `ParseIntPipe`. Query `targetUserId` (optional). Purpose: hard-delete `{id,userId}`. Returns the raw deleted record. Missing → P2025 → 500.


## Feedback

### /api/feedback
Controller: `src/modules/feedback/feedback.controller.ts`. All routes use `JwtAuthGuard` only (no PermissionsGuard, no ProfileCompletionGuard). The `UpdateFeedbackDto` class is `PartialType(CreateFeedbackDto)` and no route uses it.

**CreateFeedbackDto** (`dto/create-feedback.dto.ts`):
- `rating`: number, optional. `@IsOptional @IsNumber`. There is no Min/Max, so any number (0, negative, 100) passes.
- `comment`: string, optional. `@IsOptional @IsString`. No length limit.
- `problemFaced`: ProblemOptionEnum[], optional. `@IsOptional @IsArray @IsEnum(ProblemOptionEnum, { each: true })`. Allowed values: POOR_COMMUNICATION, UNREALISTIC_DEADLINE, UNCLEAR_REQUIREMENTS, DELAYED_FEEDBACK, MICROMANAGEMENT, DISRESPECT_FOR_TIME, LACK_OF_PROFESSIONALISM, DELAYED_PAYMENT, FREQUENT_SCOPE_CHANGES, LATE_ARRIVAL, INSUFFICIENT_TECHNICAL_SKILLS, UNPROFESSIONAL_CONDUCT, INCOMPLETE_OR_POOR_QUALITY_WORK, DID_NOT_FOLLOW_INSTRUCTIONS, UNCLEAR_WORK_INSTRUCTIONS, SITE_ACCESS_ISSUES, INCORRECT_WORK_ORDER_INFORMATION, LACK_OF_ONSITE_SUPPORT, DISRESPECTFUL.
- `mostLiked`: LikedOptionEnum[], optional. `@IsOptional @IsArray @IsEnum(LikedOptionEnum, { each: true })`. Allowed values: CLEAR_COMMUNICATION, TIMELY_PAYMENT, PROFESSIONALISM, PROBLEM_SOLVING, PREPAREDNESS, RESPECTFUL_INTERACTION, FLEXIBILITY, WELL_ORGANIZED, COLLABORATIVE_ATTITUDE, DEFINED_PROJECT_SCOPE, WORKING_RELATIONSHIP, EXCELLENT_TECHNICAL_SKILLS, PROFESSIONAL_AND_COURTEOUS, ON_TIME_ARRIVAL, GREAT_COMMUNICATION, HIGH_QUALITY_WORK, WENT_ABOVE_AND_BEYOND, CLEAR_WORK_INSTRUCTIONS, EXCELLENT_COMMUNICATION, SMOOTH_SITE_ACCESS, QUICK_RESPONSE_AND_SUPPORT, PROFESSIONAL_AND_RESPECTFUL, WELL_ORGANIZED_ASSIGNMENT.
- `workOrderId`: number, **required**. `@IsNotEmpty @IsNumber`.
- `isPrivate`: boolean, optional. `@IsOptional @IsBoolean`.
- `isSkip`: boolean, optional. `@IsOptional @IsBoolean`.

- [ ] POST /api/feedback/engineer
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT or CUSTOMER (enforced in the service).
  - Purpose: create or update the feedback a company member gives to the assigned engineer on a work order (FeedbackType.FREELANCER).
  - Body: CreateFeedbackDto. **The ValidationPipe is NOT applied** (`@Body()` is plain and there is no global pipe), so none of the DTO rules are enforced on this route.
  - Service validations (`createUpdateFeedbackGivenToEngineer`):
    - The work order must have status WORKDONE, APPROVED, VERIFIED, INVOICED, DISPUTED or PAID. Otherwise it throws `new Error('Work order not found')`, a plain Error that surfaces as HTTP 500.
    - The caller must be a CLIENT or CUSTOMER in the work order's company. Otherwise it throws `UnauthorizedException()` with no message.
    - If the caller is a CLIENT, they must be a member of the work order's `teamId` or `secondaryTeamId`. Otherwise it throws `ForbiddenException("Only the work order's service delivery or operations team can give or update this feedback.")`.
    - If feedback of the same role already exists from that company, it is updated (rating, comment, isPrivate, givenToUserId); otherwise it is created. The engineer and the owner of the freelance company (vendor) are notified.
- [ ] POST /api/feedback/client
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER (the engineer assigned to the work order).
  - Purpose: create or update the engineer's feedback about the client (FeedbackType.CLIENT), using an upsert on `workOrderId_givenByUserId`.
  - Body: CreateFeedbackDto. **The ValidationPipe IS applied** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default options, no whitelist or transform).
  - Service validations (`createUpdateFeedbackGivenToClient`):
    - The work order must have status APPROVED, INVOICED or DISPUTED, and `engineerId` must equal the caller. Otherwise it throws `HttpException('Work order not found', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)`.
    - It then checks `userId !== workOrder.engineerId` and throws `new Error('Unauthorized')` (HTTP 500). This check can never fire, because the query above already filtered on engineerId.
- [ ] GET /api/feedback/marketplaceRating
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
