  - Purpose: marketplace rating aggregate for a user. For a freelance company owner, it aggregates over the owner plus all accepted members.
  - Query: `userId` (number via `+userId`; there is no validation, and a missing value gives NaN).
  - The service throws no exceptions.
- [ ] GET /api/feedback/:userId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: list the public feedback (`isPrivate: false`) for a user.
    - For a vendor profile (the user owns a company), only FREELANCER-type feedback given to the company's engineers is listed.
    - For an individual, feedback both given and received is listed.
    - The list is ordered by createdAt desc.
  - Param `userId` is cast with `+`, and there is no ParseIntPipe.
  - The service throws no exceptions.


## FreelanceCompany

### /api/freelanceCompany
Controller: `src/modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.controller.ts`. The class-level guard is `JwtAuthGuard`, and every route requires JWT. There is no PermissionsGuard or ProfileCompletionGuard. Two DTOs, `LinkFreelancerDto` (`email`: `@IsEmail @IsNotEmpty`, lower-cased and trimmed) and `RespondInviteDto` (`accept`: `@IsBoolean`), exist but no route uses them.

- [ ] POST /api/freelanceCompany/createEngineer
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER who owns a company (vendor).
  - Purpose: create a new FREELANCER account and link it to the owner's freelance company as a worker.
  - **ValidationPipe applied** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default options, so no transform). The `@Transform` on email runs only on the validated copy, which means the service gets the original, un-lowercased email. The service compares emails case-insensitively anyway.
  - Body `CreateLinkedEngineerDto`:
    - `firstName`: string, required. `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, { message: 'First name must only contain letters' })`.
    - `lastName`: string, required. `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, { message: 'Last name must only contain letters' })`.
    - `email`: string, required. `@IsEmail @IsNotEmpty`, with `@Transform` lowercase + trim.
    - `phoneNumber`: string, required. `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`. There is no format check.
    - `password`: string, required. `@IsString @IsNotEmpty @MinLength(8)`.
    - `areaCoverage`: string, optional. `@IsString @IsOptional`.
    - `address`: AddressDto, optional. It has `@IsOptional @Type(() => AddressDto)` but **no `@ValidateNested`**, so the nested rules are not enforced. The nested rules are:
      - `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`: `@IsString`.
      - `longitude`, `latitude`: `@IsDecimal`.
  - Service throws:
    - `ForbiddenException('Only freelancer company owners can create workers')` when the caller is not a FREELANCER or owns no company.
    - `BadRequestException('You cannot add yourself as a worker')` when the email equals the owner's email.
    - `ConflictException('An account with this email already exists.')` when a non-deleted user with that email exists (case-insensitive match).
  - Response message: 'Engineer account created and linked to your company'.
- [ ] GET /api/freelanceCompany/members
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: owner of a FREELANCER company.
  - Purpose: paginated list of the company's workers, with column projection.
  - **ValidationPipe applied on the query** (`new ValidationPipe({ transform: true, whitelist: true })`).
  - Query `GetMembersDto` (extends `ColumnSelectionDto`):
    - `columns`: string[], optional. `@IsOptional`, `@Transform(toColumnList)`, `@IsArray`.
    - `page`: int, optional, default 1. `@Transform(toOptionalNumber) @IsInt @IsPositive`.
    - `limit`: int, optional, default 20. `@IsInt @IsPositive`. There is no maximum.
    - `search`: string, optional. `@IsString`.
    - `approvedOnly`: boolean, optional, default false. `@Transform` accepts `true` or `'true'`. `@IsBoolean`.
    - `workOrderId`: int, optional. `@IsInt @IsPositive`.
    - `sortBy`: string, optional. `@IsString`.
    - `sortOrder`: string, optional. `@IsString`. There is no enum check.
  - Service throws `ForbiddenException('Only freelancer company owners can view workers')`.
- [ ] POST /api/freelanceCompany/route
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: freelance company owner.
  - Purpose: route an ASSIGNED work order to a company member by creating a ROUTED offer for that member.
  - **ValidationPipe applied.**
  - Body `RouteToMemberDto`:
    - `workOrderId`: int, required. `@IsInt @IsPositive`.
    - `memberId`: int, required. `@IsInt @IsPositive`. This is the FreelanceCompanyMember id, not a user id.
  - Service throws:
    - `ForbiddenException('Only freelance company owners can route work orders')`.
    - `NotFoundException('Work order not found or not assigned to your company')` when the work order is not ASSIGNED to the company, is deleted, or does not exist.
    - `NotFoundException('Member not found in your company')` when the member is not ACCEPTED or belongs to another company.
    - `BadRequestException('No assigned offer found for this work order')` when the owner has no ASSIGNED offer.
    - `BadRequestException('Work order has already been routed to a member')` when the member already has a ROUTED or ACCEPTED offer.
  - Response message: 'Work order routed to member successfully'.
- [ ] POST /api/freelanceCompany/assign
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: freelance company owner.
  - Purpose: assign an ASSIGNED work order directly to a company member.
  - **ValidationPipe applied.**
  - Body `AssignToMemberDto` has the same fields and rules as RouteToMemberDto: `workOrderId` and `memberId`, both `@IsInt @IsPositive`.
  - Service throws:
    - `ForbiddenException('Only freelance company owners can assign work orders')`.
    - `NotFoundException('Work order not found or not assigned to your company')`.
    - `NotFoundException('Member not found in your company')`.
    - `BadRequestException('No assigned offer found for this work order')`.
    - `BadRequestException('Work order has already been assigned to a member')` when the member already has a ROUTED, ACCEPTED or ASSIGNED offer.
    - `BadRequestException('Schedule Conflict: The selected engineer is already assigned to another work order during this time.')` (the constant SCHEDULE_CONFLICT_CLIENT_MESSAGE). This check only runs when the work order has both orderStartDate and orderStartTime.
  - Response message: 'Work order assigned to member successfully'.
- [ ] GET /api/freelanceCompany/companies
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT or CUSTOMER (company-side browsing). No role check is enforced.
  - Purpose: list APPROVED, non-deleted freelance companies with their stats and aggregated rating. For a CUSTOMER caller, contact details are stripped.
  - Query: `page` (default 1), `limit` (default 20), `search`. A purely numeric search matches the company id; otherwise every word must match the company name or the owner's email (case-insensitive). None of these are validated, and there is no ValidationPipe.
  - The service throws no exceptions.
- [ ] GET /api/freelanceCompany/admin/companies
  - Guards: JWT only. The route is intended for ADMIN, but **no role or permission check exists**, so any authenticated user can call it.
  - Purpose: list all companies owned by a FREELANCER, whatever their status.
  - Query: `page` (default 1), `limit` (default 20), `search` (company name contains, case-insensitive). No validation.
  - The service throws no exceptions.
- [ ] GET /api/freelanceCompany/admin/companies/:id/members
  - Guards: JWT only. The route is intended for ADMIN, CLIENT and CUSTOMER, and no role check exists. For a CUSTOMER caller, email, phoneNumber and addresses are stripped from the response.
  - Purpose: members of a freelance company (the owner plus ACCEPTED members) with their documents (signed URLs).
  - Param `id` is cast with `+`.
  - Query:
    - `page` (default 1), `limit` (default 20), `search`.
    - `approvedOnly` (the string 'true').
    - `fullName` (all words must match), `email`, `status` (the status filter is ignored when approvedOnly is set).
    - `excludeOwner` (the string 'true').
  - No ValidationPipe.
  - Service throws `NotFoundException('Freelance company not found')`, but only when `approvedOnly=true` and the owner is deleted or not APPROVED.


## Geo

Three controllers, **all public (no guards)**. Base data comes from the `Country`, `State` and `City` tables.

### `/countries`
- [ ] GET /api/countries — guards: none. Purpose: all countries sorted by name. Selected fields: id, name, iso2, iso3, phoneCode, currencyCode, currencyName, currencySymbol, nationality, emojiFlag. Message `SUCCESS`. No ValidationPipe needed.
- [ ] GET /api/countries/search — ValidationPipe: **yes** (`transform:true, whitelist:true`). Purpose: case-insensitive paginated country search. Returns `{data, meta:{total,page,limit,totalPages,hasNextPage}}`.
  - Query `SearchDto`:
    - `q`: string, required. Rules: `@IsString`, `@IsNotEmpty`, trimmed, `@MinLength(2,{message:'Search term must be at least 2 characters'})`.
    - `page`: int, optional, default 1. Rules: `@Type(Number)`, `@IsInt`, `@Min(1)`.
    - `limit`: int, optional, default 50. Rules: `@IsInt`, `@Min(1)`, `@Max(200)`.
- [ ] GET /api/countries/by-code/:code — no pipe. Purpose: a single country by ISO2 or ISO3 (trimmed, uppercased). Throws `NotFoundException('Country with code <code> not found')`.
- [ ] GET /api/countries/by-name/:name/cities — ValidationPipe: **yes** (transform+whitelist). Purpose: city names for a country looked up by name (case-insensitive).
  - Without `limit`: the full de-duplicated `string[]` (legacy behaviour).
  - With `limit`: a paginated, searchable, distinct slice, `{data:string[], meta:{page,limit,hasNextPage}}`, using a `take: limit+1` probe.
  - An unknown country returns an empty list, not a 404.
  - Query `CitiesByCountryQueryDto`:
    - `page`: int, optional, default 1, `@Min(1)`.
    - `limit`: int, optional, `@Min(1)` `@Max(200)`. The service defaults it to 20.
    - `q`: string, optional, trimmed.
- [ ] GET /api/countries/:countryId/states — `ParseIntPipe`. Purpose: all states of a country, sorted by name (id, name, stateCode, countryId). Throws `NotFoundException('Country with id <id> not found')`.
- [ ] GET /api/countries/:countryId/cities — `ParseIntPipe` + ValidationPipe (transform+whitelist). Purpose: paginated cities of a country.
  - Query `PaginationDto`: `page` (int, optional, default 1, `@Min(1)`), `limit` (int, optional, default 50, `@Min(1)` `@Max(200)`).
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Country with id <id> not found')`.

### `/states`
- [ ] GET /api/states/:stateId/cities — `ParseIntPipe` + ValidationPipe (transform+whitelist). Purpose: paginated cities of a state. Query `PaginationDto` (page default 1, `@Min(1)`; limit default 50, `@Min(1)` `@Max(200)`). Throws `NotFoundException('State with id <id> not found')`.

### `/cities`
- [ ] GET /api/cities/search — ValidationPipe: **yes** (transform+whitelist). Purpose: paginated city search, enriched with country (id, name, iso2) and state (id, name, stateCode). Query `SearchDto` (`q` required, min 2 chars with message `Search term must be at least 2 characters`; page ≥1; limit 1–200, default 50).


## Google

### (no controller — service-only)
- [ ] No REST endpoints. `GoogleService` is consumed by the locations module. It provides three methods:
  - `searchLocations(query)` (Places findPlaceFromText, then Place Details, then the Time Zone API)
  - `autocomplete(query)` (an empty string or null query returns `[]`)
  - `getFormattedPlaceDetails(placeId)`
  - Throws:
    - `InternalServerErrorException('No place found for the provided query.')`
    - `InternalServerErrorException('Error fetching time zone data: <statusText>')`
    - Wrapped finally as `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to fetch location data.')`
    - `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to autocomplete location')`
    - `InternalServerErrorException('No details found for the provided place ID.')`
    - `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to fetch place details.')`
  - Google calls time out after 1000 ms.


## Invoices

### /api/invoices

Source: `src/modules/invoices/invoices.controller.ts`, `invoices.service.ts`, `dto/*.ts`. Every route requires `JwtAuthGuard`; most also require `PermissionsGuard` (any one of the listed `module:access` pairs grants access). Responses are wrapped as `{ data, message: 'SUCCESS' }` unless noted. There is no global ValidationPipe, so DTO rules only run where a route-level pipe is listed below.

**Shared DTOs**

- `CreateInvoiceDto` (body):
  - `taxType`: `TaxType` enum (`GST | VAT | OTHERS`), optional, `@IsEnum(TaxType)`.
  - `taxPercentage`: number, optional, `@IsInt()`. Decimals are rejected.
  - `paymentTerms`: number, required, `@IsInt()`. The service sets `dueDate = now + paymentTerms days`.
  - `currency`: `CurrencyType` enum (ISO currency list in the Prisma schema), required, `@IsEnum(CurrencyType)`.
  - `clientId`: number, optional, `@IsInt()`.
  - `branchId`: number, required, `@IsInt()`.
  - `bankAccountId`: string, required, `@IsString()`.
  - `engineerId`: number, optional, `@IsInt()`.
  - `role`: `UserType` enum (`ADMIN | CLIENT | FREELANCER | MODERATOR | CUSTOMER`), required, `@IsEnum(UserType)`. `CLIENT` makes a customer invoice and `FREELANCER` makes an engineer invoice.
  - `workOrderIds`: number[], required, `@IsArray() @ArrayNotEmpty() @IsInt({ each: true })`.
- `UpdateInvoiceDto` extends `PartialType(CreateInvoiceDto)` (so all the fields above are optional) and adds:
  - `status`: `InvoiceStatus` enum (`GENERATED | VERIFIED | UNPAID | OVERDUE | PAID | UPDATED`), required, `@IsEnum`.
  - `toEmail`: string, `@ValidateIf(o => !o.skipEmail) @IsEmail()`.
  - `ccEmails`: optional. `@Transform` splits a comma-separated string into a trimmed array (a falsy value becomes `[]`), then `@IsArray() @IsEmail({}, { each: true })`.
  - `skipEmail`: optional boolean. `@Transform` turns `'true'`/`'false'` into booleans, then `@IsBoolean()`.
  - `file`: `@ValidateIf(o => o.status === PAID) @IsNotEmpty({ message: 'Attachment is required' })`. The controller overwrites this field with the uploaded files.
- `AddInvoiceWorkOrdersDto`:
  - `invoiceId`: number, required, `@IsInt()`.
  - `workOrderIds`: number[], required, `@IsArray() @ArrayNotEmpty() @IsInt({ each: true })`.
- `InvoiceFilterDto` (query). All fields are optional:
  - `role`: `@IsEnum(UserType)`.
  - `status`: `@IsEnum(InvoiceStatus, { each: true })`.
  - `taxType`: `@IsEnum(TaxType)`.
  - `currency`: `@IsEnum(CurrencyType)`.
  - `@IsInt()`: `clientId`, `customer`, `engineer`, `generatedBy`, `reviewedBy`, `sendBy`, `paidBy`, `id`, `page`, `limit`.
  - `@Type(() => Date) @IsDate()`: `creationStartDate`, `creationEndDate`, `dueStartDate`, `dueEndDate`.
  - `search`: `@IsString()`.
  - None of these rules run, because the list routes have no ValidationPipe. The controller converts `page` and `limit` with `Number()` (defaults 1 and 10).

**Common service guards.** Nearly every service method starts with these checks:

- The caller's user row must exist with `isDeleted: false` and have a `companyId`. Otherwise it throws `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`, or a plain `Error('Unauthorized')` in some methods (noted per route).
- The invoice is always scoped to `companyId = user.companyId` and `isDelete: false`.
- CUSTOMER-role users are further limited to invoices whose `clientId` is one of their scoped clients and whose status is in `CUSTOMER_VISIBLE_STATUSES` (`VERIFIED`, `UNPAID`, `OVERDUE`, …). `GENERATED` is never shown to a customer.

**Endpoints**

- [ ] POST /api/invoices
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isWrite`. For CLIENT (and delegated MODERATOR) users.
  - Purpose: generate a customer invoice from work orders.
  - Body: `CreateInvoiceDto`. ValidationPipe applied: `@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({ transform: true }))`, with no whitelist.
  - Service `createInvoice`, all inside one transaction:
    - The user must have role CLIENT and not be deleted, else `Error('Unauthorized')`.
    - Every `workOrderIds` entry must be a non-deleted work order in the caller's company, else `HttpException('Invalid request', 400)`.
    - A work order already on a live invoice of the same `role` throws `HttpException('Work order <referenceId> is already added in Invoice #<invoiceId>. Please remove it first.', 409)`.
    - Any error is re-thrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`. A plain `Error` has no status, so it surfaces as a 500.
    - Side effects: status history `GENERATED` is recorded, and the work orders' `customerStatus` becomes `INVOICED` (for CLIENT) or `status` becomes `INVOICED` (for FREELANCER).
- [ ] POST /api/invoices/engineer
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Purpose: generate an engineer (FREELANCER) invoice.
  - Same DTO, ValidationPipe and service rules as `POST /api/invoices`.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isRead`. For CLIENT and CUSTOMER users.
  - Purpose: paginated invoice list for the company. CUSTOMER users only see their scoped clients, and never `GENERATED` invoices.
  - Query: `InvoiceFilterDto`. No ValidationPipe.
  - Service: user missing or without `companyId` → `Error('Unauthorized')`. Any failure is caught and re-thrown as `Error('Unable to fetch invoices')`, which surfaces as a 500.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/engineer
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isRead`.
  - Purpose: engineer-invoice list. Same service method and errors as `GET /api/invoices`, and no ValidationPipe.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/statusCount
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard, needing `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isRead` or `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isRead`.
  - Purpose: invoice counts per status for the filter set.
  - Query: `InvoiceFilterDto`. No ValidationPipe. The response is not wrapped.
  - Errors: `Error('Unauthorized')`. Any failure becomes `Error('Unable to fetch invoice status counts')`, which surfaces as a 500.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isRead`.
  - Purpose: the invoice's work orders with their payment breakdown.
  - Query: `role` (`UserType`), which picks client or engineer payment fields. ValidationPipe is applied with `transform: true`, but the only inputs are a primitive string param and query, so nothing is validated.
  - Errors:
    - The user must have role CLIENT or CUSTOMER, else `Error('Unauthorized')`.
    - Invoice not found → `HttpException('No Data Found', 404)`.
    - A CUSTOMER whose scope or status check fails → `HttpException('No Data Found', 404)`.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/engineer/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isRead`.
  - Same as `GET /api/invoices/:id`.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/details/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isRead`.
  - Purpose: full invoice detail, with the bank account decrypted by `bankDetailsService.decryptBankRecord`. No ValidationPipe.
  - Errors: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`; `HttpException('No Invoice found', 404)`, which also covers a CUSTOMER outside their scope or statuses.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/engineer/details/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isRead`.
  - Same as `GET /api/invoices/details/:id`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/invoices/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isDelete`.
  - Purpose: soft-delete the invoice (`isDelete = true`) and its invoice-work-order links. Linked work orders go back to `APPROVED` (`customerStatus` for a CLIENT invoice, `status` for a FREELANCER invoice).
  - Errors: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`; `HttpException('No Invoice found', 404)`. Any other error is re-thrown with `error.status || 500`.
  - Note: there is no status check, so a PAID invoice can also be deleted.
- [ ] DELETE /api/invoices/engineer/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isDelete`.
  - Same as `DELETE /api/invoices/:id`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/invoices/verify/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Purpose: status transition (normally `status=VERIFIED`, which releases the invoice to the customer and sends them a notification).
  - Multipart via `FilesInterceptor('file')`: files go in field `file`. No `maxCount`, no multer `fileSize` limit and no mimetype filter.
  - Body: `UpdateInvoiceDto`, but **no ValidationPipe**, so none of the DTO rules or `@Transform`s run.
  - Service `updateInvoiceStatus` errors:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Invoice not found', 404)`.
    - A CUSTOMER acting on an invoice outside their clients → `HttpException('You can only act on invoices for your own clients', 403)`.
    - A CUSTOMER acting on an invoice not yet released → `HttpException('Invoice not found', 404)`.
  - The service branches on `status`:
    - `VERIFIED`: records the status history and notifies the customer.
    - `UNPAID`: sends the invoice (see `send`).
    - `PAID`: marks the invoice paid (see `paid`).
    - Any other value does nothing, with no error.
  - All errors are re-thrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/invoices/engineer/verify/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ACTION_VERIFY_ENGINEER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Same as `PATCH /api/invoices/verify/:id`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/invoices/send/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Purpose: send the invoice PDF by email (`status=UNPAID`).
  - Multipart `file`, same as `verify`. No ValidationPipe.
  - Body fields used: `toEmail`, `ccEmails`, `skipEmail`. `skipEmail` is accepted as `true` or `'true'`. Because the ValidationPipe is missing, the `ccEmails` Transform does not run and the raw value is passed through.
  - Errors: the same as `verify`, plus `HttpException('Invalid Email', 400)` when `skipEmail` is not set and `toEmail` is empty.
  - Mail: one email per unique recipient, with `ccEmails` on the first copy only. Sender is `accounts@techdomain.com.au` ("Tech Domain Accounts").
  - A PDF failure throws `HttpException('Cannot generate pdf at the moment', 502)`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/invoices/engineer/send/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ACTION_SEND_ENGINEER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Same as `PATCH /api/invoices/send/:id`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/invoices/paid/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ACTION_PAID_CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Purpose: mark the invoice PAID and upload the payment proof (`status=PAID`).
  - Multipart `file`. `file[0]` is uploaded as the invoice proof.
  - No ValidationPipe, so the DTO's `'Attachment is required'` message is never produced.
  - The service throws `HttpException('Invoice Proof is required', 400)` when no file is sent. Other errors are the same as `verify`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/invoices/engineer/paid/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ACTION_PAID_ENGINEER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Same as `PATCH /api/invoices/paid/:id`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/invoices/comment/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard, needing `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isWrite` or `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isWrite`.
  - Purpose: set the invoice `comment`.
  - Body: untyped `{ comment }`. No DTO, no ValidationPipe.
  - Errors: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`; `HttpException('No Invoice found', 404)`.
  - Returns `data: undefined`.
- [ ] POST /api/invoices/workOrder
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isUpdate`.
  - Purpose: remove one work order from an invoice and recalculate the totals from the latest snapshot.
  - Body: untyped `{ invoiceId, workOrderId }`. No ValidationPipe.
  - Errors:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('No Invoice found', 404)`.
    - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` when the work order is not on the invoice.
    - `HttpException('Work order History not found', 404)` when there is no `workOrderSnapshot`.
  - Everything is re-thrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.
- [ ] POST /api/invoices/engineer/workOrder
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isUpdate`.
  - Same as `POST /api/invoices/workOrder`.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/:id/availableWorkOrders
  - Guards: JWT only, with **no PermissionsGuard**.
  - Purpose: the "Add Work Order" picker. It lists company work orders that can be added: same client (for a CLIENT invoice) or same engineer (for a FREELANCER invoice), in an invoiceable status, and not already on a live invoice of that role.
    - Invoiceable for a CLIENT invoice: `customerStatus` in `APPROVED` or `CANCELLED_PAYABLE`.
    - Invoiceable for a FREELANCER invoice: `status = APPROVED`.
  - Errors: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`; `HttpException('No Invoice found', 404)`.
- [ ] POST /api/invoices/workOrder/add
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `CUSTOMER_INVOICE:isUpdate`.
  - Purpose: add approved work orders to an existing invoice and recalculate the totals.
  - Body: `AddInvoiceWorkOrdersDto`. ValidationPipe applied with `transform: true`.
  - Duplicate IDs in `workOrderIds` are removed before any checks.
  - Service errors, inside a transaction:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('No Invoice found', 404)`.
    - `HttpException('This invoice has been paid and can no longer be changed.', 409)`.
    - `HttpException('Invalid request', 400)` when any ID is not a company work order.
    - `HttpException('Work order <ref> is not ready to be invoiced and cannot be added to this invoice.', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Work order <ref> is priced in <cur> and cannot be added to a <invoiceCur> invoice.', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Work order <ref> belongs to a different client and cannot be added to this invoice.', 400)`, for a CLIENT invoice.
    - `HttpException('Work order <ref> belongs to a different engineer and cannot be added to this invoice.', 400)`, for a FREELANCER invoice.
    - `HttpException('Work order <ref> is already on this invoice.', 409)`, or `HttpException('Work order <ref> is already added in Invoice #<id>. Please remove it first.', 409)` when it is on another invoice.
  - A previously removed link row is revived instead of creating a duplicate.
- [ ] POST /api/invoices/engineer/workOrder/add
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ENGINEER_INVOICE:isUpdate`.
  - Same as `POST /api/invoices/workOrder/add`, including the ValidationPipe.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/download/:id
  - Guards: JWT only.
  - Purpose: stream the invoice PDF (`StreamableFile`).
  - Errors:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Invoice not found', 404)`.
    - Errors from `getAllInvoiceWorkOrdersByInvoiceId`, e.g. `Error('Unauthorized')` for users who are neither CLIENT nor CUSTOMER.
    - `HttpException('Cannot generate pdf at the moment', 502)` when PDF rendering fails.
- [ ] GET /api/invoices/download/proof/:id
  - Guards: JWT only.
  - Purpose: fetch the uploaded payment-proof URL and stream it back over https, as `attachment; filename="invoice-proof.jpg"`.
  - Errors: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)`.
  - Note: there is no null check on the invoice lookup, so an unknown ID causes a TypeError (500).

**Scheduler (not an endpoint).** `invoice.scheduler.ts` runs `@Cron(EVERY_DAY_AT_MIDNIGHT)` to mark overdue invoices. The business rules are covered elsewhere.


## Language

### `/language`
**No ValidationPipe**. Roles: FREELANCER; ADMIN via `targetUserId` / `userId`.
- [ ] POST /api/language — guards: JWT. Query `targetUserId` (optional; no ownership check). Purpose: bulk-add languages (`createMany`, `skipDuplicates`). Message `SUCCESS`.
  - Body `CreateLanguageDto` (not enforced):
    - `languages`: array, required. Rules: `@IsArray`, `@ValidateNested({each:true})`, `@ArrayMinSize(1)`.
    - `languages[].language`: `@IsEnum(LanguageType)`. About 107 values, e.g. ENGLISH, URDU, FRENCH, ARABIC, HINDI, SPANISH_CASTILIAN, and so on.
    - `languages[].proficiency`: `@IsEnum(LanguageLevel)`, one of `A1|A2|B1|B2|C1|C2`.
  - Throws `ConflictException('User already has this language')` on P2002 (in practice `skipDuplicates` avoids it). Other errors are **swallowed** and return `data: undefined`.
- [ ] GET /api/language — guards: JWT. Query `userId` (optional; defaults to the caller). Purpose: list a user's languages (id, language, proficiency). Message `SUCCESS`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/language/:id — guards: JWT. Query `targetUserId` (optional). Purpose: hard-delete `{id,userId}`. Returns the raw deleted row. Missing → P2025 → 500.
- GET `/:id` and PATCH `/:id` are commented out (not registered).


## locations

### /api/locations
Controller: `LocationsController`. There is **no controller-level guard**; each route is listed with its own guard below. DTOs `CreateLocationDto` and `UpdateLocationDto` are empty classes.

- [ ] POST /api/locations. Guard: **none**. **Stub.** It returns the string 'This action adds a new location'. Body `CreateLocationDto` is empty. ValidationPipe: NO.
- [ ] GET /api/locations. Guard: JWT. Roles: any authenticated user. Purpose: list the caller's saved addresses (`saveForFuture=true`, not deleted). Fields returned: id, displayName, addressLine1, timeZone, latitude, longitude.
- [ ] DELETE /api/locations/:id. Guard: JWT. Roles: owner. Purpose: soft-delete a saved address. `:id` is a string (uuid).
  - Throws: plain `Error('Location not found or access denied')`. This is not an HttpException, so the client receives a **500** Internal Server Error.
- [ ] GET /api/locations/search?q=. Guard: **none** (public). Purpose: Google Places autocomplete, returning `[{description, place_id}]`.
  - An empty or null `q` returns `[]`. The Google request timeout is 1000 ms.
  - Throws: `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to autocomplete location')`.
- [ ] GET /api/locations/details?place_id=. Guard: **none** (public). Purpose: Google Place Details plus the Time Zone API.
  - Returns: `{name, completeAddress, latitude, longitude, timeZone, timeZoneName, zipCode, country, city, state, area, place_id}`.
  - Throws: `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to fetch place details.')`. All internal errors are wrapped in this, including the inner messages 'No details found for the provided place ID.' and `Error fetching time zone data: <statusText>`.
- [ ] GET /api/locations/nationalities. Guard: **none** (public). Purpose: a static list of 225 `{label, value}` nationality options (for example `{label:'Afghan', value:'AFGHAN'}` through `ZIMBABWEAN`). Note that one value contains a hyphen: `CITIZEN_OF_GUINEA-BISSAU`.


## Mail

### `/mail`
- [ ] **stub** — `MailController` declares no routes. `MailService` (template emails via `@nestjs-modules/mailer`/SES) is used internally only and is covered in the notifications/emails section.


## Notifications

### /api/notifications
Controller: `src/modules/notifications/notifications.controller.ts`. Every route has `JwtAuthGuard`, is open to any role, and has no ValidationPipe. The DTOs `CreateNotificationDto` and `UpdateNotificationDto` are empty stubs and no route uses them. The service has no `throw new` statements; Prisma errors surface as 500. (The Socket.IO gateway `notifications.gateway.ts` and the scheduler are not REST and are out of scope.)

- [ ] GET /api/notifications
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: all notifications for the current user (`getUserNotifications`).
- [ ] GET /api/notifications/messages
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: chat-message notifications, grouped by chat room.
  - Query: `cursor` (optional, becomes a Number and is the chat-room cursor) and `limit` (default '10', becomes a Number). There is no maximum or validation, and a non-numeric limit gives NaN.
  - Pagination slices the list of chat-room ids in memory.
- [ ] GET /api/notifications/others
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: non-message notifications, using cursor pagination.
  - Query: `cursor` (optional, Number) and `limit` (default '10'). The service fetches `limit + 1` rows to compute `hasNextPage`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/notifications/read/:id
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: mark one notification read. The update uses `where: { id, receiverId: userId }`.
  - Param `id` is cast with `+`. If the id is not the caller's, Prisma raises P2025 (record not found), which surfaces as 500 with no friendly message.
  - The raw result (receiver deviceToken) is returned without the `{data,message}` wrapper.
- [ ] GET /api/notifications/chat
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: chat notifications for the user. The raw result is returned without the wrapper.
- [ ] PATCH /api/notifications/readAll
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: mark all of the user's non-message notifications read. The raw result is returned.
- [ ] PATCH /api/notifications/messages/readAll
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: mark all of the user's message notifications read.
- [ ] PATCH /api/notifications/messages/readAll/:chatroomId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any.
  - Purpose: mark all message notifications in one chat room read (`markAllMessagesAsReadByWorkOrder`).
  - Param `chatroomId` is cast with `+`.


## Offers

### /api/offers

Source: `src/modules/offers/offers.controller.ts`, `offers.service.ts`, `dto/*.ts`. **No route uses PermissionsGuard**; all require `JwtAuthGuard`. Validation, where present, is `@Body(ValidationPipe)`: the default pipe, with no `transform` and no `whitelist`, so string numbers in JSON are rejected by `@IsNumber()`.

**DTOs**

- `CreateOfferDto`:
  - `workOrderId`: number, required, `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber()`.
  - `isCounter`: optional, `@IsBoolean()`.
  - `referredEngineerId`: optional, `@IsNumber()`.
  - `offerId`: optional, `@IsNumber()`.
- `RouteOfferDto`: `workOrderId` and `userId`, both numbers, required, `@IsNumber() @IsNotEmpty()`.
- `AcceptRoutedOfferDto`: `referredEngineerId`, optional, `@IsNumber()`.
- `AssignOfferDto`: `workOrderId` and `userId`, both required, `@IsNumber() @IsNotEmpty()`.
- `UpdateOfferStatusDto`:
  - `workOrderId`: required, `@IsNumber() @IsNotEmpty()`.
  - `userId`: required, `@IsNumber() @IsNotEmpty()`.
  - `status`: `OfferStatus` (`PENDING | ASSIGNED | REJECTED | COUNTER | DECLINED | ROUTED | ACCEPTED | PAID | CANCELLED`), required, `@IsNotEmpty()` only. There is **no `@IsEnum`**.
  - `offerId`: optional, `@IsNumber()`.
- `CreateCounterOfferDto`:
  - `workOrderId`: required, `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber()`.
  - Optional `@IsNumber()` fields: `hourlyRate`, `halfDayRate`, `fixedAmount`, `fullDayRate`, `minimumHours`, `additionalHourlyRate`, `referredEngineerId`.
  - `expenses`: optional, `@IsArray() @ValidateNested({ each: true }) @Type(() => ExpensesDto)`.
  - `currency`: optional, `@IsString()`.
  - `ExpensesDto` (from work-orders) has these optional fields:
    - `id`: `@IsNumber()`.
    - `expenseType`: `@IsString()`, holding an `ExpenseType` value (`TRAVEL | TOOLS | MATERIAL | ALL | OTHER`) but not enum-checked.
    - `description`: `@IsString()`.
    - `amount`: `@IsNumber()`.
    - `expenseKey`: `@IsString()`.
- `UpdateOfferDto` = `PartialType(CreateOfferDto)`. It is not used by any route; the `update()` service method is a stub.

**Shared service messages**

- `SCHEDULE_CONFLICT_TECH_MESSAGE` = `'Schedule Conflict: This work order overlaps with an existing assignment.'`
- `SCHEDULE_CONFLICT_CLIENT_MESSAGE` = `'Schedule Conflict: The selected engineer is already assigned to another work order during this time.'`
- The schedule check runs before the offer checks. It throws `HttpException('The requested work order does not exist.', 404)` for an unknown work order.

**Endpoints**

- [ ] POST /api/offers/engineer/notInterested
  - Guards: JWT. For FREELANCER users (the engineer or company owner).
  - Purpose: mark the caller's offer on a work order `DECLINED` ("not interested"). It is matched by `offerId`, or by `workOrderId` + (`userId` or `referredEngineerId` = caller). A work-order snapshot is taken.
  - Body: `CreateOfferDto` with ValidationPipe.
  - Errors: `BadRequestException('Offer does not exist.')`. Any non-400 error becomes `InternalServerErrorException('An error occurred while while updating.')`.
- [ ] POST /api/offers/route
  - Guards: JWT. For CLIENT users.
  - Purpose: route a published work order to a specific engineer. This calls `createOffer` with `status=ROUTED` and `userId=body.userId`, sends a routed notification and creates a routed group chat room.
  - Body: `RouteOfferDto` with ValidationPipe.
  - Errors (from `createOffer`):
    - `HttpException('Engineer is not approved', 400)` when the target is not APPROVED or is deleted.
    - A schedule conflict throws a 409 with the CLIENT message (or the TECH message when routing to yourself).
    - An existing offer that is not DECLINED throws a 409: `'Already Routed to you, please refresh the page'` when it is ROUTED, else `'You have already submitted an offer for this job.'`. A DECLINED offer is re-routed instead.
    - A work order that is not PUBLISHED or already has an engineer → `HttpException('Only published work order can be routed', 403)`.
    - `HttpException('Engineer billing rates are missing.', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Hourly, Half Day and Full Day rates must not be empty.', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('All rates must be greater than 0.', 400)`.
    - Any other error becomes `InternalServerErrorException('An error occurred while creating the offer.')`.
  - The billing-rate checks are skipped for freelance-company owners and members.
- [ ] POST /api/offers/route/accept/:id
  - Guards: JWT. For FREELANCER users.
  - Purpose: the engineer accepts a routed offer (`:id` = offerId). It logs `OFFER_ACCEPTED` and notifies the work order's teams.
  - Body: `AcceptRoutedOfferDto` with ValidationPipe.
  - Errors: an unknown offer throws `Error('Offer with id <id> not found')` internally. **All** errors are replaced by `HttpException('Failed to accept offer', 500)`.
- [ ] POST /api/offers/engineer/request
  - Guards: **JWT + ProfileCompletionGuard**. For FREELANCER users.
  - ProfileCompletionGuard throws `ForbiddenException('Please complete your profile to access this feature. Missing: <fields>')`, or `ForbiddenException('Unauthorized')` when there is no user ID.
  - Purpose: the engineer requests a public work order at the posted rates (`createOffer`, status `PENDING`). It notifies team members with a `REQUESTED` notification.
  - Body: `CreateOfferDto` with ValidationPipe. The response is not wrapped.
  - Errors: everything listed under `/route`, except that the 403 text is `'This work order is no longer open for offers.'` Also:
    - `BadRequestException('You are linked to a freelance company. Work orders are assigned to you by your company.')`, for a self-submit by an accepted company member without `referredEngineerId`.
    - `BadRequestException('No custom rates are set for this work order. You can only send a counter offer.')`, for a freelance-company owner when the work order has no `engineerPaymentType` and `isCounter` is false.
    - A schedule conflict throws a 409 with the TECH message for a self-submit, else the CLIENT message.
- [ ] PATCH /api/offers/engineer/counter
  - Guards: JWT. For FREELANCER users. There is **no** ProfileCompletionGuard.
  - Purpose: create or update a counter offer, with its rates and expenses (status `PENDING`, `isCounter`). An existing `PENDING` or `ROUTED` offer is updated.
  - Body: `CreateCounterOfferDto` with ValidationPipe. Nested `ExpensesDto` entries are validated.
  - Errors (`createOrUpdateCounterOffer`):
    - A schedule conflict throws a 409, re-thrown as `ConflictException(<same message>)`.
    - `HttpException('This work order is no longer open for offers.', 409)`, re-thrown as `ConflictException`, when the work order already has an engineer.
    - Every non-409 error, including `BadRequestException('You are linked to a freelance company. Work orders are assigned to you by your company.')` and `HttpException('Engineer billing rates are missing.', 400)`, is swallowed into `InternalServerErrorException('An error occurred while creating the offer.')`.
    - Bug: the "Engineer is not approved" check tests a Promise that is never awaited, so it never fires.
- [ ] POST /api/offers/assign
  - Guards: JWT. For CLIENT users.
  - Purpose: assign the work order to an engineer's offer. Other offers are updated, a group chat room is created and the customer is notified.
  - Body: `AssignOfferDto` with ValidationPipe.
  - Errors (`assignWorkOrderToFreelancer`):
    - A schedule conflict → `HttpException(SCHEDULE_CONFLICT_CLIENT_MESSAGE, 409)`.
    - `HttpException('User Not found', 404)`.
    - `HttpException('Work order already assigned or completed', 400)` when an ASSIGNED or PAID offer already exists.
    - `HttpException('Offer does not exist or already assigned', 400)` when there is no PENDING or ACCEPTED offer for the user.
    - `HttpException('Work order not found.', 404)`.
    - `HttpException('Missing qualifications: <names>', 400)` when the engineer lacks a required Equipment or Certification qualification.
    - `HttpException('The following qualifications are not verified: <names>', 406)`.
    - `HttpException('Work Order does not exist or already assigned', 400)`.
    - Mapping: 400 errors are re-thrown as `BadRequestException(message)`; every other status (409, 404, 406) becomes `InternalServerErrorException(message)`, i.e. a 500 that keeps the message.
- [ ] POST /api/offers/counter/accept/:id
  - Guards: JWT. For CLIENT users.
  - Purpose: accept a counter offer (`:id` = offerId). It approves the engineer's unapproved expenses, then runs the `assign` flow for `referredEngineerId ?? userId`.
  - No body.
  - Errors: `HttpException('Offer does not exist.', 400)`, plus all `assign` errors, re-thrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`. Returns `data: offerId`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/offers/engineer/withdraw/:id
  - Guards: JWT. For FREELANCER users.
  - Purpose: withdraw the caller's offer. **`:id` is the workOrderId**, not an offerId. Only offers in `PENDING`, `COUNTER` or `REJECTED` qualify; the offer and its histories are deleted.
  - Response message: `'Withdrawn Successfully.'`
  - Errors: internally `"Offer doesn't exists or either Assigned to you"` (404) or `"Offer History doesn't exists"` (404). **All** errors are replaced by `HttpException('Something went wrong with withdraw request, Try later', 404)`.
- [ ] POST /api/offers/status
  - Guards: JWT. For CLIENT users.
  - Purpose: set an offer's status, e.g. REJECTED (hide). `status=PENDING` un-assigns the work order: engineer is cleared, the work order goes back to PUBLISHED and the offer to PENDING.
  - Body: `UpdateOfferStatusDto` with ValidationPipe. `status` is not enum-validated.
  - Errors: `HttpException('Offer does not exist.', 400)`, re-thrown as `BadRequestException`. Any other error becomes `InternalServerErrorException('An error occurred while while updating.')`.
- [ ] POST /api/offers/:id
  - Guards: JWT. For CLIENT users.
  - Purpose: paginated list of offers for work order `:id`, scoped to the caller's company, with talent-pool tags.
  - Body (untyped, no ValidationPipe):
    - `limit` (default 10) and `page` (default 1).
    - `sortBy`: `'distance'`, `'id'` or any offer column (default `updatedAt`).
    - `sortOrder` (default `desc`, or `asc` when sorting by `id`).
    - `filters`: an `OfferStatus[]` for the status `in` filter.
    - `filterByRequiredSkills` and `filterByRequiredTools`, both booleans.
  - The PENDING tab without `sortBy` orders by type (direct, then counter, then routed) and then by lowest billing rate.
  - Note: there are no service throws; an unknown work order gives a TypeError (500).
- [ ] POST /api/offers/engineer/workOrder/:id
  - Guards: JWT. For CLIENT and FREELANCER users.
  - Purpose: fetch the counter offer and current offer for work order `:id` and engineer `body.userId`, including merged expenses, `totalWorkOrderCost` and `isEngineerQualificationsVerified`.
  - Body (untyped): `userId` and `companyOwnerId` (optional), which scopes expenses for company offers.
  - Errors: `Error('Work order not found.')` and `Error('Offer not found for this work order.')`, re-thrown as `HttpException(message, undefined)` (a 500).
- [ ] DELETE /api/offers/:id
  - Guards: JWT. For CLIENT users.
  - Purpose: un-route. Deletes a `ROUTED` offer (`:id` = offerId) and its routed history, logs `OFFER_REJECTED`, removes the work order from the engineer's list, and deletes the routed chat room if it has no messages.
  - Errors: `HttpException('WO Accepted', 409)` when the offer is not ROUTED. **All** errors are re-thrown as `HttpException(error.message, 409)`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/offers/engineer/expense/:id
  - Guards: JWT. For FREELANCER users.
  - Purpose: hard-delete an expense row the caller owns (`where { id, userId: caller }`).
  - Errors: there is no try/catch, so a missing or not-owned expense surfaces as a Prisma `P2025` error (500).

## OTPs

### `/otps`
All routes are public (no guards).
- [ ] POST /api/otps — guards: none. No ValidationPipe. Purpose: create an OTP row (6 digits, `SIGNUP` use case, expires in 5 min).
  - Body is bound as `userId: number`, but it receives the whole body object, so Prisma would get an object for `userId` and fail. Effectively broken/internal.
- [ ] GET /api/otps — **stub**. Returns `This action returns all otps`.
- [ ] GET /api/otps/resend/:email — guards: none. Purpose: generate a new 6-digit SIGNUP OTP (5-minute expiry) and email it with template `verify-otp` and subject ` Tech Domain - Verify OTP`. The email is trimmed and lowercased; the lookup is case-insensitive among non-deleted users. Message: `Verification otp sent`.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('User not found')`.
- [ ] POST /api/otps/verify — guards: none. ValidationPipe: **yes**. Purpose: verify a SIGNUP OTP.
  - Effects: sets `isVerified=true`; auto-approves (`status=APPROVED`) CLIENT/CUSTOMER users that were created by someone else (`createdById` set); creates a Session (7-day `expiresAt`); issues JWT access and refresh tokens (the refresh token is bcrypt-hashed on the session). For a self-signed-up CUSTOMER owner, it sends a customer-registration notice email after the transaction.
  - Response: `{data:{user (includes isCustomerOwner, role.permissions), accessToken, refreshToken, message:'OTP successfully verified'}}`.
  - Body `VerifyOtpDto`: `email` (required, `@IsNotEmpty`, `@IsEmail`), `code` (required, `@IsNotEmpty`).
  - Throws (inside the transaction, rewrapped as `HttpException(error.response || 'Something went wrong', status || 500)`):
    - `HttpException('Invalid OTP', 404)` (no OTP with that code and use case; the lookup is by code only, not scoped to the user)
    - `HttpException('Expired OTP', 400)`
    - `HttpException('User not found', 404)` (the OTP's user does not match the email)
  - The OTP is **not** consumed/deleted after verification.
- [ ] PATCH /api/otps/:id — **stub**. Returns `This action updates a #<id> otp`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/otps/:id — **stub**. Returns `This action removes a #<id> otp`.


## Payment

### `/payment`
- [ ] **stub** — `PaymentController` declares no routes. `PaymentService` is internal only:
  - `createPayment` (an upsert by `workOrderId`; errors are logged and swallowed)
  - `updateEngineerPayment` / `updateCustomerPayment` (recalculate via `WorkOrdersService.updatedCalculateWorkOrderPayment`, then update the linked invoice; errors → `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`)
  - `updatePaymentStatus` (always sets `PENDING`, which ignores the `status` arg)
  - `CreatePaymentDto` exists (numeric/enum fields for client, engineer and company payments) but is not bound to any route.


## Permissions

### `/permissions`
- [ ] GET /api/permissions/user/:userId — guards: JWT (no PermissionsGuard). Roles: ADMIN (intended). Purpose: return every `ModuleType` permission row for the target user, auto-creating missing modules with all flags false, ordered by module. The response key is misspelt `mesaage: 'SUCCESS'`.
  - The admin check `HttpException('unathorized resource', 403)` **never fires**: `findUnique` is not awaited, so the Promise is always truthy. Any authenticated user can read (and seed) any user's permissions.
- Other: `createClientPermissions(prisma, userId, designationId)` is used by designations. It seeds `ClientPermissionModules` (COMPANIES, WORK_ORDERS, CUSTOMER_WORK_ORDERS, ACTION_* WO actions, USERS, CLIENT_CUSTOMER, CLIENT_PROJECT, JOBS, CLIENT_BANK_ACCOUNTS, COMPANY_BRANCHES, INVOICE, CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_* invoice actions, …) with all flags false. Errors are swallowed.


## projects

### /api/projects
Controller: `ProjectsController`. Each route uses `JwtAuthGuard, PermissionsGuard` with module `CLIENT_PROJECT`. The service helper `isCompanyUser` throws `NotFoundException('Unauthorized.')` when the caller is missing, deleted, or has no `companyId`. This applies to every route.

- [ ] POST /api/projects. Guards: JWT + Permissions `CLIENT_PROJECT:isWrite`. Roles: CLIENT company users. Purpose: create a project under a client, with an optional contract file.
  - Content type: multipart/form-data. `FileFieldsInterceptor([{ name: 'projectContractFile', maxCount: 1 }])`. There is **no size limit and no mimetype filter** at the controller. The uploader (`uploadClientDigitalAsset`) throws `BadRequestException('File is required')` only if called with no file. Note that create uses `uploadClientDigitalAsset`, not `uploadProjectDigitalAsset`.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES**, applied twice: method-level `@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({ transform: true }))` and `@Body(ValidationPipe)`.
  - Body `CreateProjectDto`:
    - `title`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsString()`, required
    - `managerName`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
    - `phoneNumber`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
    - `email`: `@IsOptional() @IsEmail()`
    - `policies`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
    - `instructions`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
    - `clientId`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsInt()`, with `@Transform` doing `parseInt` on strings. Required in practice, although typed `?`.
    - `isPoRequired`: `@IsOptional() @IsBoolean()`, with `@Transform` mapping 'true' to true, 'false' to false, and anything else to `!!value`.
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Client not found', 404)` when the client is not in the caller's company or is deleted.
    - Any error is rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.
- [ ] GET /api/projects. Guards: JWT + Permissions `CLIENT_PROJECT:isRead`. Purpose: company project list with `totalCount`.
  - ValidationPipe: NO.
  - Query parameters:
    - `limit`: no pagination if omitted
    - `page`: default 1
    - `searchTerm`: **Bug:** the controller type declares `searchTerm`, but the service reads `search`. Only `?search=` actually filters (case-insensitive title match).
    - `clientId`
- [ ] GET /api/projects/:id. Guards: JWT + Permissions `CLIENT_PROJECT:isRead`. Purpose: project detail plus the contract as `{fileName, url}`, with a signed URL.
  - Throws: `NotFoundException('Project not found')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/projects/:id. Guards: JWT + Permissions `CLIENT_PROJECT:isUpdate`. Purpose: update a project and optionally replace the contract.
  - File: `projectContractFile`, maxCount 1, no limits. Upload uses `uploadProjectDigitalAsset`.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES** (method-level `@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({ transform: true }))`).
  - Body `UpdateProjectDto` = `PartialType(CreateProjectDto)`: all fields optional, same validators.
  - Throws: `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to update this project')` when the project is not found in the caller's company.
  - **Note:** the entire method is wrapped in `try/catch { console.log(error) }`, so every error, including the 403, is swallowed and the response is `{ data: undefined, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.
  - **Bug:** `clientId: Number(clientId)` is always written, so omitting `clientId` sends NaN and the update fails silently.
- [ ] DELETE /api/projects/:id. Guards: JWT + Permissions `CLIENT_PROJECT:isDelete`. Purpose: soft-delete a project, delete the contract from S3, and clear `digitalAssetId`. Returns `{ message: 'project deleted successfully' }` without the data wrapper.
  - Throws: `NotFoundException('Project not found')`.


## Qualifications

### `/qualifications`
- [ ] POST /api/qualifications — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT:isWrite`. Roles: ADMIN/MODERATOR. **No ValidationPipe**. Purpose: create a qualification type.
  - Body `CreateQualificationDto`: `name` (string, required, `@IsString` `@IsNotEmpty`; not enforced).
  - Throws `ConflictException('Qualification type already exists')` (case-insensitive name match).
- [ ] POST /api/qualifications/bulk — same guard/permission. Purpose: bulk-create qualification types (`skipDuplicates`). ValidationPipe is declared but skipped because the body type is an array.
  - Actual body: `{ types: string[] }`.
- [ ] POST /api/qualifications/subQualifications/bulk — same guard/permission. Purpose: bulk-create sub-qualification types. Validation is effectively skipped.
  - Actual body: `{ subQualifications: string[], qualificationTypeId: number }`. `CreateSubQualificationDto.name` has its validators commented out.
- [ ] GET /api/qualifications — guards: JWT. Purpose: paginated qualification types. Query `ListQueryDto` (not validated): `search`, `page` (default 1), `limit` (default **20**). Returns `{data:{data,total}, message:'Qualifications fetched successfully'}`.
- [ ] GET /api/qualifications/subQualifications — guards: JWT. Purpose: sub-qualifications with their parent type. Query `ListQueryDto`: no limit → all rows. Message `Sub Qualifications fetched successfully`.
- [ ] GET /api/qualifications/subQualificationsByQualificationId/:id — guards: JWT. Purpose: sub-types of a qualification type, sorted by name. Message `SUCCESS`.


## S3

### (no controller — service-only)
- [ ] No REST endpoints. `S3Service` is used by upload endpoints in other modules. It provides:
  - `uploadFile(buffer, mime, originalname, folder)`:
    - Runs `processFileForUpload` first: raster images (image/* except svg/gif, or files with a jpg/png/tiff/bmp/webp/avif/heic/heif extension) are **converted to WebP**. The target is ≤ ~1.9 MB (95% of a 2 MB threshold): quality steps down from 82 to a floor of 40, then width shrinks by 15% per step to a minimum of 320 px. If conversion fails, the original file is uploaded.
    - The object key is `<folder>/<YYYY-MM-DD>-<uuid>-<originalname>`.
    - Returns a presigned GET URL with expiry `AWS_S3_OBJECT_EXPIRY_SECONDS`.
  - `deleteFile(key)`
  - `generateSignedUrl(key, expiresIn)`
  - No size limit is enforced here; limits are per-controller (see the other modules).

### `/selectionRules`
- [ ] POST /api/selectionRules — guards: JWT. **Stub**: returns `This action adds a new selectionRule`. Body `CreateSelectionRuleDto` is empty. No ValidationPipe.
- [ ] POST /api/selectionRules/criteriaType — guards: JWT. ValidationPipe: **yes**. Roles: ADMIN (not enforced). Purpose: create a CriteriaType with nested criteriaOptions.

        - `options[].label`: string, required (`@IsString` `@IsNotEmpty`).

- [ ] GET /api/selectionRules — **stub**, guards none. Returns `This action returns all selectionRules`.
- [ ] GET /api/selectionRules/:id — **stub**, guards none.
- [ ] PATCH /api/selectionRules/:id — **stub**, guards none.
- [ ] DELETE /api/selectionRules/:id — **stub**, guards none.


## talent-pool

### /api/talentPool
Controller: `TalentPoolController`. **No ValidationPipe on any route.** All DTO validators are declarative only.

- [ ] POST /api/talentPool. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL:isWrite`. Roles: CLIENT. Purpose: create a talent pool for the caller's company.
  - Body `CreateTalentPoolDto`: `name` with `@IsNotEmpty()`.
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400 BAD_REQUEST)` when the caller is not role CLIENT.
    - `HttpException('Talent pool with this name already exists for the company', 409 CONFLICT)` on a case-insensitive name match within the company.
- [ ] PATCH /api/talentPool/:id. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL:isUpdate`. Purpose: rename a pool.
  - Body `UpdateTalentPoolDto` = `PartialType(Create)`: `name` optional.
  - Throws: `NotFoundException('Talent pool not found')`.
  - **Not company-scoped:** any permitted user can rename any pool id.
- [ ] DELETE /api/talentPool/:id. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL:isDelete`. Roles: CLIENT. Purpose: hard-delete a pool in the caller's company. Returns `{ success: true, message: 'Talent pool deleted successfully' }` without the data wrapper.
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 401)` when the caller is not CLIENT.
    - `NotFoundException('Talent pool not found')`.
- [ ] GET /api/talentPool/user/:userId. Guard: JWT only. Roles: company users. Purpose: list the caller company's pools that contain `:userId`, as `[{id, name}]`.
  - Throws: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 401)` when the caller has no `companyId`.
- [ ] GET /api/talentPool. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL:isRead`. Roles: CLIENT. Purpose: paginated company pool list with the creator and a member count.
  - Query parameters:
    - `search`: pool name, or creator first or last name (every word must match)
    - `page`: default 1
    - `limit`: default 20
  - Throws: `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)` when the caller is not CLIENT.
- [ ] POST /api/talentPool/addUsersToTalentPool. Guards: JWT + Permissions **`WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS:isUpdate`** (a different module). Purpose: add engineers to a pool. Returns a per-user result: `{success, user}` or `{success:false, userId, reason:'Already in pool'|'Unknown error'}`. P2002 duplicates are handled gracefully.
  - Body `AddUsersToTalentPoolDto`:
    - `talentPoolId`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsInt()`
    - `userIds`: `@IsNotEmpty()`, number[]
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('User not found', 404)` when the caller is not found.
    - `HttpException('Talent Pool not found', 404)` when the pool is not in the caller's company.
- [ ] POST /api/talentPool/removeUserFromTalentPool. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL:isDelete`. Purpose: remove one engineer from a pool.
  - Body `RemoveUserFromTalentPoolDto`:
    - `talentPoolId`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsInt()`
    - `userId`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsInt()`
  - Throws: `HttpException('Talent Pool not found', 404)`.
  - The 'User is not part of this talent pool' (400) branch is unreachable: `delete` throws Prisma P2025, which surfaces as a 500 when the membership does not exist.
  - Not company-scoped.


## tasks

### /api/tasks
Controller: `TasksController`. Only PATCH is guarded. Work-order task business rules are covered by another section; this entry is the API surface only.

- [ ] POST /api/tasks. Guard: **none**. **Stub.** Returns 'This action adds a new task'. Body `CreateTaskDto` is empty.
- [ ] GET /api/tasks. Guard: **none**. **Stub.** Returns 'This action returns all tasks'.
- [ ] GET /api/tasks/:id. Guard: **none**. **Stub.** Returns 'This action returns a #<id> task'.
- [ ] PATCH /api/tasks/:id. Guard: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER (assigned engineer), or client staff for custom tasks. Purpose: complete or update a work-order `completedTask` by id. It dispatches on `taskType`:
  - START_TIME (when not completed): sets the work order subStatus to READY_TO_GO.
  - CHECK_IN (when not completed): no-op, returns undefined.
  - CLOSE_NOTES: saves `text` as close-out notes.
  - ON_THE_WAY.
  - RUNNING_LATE: `text` is the reason.
  - MARK_COMPLETE: calls `markWorkOrderComplete` and stores `text` as `task.reason`.
  - Any other type: generic complete with `isCompleted`, then emails the task's `emailsToNotify` when completed.
  - ValidationPipe: **NO**.
  - Body `UpdateTaskDto`:
    - `isCompleted`: `@IsBoolean()`
    - `text`: `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`
  - Throws: none active. Every HttpException in the service is commented out, including 'Cannot update task. More than 24 hours remain before the work order start time.', 'Cannot On the Way. The work order has not started yet.', 'Cannot On the Running Late. The work order has not started yet.', and 'Cannot Mark Complete'.
  - An unknown id causes a TypeError, returned as 500.
  - **No ownership check:** any authenticated user can update any task id.
- [ ] DELETE /api/tasks/:id. Guard: **none**. **Stub.** Returns 'This action removes a #<id> task'.


## Teams

### `/teams`
Roles: CLIENT company (owner/staff with the `COMPANIES` permission).
- [ ] POST /api/teams — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `COMPANIES:isWrite`. ValidationPipe: **yes** (`transform:true`, no whitelist). Purpose: create a team in the caller's company with members and a department. Message `Team created successfully`.
  - Body `CreateTeamDto`:
    - `name`: string, required. Rules: `@IsString`; `@IsNotEmpty({message:'Team name is required'})`; `@MaxLength(100,{message:'Team name must be at most 100 characters'})`; trimmed.
    - `email`: string, required. Trimmed. Rules: `@IsNotEmpty({message:'Team email is required'})`; `@IsEmail({},{message:'Enter a valid team email'})`.
    - `members`: number[], required. Rules: `@IsArray`; `@ArrayNotEmpty({message:'Select at least one member'})`; `@ArrayUnique({message:'Member IDs must be unique'})`; `@IsInt({each:true,message:'Member IDs must be integers'})`.
    - `departmentId`: number, required. Rules: `@IsNotEmpty({message:'Department is required'})`; `@IsInt({message:'Department ID must be an integer'})`.
  - Throws:
    - plain `Error('User does not belong to any company')` → 500
    - `BadRequestException('Selected department is invalid')` (the department is not in the company)
  - Member IDs are not checked against the company.
- [ ] PATCH /api/teams/:id — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `COMPANIES:isWrite`. ValidationPipe: **yes** (transform). Purpose: update name, email or department. `departmentId: null` detaches the team. `members`, if provided, **replaces** all members. Message `Team updated successfully`.
  - Body `UpdateTeamDto`: PartialType of Create, plus overrides:
    - `name`: optional; `@IsString`; `@IsNotEmpty({message:'Team name cannot be empty'})`.
    - `email`: optional; trimmed; `@IsNotEmpty({message:'Team email is required'})`; `@IsEmail({},{message:'Enter a valid team email'})`.
    - `members`: optional; `@IsArray`; `@ArrayUnique({message:'Member IDs must be unique'})`; `@IsInt({each:true,message:'Member IDs must be integers'})`. An empty array is allowed and clears the members.
    - `departmentId`: optional (inherits the Create rules; null is allowed).
  - Throws: `NotFoundException('Team not found')`, `BadRequestException('Selected department is invalid')`. There is no company-ownership check on the team id.
- [ ] GET /api/teams — guards: **JWT only**. `@RequiredPermissions(COMPANIES:isRead)` is declared, but PermissionsGuard is not applied, so it is not enforced. Query params: `page`, `limit`, `search`, `departmentId` (raw strings). Purpose: list non-deleted company teams with members, creator and department, ordered by createdAt desc. No company → `{data:[], totalCount:0, page:1, limit:null}`.
- [ ] GET /api/teams/teamMembers — guards: JWT. Query `teamIds` (comma-separated UUIDs, optional). Purpose: de-duplicated members (id, firstName, lastName, profilePicture) of the given teams, limited to the caller's company. Without `teamIds`, it returns **all** company users.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('User not found')`, wrapped as `HttpException(message||'Something went wrong', status||500)`.
- [ ] GET /api/teams/:id — guards: JWT only (the declared `COMPANIES:isRead` is not enforced). Purpose: team with members and the full `createdBy` user. No company scoping. Throws `NotFoundException('Team not found')`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/teams/:id — guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `COMPANIES:isWrite`. Purpose: delete team members, then **hard-delete** the team. No company scoping. A missing team → Prisma P2025 → 500. Message `Team deleted successfully`.


## TeamTasksManagement

### /api/teamTasks
Controller: `src/modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.controller.ts`. Every route has `JwtAuthGuard`. The intended roles are CLIENT company users (internal task board). No PermissionsGuard or ProfileCompletionGuard is used. The DTOs `UpdateTeamTasksManagementDto` and `ReassignTaskDto` (`toUserId` @IsInt, `reason` @IsString, `workOrderId` @IsInt) are not used, and the reassign service method is commented out.

**Enums:**
- `TeamTaskPriority`: LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, URGENT.
- `TeamTaskStatusType`: TODO, INRPOCESS (a typo that is still in the enum), INPROCESS, COMPLETED, ON_HOLD.

**Upload config (POST and PATCH):**
- Field name: `attachments`. There is **no `fileSize` limit**.
- `fileFilter` allowlist:
  - image/jpeg, image/jpg, image/png
  - application/pdf, application/msword
  - application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document
  - application/vnd.ms-excel
  - application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet
  - application/vnd.ms-excel.sheet.macroEnabled.12
  - application/vnd.ms-excel.sheet.binary.macroEnabled.12
  - application/vnd.ms-excel.template.macroEnabled.12
  - application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.template
  - text/csv, application/csv
  - application/octet-stream
- Any other mimetype is rejected with `BadRequestException('Unknown file type')`.

- [ ] POST /api/teamTasks
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT (team member or company owner).
  - Purpose: create a team task. It optionally assigns users (they receive a notification titled '📝 New Task Assigned'), writes a CREATED log, and uploads attachments.
  - Multipart. `FilesInterceptor('attachments', 10, { limits: { files: 5 } })`. The maxCount says 10, but the multer `limits.files` of 5 wins, so more than 5 files is rejected by multer with a "Too many files" error.
  - **ValidationPipe applied** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, default options, no transform).
  - Body `CreateTeamTasksManagementDto`:
    - `title`: string, **required**. `@IsString`.
    - `description`: string, optional. `@IsString`.
    - `priority`: optional. `@IsEnum(TeamTaskPriority)`.
    - `status`: optional. `@IsEnum(TeamTaskStatusType)`.
    - `startDate`: optional. `@IsOptional` only, with no date validation.
    - `dueDate`: optional. `@IsOptional` only, with no date validation.
    - `workOrderId`: int, optional. `@IsInt`, with `@Transform` to Number.
    - `assigneeIds`: number[], optional. `@IsArray @ArrayUnique @IsInt({ each: true }) @Type(() => Number)`.
    - `teamId`: string, **required**. `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`.
  - The service catches every error and rethrows it as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`. Prisma errors (for example an invalid teamId or workOrderId connect) become an HttpException with an undefined status.
  - There is no check that the caller or the assignees belong to the team.
- [ ] GET /api/teamTasks
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT.
  - Purpose: paginated task board, ordered by updatedAt desc.
  - Which teams are shown:
    - If no `teamIds` are given: a company owner sees all company teams, and anyone else sees only their own member teams.
    - If `teamIds` are given by a non-owner, they are checked, and a team the user is not in throws `ForbiddenException('You are not authorized to perform this action')`.
    - If the user belongs to no teams, the response is `{ totalCount: 0, data: [] }`.
  - **No ValidationPipe** (`@Query()` is plain), so the DTO decorators and transforms do not run.
  - Query `FilterTaskDto`:
    - `taskIds`: CSV or array.
    - `assignees`: CSV of numbers. This field has no @IsOptional.
    - `workOrderIds`: string.
    - `priority`: TeamTaskPriority.
    - `search`: string.
    - `teamIds`: CSV.
    - `createdByIds`: CSV.
    - `status`: TeamTaskStatusType.
    - `page`: default 1 in the DTO transform. The service parses it with parseInt, falling back to 1.
    - `pageSize`: default 4 in the DTO transform, but that transform does not run, so the service default of 10 applies.
- [ ] GET /api/teamTasks/:taskId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT.
  - Purpose: task details (logs, attachments with signed URLs, comments with edit history).
  - **The `taskId` here is the task's `referenceId`**, not the UUID `id`.
  - There is no team or company access check.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Task not found')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/teamTasks/:taskId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT.
  - Purpose: update a task by its UUID `id`. It edits the fields, syncs assignees (a missing `assigneeIds` removes all assignees), writes field-change, ASSIGNED and UNASSIGNED logs, sends notifications, and uploads new attachments.
  - Multipart. `FilesInterceptor('attachments', 5, { limits: { files: 5 } })`, with the same fileFilter as POST.
  - **No ValidationPipe** (`@Body()` is plain).
  - Body `UpdateTeamTaskDto`, all fields optional:
    - `title`, `description`: `@IsString`.
    - `priority`: `@IsEnum(TeamTaskPriority)`.
    - `status`: `@IsEnum(TeamTaskStatusType)`.
    - `startDate`, `dueDate`: string or null. Null clears the date.
    - `workOrderId`: `@IsString`. Null or an empty string disconnects the work order.
    - `teamId`: `@IsString`.
    - `assigneeIds`: `@IsArray @ArrayUnique @IsInt({each:true})`.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Task not found')`. Every error is rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status || 500)`.
  - There is no ownership or team-membership check.
- [ ] PATCH /api/teamTasks/status/:taskId/:status
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT.
  - Purpose: change the task status (by UUID) and write a STATUS_CHANGED log.
  - The `status` path param is **not validated**. An invalid enum value causes a Prisma error, which surfaces as 500.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Task not found')`.
  - Response message: 'Task status updated'.
- [ ] DELETE /api/teamTasks/attachment/:id
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT and a member of the task's team.
  - Purpose: soft-delete a task attachment. It deletes the S3 object, sets DigitalAsset.isDeleted, and writes an ATTACHMENT_REMOVED log.
  - Param `id` is cast with `+`.
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('Attachment ID is required')` for a falsy or NaN id.
    - `NotFoundException('Attachment not found')`.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not authorized to perform this action')` when the caller is not in the task's team.
  - The raw result `{id, fileName}` is returned without the wrapper.
- [ ] POST /api/teamTasks/:taskId/comments
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT.
  - Purpose: add a comment to a task (by UUID). It writes a COMMENT_ADDED log and notifies the assignees and earlier commenters, except the author.
  - **ValidationPipe applied.**
  - Body `CreateTaskCommentDto`: `comment` is a string, required, `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`.
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('Comment is required')` when the comment is only whitespace.
    - `NotFoundException('Task not found')`.
- [ ] GET /api/teamTasks/:taskId/comments
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT.
  - Purpose: list a task's non-deleted comments (by UUID), ordered by createdAt desc.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Task not found')`.
  - There is no team check.
- [ ] PATCH /api/teamTasks/comments/:commentId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: the author of the comment.
  - Purpose: edit a comment. It writes a COMMENT_UPDATED log when the text changed. If the text is unchanged, it returns early.
  - **ValidationPipe applied.**
  - Body `UpdateTaskCommentDto`: `comment` is a string, required, `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`.
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('Comment is required')`.
    - `NotFoundException('Comment not found')`.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not authorized to edit this comment')`.
  - Bug: the lookup filters by `createdById: userId`, and `existing.comment` is read before the null check. A comment that is missing or belongs to someone else therefore causes a TypeError (500) instead of 404 or 403.
- [ ] DELETE /api/teamTasks/:taskId/comments/:commentId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: the author of the comment.
  - Purpose: soft-delete a comment and write a COMMENT_DELETED log. The `taskId` param is ignored.
  - Throws:
    - `NotFoundException('Comment not found')`.
    - `ForbiddenException('You can only delete your own comment')`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/teamTasks/:taskId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: the creator of the task.
  - Purpose: soft-delete a task (by UUID). Only the creator can do this.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Task not found')` when the task does not exist, is already deleted, or was created by someone else. Errors are rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.

## time-logs

### /api/timeLogs
Controller-level guard: `JwtAuthGuard`. `RequiredPermissions` is imported but commented out. There is no check that the caller is the assigned engineer.

- [ ] POST /api/timeLogs/checkIn. Guard: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER (engineer). Purpose: engineer check-in.
  - What it does:
    - Marks the CHECK_IN completedTask as done.
    - Creates a TimeLog with the distance to the work-order address (PostGIS `ST_DistanceSphere`, in km).
    - Resets the CHECK_OUT task.
    - Sets the work order to `INPROGRESS / CHECK_IN`.
    - Logs `ENGINEER_CHECKED_IN` and sends notifications.
  - Transaction timeout is 15000 ms. The time is truncated to the minute.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, no transform).
  - Body `CreateTimeLogDto`:
    - `workOrderId`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber() @Type(() => Number)`
    - `checkInLatitude`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber() @Type(() => Number)`
    - `checkInLongitude`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber() @Type(() => Number)`
    - `taskId`: `@IsNotEmpty()`
    - `checkInTimeZone`: `@IsString() @IsOptional()`
  - Time window (skipped when the work order status is INCOMPLETE or INPROGRESS; in that case the check-in task is not marked complete): allowed from 5 minutes before to 4 hours after the scheduled start, in the work order's timezone.
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Work order not found', 404)` when the work order is missing or has no engineer.
    - `HttpException('Too early to check in. Current time: <YYYY-MM-DD hh:mm:ss A>', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Check-in window expired. You can only check in within 4 hours of the scheduled time.', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Location is required, Please enable location', 400)` when latitude or longitude is falsy. Note that a value of 0 counts as falsy.
    - Any other error becomes `HttpException(error.message || 'Internal server error during check-in', 500)`.
    - An unknown `taskId` causes a TypeError, returned as 500.
- [ ] POST /api/timeLogs/checkOut. Guard: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER. Purpose: engineer check-out.
  - What it does:
    - Updates the latest TimeLog with the checkout coordinates, distance, `totalHours` (fractional), `type=ON_CHECKOUT`, and `checkOutTimeZone`.
    - Marks the CHECK_OUT task done.
    - Sets subStatus to `CHECK_OUT`.
    - Logs `ENGINEER_CHECKED_OUT`.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES**.
  - Body `CreateCheckOutTimeLogDto`:
    - `workOrderId`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber() @Type(() => Number)`
    - `checkOutLatitude`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber() @Type(() => Number)`
    - `checkOutLongitude`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber() @Type(() => Number)`
    - `checkOutTimeZone`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
    - `taskId`: `@IsNotEmpty()`
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Check-in has not been recorded for this task.', 400)`. If no time log exists at all, a TypeError is returned as 500.
    - `HttpException('Work order not found', 404)` when the work order is not INPROGRESS or has no engineer.
    - `HttpException('Completed task not found.', 404)`.
    - `HttpException('Parent task not found.', 404)`.
    - `HttpException('Cannot check out. Check-out time must be after check-in time.', 400)`.
    - `HttpException('Location is required, Please enable location', 400)`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/timeLogs/updateTimelogs. Guard: JWT (declared twice). Roles: CLIENT or ADMIN editing logs. Purpose: bulk-edit check-in and check-out times. It recalculates `totalHours` and logs `TIMELOG_UPDATED`, all in a single transaction. Times are parsed with the format `'YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss'`.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES** (nested validation runs through `@ValidateNested` / `@Type`).
  - Body `UpdateMultipleTimeLogsDto`:
    - `logs`: `@IsArray() @ValidateNested({ each: true }) @Type(() => UpdateSingleTimeLogDto)`
  - Each `UpdateSingleTimeLogDto` item:
    - `id`: `@IsNumber() @Type(() => Number)`
    - `checkInTime`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
    - `checkOutTime`: `@IsOptional() @IsString()`
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Time log with ID <id> not found', 404)`.
    - `HttpException('Check-out time cannot be before check-in time', 400)`.
  - No company or ownership scoping.
- [ ] POST /api/timeLogs. Guard: JWT. Roles: CLIENT (custom or manual log). Purpose: create a CUSTOM TimeLog with both check-in and check-out, using the same coordinates for both. Logs `TIMELOG_ADDED`. Returns the raw record without a wrapper.
  - ValidationPipe: **YES**.
  - Body `CreateCustomTimeLogDto`:
    - `workOrderId`: `@IsNotEmpty() @IsNumber()`
    - `latitude`: `@IsNotEmpty()`
    - `longitude`: `@IsNotEmpty()`
    - `checkInTime`: `@IsDate() @IsNotEmpty() @Type(() => Date)`
    - `checkOutTime`: `@IsDate() @IsNotEmpty() @Type(() => Date)`
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Work order not found', 404)`.
    - `BadRequestException('This time log already exists.')` when the same work order has identical check-in and check-out times.
    - `HttpException('Location is required, Please enable location', 400)`.
    - Anything else becomes `HttpException(error.message || 'Internal server error during timelog', 500)`.
  - **No validation that checkOut is after checkIn**, so negative hours are possible.
- [ ] GET /api/timeLogs. Guard: JWT. **Stub.** Returns 'This action returns all timeLogs'.
- [ ] GET /api/timeLogs/:id. Guard: JWT. Purpose: list the completed time logs (where `totalHours` is not null) for **work order** `:id`, including the check-in and check-out loggers. No scoping.
- [ ] PATCH /api/timeLogs/:id. Guard: JWT. **Stub.** Returns 'This action updates a #<id> timeLog'. Body `UpdateTimeLogDto` = `PartialType(CreateTimeLogDto)`. ValidationPipe: NO.
- [ ] DELETE /api/timeLogs/:id. Guard: JWT. **Stub.** Returns 'This action removes a #<id> timeLog'.


## Users

Files: `src/modules/users/users.controller.ts`, `users.service.ts`, `dto/*`.

### /api/users (UsersController)

Route order: every static GET (`me`, `workedWith`, `verify`, `nearby`, `qualifications`, `jobActivity`) is declared before `GET :id`, so they resolve correctly.

- [ ] POST /api/users
  - Guards: **none** (public signup). Roles: signup for FREELANCER, CLIENT company owner, CUSTOMER and freelance-company owner.
  - Purpose: register a user. Two things decide the account type:
    - `createUser` **always assigns the FREELANCER role**, and `roleId` is ignored.
    - Company or customer entities are then created from the body: `customerCompanyName` creates a CUSTOMER Client entity through `createCustomerAccount`; `name` creates a client company through `createCompanyUser`; `freelanceCompanyName` creates a freelance company for a FREELANCER. With no `freelanceCompanyName`, the user is assigned the company from env `TECHDOMAIN_COMPANY_ID`, and an 'Engineer' designation is created with JOBS (R/W/U) and USERS (R) permissions, plus FREELANCE_COMPANY_WORKERS (all access) when `freelanceCompanyName` is given.
  - An email signup gets an OTP generated and the `'Tech Domain - Verify OTP'` email (`verify-otp` template).
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED** (`@Body(ValidationPipe)`, no transform or whitelist, so the lower-cased email is not passed through; `createUser` lower-cases it itself).
  - Body `CreateUserDto`:
    - `firstName`: string, required. `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty() @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, { message: 'First name must only contain letters' })`.
