    - `lastName`: string, required. `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty() @Matches(/^[A-Za-z\s]+$/, { message: 'Last name must only contain letters' })`.
    - `email`: required. `@IsNotEmpty() @IsEmail() @Transform(toLowerCase)`.
    - `phoneNumber`: string, required. `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()`.
    - `roleId?`: `@IsNumber() @IsOptional()`. Ignored by the service.
    - `ticket?`: `@IsString() @IsOptional()`. A social-signup ticket; its presence marks the user `isVerified: true` and skips OTP.
    - `password`: required **only when `ticket` is absent** (`@ValidateIf(!dto.ticket)`). `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty() @MinLength(8) @Matches(/^(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*[0-9])(?=.*[!@#$%^&*]).{8,20}$/, { message: 'Password must be 8-20 characters and include upper and lower case letters, a number and a symbol' })`.
    - `areaCoverage?`: `@IsString() @IsOptional()`.
    - `skillset?`: string[]. `@IsArray() @IsString({ each: true }) @IsOptional()`.
    - `freelanceCompanyName?`: `@IsString() @IsOptional()`.
    - `companyId?`: `@IsNumber() @IsOptional()`.
    - `name?` (client company name), `noOfEmployees?`, `companyAnnualRevenue?`, `technicianFrequency?`, `technicianUrgency?`: each `@IsString() @IsOptional()`.
    - `customerCompanyName?`: `@IsString() @IsOptional()`.
    - `companyEmail?`: `@IsEmail() @IsOptional()`.
    - `website?`: `@IsString() @IsOptional()`.
    - `referralSource?`: `@IsString() @IsOptional()`.
    - `permissionId?`: `@IsNumber() @IsNotEmpty() @IsOptional()`.
    - `isVerified?`: `@IsOptional() @IsBoolean()`. Not forwarded by the service.
    - `address?`: `@IsOptional() @Type(() => AddressTypeDto)`. It has **no `@ValidateNested`**, so the nested rules are not enforced. Nested `AddressTypeDto`:
      - `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`, `type` (AddressType): each `@IsString()`.
      - `longitude` and `latitude`: each `@IsDecimal()`.
  - Service throws (`create`). **Every error is re-wrapped as `HttpException(error.message || 'Something went wrong', 500)`, so the intended status codes below come back as 500 with the same message:**
    - `HttpException('Account deleted. Please contact support.', FORBIDDEN)`: the email belongs to a soft-deleted user (case-insensitive match).
    - `HttpException('User already exists.', CONFLICT)`: the existing user is verified.
    - `HttpException('User already exists with this email, OTP code sent', FORBIDDEN)`: the existing user is unverified, so the OTP is re-sent first.
    - `HttpException('Customer role is not configured', INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)`: raised in `createCustomerAccount`.
    - `createUser` re-throws any Prisma or bcrypt error as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.
  - Note: the `ticket` is not verified in this service. A ticket with no password reaches `bcrypt.hash(undefined)`, which will fail.
- [ ] PATCH /api/users
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: any logged-in user (self).
  - Purpose: update your own profile fields.
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED**.
  - Body `UpdateUserDto`: `PartialType(OmitType(CreateUserDto, ['email','password','roleId','companyId','isVerified','permissionId']))`. All remaining CreateUserDto fields become optional with the same rules, including the firstName/lastName `Matches` messages. It adds:
    - `addressLine1?`, `australianBusinessNumber?`, `addressLine2?`, `city?`, `country?`, `state?`, `zipCode?`, `description?`, `nationality?`: each `@IsString() @IsOptional()`.
    - `gstPercentage?`: `@IsNumber() @IsOptional()`.
    - `isForeignContractor?`: `@IsBoolean() @IsOptional()`.
    - `longitude?`: `@IsNumber({}, { message: 'latitude must be a number' }) @IsNotEmpty() @IsOptional()`. The message text says "latitude" even though the field is `longitude`.
    - `latitude?`: `@IsNumber() @IsNotEmpty() @IsOptional()`.
    - `timeZone?`: `@IsOptional() @IsNotEmpty()`.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('User not found')`.
  - Returns `{ data, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/users/:id
  - Guards: JWT. Roles:
    - FREELANCER: self only, with an OTP.
    - CLIENT: self, or any user if the caller owns a company.
    - ADMIN: anyone.
  - Purpose: soft-delete an account and send the `'Tech Domain - Account Deletion'` mail to the user and to affected users.
  - Body (no DTO, no validation):
    - `otp?`: string.
    - `reason?`: string. Required unless the user already has a `deletionReason`.
  - Throws (all re-wrapped as `HttpException(error.message, error.status || 500)`):
    - `HttpException('Authenticated user not found', UNAUTHORIZED)`.
    - `HttpException('You are not allowed to delete this account', FORBIDDEN)`: a FREELANCER deleting another user, or a CLIENT who is neither owner nor self.
    - `HttpException('User not found', NOT_FOUND)`: the target is missing or already deleted.
    - `HttpException('Reason is required', BAD_REQUEST)`.
    - `HttpException('Invalid or expired OTP', BAD_REQUEST)`: FREELANCER only.
  - FREELANCER without an `otp`: the OTP is re-sent (UseCase `ACCOUNT_DELETE`) and the response is `{ message: 'OTP sent to your email for account deletion confirmation.' }`.
  - The reason is stored with a role prefix: `admin: `, `client: ` or `engineer: `.
  - Success: `{ message: 'User deleted successfully.' }`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/users/address
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: self.
  - Purpose: upsert your personal (USER-type) address and link `personalAddressId`.
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED**.
  - Body: `UpdateUserDto`, same as above. Fields used: `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`, `country`, `longitude`, `latitude`, `timeZone`.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('User not found')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/users/admin/:id
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `{ module: ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT, access: 'isWrite' }`. Roles: ADMIN.
  - Purpose: admin edits a user's basic profile.
  - ValidationPipe: **NOT applied** (`@Body()` only).
  - Body `UpdateUserProfileDto`:
    - `firstName?`, `lastName?`, `australianBusinessNumber?`: each `@IsOptional() @IsString()`.
    - `email?`: `@IsOptional() @IsEmail()`.
    - `phoneNumber?`: `@IsOptional()` with no type check.
    - `gstPercentage?`: `@IsOptional() @IsNumber()`.
    - `isForeignContractor?`: `@IsOptional() @IsBoolean()`.
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('User not found.', NOT_FOUND)`.
    - `HttpException('Email is already in use.', CONFLICT)`: another non-deleted user already has the email.
    - Any other error becomes `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to update profile.')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/users/billingRates
  - Guards: JWT. Roles:
    - FREELANCER: self, only while the profile is PENDING or UNAPPROVE.
    - ADMIN: any user.
  - Purpose: upsert the HOURLY, HALF_DAY and FULL_DAY billing rates.
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED**.
  - Body `CreateBillingRatesDto`:
    - `userId?`: `@IsOptional() @Type(() => Number) @IsInt()`. Defaults to the caller.
    - `billingRates`: required. `@ArrayNotEmpty() @ValidateNested({ each: true }) @Type(() => BillingRateDto)`. Each `BillingRateDto` item has:
      - `paymentType`: `@IsEnum(PaymentType)`.
      - `currency`: `@IsEnum(CurrencyType)`.
      - `rate`: `@IsNumber()`.
  - Throws:
    - `ForbiddenException('You may only update your own billing rates.')`: the caller is neither the FREELANCER owner nor an ADMIN.
    - `NotFoundException('You are not allowed to update rates.')`: the target is missing, or the self status is not PENDING or UNAPPROVE.
    - `BadRequestException('For currency <currency of first item>, missing <Hourly, Half Day, Full Day> rates')`: all three payment types are required.
- [ ] GET /api/users/me
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `{ module: 'USERS', access: 'isRead' }`. Roles: all users with USERS read permission.
  - Purpose: get the current user's full profile (`findById(self, self)`).
  - Throws `NotFoundException('User not found')`.
- [ ] GET /api/users/workedWith
  - Guards: **none**. Roles: intended for CLIENT.
  - Purpose: engineers who have worked with a company.
  - Query:
    - `companyId`: required.
    - `status?`: WorkOrderStatus.
    - `customerStatus?`: WorkOrderStatus.
    - `email?`.
  - Throws:
    - `BadRequestException('Company ID is required')`.
    - `BadRequestException('Invalid Company ID')`.
- [ ] GET /api/users/verify
  - Guards: **none**. Roles: public or internal.
  - Purpose: mark an email as verified.
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED** (`@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({ transform: true }))`).
  - Query `VerifyEmailDto`: `email` is required. `@Transform` trims it, replaces spaces with `+` and lower-cases it, then `@IsEmail({}, { message: 'Invalid email format' })`.
  - Throws:
    - `NotFoundException('User not found with this email')`.
    - `BadRequestException('Email is already verified')`.
  - Returns `{ message: 'Email verified successfully', email }`.
- [ ] GET /api/users/backgroundVerification/:id
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: ADMIN or CLIENT (no role check).
  - Purpose: the user's background-verification documents.
- [ ] GET /api/users/nearby
  - Guards: JWT + PermissionsGuard `{ module: WORK_ORDERS, access: 'isRead' }`. Roles: CLIENT or ADMIN.
  - Purpose: find engineers near a work order (modes: nearby, workedWith, talentPool).
  - Query (untyped): `radius` (km), `latitude`, `longitude`, `workOrderId`, `page` (default 1), `limit` (default 10), `search`, `sortBy`, `sortOrder`, `mode`, `talentPoolId`, `filterByRequiredSkills` (`'true'`), `filterByRequiredTools` (`'true'`).
  - Throws `BadRequestException('Invalid work order or company')` when the work order has no companyId. A missing work order causes a TypeError (500) before this check runs.
- [ ] GET /api/users/nearby/rateComparison
  - Guards: **none**. Roles: intended for CLIENT.
  - Purpose: rate statistics for APPROVED freelancers inside a bounding box.
  - Query: `distance` (km), `paymentType` (PaymentType), `latitude`, `longitude`, `currency?` (CurrencyType). All are unvalidated.
- [ ] POST /api/users/qualifications
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER (self), or ADMIN/CLIENT via `targetUserId` (no authorization check on `targetUserId`).
  - Purpose: add a qualification with an optional file. An existing qualification of the same subtype is soft-deleted first.
  - Multipart: `FileInterceptor('file')`. **No size limit and no mimetype filter.** The file is optional. S3 path: `<userId>/qualifications/<typeName>`, category `USER_QUALIFICATION`.
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED** (`transform: true`).
  - Body `CreateUserQualificationDto`:
    - `qualificationSubTypeId`: required. `@IsInt() @Type(() => Number)`.
    - `companyName?`, `policyNumber?`, `coveragePerIncident?`: each `@IsOptional() @IsString()`.
    - `startDate?`, `endDate?`: each `@IsOptional() @IsString()`, not date-validated.
  - Query: `targetUserId?`.
  - Throws:
    - Controller: `BadRequestException('Type not found in request')` when there is no `qualificationSubTypeId`.
    - Service: `BadRequestException('Qualification subtype not found')`.
    - Service: `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to upload file to S3')`. If the DB transaction fails, the S3 file is rolled back and the original error is re-thrown.
- [ ] PATCH /api/users/qualifications/:id
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: same as POST.
  - Purpose: update a qualification, optionally replacing the file (the old S3 object is deleted).
  - Multipart: `FileInterceptor('file')` with no limits.
  - ValidationPipe: **APPLIED** (`transform: true`).
  - Body `UpdateUserQualificationDto`: identical fields and rules to the create DTO. `qualificationSubTypeId` is still **required**.
  - Query: `targetUserId?`.
  - Throws:
    - Controller: `BadRequestException('Qualification not found in request')` when there is no id.
    - Service: `NotFoundException('user Qualification not found')`: no row matches `{id, userId}`.
    - Service: `BadRequestException('Qualification subtype not found')`: only when a file is sent.
    - Service: `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to upload file to S3')`.
- [ ] GET /api/users/qualifications/:userId/:qualificationTypeId
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: a user's non-deleted qualifications of one type.
- [ ] GET /api/users/qualifications
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: all qualifications for the caller, or for the `userId` query parameter.
  - Query: `userId?`.
- [ ] POST /api/users/skills/:userId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: ADMIN, CLIENT, FREELANCER.
  - Purpose: add skills (subcategories). They are `isVerified` when added by an ADMIN or CLIENT.
  - Body (no DTO, no validation): `subCategoryIds`: string[].
  - Throws (re-wrapped as `HttpException(error.response || message, status || 500)`):
    - `NotFoundException('User not found')`: the caller or the target is missing.
    - `HttpException('Not allowed to perform this action', FORBIDDEN)`: any other role.
    - `BadRequestException('One or more skills not found')`.
    - `BadRequestException({ message: 'Some skills already exist for this user', existingSkills: [...] })`.
- [ ] GET /api/users/skills/:id
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: a user's skills.
- [ ] GET /api/users/workSummary/:id
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: completed-job summary grouped by category.
- [ ] GET /api/users/reliability/:id
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: reliability, timeliness and cancellation metrics. They aggregate across a freelance company and are scoped to the viewer's company.
- [ ] GET /api/users/jobActivity
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: offer and job activity statistics for a user.
  - Query: `userId`.
  - Throws `HttpException('Unauthorized', HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN)` when the caller is not found.
- [ ] DELETE /api/users/skills/:id
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: effectively any ADMIN, CLIENT or FREELANCER.
  - Purpose: hard-delete a UserSkill.
  - Query: `targetUserId?`.
  - Throws (re-wrapped as `HttpException(message, status)`):
    - `NotFoundException('User skill not found')`.
    - `NotFoundException('user not found')`.
    - `HttpException('Not allowed to delete this skill', FORBIDDEN)`.
  - Success: `{ message: 'Skill deleted successfully' }`.
- [ ] DELETE /api/users/qualifications/:id
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: soft-delete a user qualification.
  - Query: `targetUserId?`.
  - Throws `BadRequestException('user qualification not found')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/users/deviceToken
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: store the push device token.
  - Body: `deviceToken`, string, not validated.
- [ ] GET /api/users/:id
  - Guards: JWT.
  - Purpose: get a user profile with a role-shaped response. When someone else views an accepted freelance-company member, the FC owner's profile is returned unless `direct=true` or the viewer is ADMIN.
  - Query: `direct?` (`'true'`).
  - Throws:
    - `NotFoundException('User not found')`: the caller or the target is missing.
    - `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to view this profile.')`: a FREELANCER viewing another FREELANCER outside their own freelance company.
- [ ] GET /api/users/:id/jobsCompleted
  - Guards: JWT. `ParseIntPipe` on `id`: a non-numeric id gives a 400 `Validation failed (numeric string is expected)`.
  - Purpose: paginated completed jobs (scoped to the company for CLIENT viewers).
  - Query: `page?`, `limit?` (paginated only when both are set), `search?`.
  - Throws `NotFoundException('Auth user not found for ID: <userId>')`.
- [ ] PATCH /api/users/profileEdited
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER.
  - Purpose: flag `profileNeedsResubmit=true` when the status is SUBMITTED.
- [ ] GET /api/users/engineer/submitProfile
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: FREELANCER.
  - Purpose: set the status to SUBMITTED and `profileNeedsResubmit=false`, then notify the first ADMIN with a SYSTEM notification titled 'Profile Submitted'.
  - This is a **GET that mutates state**.
  - Internal `HttpException('Admin not found', NOT_FOUND)` is **swallowed** by an empty `catch {}`, so the endpoint always returns `{ data: undefined, message: 'SUCCESS' }`.
- [ ] (commented out, not routed) `POST /api/users/qualifications` (old version taking `qualificationIds`) and `GET /api/users/qualifications/:id`.
- Response-only DTOs, not used for validation: `admin-response`, `client-response`, `customer-response`, `engineer-response`, `user-response`.

## workforce

### /api/workforce
Controller-level pipe: `@UsePipes(new ValidationPipe({ transform: true, whitelist: true }))`, so **ValidationPipe is YES on all routes**. `CreateWorkforceDto` and `UpdateWorkforceDto` are empty and unused.

Query `WorkForceFilterDto`, shared by GET `/` and GET `/:talentPoolId`. All fields are optional:
- `limit`: `@Type(() => Number) @IsNumber()`, default 10
- `page`: `@Type(() => Number) @IsNumber()`, default 1
- `search`: `@IsString()`
- `fullName`: `@IsString()`
- `country`: `@IsString()`
- `city`: `@IsString()`
- `talentPool`: `@IsString()`
- `jobsCompleted`: `@Type(() => Number) @IsNumber()`. When set (and the caller is not a CUSTOMER), pagination is done in memory.
- `workedWith`: `@Transform(value === 'true') @IsBoolean()`
- `backgroundCheck`: `@Transform(value === 'true') @IsBoolean()`
- `certifications`: `@IsString()`, used as a numeric id
- `equipment`: `@IsString()`, used as a numeric id
- `skillSet`: `@IsString()`
- `freelanceCompanyId`: `@Type(() => Number) @IsNumber()`

- [ ] GET /api/workforce. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS:isRead`. Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER. Purpose: search approved FREELANCER engineers.
  - Without `freelanceCompanyId`, only engineers who do not own a company are returned. With it, only ACCEPTED members of that freelance company, whose owner is approved.
  - For a CUSTOMER, the talentPool, workedWith, backgroundCheck and jobsCompleted filters are ignored.
  - Throws: `HttpException('You are not authorized', 403)` when the caller is not CLIENT or CUSTOMER.
- [ ] GET /api/workforce/map. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_COVERAGE_MAP:isRead`. Purpose: coordinates of every approved engineer that has a personal address latitude and longitude, as `[{id, name, lat, lng, city, country}]`. This is global and not company-scoped.
  - Throws: `HttpException('You are not authorized', 403)` when the caller is not found.
- [ ] GET /api/workforce/map/image. Guards: JWT + Permissions `WORK_FORCE_COVERAGE_MAP:isRead`. Purpose: a Google Static Maps PNG of engineer density clusters, served raw with headers `Content-Type`, `Content-Length`, and `Cache-Control: public, max-age=600`. It is cached in memory for 10 minutes. The image is `size=640x360&scale=2`.
  - Throws:
    - `InternalServerErrorException('GOOGLE_API_KEY is not configured')`.
    - `InternalServerErrorException('Google Static Maps API error: <status>')` when the response is not OK or is JSON.
- [ ] GET /api/workforce/:talentPoolId. Guard: JWT only (no PermissionsGuard). Roles: CLIENT. Purpose: engineers in the given talent pool, with the same filters.
  - `:talentPoolId` uses `ParseIntPipe`, which returns 400 'Validation failed (numeric string is expected)' for a non-numeric value.
  - Throws: `HttpException('You are not authorized', 403)` when the caller is not CLIENT.
  - **The pool id is not checked against the caller's company.**

## WorkOrderLogs

### /api/workOrderLogs
Controller: `src/modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.controller.ts`. There is no ValidationPipe on any route. `UpdateWorkOrderLogDto` (`PartialType`) is not used by any route.

- [ ] POST /api/workOrderLogs
  - Guards: **NONE**. The route is unauthenticated and was meant for internal use only.
  - Purpose: create a raw work-order log row directly.
  - **No ValidationPipe**, so no DTO rule is enforced. This is a security gap: anyone can write audit logs.
  - Body `CreateWorkOrderLogDto`:
    - `workOrderId`: int, required. `@IsInt`.
    - `performedById`: int, required. `@IsInt`.
    - `logType`: required. `@IsEnum(WorkOrderLogType)`. Allowed values:
      - WORK_ORDER_CREATED, WORK_ORDER_UPDATED, WORK_ORDER_DELETED
      - WORK_ORDER_PO_UPDATED, WORK_ORDER_PO_ADDED, WORK_ORDER_PO_DELETED
      - WORK_ORDER_CANCELLED_BY_CLIENT, WORK_ORDER_CANCELLED_BY_ENGINEER
      - WORK_ORDER_NOTE_ADDED, WORK_ORDER_NOTE_UPDATED, WORK_ORDER_STATUS_UPDATED
      - ASSIGNED_ENGINEER, UNASSIGNED_ENGINEER
      - OFFER_ROUTED, OFFER_SENT, OFFER_UPDATED, OFFER_ACCEPTED, OFFER_ASSIGN, OFFER_UNASSIGNED, OFFER_REJECTED, OFFER_DECLINED, OFFER_WITHDRAWN
      - CUSTOMER_PAYMENT_UPDATED, ENGINEER_PAYMENT_UPDATED, PAYMENT_APPROVED
      - TIMELOG_ADDED, TIMELOG_UPDATED
      - ENGINEER_CHECKED_IN, ENGINEER_CHECKED_OUT
      - CUSTOMER_TIMESHEET_UPDATED, ENGINEER_TIMESHEET_UPDATED
      - EXPENSE_ADDED, EXPENSE_REMOVED, EXPENSE_APPROVED
      - COMMENT_ADDED
      - CUSTOMER_FILE_UPLOADED, DELIVERABLE_UPLOADED, CUSTOMER_FILE_DELETED, DELIVERABLE_UPLOADED_DELETED
      - SIGN_OFF_SHEET_CREATED, SIGN_OFF_SHEET_UPDATED
      - OTHER
    - `field`: string, optional. `@IsString`. The service ignores it.
    - `metaData`: object, optional. `@IsObject`.
    - `oldStatus`, `newStatus`, `oldCustomerStatus`, `newCustomerStatus`: WorkOrderStatus, optional. `@IsOptional` only.
    - `oldSubStatus`, `newSubStatus`, `oldCustomerSubStatus`, `newCustomerSubStatus`: WorkOrderSubStatus, optional. `@IsOptional` only.
  - The service does not catch errors; Prisma errors surface as 500.
- [ ] GET /api/workOrderLogs/workOrder/:workOrderId
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT or CUSTOMER company users. Rows are scoped to the caller's `companyId`.
  - Purpose: paginated work-order history. It hides these log types: OFFER_SENT, WORK_ORDER_NOTE_ADDED, WORK_ORDER_NOTE_UPDATED, OFFER_ROUTED, OFFER_REJECTED, OFFER_ACCEPTED, OFFER_UPDATED, OFFER_DECLINED, OFFER_WITHDRAWN. Each row gets a smart event label, the changed field labels, and the performer's name, designation and profile picture.
  - Params and query:
    - Param `workOrderId`: `ParseIntPipe`. A non-integer gives a 400 "Validation failed (numeric string is expected)".
    - Query `page`: `DefaultValuePipe(1)` + `ParseIntPipe`.
    - Query `limit`: `DefaultValuePipe(10)` + `ParseIntPipe`. The service caps it at 100.
  - Every error is caught and rethrown as `InternalServerErrorException('Failed to fetch work order logs')`. That includes a missing user from verifyUser, which would have thrown `HttpException('Unauthorized', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)`.
  - Note: the `count` query for totalItems is **not** scoped by companyId, while the rows are, so the total can reveal how many logs exist for another company's work order.
- [ ] GET /api/workOrderLogs/:id
  - Guards: JWT. Roles: CLIENT or CUSTOMER. The row is scoped to the caller's company.
  - Purpose: details of one log entry by string id: change details grouped by section (plus Expense), the performer, and the work order's timeZone.
  - Throws:
    - `HttpException('Log Details not found', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)` when the log is not found or belongs to another company.
    - `HttpException('Unauthorized', HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND)` from verifyUser when the user does not exist.

## Work Orders (`src/modules/work-orders`)

Controller: `work-orders.controller.ts` (the only REST controller in the module; `work-orders.gateway.ts` is WebSocket-only and not covered here).

Legend: **JWT** = `JwtAuthGuard` (Bearer token; the strategy rejects with `UnauthorizedException('Session invalid or logged out' | 'Session expired' | 'User not found' | 'User deleted' | 'User deactivated')`). **Perm(X:acc)** = `PermissionsGuard` + `@RequiredPermissions`, where the entries are OR'd (having any one of them is enough) and a failure returns 403 Forbidden resource. **VP** = a `ValidationPipe` is attached to the route. There is no global ValidationPipe, so on routes without **VP** the DTO decorators are **not enforced**. No route in this controller uses `ProfileCompletionGuard`.

Many service checks throw `HttpException(message, status)`. The status is shown in brackets, e.g. `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)`.

### `/api/workOrders`

#### Create / publish lifecycle

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isWrite` OR `CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS:isWrite`). **VP applied** (default `ValidationPipe`, no `transform`/`whitelist`). Roles: CLIENT (creates directly), CUSTOMER (creates as `PENDING_APPROVAL`, kept off-market until a client publishes it). Purpose: create a work order or a draft.
  - Body `CreateWorkOrderDto`:
    - `title`: string, **required** (`@IsString`)
    - `purchaseOrderNumber`: string, optional
    - `serviceLevelAgreeement` (misspelling is in the code): string, optional
    - `clientId`: number, optional (`@IsNumber`)
    - `incidentId`, `templateName`, `createdById`, `teamId`, `secondaryTeamId`, `description`, `confidentialInformation`, `categoryId`, `vendorId`, `paymentTerms`, `providerAssessmentId`, `displayName`, `country`, `addressId`, `addressLine1`, `addressLine2`, `city`, `state`, `zipCode`: string, optional
    - `projectId`, `workOrderManagerId`: number, optional (`@IsNumber`)
    - `isExportDisable`, `isCustomerFilesVisible`, `isCounterOffersDisable`, `isGpsRequire`, `isTemplate`, `isDraft`, `isRemote`, `saveForFuture`: boolean, optional (`@IsBoolean`)
    - `workOrderServices`: optional array, `@ValidateNested({each})` of `{ serviceId: @IsString }` (`serviceId` has no `@IsOptional`, so it is required inside each item)
    - `qualificationSubTypes`: optional `@IsArray` (`number[]`; elements not validated)
    - `contacts`: optional array of `ContactsDto`: `id` int optional; `name`, `phoneNumber`, `note`, `position` string optional; `email` optional, custom `@IsEmailOrEmpty({ message: 'Invalid email address' })`. That rule accepts `''`, `null` or `undefined`; otherwise the value must match `/\S+@\S+\.\S+/` (default message `"<property> must be a valid email or empty"`).
    - `expenses`, `engineerExpenses`: optional arrays of `ExpensesDto`: `id` number optional; `expenseType` (ExpenseType enum, but only `@IsString` is checked) optional; `description` string optional; `amount` number optional; `expenseKey` string optional
    - `tasks`: optional array of `TasksTypeDto`: `taskType` (TaskType, `@IsString`) optional; `phone`, `reason`, `signature`, `description`, `document` string optional; `emailsToNotify` `@IsArray` optional
    - `shipments`: optional array of `ShipmentTypeDto`: `trackingNumber` **required** `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`; `shipmentDescription` string optional; `carrier` (CarrierType) **required** `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`; `shipmentDirection` (ShipmentDirectionType) **required** `@IsString @IsNotEmpty`
    - `scheduleType` (ScheduleType), `orderStartDate`, `orderStartTime`, `timeZone`, `paymentType` (PaymentType), `currency` (CurrencyType): `@ValidateIf(o => !o.isDraft) @IsString`. They are **required unless `isDraft` is true**. Only the string type is checked, not the enum.
    - `orderEndDate`, `orderEndTime`: string, optional
    - `engineerMinimumHours`: int, optional (`@IsInt`)
    - `engineerPaymentType` (PaymentType), `engineerCurrency` (CurrencyType): string, optional
    - `engineerFixedAmount`, `engineerHourlyRate`, `engineerAdditionalHourlyRate`, `engineerHalfDayRate`, `engineerFthRate`, `engineerFullDayRate`, `fixedAmount`, `nonBusinessHourRate`, `hourlyRate`, `halfDayRate`, `fthRate`, `fullDayRate`, `customHoursRate`, `customHours`, `additionalHourlyRate`, `hoursWorked`, `minimumHours`: `@Type(() => Number) @IsNumber`, optional. `@Type` has no effect because the VP has no `transform`.
    - `locationType` (LocationType): `@IsNotEmpty @IsOptional`
    - `longitude`, `latitude`, `type`: optional, not validated
    - `customerFiles`: optional array of `{ id: @IsInt }`
  - Service `create()` throws:
    - `HttpException('You are not authorized to create work order', 403)` if the user's role is not CLIENT or CUSTOMER
    - CUSTOMER only: `HttpException('Please select a client', 400)` if not a draft and there is no `clientId`
    - CUSTOMER only: `HttpException('Invalid client selected', 403)` if `clientId` is not a child client of `user.customerId` (checked for drafts too)
    - Non-draft: `HttpException('Service delivery team is required', 400)` and `HttpException('Operations team is required', 400)`. Missing `teamId`/`secondaryTeamId` fall back to the selected client's teams first.
    - Non-draft: `HttpException('Location is required', 400)` if there is no `addressId`, `isRemote` is false and no location field has content. A new Address row is created only when `isRemote === false`, there is no `addressId`, and some location data is present.
    - Prisma P2002 on Shipment: `HttpException('Tracking number already exists', 409)`
    - `PrismaClientValidationError`: `HttpException('Validation failed', 400)`
    - Any other error: `HttpException('Something went wrong', 503)`
  - Drafts return early, with no marketplace broadcast, emails or chat room.

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/approvePublish**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isWrite`). No VP, no body. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: publish a customer-raised `PENDING_APPROVAL` ticket to the marketplace. Response message `'Work order published.'`
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found or not pending approval', 404)` if the work order is not in the user's company or is not `PENDING_APPROVAL`
  - `assertPublishable()` (teams not required) can throw `HttpException({ message: 'Cannot publish — please complete: <list>', missingFields, statusCode: 400 }, 400)`. The list can contain: `client`, `technology stack`, `schedule type`, `start date`, `start time`, `time zone`, `location`, `payment`. `payment` is missing when `paymentType` or `currency` is empty, or when the rate for the payment type is not > 0 (HOURLY→hourlyRate, HALF_DAY→halfDayRate, FULL_DAY→fullDayRate, FIXED→fixedAmount, FTH→fthRate).
  - `HttpException('Work Order already processed', 409)` if the guarded `updateMany` updates 0 rows (race)
  - Sets `workOrderManagerId` to the publisher if it is null.

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/draft/:id**: JWT only (no Perm), no VP, no body. Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER. Purpose: move a PUBLISHED or PENDING_APPROVAL work order back to DRAFT.
  - `HttpException('not found', 404)` if the user is missing
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` if the work order is not in the user's company
  - `HttpException('Work Order Already Drafted', 409)`
  - `HttpException('A work order can only be moved back to draft while it is awaiting approval or published. This one is <status>.', 400)`
  - CUSTOMER not the creator and not in their scoped clients: `ForbiddenException('You can only move back to draft the work orders you raised or that belong to your clients')`

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/publish/:id**: JWT only, no VP, no body. Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER. Purpose: publish a DRAFT. A CLIENT moves it to PUBLISHED; a CUSTOMER moves it to PENDING_APPROVAL.
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` if not a DRAFT in the user's company
  - `HttpException('Work Order Already Published', 409)`: unreachable, because the query filters on status DRAFT
  - CUSTOMER: `assertCustomerSubmittable()`:
    - `HttpException({ message: 'Cannot submit — please complete: <list>', missingFields, statusCode: 400 }, 400)`. The list can contain: `title`, `entity`, `location`, `schedule type`, `start date`, `start time`, `time zone`, `payment`.
    - `HttpException({ message: 'This entity has no service delivery or operations team set up yet. Please ask your administrator to configure it, then submit again.', statusCode: 400 }, 400)` if the teams are still unset after inheriting them from the client
  - CLIENT: `assertPublishable(..., { requireTeams: true })`. This uses the same message as above, and the list can also contain `service delivery team` and `operations team`.
  - `isCompanyUser()` never throws its `HttpException('not found', 404)` because the promise is not awaited (bug).

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/review**: JWT + Perm(`ACTION_REVIEW_WORK_ORDER:isWrite`), no VP. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: mark a WORKDONE or INCOMPLETE work order as reviewed (subStatus REVIEWED).
  - `HttpException("you don't has access", 400)` if the user is missing
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` if not in the company or not WORKDONE/INCOMPLETE
  - Other errors are rethrown with `error.status || 500`.

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/verify**: JWT + Perm(`ACTION_VERIFY_WORK_ORDER:isWrite`), no VP. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: move a WORKDONE/INCOMPLETE work order with subStatus REVIEWED to VERIFIED. SubStatus becomes `PO_PENDING` when the project has `isPoRequired` and there is no PO; otherwise it becomes null.
  - `HttpException("you don't has access", 400)`
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)`

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/approve**: JWT + Perm(`ACTION_APPROVE_WORK_ORDER:isWrite`), no VP. Role: CLIENT (the user's role must be CLIENT). Purpose: approve a VERIFIED work order and create the final payment.
  - `HttpException("you don't has access", 400)` if the user is not a CLIENT
  - `HttpException('Work Order is in process', 404)` if not VERIFIED in the company
  - `HttpException('Something went seriouslly wrong', 404)` if there is no ASSIGNED offer for the engineer (misspelling is in the code)

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/markPayable**: JWT + Perm(`ACTION_APPROVE_WORK_ORDER:isWrite`), no VP. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: mark a CANCELLED work order as payable to the engineer.
  - `HttpException("you don't has access", 400)` if not a CLIENT
  - `HttpException('Work Order is in process', 404)` if not CANCELLED in the company
  - `HttpException('Something went seriouslly wrong', 404)` if there is no CANCELLED offer for the engineer

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/poPending**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isUpdate`), no VP. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: set subStatus PO_PENDING. Allowed when status is VERIFIED, or `customerStatus` is APPROVED or CANCELLED_PAYABLE.
  - `HttpException("you don't has access", 400)`
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/republish/:id**: JWT only (a GET that changes state), no VP. Role: CLIENT (the creator). Purpose: republish a work order, resetting expenses and tasks.
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` if `createdById != user`
  - From `resetWorkOrderExpenses`: `HttpException('No work order expenses found to reset.', 404)` and `HttpException('An error occurred while resetting work order expenses.', 500)`
  - From `resetWorkOrderTasks`: `HttpException('No tasks found for the given workOrderId.', 404)`, `HttpException('No completedTask records found to update.', 404)` and `HttpException('An error occurred while updating completedTask records.', 500)`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/:id/complete**: JWT only (a GET that changes state), no VP. Roles: FREELANCER (engineer), CLIENT. Purpose: mark an INPROGRESS or INCOMPLETE work order as WORKDONE.
  - FREELANCER with outstanding `COMPLETE_CUSTOM_TASK` tasks: `BadRequestException('Complete the remaining task[s] before marking this work order complete: <titles>')`. Missing titles are shown as `'Untitled task'`. A client can bypass this check.
  - There is no explicit not-found check: a work order that is missing or in the wrong status causes a TypeError, which is rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/incomplete**: JWT only, **VP applied**. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: send a WORKDONE work order back to INCOMPLETE.
  - Body `CreateMarkIncompleteDto`:
    - `workOrderId`: number, **required** (`@IsNotEmpty @IsNumber`)
    - `reasonType` (ReasonType): **required** (`@IsNotEmpty @IsString`)
    - `reason`: string, optional
  - `HttpException('Work Order is in process', 404)` if the work order is not WORKDONE. There is no company or ownership check.

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/engineer/cancel/:id**: JWT only, no VP. Role: FREELANCER (or a company owner through `referredEngineerId`). Purpose: the engineer cancels an ASSIGNED work order. This recalculates `user.cancellationRate` and emails the client.
  - Body (untyped): `cancellationReason?`, `reason?`
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` if there is no ASSIGNED offer for the user
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` if the work order is not ASSIGNED
  - Every error is rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, 400)`.

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/client/cancel/:id**: JWT + Perm(`ACTION_CANCEL_WORK_ORDER:isWrite`), no VP. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: the client cancels an ASSIGNED work order. This cancels the ASSIGNED offer and the engineer's ROUTED offer, but not PENDING or COUNTER offers.
  - Body (untyped): `cancellationReason?`, `reason?`
  - `HttpException('User not found', 404)`
  - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)` if not ASSIGNED in the company
  - Every error is rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, 400)`.

- [ ] **DELETE /api/workOrders/:id**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isDelete` OR `CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS:isDelete`), no VP. Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER. Purpose: soft-delete a work order, which is allowed only for a **DRAFT**. A CUSTOMER can delete only drafts they created.
  - `NotFoundException('User Not found')`
  - `ForbiddenException('You are not allowed to perform this action')` if the work order is not a DRAFT in the company, or not owned by the customer
  - Other errors are rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.

#### Status / notes / edits

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/:id/subStatus**: JWT only, **VP applied**. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: set `subStatus` directly. Response message `'Status updated successfully'`.
  - Body `UpdateWorkOrderSubStatusDto`: `subStatus` (WorkOrderSubStatus), **required** (`@IsString @IsNotEmpty`; the enum is not checked)
  - `HttpException("Work Order doesn't exist.", 404)`. There is **no company or ownership check**.

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/:id/status**: JWT only, **VP applied**. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: set `status` directly within the user's company. Response message `'Status updated successfully'`.
  - Body `UpdateWorkOrderStatusDto`: `status` (WorkOrderStatus), **required** (`@IsString @IsNotEmpty`; the enum is not checked)
  - `HttpException("User doesn't exist.", 404)`
  - `HttpException("Work Order doesn't exist.", 404)` if the work order is not in the company

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/:id/addNote**: JWT only, no VP. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: set `notes` on the work order. Response message `'SUCCESS'`.
  - Body (untyped): `notes`
  - `HttpException('Unauthorized', 400)` if the user has no `companyId`
  - `HttpException('No workOrder found', 404)`

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/:id**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isUpdate` OR `CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS:isUpdate`). **No VP**, so `UpdateWorkOrderDto` is not validated. Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER. Purpose: partial update of any section.
  - Body `UpdateWorkOrderDto`: `PartialType(CreateWorkOrderDto)` (all fields optional) plus `status?` string (WorkOrderStatus)
  - `NotFoundException('User Not found')`
  - `NotFoundException('Work Order with id <id> not found')` if not in the company
  - CUSTOMER not the creator and not in their scoped clients: `ForbiddenException('You can only edit work orders you raised or that belong to your clients')`
  - CUSTOMER when status is WORKDONE or VERIFIED, or `customerStatus` is APPROVED: the body is silently filtered down to `expenses` and `purchaseOrderNumber`.
  - Non-DRAFT with `teamId` or `secondaryTeamId` in the body, or `status=PUBLISHED`: `BadRequestException('Service delivery team is required')` and `BadRequestException('Operations team is required')`. Teams fall back to the client's teams first.
  - If `engineerPaymentType` is sent while the status is not DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, PUBLISHED, WORKDONE or VERIFIED: `BadRequestException('Engineer pay can only be changed before a work order is assigned, or once the work is done. This one is <status>.')`
  - Non-HTTP errors become `BadRequestException('Failed to update work order.')`.

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/:id/engineerPayment**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isUpdate`). `ParseIntPipe` is applied on `:id` (non-integer → 400 `Validation failed (numeric string is expected)`). **No VP.** Role: CLIENT. Purpose: edit engineer pay and engineer expenses.
  - Body `UpdateWorkOrderEngineerPaymentDto` (not enforced):
    - `engineerPaymentType`: `@IsEnum(PaymentType)`, required
    - `engineerFixedAmount`, `engineerHourlyRate`, `engineerAdditionalHourlyRate`, `engineerHalfDayRate`, `engineerFullDayRate`, `engineerMinimumHours`: number, optional
    - `engineerCurrency`: `@IsEnum(CurrencyType)`, optional
    - `engineerExpenses`: `ExpensesDto[]`, optional
    - `deletedEngineerExpenses`: `number[]` (`@IsNumber({}, {each:true})`), optional
  - `NotFoundException('Work order not found')` unless the status is PUBLISHED, ASSIGNED, WORKDONE, VERIFIED, APPROVED or INVOICED in the company
  - Errors are rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/:id/companyExpenses**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isUpdate`), `ParseIntPipe` on `:id`, **no VP**. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: edit company-side expenses.
  - Body `UpdateWorkOrderCompanyExpensesDto` (not enforced):
    - `companyCurrency`: `@IsEnum(CurrencyType)`, optional
    - `companyExpenses`: `ExpensesDto[]`, optional
  - `NotFoundException('Work order not found')` (same status list as above)

- [ ] **PATCH /api/workOrders/timeLog/:id**: JWT only, no VP. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: set additional and non-business hours on the client or engineer side.
  - Query: `type`. `CLIENT` updates the client fields; any other value updates the engineer fields.
  - Body (untyped): `additionalHours` (0 is converted to null), `nonBusinessHourRate`, `nonBusinessHours` (parsed with `parseFloat`)
  - `HttpException('User not found', 404)`

#### Purchase orders / expenses / deliverables / sign-off (file uploads)

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/purchaseOrder**: JWT only, no VP. Multipart `FilesInterceptor('files')`: **no maxCount, no fileSize limit, no mimetype filter** (the service does no mimetype check either). Roles: CLIENT, CUSTOMER. Purpose: create or update purchase orders with optional attachments.
  - Body field `purchaseOrders`: JSON string `[{ id?, number, uid }]`. Invalid JSON causes an unhandled `JSON.parse` error (500).
  - Each file's `originalname` must be `<uid>__-<originalFileName>`. The file is matched to a purchase order by `uid`.
  - Reconciles the `PO_PENDING` sub-status and pushes real-time changes.
  - There is no company or ownership check. Service errors are swallowed (logged), and the endpoint then returns an empty body.

- [ ] **DELETE /api/workOrders/:id/purchaseOrder/:poId**: JWT only. Purpose: delete a purchase order and its S3 attachment.
  - `BadRequestException('Purchase Order not found.')`
  - There is no ownership check.

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/expenseAttachments**: JWT only, no VP. `FilesInterceptor('files')`: no maxCount, size or type limits. Purpose: attach files to existing expenses.
  - Body field `expenses`: JSON string `[{ expenseKey }]`
  - Each file's `originalname` must be `<expenseKey>__-<name>`. Expenses that don't match are skipped (null).
  - Errors are swallowed.

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/:id/deliverables**: JWT only, no VP. `FilesInterceptor('files', 5)`: **max 5 files**, no size or mimetype limit. Role: FREELANCER (the assigned engineer).
  - Controller: `BadRequestException('Work order id is missing.')`
  - Controller: `BadRequestException('No files uploaded.')`
  - `validateEngineerAndStatus` throws:
    - `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)`
    - `HttpException('Only the assigned engineer can perform this action', 403)`
    - `HttpException('Action not allowed!', 400)` unless the status is at or after INPROGRESS in `statusOrder` (INPROGRESS, INCOMPLETE, CANCELLED, WORKDONE, APPROVED, PAID)
  - These are wrapped as `HttpException(error.message || 'Failed to upload deliverables', error.status || 500)`.

- [ ] **DELETE /api/workOrders/deliverables/:assetId**: JWT only. Role: FREELANCER (the assigned engineer). Purpose: delete a deliverable.
  - `HttpException('Deliverable not found', 404)`
  - The same `validateEngineerAndStatus` errors as above

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/signOffSheet**: JWT only. **VP applied** (`new ValidationPipe({ transform: true })`). Multipart `FileFieldsInterceptor`: `engineerSignatureFile` (maxCount 1), `clientSignatureFile` (maxCount 1), `physicalSheetFile` (maxCount 1); no size or mimetype limits. Roles: FREELANCER, CLIENT. Purpose: create or update (upsert by `workOrderId`) the sign-off sheet.
  - Body `CreateSignOffSheetDto`:
    - `workOrderId`: **required** `@IsInt`, transformed with `parseInt`
    - `id`, `engineerId`, `clientId`: optional `@IsInt`, transformed with `parseInt`
    - `engineerComment`, `clientComment`, `clientName`, `clientDesignation`: optional `@IsString`
  - The service has no explicit exceptions. Upload helpers throw `BadRequestException('File is required')` only when called without a file.
  - There is no ownership check.

#### Listing / search / details

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/engineer**: JWT only, no VP. Role: FREELANCER. Purpose: the engineer's own work-order list.
  - Body (untyped): `page` (default 1), `limit` (default 25), `search`, `latitude`, `longitude`, `status`, `categoryId`, `vendorId`, `paymentType`, `isCounter`, plus the `getFilterParamsFromQuery` filters `subCategoryIds`, `minRate`, `maxRate`, `startDate`, `endDate`, `createStartDate`, `createEndDate`, `minRequests`, `maxRequests`
  - Every error is rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, 404)`.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders**: JWT + Perm(`WORK_ORDERS:isRead`). **No VP**, so the query DTO is not validated. Role: CLIENT. Purpose: company work-order board. Returns `{ workOrders, totalCount, statusCounts }`.
  - Query `FilterWorkOrderDto` (extends `ColumnSelectionDto`):
    - `columns?` (comma-list → array via `toColumnList`; the transform only runs when a pipe is present)
    - `status?`, `latitude?` (`@IsDecimal`), `longitude?` (`@IsDecimal`), `radius?` (`@IsNumber`), `workOrderId?`, `limit?` (`@IsNumberString`), `page?` (`@IsNumber`), `search?`
    - `categoryId?`, `vendorId?`, `subCategoryIds?`, `role?`
    - `startDate?`, `endDate?`, `createStartDate?`, `createEndDate?` (`@Type(Date) @IsDate`)
    - `minRate?`, `maxRate?`, `paymentType?`, `minRequests?`, `maxRequests?`
    - `teamIds?`, `clientIds?`, `teamId?`, `clientId?`, `projectId?`, `providerId?`, `country?`, `city?`, `createdById?` (`@IsNumber`), `id?`, `currency?`, `engineerCurrency?`
    - `sortBy?` (`createdAt`, `orderStartDate`, `orderEndDate` or `orderStartTime`), `sortOrder?` (`asc` or `desc`)
    - `canceledBy?`, `cancellationReason?`
  - `HttpException('User not found', 404)`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/customer**: JWT + Perm(`CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS:isRead`), no VP. Roles: CUSTOMER and client users on the customer board. Same query as `GET /workOrders`.
  - `HttpException('User not found', 404)`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/countries**: JWT only. Purpose: distinct countries across the user's work orders.
  - `HttpException('User not found', 404)`
  - `HttpException('Failed to fetch work order countries', 500)`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/maxRate**: **No guard (public).** Purpose: the highest rate for a payment type.
  - Query: `paymentType`
  - `BadRequestException('Invalid payment type')` if the value is not in the `PaymentType` enum. Only HOURLY, FIXED, HALF_DAY and FULL_DAY are mapped; other valid enum values (such as FTH) hit an undefined field.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/maxOffersCount**: **No guard (public).** Purpose: the maximum offer count on any non-deleted work order (offers from deleted users are excluded).

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/statusCounts**: JWT only, no VP. Purpose: counts per status.
  - Query `FilterFindJobDto` (not enforced; its `status` has no `@IsOptional`)
  - `HttpException('User not found', 404)`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/findJobs**: JWT only, no VP. Role: FREELANCER. Purpose: marketplace search within a radius. Members of an ACCEPTED freelance company get `{ totalCount: 0, workOrders: [] }`.
  - Query `FilterFindJobDto`: `latitude`, `longitude` (`@IsDecimal`), `radius` (km, `@IsNumber`), `limit` (default 10), `page` (default 1), `search`, `role`, the same filter set as `FilterWorkOrderDto` (no `sortBy`/`sortOrder`), `status` (no `@IsOptional`), and `isCoverageApplied`
  - Every error is rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, 404)`.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/jobsCount**: JWT only, no VP. Query `FilterFindJobDto`. Purpose: total job count.
  - `getPublishedJobsCount` throws `BadRequestException('Invalid location or radius')` when lat, lng or radius is missing or NaN.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/providersCount**: JWT only, no VP. Query `FilterFindJobDto`. Purpose: count of providers.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/documents**: JWT only. Purpose: the user's work-order documents, delegated to `digitalAssetService.findUserWorkOrderDocuments`.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/:id**: JWT only. Roles: all. Purpose: work-order details, with role-based masking. CUSTOMER users are scoped to their clients; freelance-company members see only routed work orders; engineers without access get their snapshot.
  - Query: `referenceId` (optional boolean, validated with `ParseBoolPipe({ optional: true })`; any other value → 400). When true, `:id` is treated as a reference id.
  - `NotFoundException('Work Order with this referenceId not found')`
  - Declined offer: `NotFoundException('Work Order with id <ref> is no longer accessible')`. The code reads `referenceId` from a null object here, so this surfaces as a BadRequest TypeError (bug).
  - `NotFoundException('This work order is no longer available')`
  - `NotFoundException('Work Order not found or access denied.')` when the message contains 'Work Order not found'
  - Other errors: `BadRequestException(error.message || 'An unexpected error occurred.')`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/actions/allowed/:id**: JWT only, `ParseIntPipe` on `:id`. Purpose: the actions the current user may take on the work order.
  - The service throws plain `Error('Invalid workOrderId: <id>')` and `Error('Invalid userId: <id>')`.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/estimatedPayment/:id**: JWT only. Purpose: calculate the payment breakdown for the current user.
  - Errors are rethrown as `HttpException(error.message, error.status)`.
  - Internal helpers can throw `HttpException('Work Order not found', 404)`, `UnauthorizedException()` (role lookup), `HttpException('Penalty record not found for the canceled work order.', 404)`, and plain `Error('Invalid payment type')`.

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/download/deliverables/:id**: JWT only. Purpose: ZIP of deliverables (`application/zip` StreamableFile). Access follows `findOne`.
  - `BadRequestException('Work order has no deliverables.')`

- [ ] **GET /api/workOrders/download/timesheet/:id**: JWT only. Purpose: timesheet PDF (StreamableFile).
  - `BadRequestException('No timesheet data found.')`

- [ ] **POST /api/workOrders/convertImagesToBase64**: JWT only, no VP. Purpose: fetch images and return them as base64.
  - Body: array of `ConvertImagesDto` `{ id?: string|number, filename?: string, url?: string, type?: string }` (all optional, not enforced)
  - The server fetches arbitrary `url` values (SSRF surface).

#### Notes
- Route order is safe: static paths (`countries`, `maxRate`, `documents`, and so on) are declared before `GET :id`.
- Routes with **VP**: POST `/`, PATCH `:id/subStatus`, PATCH `:id/status`, POST `incomplete`, POST `signOffSheet`. No other route validates its DTO.
- Upload limits: only `deliverables` (max 5 files) and `signOffSheet` (1 file per field) cap the file count. No work-order upload sets a `fileSize` limit or a mimetype filter.
- DeviceMiddleware runs on every route here (global). It reads the `x-device-*` headers and populates `req.deviceInfo`, which these handlers don't use.


---

# Backend — Business Rules

Testable business rules for the TDP backend (NestJS/Prisma). Each item cites its source as `(path:line)`; paths are relative to `src/` unless prefixed `prisma/` or `../prisma/`. Items marked BUG/CHECK/gap/NOTE record suspicious behaviour found while reading the code.

## 1. Work Orders

### Work Order Status Model (enums, sequence, real-time)

- [ ] WorkOrderStatus enum: DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, PUBLISHED, ROUTED, ASSIGNED, START_TIME_SET, INPROGRESS, CONFIRM, INCOMPLETE, CANCELLED, CANCELLED_PAYABLE, AT_RISK, DELAYED, ON_HOLD, ON_MY_WAY, CHECK_IN, CHECK_OUT, WORKDONE, VERIFIED, APPROVED, INVOICED, PAID, DISPUTED; default status PUBLISHED (prisma/schema.prisma:527-556,1816)
- [ ] WorkOrderSubStatus enum: NOT_CONFIRMED, CONFIRMED, NOT_READY, READY_TO_GO, ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE, CANCELLED (assigned phase); CHECK_IN, CHECK_OUT, REVIEWED, PO_PENDING, CANCELLED_PAYABLE (in-progress/post phase) (prisma/schema.prisma:558-573)
- [ ] OfferStatus enum: PENDING, ASSIGNED, REJECTED (client rejected/hidden), COUNTER, DECLINED (engineer not interested), ROUTED, ACCEPTED, PAID, CANCELLED (engineer cancelled after assignment) (prisma/schema.prisma:575-585)
- [ ] PaymentType enum: HOURLY, FULL_DAY, HALF_DAY, FIXED, FTH (first two hours), CUSTOM_HOURS, PER_DEVICE, BLENDED (prisma/schema.prisma:397-406)
- [ ] `customerStatus`/`customerSubStatus` are nullable and track the client-billing side: null until approval → APPROVED (approve) → INVOICED (invoice) → PAID (invoice paid); CANCELLED_PAYABLE (+customerSubStatus CANCELLED) via markPayable (prisma/schema.prisma:1817-1819; modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8682,8819-8820; modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:209,1360,2479)
- [ ] Status ordering used for "at/after" checks: DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, PUBLISHED, ROUTED, ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, INCOMPLETE, CANCELLED, WORKDONE, APPROVED, PAID (VERIFIED/INVOICED absent) (utils/variables.ts:4-16)
- [ ] FULL_DAY_HOURS = 8, HALF_DAY_HOURS = 4 (utils/variables.ts:18-19)
- [ ] WorkOrder.referenceId is a unique Int from Postgres sequence `workorder_ref_seq` starting at 10000, increment 1 (displayed as "WO-<referenceId>") (prisma/schema.prisma:1803; prisma/migrations/20250324055030_init/migration.sql:511-522)
- [ ] TeamTask.referenceId defaults to 6-digit zero-padded value from `team_task_reference_seq` starting at 110101 (prisma/migrations/20260313112741_add_reference_id/migration.sql:30-60)
- [ ] Happy path: (DRAFT →) PUBLISHED [customer: PENDING_APPROVAL → PUBLISHED] → offers (ROUTED/ACCEPTED) → ASSIGNED (sub NOT_CONFIRMED/CONFIRMED/ON_THE_WAY/RUNNING_LATE) → INPROGRESS (check-in) → WORKDONE (mark complete) → sub REVIEWED → VERIFIED (sub PO_PENDING if PO required) → APPROVED (+customerStatus APPROVED, Payment created) → INVOICED → PAID (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:706-710,8006-8104,8476-8765)
- [ ] Side paths: WORKDONE → INCOMPLETE (mark incomplete) → WORKDONE again; ASSIGNED → CANCELLED (client or engineer) → customerStatus CANCELLED_PAYABLE; APPROVED → WORKDONE (revert); any → PUBLISHED (republish by creator); PUBLISHED/PENDING_APPROVAL → DRAFT (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6328,6979,8105,8239,8767,9822,10173)
- [ ] Socket connection without handshake auth userId+role is disconnected (modules/work-orders/work-orders.gateway.ts:31-42)
- [ ] `workOrder:published` is emitted to users whose personal address is within 50 km of the WO address, plus owners of freelance companies with an ACCEPTED member within 50 km; skipped if WO has no lat/lng (modules/work-orders/work-orders.gateway.ts:76-152)
- [ ] `workOrder:update:<id>`: assigned engineer gets full payload (accessRevoked:false); other engineers with offers get accessRevoked:true + "assigned to someone else" and are removed from the WO room; creating CLIENT gets client payload (modules/work-orders/work-orders.gateway.ts:154-194)
- [ ] `workOrder:list:update` and `workOrder:list:remove` are emitted to `user:<id>` rooms (modules/work-orders/work-orders.gateway.ts:206-216)

### Work Orders — Creation, Draft & Publish

- [ ] Only users with role CLIENT or CUSTOMER can create a work order; others get 403 "You are not authorized to create work order" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:505-519)
- [ ] `isDraft === true` saves as status DRAFT and skips completeness checks (teams, location) which are re-run on publish (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:496,559-568,706-707)
- [ ] CUSTOMER non-draft create without `clientId` → 400 "Please select a client" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:524-526)
- [ ] CUSTOMER may only use a `clientId` whose `parentClientId` equals the customer's `user.customerId` (child client), even for drafts → 403 "Invalid client selected" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:531-542)
- [ ] Missing teamId/secondaryTeamId are inherited from the selected client's `teamId`/`secondaryTeamId` (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:547-557)
- [ ] Non-draft create requires Service delivery team (teamId) → 400 "Service delivery team is required" and Operations team (secondaryTeamId) → 400 "Operations team is required" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:561-568)
- [ ] Non-draft create requires a location: addressId OR isRemote OR any non-blank location field, else 400 "Location is required" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:575-597)
- [ ] New address (type WORK_ORDER) created when no addressId, isRemote===false and location data exists; a remote address row `{isRemote:true}` created when isRemote (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:599-625)
- [ ] Address inputs: empty strings normalised to null; latitude/longitude coerced to finite numbers or null (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1441-1454)
- [ ] orderEndDate is saved equal to orderStartDate on create (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:656)
- [ ] `serviceFeePercentage` is stamped from env SERVICE_FEE at creation (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:665)
- [ ] CUSTOMER-created ticket has workOrderManagerId = null (client assigns on publish) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:663)
- [ ] `minimumHours` defaults to 1 unless >1 supplied; `engineerMinimumHours` defaults to 0; additionalHours/engineerAdditionalHours start at 0 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:680-699)
- [ ] paymentType/currency/engineerPaymentType empty values fall back to DB defaults (paymentType default HOURLY) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:669-686,1090-1092)
- [ ] engineerFthRate entered on create is persisted (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:696)
- [ ] Initial status: DRAFT if draft; PENDING_APPROVAL if CUSTOMER; else PUBLISHED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:706-710)
- [ ] On create: work-order services, contacts, engineer expenses (isEngineerExpense=true), client expenses, tasks (+ a CompletedTask row isCompleted=false per task), shipments, and customerFiles (digital assets re-linked) are created in the same transaction (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:717-781,1393-1433)
- [ ] Creation writes a WorkOrder creation log (old statuses null) in the same transaction (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:789-801)
- [ ] Duplicate shipment tracking number → 409 "Tracking number already exists"; Prisma validation error → 400 "Validation failed"; other errors → 503 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:809-820)
- [ ] Draft create emits nothing (no broadcast, no email, no chat room) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:827-829)
- [ ] CUSTOMER non-draft create: notify company owner + WO manager (excluding sender) "New ticket pending review", email the parent client's `teamEmail` ("New Work Order Submitted: WO-<ref>"), and best-effort create customer↔team chat room for both teams (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:831-847,857-941)
- [ ] Pending-ticket notification names the parent customer's clientName, falling back to sender full name, then "A customer" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:873-883)
- [ ] CLIENT non-draft create: sends published notification to engineers within 50 (km) and broadcasts `workOrderPublished` over socket (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:848-852)
- [ ] assertCustomerSubmittable requires title, entity (clientId), location (addressId), schedule type, start date, start time, time zone, and payment (paymentType + currency + positive rate for HOURLY/HALF_DAY/FULL_DAY/FIXED/FTH) → 400 "Cannot submit — please complete: …" with `missingFields` (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:960-1012)
- [ ] assertCustomerSubmittable: if teamId or secondaryTeamId still missing → 400 "This entity has no service delivery or operations team set up yet…" (not listed as missing fields) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1019-1028)
- [ ] assertPublishable requires client, technology stack (categoryId), schedule type, start date, start time, time zone, location, payment (type+currency+positive rate field for HOURLY→hourlyRate, HALF_DAY→halfDayRate, FULL_DAY→fullDayRate, FIXED→fixedAmount, FTH→fthRate); teams only when `requireTeams` → 400 "Cannot publish — please complete: …" + `missingFields` (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1031-1110)
- [ ] Payment types other than HOURLY/HALF_DAY/FULL_DAY/FIXED/FTH (e.g. CUSTOM_HOURS, PER_DEVICE, BLENDED) have no single-rate positivity check on publish (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1079-1087)

### Work Orders — Approve & Publish customer ticket (PENDING_APPROVAL)

- [ ] Caller must be a company user; WO must belong to caller's company and be PENDING_APPROVAL else 404 "Work Order not found or not pending approval" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1112-1124)
- [ ] assertPublishable (without team requirement) runs before publishing (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1126)
- [ ] Publish is an atomic updateMany guarded on status PENDING_APPROVAL; count 0 → 409 "Work Order already processed" (double-publish race) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1128-1144)
- [ ] If WO has no workOrderManagerId, the publishing user becomes the manager (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1139)
- [ ] Writes WORK_ORDER_STATUS_UPDATED log PENDING_APPROVAL→PUBLISHED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1146-1176)
- [ ] After publish: 50-radius published notification + socket broadcast, and customer creator (if not the publisher) notified "Your work order has been accepted." (WORK_ORDER_PUBLISHED) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1183-1203)

### Work Orders — Expenses, Shipments, Purchase Orders

- [ ] Expense upsert is scoped by (isEngineerExpense, isCompanyExpense); client expenses match those added by CLIENT, CUSTOMER or null addedBy (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1217-1250)
- [ ] Incoming expense with missing/non-numeric/unknown id is created; known id updated; existing ones absent from payload are deleted along with their attachment DigitalAsset and S3 object (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1257-1325)
- [ ] Saved expenses are always `isApproved: true`, amount coerced to Number or 0 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1261-1271)
- [ ] Expense attachments are matched by `expenseKey` and uploaded/linked per expense (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1333-1380)
- [ ] Purchase orders: existing id → update number (+replace file if uploaded), else create; logs WORK_ORDER_PO_UPDATED vs WORK_ORDER_PO_ADDED with old/new number+file (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1625-1737)
- [ ] After PO batch save/delete, PO_PENDING sub-status is reconciled once and real-time changes pushed (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1752-1753,1819-1820)
- [ ] Delete PO: must belong to the WO else 400 "Purchase Order not found."; deletes attachment asset + S3 and logs WORK_ORDER_PO_DELETED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1764-1826)
- [ ] A WO "has a PO" if purchaseOrderNumber non-blank OR any PurchaseOrder has a non-blank number or a digitalAssetId (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1828-1836)
- [ ] Customer PO/expense-only edit window = status WORKDONE or VERIFIED, or customerStatus APPROVED; only `expenses` and `purchaseOrderNumber` fields allowed (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:121-127,1838-1846)
- [ ] syncPoPendingSubStatus: if WO has a PO and subStatus is PO_PENDING → clears subStatus to null (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1878-1881)
- [ ] syncPoPendingSubStatus: if no PO AND (status VERIFIED or customerStatus APPROVED/CANCELLED_PAYABLE) AND project.isPoRequired AND subStatus empty → sets PO_PENDING; logs the change (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1872-1917)

### Work Orders — Sub-status / Status manual updates, Notes

- [ ] updateWorkOrderSubStatus: any caller can set subStatus on an existing WO (no role check); 404 if missing; logs + pushes socket update (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1472-1523)
- [ ] Setting subStatus CONFIRMED notifies both team members + assigned freelance company owner (WORK_ORDER_CONFIRMED "<engineer> has confirmed the work order.") and the customer ("The engineer has confirmed your work order.") (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1525-1556)
- [ ] updateWorkOrderStatus: WO must belong to caller's company; sets arbitrary status; logs + push (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1561-1624)
- [ ] pushRealTimeChanges sends client payload to creator and per-engineer payload (work order + allowed actions) to every engineer who has an offer (or the explicit list); engineers without access are skipped (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1920-1997)
- [ ] addNote: user must be non-deleted with a companyId and WO in same company; logs WORK_ORDER_NOTE_UPDATED if prior notes non-blank else WORK_ORDER_NOTE_ADDED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:1999-2064)
- [ ] Deliverables zip: 400 "Work order has no deliverables." if none (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2066-2081)

### Work Orders — Listing, Scoping & Status Counts

- [ ] findAll (company list): scoped to caller's companyId and isDeleted=false; default limit 50, page 1; default sort orderStartDate asc, orderStartTime asc; custom sortBy (comma list) with sortOrder asc else desc (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2083-2139)
- [ ] Client filter IDs that are parent clients (isParentClient, not deleted) are expanded to include all their child client IDs (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2113-2123,2499-2514,2932-2942)
- [ ] When `role=CLIENT` is passed, status filtering and counts use `customerStatus`; otherwise `status` (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2141-2157,2198-2203)
- [ ] Rate / engineer currency / provider filters are AND-combined via mergeFilters (not spread) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2182-2186)
- [ ] minRequests/maxRequests filter on offer count (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2189-2196)
- [ ] findAll statusCounts computed over unfiltered company base and add CUSTOMER_APPROVED / CUSTOMER_INVOICED from customerStatus counts (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2245-2440)
- [ ] Per-row counters: countered = PENDING counter offers; requested = PENDING non-counter offers; routed = ROUTED or ACCEPTED offers (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2357-2367,2737-2750)
- [ ] If WO assigned to a freelance company, the company owner is presented as `engineer`/`engineerId` with `engineerCompany` (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2370-2374,2753-2757)
- [ ] messageCount = total chat messages across WO chat rooms; unreadMessageCount = unread MESSAGE notifications for the caller on that WO (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2299-2387)
- [ ] Offer/message/PO lookups only run when a column needing them is visible; hidden columns are stripped from the response (pickColumns) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2268-2273,2426-2433)
- [ ] Time logs in list rows sorted by checkInTime then checkOutTime ascending (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2408-2420)
- [ ] findAllClientWorkorders: user with customerId is scoped to that client (+ its child clients if it is a parent) when no explicit client filter (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2469-2484,2552-2559)
- [ ] CUSTOMER users additionally see their own entity-less drafts (status DRAFT, createdById = self, clientId null) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2538-2546)
- [ ] CUSTOMER status filter: if all requested statuses are in {APPROVED, INVOICED, PAID} filter on customerStatus, otherwise on status (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2464-2468,2572-2601)
- [ ] findAllClientWorkorders without status and without role CLIENT excludes CANCELLED by default (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2583-2593)
- [ ] For CUSTOMER the WORKDONE count = status in (WORKDONE, VERIFIED) AND customerStatus null; VERIFIED bucket removed; APPROVED/INVOICED/PAID counts come from customerStatus (default 0) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2707-2716,2841-2850)
- [ ] findAllWorkOrderCountries returns distinct non-empty address countries of company's non-deleted WOs (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2856-2897)
- [ ] getWorkOrderStatusCounts: non-CLIENT role counts status plus customerStatus when different from status and not APPROVED; CUSTOMER buckets APPROVED/INVOICED/PAID from customerStatus and WORKDONE = WORKDONE|VERIFIED with null customerStatus (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:2898-3106)

### Engineer Discovery — My Jobs (findAllFreelancerWorkOrders)

- [ ] Engineer who is an ACCEPTED member of a freelance company ("company member") has restricted views; engineer who owns a company sees work of its ACCEPTED members (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3136-3155)
- [ ] Numeric search matches `referenceId` exactly; otherwise case-insensitive title contains (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3157-3174)
- [ ] "All" tab (status ''): non-DRAFT WOs where the caller's offer is not DECLINED; company members: non-DRAFT/non-PUBLISHED WOs where referredEngineerId = caller and offer not DECLINED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3184-3234)
- [ ] PENDING tab: non-DRAFT WOs with caller offers in PENDING or REJECTED, split by isCounter (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3235-3247)
- [ ] ASSIGNED tab: WO status ASSIGNED with engineerId = caller (or assignedFreelanceCompanyId = caller's owned company); offer ASSIGNED by userId (or referredEngineerId for members) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3248-3258)
- [ ] ROUTED tab: non-DRAFT WOs with caller offer ROUTED or ACCEPTED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3259-3270)
- [ ] INPROGRESS / WORKDONE (WORKDONE+VERIFIED) / INCOMPLETE tabs require an ASSIGNED offer by caller (or referred) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3272-3323)
- [ ] APPROVED tab = APPROVED+INVOICED (members also PAID, with offers ASSIGNED or PAID); PAID tab = WO PAID with offer PAID (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3276-3305)
- [ ] DECLINED tab = PUBLISHED WOs with caller's DECLINED offer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3324-3333)
- [ ] Company members get empty results for AVAILABLE, ROUTED, VERIFIED, PAID tabs; AVAILABLE always returns empty on this endpoint (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3335-3350)
- [ ] Engineer-facing status mapping for the assigned engineer: VERIFIED→WORKDONE, INVOICED→APPROVED, (company member) PAID→APPROVED; sub-status REVIEWED/PO_PENDING hidden (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3436-3462)
- [ ] Non-assigned engineers always see the WO as PUBLISHED with no sub-status (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3463-3466)
- [ ] If a WorkOrderSnapshot exists for (WO, caller) and its status matches the current WO status, the snapshot data is returned instead of live data (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3411-3480)
- [ ] Each job row includes calculated payment, distance from the engineer's lat/lng, the caller's own offer only, grouped tasks (Prep/On Site/Post) and totalOffers (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3503-3526)
- [ ] Task grouping: START_TIME→Prep, CLOSE_NOTES→Post, everything else→On Site (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4284-4307)

### Engineer Discovery — Find Jobs (radius search)

- [ ] ACCEPTED freelance company members cannot search jobs (always 0 results) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3696-3702)
- [ ] Radius param is in km (×1000 → metres); default limit 10, page 1; results sorted createdAt desc (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3704-3709,3762)
- [ ] Only PUBLISHED, non-deleted WOs where the caller has no offer are returned (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3741-3748)
- [ ] Company owner (no `isCoverageApplied=true`): coverage = union of radius circles around every ACCEPTED member's personal address; owner with no located members sees nothing (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3641-3692,3728-3740)
- [ ] `isCoverageApplied=true` restricts to the supplied lat/lng radius only (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3730-3733)
- [ ] Find-job rows' totalOffers = number of PENDING offers (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3782-3783)
- [ ] getPublishedJobsCount rejects missing/NaN lat/lng/radius → 400 "Invalid location or radius" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3875-3877)
- [ ] getMaxRateForPaymentType: invalid type → 400 "Invalid payment type"; max of hourlyRate/fixedAmount/halfDayRate/fullDayRate over non-deleted WOs of that type (other types have no field mapping) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3814-3842)
- [ ] getMaxOffersCount = max number of offers (from non-deleted users) on any non-deleted WO (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3843-3862)
- [ ] Job tab counts: AVAILABLE (radius count, 0 for members), COUNTERED/REQUESTED (PENDING+REJECTED offers, counter vs not), ROUTED (ROUTED+ACCEPTED), ASSIGNED/INPROGRESS (engineer or owned-company), WORKDONE (WORKDONE+VERIFIED), INCOMPLETE, VERIFIED (0 for members), APPROVED (APPROVED+INVOICED, +PAID for members), PAID (0 for members), ALL (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:3931-4164)
- [ ] Provider tab counts for a WO: NEARBY / TALENTPOOL / WORKEDWITH via findNearbyUsers modes; PENDING_ASSIGNMENT = PENDING+ROUTED+ACCEPTED offers by non-deleted users; ASSIGNED = ASSIGNED+PAID+CANCELLED offers; REJECTED offers (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4166-4234)
- [ ] CLOSE_NOTES view merges stored close notes with incomplete reasons (type INCOMPLETE_TASK) sorted by createdAt (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4236-4282)
- [ ] Total hours = sum of timeLog.totalHours rounded to 2 dp (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4308-4315)

### Work Order Detail Access & Visibility (findOne)

- [ ] `findOne` by referenceId resolves non-deleted WO else 404 "Work Order with this referenceId not found" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4348-4357)
- [ ] ACCEPTED freelance-company members can only open WOs where an offer (userId or referredEngineerId = them) is ASSIGNED/PAID and WO status in ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, WORKDONE, INCOMPLETE, VERIFIED, APPROVED, INVOICED, PAID, CANCELLED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4366-4392)
- [ ] Other users can open a WO if: same companyId; OR status PUBLISHED; OR status in ASSIGNED..CANCELLED set and they are the engineer (or same company); OR it is assigned to their owned freelance company; OR they have a ROUTED/ACCEPTED/ASSIGNED offer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4393-4434)
- [ ] CLIENT role is additionally restricted to WOs of their own companyId (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4436-4441)
- [ ] CUSTOMER is restricted to WOs they created or whose clientId is in their scoped clients (own client + children if parent) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4442-4455,5515-5531)
- [ ] Any user with a CANCELLED offer on the WO is denied access (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4456-4464)
- [ ] Detail Expense list shows only approved, non-engineer expenses (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4469-4485)
- [ ] If live access fails but the user sent an offer: DECLINED offer → 404 "no longer accessible"; else return their WorkOrderSnapshot (or any snapshot of that WO for company owners) with VERIFIED→WORKDONE and INVOICED→APPROVED masking (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4513-4552)
- [ ] No access and no snapshot → 404 "This work order is no longer available" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4554)
- [ ] If assigned engineer is an ACCEPTED freelance-company member, response carries `engineerCompany` (id, name, owner) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4557-4586)
- [ ] FREELANCER assigned engineer / company owner: VERIFIED shown as WORKDONE, INVOICED as APPROVED, PAID as APPROVED for company members; REVIEWED and PO_PENDING sub-statuses hidden (null) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4588-4614)
- [ ] Other freelancers (routed, countering) never see subStatus (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4615-4618)
- [ ] Deleted engineer shown as ghost with `isDeletedUser=true` (engineer.isDeleted or id == SYSTEM_USER_ID) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4621-4638)
- [ ] Customer files hidden (empty) from FREELANCERs while WO is DRAFT, PUBLISHED or ROUTED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4712-4726)
- [ ] For company-assigned WOs, offers list keeps ROUTED/ACCEPTED/ASSIGNED offers plus caller's own; otherwise only the caller's own offer (user nulled) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4733-4744)
- [ ] Expense attachments are stripped for roles where hidden (isExpenseAttachmentHiddenForRole), otherwise signed URLs attached (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4750-4758)
- [ ] Sign-off sheet signatures and physical sheet returned as fresh signed URLs (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4760-4775)

### Timesheet PDF & Deliverables

- [ ] Timesheet PDF (A4, timesheet.pdf) excludes task types ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE, MARK_COMPLETE, CHECK_IN, CHECK_OUT, START_TIME, CLOSE_NOTES from task list (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4803-4834,4882-4887)

- [ ] Deliverable upload/delete: only the assigned engineer (403 "Only the assigned engineer can perform this action") and WO status at/after INPROGRESS in statusOrder (INPROGRESS, INCOMPLETE, CANCELLED, WORKDONE, APPROVED, PAID — VERIFIED/INVOICED not in list) else 400 "Action not allowed!" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4904-4927; utils/variables.ts:4-16)
- [ ] Deliverable upload logs DELIVERABLE_UPLOADED; deleting a WORK_ORDER_DELIVERABLE_FILE logs DELIVERABLE_UPLOADED_DELETED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:4948-4961,4991-5006)
- [ ] Qualification sub-types update disconnects removed IDs and connects new ones (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5039-5085)

### Engineer Payment & Company Expense Edits (client side)

- [ ] updateWorkOrderEngineerPayment: company user only; WO in caller's company and status PUBLISHED, ASSIGNED, WORKDONE, VERIFIED, APPROVED or INVOICED else 404 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5086-5113)
- [ ] Engineer expenses are upserted with createdById as owner (isEngineerExpense=true); deletedEngineerExpenses deletes by ids (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5170-5187)
- [ ] If an ASSIGNED offer exists: offer rates (hourly/halfDay/fullDay/fixed/minHours/currency) updated from dto (keeping old when blank), engineerRate = dto rate by WO engineerPaymentType (HOURLY/FTH→hourly, HALF_DAY, FULL_DAY, FIXED), prior values archived to OfferHistory, ENGINEER_PAYMENT_UPDATED log with old/new payment fields + expenses (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5276-5412)
- [ ] If no ASSIGNED offer: all PENDING/ROUTED/ACCEPTED offers (and their OfferHistory and unapproved expenses of those engineers) are deleted and engineers notified "Your offer has been removed due to payment update." + forced socket push (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5415-5470)
