- [ ] After engineer payment edit, PaymentService.updateEngineerPayment recalculates engineer payment (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5203)
- [ ] updateWorkOrderCompanyExpenses: same status gate; optional companyCurrency update; company expenses upserted (isCompanyExpense=true) and Payment.companyTotalExpenseCost re-synced (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5211-5265)

### Work Order Update (edit)

- [ ] Update requires WO in caller's companyId else 404 "Work Order with id X not found" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5554-5600)
- [ ] CUSTOMER may edit only WOs they created or whose clientId is in their scoped clients → 403 "You can only edit work orders you raised or that belong to your clients" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5605-5615)
- [ ] CUSTOMER editing a WO in WORKDONE/VERIFIED or customerStatus APPROVED: all fields except `expenses` and `purchaseOrderNumber` are silently dropped (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5616-5622)
- [ ] Non-DRAFT WO missing teams inherits them from client on save; team fields required only when the update touches teams or publishes (status PUBLISHED) → 400 "Service delivery team is required"/"Operations team is required" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5631-5673)
- [ ] Engineer pay (engineerPaymentType in payload) may only be changed while WO is DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, PUBLISHED, WORKDONE or VERIFIED → 400 "Engineer pay can only be changed before a work order is assigned, or once the work is done…" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5675-5697)
- [ ] Schedule change detection compares date(YYYY-MM-DD)+time(HH:mm) in WO timezone; adding a schedule to a draft that had none counts as change (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5704-5739)
- [ ] Location type EXACT_LOCATION creates a new WORK_ORDER address and takes its timezone; isRemote creates a remote address (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5741-5769)
- [ ] Setting purchaseOrderNumber while subStatus PO_PENDING clears subStatus (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5838-5841)
- [ ] Schedule change while subStatus CONFIRMED resets subStatus to NOT_CONFIRMED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5842-5845)
- [ ] Any engineer pay field actually changing triggers updateWorkOrderOffers (assigned offer re-rated + OfferHistory, or pending/routed/accepted offers deleted and engineers notified) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5854-5875)
- [ ] Schedule change on a PUBLISHED WO deletes all PENDING/COUNTER/ROUTED offers with their OfferHistory, unapproved expenses and WorkOrderSnapshots, and notifies those engineers "The work order schedule has been updated. Your offer has been removed." (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:5940-5978,6101-6111)
- [ ] Schedule change with an assigned engineer notifies engineer "Schedule has been updated to DD/MM/YYYY, hh:mm a. Please confirm." (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6082-6099)
- [ ] Update log type: CUSTOMER_PAYMENT_UPDATED if paymentType given, ENGINEER_PAYMENT_UPDATED if engineerPaymentType given, else WORK_ORDER_UPDATED; running-late/on-the-way/confirmed timestamps excluded from diff (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6033-6071)
- [ ] If payload contains purchaseOrderNumber, PO_PENDING sub-status reconciled after save (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6075-6077)
- [ ] Non-customer edits recalculate customer payment (PaymentService.updateCustomerPayment); customer edits do not (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6079-6081)
- [ ] Non-HTTP errors during update → 400 "Failed to update work order." (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6114-6121)
- [ ] Custom tasks (COMPLETE_CUSTOM_TASK) are diffed: new created (with CompletedTask row), existing updated (title, description, emailsToNotify with blanks removed), missing deleted (completedTask rows first); empty list deletes all custom tasks (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6127-6190)
- [ ] Contacts diffed by id: update existing, create new, delete absent (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6192-6243)
- [ ] Work-order services: if category or vendor changed all services are wiped first; then add new / remove missing (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6245-6318)

### Move to Draft (revert publish)

- [ ] Only PUBLISHED or PENDING_APPROVAL WOs can be moved to DRAFT; already DRAFT → 409 "Work Order Already Drafted"; other statuses → 400 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6328-6364)
- [ ] WO must be in caller's company; CUSTOMER only for own/their clients' WOs → 403 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6350-6378)
- [ ] Moving a PUBLISHED WO to draft removes it from list of every engineer with an offer via socket `removeWorkOrderFromList` (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6423-6431)
- [ ] Team members (except actor) notified "This work order has been moved to draft." (customer actor: "<name> has moved this work order to draft."); customer recipients notified "Your work order has been moved to draft." (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6437-6495)
- [ ] Offers are NOT deleted when moving to draft (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6379-6386)

### Draft → Publish

- [ ] draftToPublishWorkOrder: WO must be DRAFT in caller's company else 404 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6499-6509)
- [ ] CUSTOMER publishing own draft → PENDING_APPROVAL; CLIENT → PUBLISHED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6511-6521)
- [ ] Missing teams inherited from client before validation and persisted on publish (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6523-6568)
- [ ] CUSTOMER draft validated with assertCustomerSubmittable; CLIENT draft with assertPublishable({requireTeams:true}) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6551-6555)
- [ ] Publish → 50 km engineer notification + socket broadcast; customer submit → client team pending-ticket notification/email + customer↔team chat room (best-effort) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6609-6640)
- [ ] Author (if not the publisher) notified "Your work order has been published." or "…submitted for approval." (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6642-6661)
- [ ] Customer recipients of a WO = non-deleted CUSTOMER users whose customerId = WO client or its parent client; actor excluded (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6675-6737)

### Time Log Adjustments (additional / non-business hours)

- [ ] updateWorkOrderTimeLog `type=CLIENT` sets additionalHours (0 → null), nonBusinessHourRate, nonBusinessHours; logs CUSTOMER_TIMESHEET_UPDATED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6781-6824)
- [ ] `type=FREELANCER` sets engineerAdditionalHours (0 → null), engineerNonBusinessHourRate, engineerNonBusinessHours; logs ENGINEER_TIMESHEET_UPDATED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6825-6868)
- [ ] Time-log adjustments are scoped to WO companyId = caller's companyId (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6765-6787)

### Delete & Revert to Work Done

- [ ] Only a DRAFT WO in caller's company can be deleted (soft delete isDeleted=true); CUSTOMER only drafts they created; otherwise 403 "You are not allowed to perform this action"; logs WORK_ORDER_DELETED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6873-6934)
- [ ] revertToWorkDoneState: CLIENT only ("you don't has access" 400); WO must have status APPROVED and customerStatus APPROVED in caller's company else 404 "This work order cannot be revert back" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:6979-7013)
- [ ] Revert requires engineer's ASSIGNED/PAID offer; in a transaction: deletes Payment, sets offer ASSIGNED, WO status WORKDONE with customerStatus/subStatus null, deletes first OfferHistory row, reverts engineer skills, deletes engineer snapshot, logs status change (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7015-7088)

### Payment Calculation — Client Side

- [ ] Payment view "authUser": if engineer assigned and requester is engineer or WO creator, rates computed for the engineer; otherwise for requester (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7117-7123)
- [ ] Payment view expenses = approved, non-company expenses added by the WO creator (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7163-7191)
- [ ] Engineer offer rate lookup matches offers where userId or referredEngineerId = engineer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7251-7271)
- [ ] Engineer minimum hours = offer.minimumHours if set else WO.engineerMinimumHours; engineer additional hourly rate = offer.additionalHourlyRate if set else WO.engineerAdditionalHourlyRate (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7297-7338)
- [ ] Service fee = penalty × serviceFeePercentage/100 when penalty>0, else totalWorkOrderCost × parseInt(serviceFeePercentage)/100 (integer percent) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7340-7350)
- [ ] getPenaltyIfCanceled always returns 0 (cancelled by engineer or client); 404 thrown internally if CANCELLED WO has no cancel record (swallowed) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7352-7375)
- [ ] Grand total: CANCELLED by CLIENT → totalWorkOrderCost + penalty + serviceFee; FREELANCER viewer → totalWorkOrderCost − serviceFee; others → totalWorkOrderCost + serviceFee (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7377-7399)
- [ ] Client rates per payment type: HOURLY→hourlyRate+minimumHours; HALF_DAY→halfDayRate+hourlyRate (overtime); FTH→fthRate+hourlyRate; CUSTOM_HOURS→customHoursRate+hourlyRate; FULL_DAY→fullDayRate+hourlyRate; FIXED→fixedAmount (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7409-7431)
- [ ] Client payment response includes totalNonBusinessPayment = nonBusinessHours × nonBusinessHourRate, and message "Cancellation fee applied…" when penalty>0 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7469-7505)
- [ ] Engineer current rates from user billingRates (HOURLY, HALF_DAY, FULL_DAY) and currency of first billing rate; null set when none (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7507-7527)

### Payment Calculation — Engineer Side

- [ ] Engineer expense list = the engineer's own non-company expenses + all engineer expenses (isEngineerExpense=true, added by client) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7529-7574)
- [ ] Company expenses (isCompanyExpense=true) sorted by createdAt asc; companyTotalExpenseCost = sum(amount) persisted on Payment via updateMany (no-op if no Payment yet) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7576-7649)
- [ ] Expense attachments hidden only from FREELANCER viewers; CLIENT, CUSTOMER, ADMIN, MODERATOR see receipts (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7618-7627)
- [ ] Engineer rates are computed for the WO's engineer when viewer is CLIENT, else for the viewer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7652)
- [ ] Freelance company owners have no personal billing-rate fallback (all null) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7654-7663)
- [ ] Engineer rate precedence per type: offer rate → WO engineer rate → engineer's billing rate (HOURLY/FTH hourly; HALF_DAY half-day + hourly; FULL_DAY full-day + hourly); FIXED: offer fixedAmount → WO engineerFixedAmount (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7674-7715)
- [ ] Engineer currency precedence: offer.engineerCurrency → WO.engineerCurrency → billing-rate currency (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7728-7729)
- [ ] Engineer grand total = totalWorkOrderCost − serviceFee; CANCELLED WO shows totalWorkOrderCost 0 and grandTotal 0 for engineer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7738-7775)
- [ ] Engineer additionalHourlyRate reported = engineerAdditionalHourlyRate for HOURLY, else calculated overtime rate (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7756-7759)
- [ ] Engineer totalNonBusinessPayment = engineerNonBusinessHours × engineerNonBusinessHourRate (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7773-7774)
- [ ] Payment endpoint response: CLIENT gets engineerRates + clientRates + companyExpenses(currency, list, total); CUSTOMER gets only clientRates (with attachments); FREELANCER gets engineerRates with attachments stripped (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7784-7824)
- [ ] Engineer calc dispatch: FULL_DAY, HALF_DAY, HOURLY, FTH (uses hourly calc), FIXED; any other engineer type (CUSTOM_HOURS, PER_DEVICE, BLENDED) → Error "Invalid payment type" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7951-7975)
- [ ] Client calc dispatch: FULL_DAY, HALF_DAY, FTH, CUSTOM_HOURS, HOURLY, FIXED; PER_DEVICE/BLENDED → Error "Invalid payment type" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7977-8004)
- [ ] Legacy calculateWorkOrderPayment uses ASSIGNED offer rates and env SERVICE_FEE (FREELANCER: total − fee; others: total + fee) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7826-7949)

### Complete / Incomplete / Republish

- [ ] Mark complete: WO must be INPROGRESS or INCOMPLETE (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8009-8014)
- [ ] FREELANCER cannot complete while any COMPLETE_CUSTOM_TASK is not completed (no completedTask row or isCompleted=false) → 400 "Complete the remaining task(s) before marking this work order complete: <titles>"; client may bypass (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8016-8043)
- [ ] Mark complete sets status WORKDONE, subStatus null, stores hoursWorked (client calc) and engineerHoursWorked (engineer calc); logs and pushes socket update (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8045-8096)
- [ ] Republish: only the WO creator (createdById) → 404 otherwise; deletes the engineer's expenses, resets all CompletedTask rows (isCompleted=false, userId/text null), sets status PUBLISHED, engineerId null, assignedFreelanceCompanyId null, subStatus null; logs (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8105-8163)
- [ ] Republish fails 404 "No tasks found for the given workOrderId." when the WO has no tasks (surfaced as 500 wrapper) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8193-8237)
- [ ] Mark incomplete: WO must be WORKDONE else 404 "Work Order is in process"; sets INCOMPLETE and clears subStatus; creates IncompleteWorkOrderReason {workOrderId, reasonType, reason, userId}; notifies engineer "<reasonType>: <reason>" and customer "Your work order has been marked incomplete." (WORK_ORDER_INCOMPLETE); pushes engineer list update (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8239-8318)

### Skills Verification on Approval

- [ ] addSkillsToEngineer: for each WO service subcategory, existing verified skill untouched, unverified skill set isVerified=true, missing skill created verified with addedById (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8320-8367)
- [ ] revertSkillsFromEngineer disconnects the WO's service subcategories from engineer.skills (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8369-8392)

### Final Payment Record (on approval)

- [ ] createFinalPayment snapshots client amount by type (FULL_DAY→fullDayRate, HALF_DAY→halfDayRate, HOURLY→hourlyRate, FIXED→fixedAmount, FTH→fthRate, CUSTOM_HOURS→customHoursRate with clientMinimumHours=customHours) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8394-8419)
- [ ] Engineer amount by type (FULL_DAY, HALF_DAY, HOURLY, FIXED; FTH engineer amount not mapped → null) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8421-8432)
- [ ] Payment payload stores service fee & %, client/engineer totals (work order, expense, labour, grand), hours, additional hours (default 0), currencies, companyTotalExpenseCost (default 0), approvedById (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8434-8474)

### Review (sub-status REVIEWED)

- [ ] reviewWorkOrder: WO in caller's company with status WORKDONE or INCOMPLETE; sets subStatus REVIEWED; logs (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8476-8531)

### Verify / Approve / Mark Payable / PO Pending / Paid

- [ ] verifyWorkOrder: WO in caller's company, status WORKDONE or INCOMPLETE AND subStatus REVIEWED (must be reviewed first) else 404 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8540-8580)
- [ ] Verify sets status VERIFIED and subStatus PO_PENDING when project.isPoRequired and WO has no PO, otherwise null; logs (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8582-8617)
- [ ] approveWorkOrder: CLIENT role only (400 "you don't has access"); WO in caller's company with status VERIFIED else 404 "Work Order is in process" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8629-8660)
- [ ] Approve requires an ASSIGNED offer for engineer (userId or referredEngineerId) else 404 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8662-8672)
- [ ] Approve: creates final Payment, sets status APPROVED and customerStatus APPROVED, logs, archives offer in OfferHistory, verifies/adds engineer skills from WO services, pushes socket update, notifies engineer "Congratualations! your worked is approved.", pushes engineer list update (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8674-8743)
- [ ] After approval commit: customer notified WORK_ORDER_APPROVED "Your work order has been approved.", engineer notified of client feedback, and a WorkOrderSnapshot taken for the engineer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8750-8760)
- [ ] markPayableWorkOrder: CLIENT only; WO in company and status CANCELLED; engineer's offer must be CANCELLED; sets customerStatus CANCELLED_PAYABLE + customerSubStatus CANCELLED; logs; archives OfferHistory; creates final Payment; takes snapshot (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8767-8878)
- [ ] markAsPaid schedules status PAID 5 minutes later via setTimeout (not persisted across restarts) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8880-8903)
- [ ] markAsPOPending: WO in company with status VERIFIED or customerStatus APPROVED or CANCELLED_PAYABLE; sets subStatus PO_PENDING; logs (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8905-8976)
- [ ] Offer counts: requested = PENDING non-counter; counter = PENDING counter; routed = ROUTED/ACCEPTED non-counter; missing workOrderId → 400 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8978-9015)

### Hours & Payment Formulas (per PaymentType)

- [ ] Client total hours = Σ timeLog.totalHours truncated to 3 dp; if fractional part ≥ 15 min (0.25 h) round UP to next whole hour, else keep (ROUNDING_BUFFER_MINIUTES=15) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:111,9017-9046)
- [ ] Engineer total hours = Σ timeLog.totalHours truncated to 3 dp, no rounding (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9048-9065)
- [ ] Client expense total = Σ amount of expenses where !isEngineerExpense; engineer expense total = Σ all passed expenses (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9067-9087)
- [ ] Engineer HOURLY/FTH: hoursWorked = max(worked, engineerMinimumHours) (1 if min 0 or worked 0); additionalHours = hoursWorked − min (only when min>0); additional rate = engineerAdditionalHourlyRate or 0; labour = rate×min + additional + nonBusiness (min>0) else rate×hoursWorked + nonBusiness; totalHoursApproved = nonBusinessHours + hoursWorked (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9089-9152)
- [ ] Client HOURLY: hoursWorked = max(worked, minimumHours); additionalHours = 0 if WO.additionalHours null (timesheet-edited), explicit value if non-zero, else hoursWorked − min; additional rate = additionalHourlyRate or hourlyRate; labour = hourlyRate×min + additional×rate + nonBusinessHours×nonBusinessHourRate; approved = min + nonBusiness + additional (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9154-9197)
- [ ] Client FIXED: labour = fixedAmount; total = fixedAmount + client expenses (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9199-9213)
- [ ] Engineer FIXED: labour = engineerFixedAmount; total = engineerFixedAmount + all expenses (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9215-9229)
- [ ] Engineer HALF_DAY: half day = 4h (8/2); additional = engineerAdditionalHours rule (null→0, non-zero→value, else worked−4 if >4); rate = engineerHourlyRate; labour = engineerHalfDayRate + additional + nonBusiness; approved = 4 + nonBusiness + additional (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9231-9273)
- [ ] Client HALF_DAY: same with halfDayRate, hourlyRate, additionalHours, nonBusinessHours (4h threshold) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9275-9314)
- [ ] Client FTH (first two hours): base = fthRate covering 2h; additional beyond 2h at hourlyRate; approved = 2 + nonBusiness + additional (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9316-9356)
- [ ] Client CUSTOM_HOURS: base = customHoursRate covering customHours; additional beyond customHours at hourlyRate; approved = customHours + nonBusiness + additional (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9358-9398)
- [ ] Engineer FULL_DAY: 8h threshold; labour = engineerFullDayRate + additional×engineerHourlyRate + nonBusiness; additionalHours returned as 2-dp string (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9400-9440)
- [ ] Client FULL_DAY: 8h threshold; labour = fullDayRate + additional×hourlyRate + nonBusiness; approved = 8 + nonBusiness + additional (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9442-9482)
- [ ] Engineer feedback statuses = APPROVED, INVOICED, DISPUTED; feedback visible statuses additionally include PAID (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9484-9493)

### Allowed Actions (findAllowedActionsToUsers)

- [ ] Invalid/non-numeric workOrderId or userId → error (swallowed; returns undefined) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9497-9503,9742-9744)
- [ ] Offer flags match offers where userId = caller only: isRouted (ROUTED) + routedOfferId, isAccepted (ACCEPTED), isRequested (PENDING/REJECTED non-counter), isCounter (PENDING/REJECTED counter), isDeclined (DECLINED), isOfferRejectedByClient (REJECTED) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9511-9545,9719-9726)
- [ ] isStartTime = START_TIME task completed on a WO where caller is engineer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9547-9563,9729)
- [ ] isCheckOut = CHECK_OUT task completed; isCheckIn is derived as NOT checked-out (not from CHECK_IN task) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9629-9663,9727-9728)
- [ ] isWorkOrderConfirmByEngineer = WO ASSIGNED with subStatus CONFIRMED and caller as engineer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9565-9572)
- [ ] isWorkOrderCancelByEngineer / isWorkOrderCancelByClient = CancelWorkOrder record with canceledBy FREELANCER/CLIENT on a CANCELLED WO (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9574-9592)
- [ ] isWorkOrderAlreadyAssigned = true unless caller is the engineer, or WO unassigned (no engineer, no company), or assigned only to caller's owned company with no engineer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9599-9613,9732)
- [ ] isFeedbackGivenByClient = Feedback type FREELANCER exists; isFeedbackGivenByEngineer = Feedback type CLIENT exists (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9615-9627)
- [ ] isChatInitiated = a CLIENT-role user has sent a message in the WO chat room for this engineer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9665-9675)
- [ ] isAssignedMember = caller has ASSIGNED offer (userId/referred) on a WO whose assigned freelance company owner is not the caller (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9677-9691)
- [ ] showCancellationReason only for WO creator, assigned engineer, or assigned company owner when WO is CANCELLED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9693-9706)
- [ ] canEngineerGiveFeedback = caller is WO engineer and status in APPROVED/INVOICED/DISPUTED; isEngineerFeedbackVisible when status in APPROVED/INVOICED/DISPUTED/PAID (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9708-9717)

### Cancellation

- [ ] Cancellation reason formatting: enum code (≠ OTHER) title-cased ("CUSTOMER_RESCHEDULED" → "Customer Rescheduled"), else free-text reason, else '' (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9750-9759)
- [ ] Engineer cancel: caller must have an ASSIGNED offer (userId or referredEngineerId) and WO must be ASSIGNED (not INPROGRESS) else 404 → surfaced as 400 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9822-9845,10028-10032)
- [ ] Engineer cancel sets WO CANCELLED, offer CANCELLED, recalculates and stores user.cancellationRate, logs WORK_ORDER_CANCELLED_BY_ENGINEER, creates CancelWorkOrder {canceledBy FREELANCER, cancellationReason, reason} (no penalty) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9847-9937)
- [ ] After engineer cancel: snapshot taken (failure only logged); if company-assigned and cancelled by company owner → email the tech; if cancelled by the tech → email the company (company email or owner email) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9940-9996)
- [ ] Cancellation emails to env CANCELLED_EMAIL_RECIPIENTS list ("assigned vendor"/"assigned engineer" + name, reason, scheduled date/time in WO tz, site) are fire-and-forget (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9950-10009,9760-9820)
- [ ] Engineer cancel notifies WO creator WORK_ORDER_CANCELLED "Cancelled[: reason]" and pushes socket update (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10011-10025)
- [ ] Client cancel: WO must be ASSIGNED in caller's company else 404 → 400; sets CANCELLED; offers cancelled = all ASSIGNED offers + the current engineer's ROUTED offer (other PENDING/COUNTER untouched) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10173-10238)
- [ ] Client cancel penalty = hourly-type labour cost × env PENALTY_PERCENTAGE / 100, stored with penaltyPercentage and canceledBy CLIENT; logs WORK_ORDER_CANCELLED_BY_CLIENT (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10240-10277)
- [ ] Client cancel notifies engineer WORK_ORDER_CANCELLED; snapshot taken; emails company + tech (company-assigned) or the solo engineer, with availability note only when recipientName set (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10283-10359)
- [ ] Cancellation rate = (# CancelWorkOrder by engineer with canceledBy FREELANCER) / (# WOs with engineerId = engineer and status ≠ CANCELLED) × 100; 0 if none assigned (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10367-10385)

### Snapshots

- [ ] takeSnapshot stores WO (expenses of creator + engineer, engineer's offer only), formatted feedback, offer counts and total hours; upserts one snapshot per (workOrderId, engineerId) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10035-10148)
- [ ] Snapshots are taken on approve, mark payable, engineer cancel and client cancel; deleted on revert-to-workdone (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8760,8873,9940,10298,7065)

### Response Shaping

- [ ] Responses are serialised per role with excludeExtraneousValues: FREELANCER → EngineerWorkOrderResponseDto, CLIENT → ClientWorkOrderResponseDto, CUSTOMER → CustomerWorkOrderResponseDto; ADMIN/MODERATOR get undefined (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10394-10433)

### Sign-off Sheet

- [ ] One sign-off sheet per WO: existing sheet is updated, otherwise created (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10434-10468)
- [ ] Create uploads engineer signature (SIGNATURE, under engineerId), client signature (SIGNATURE, under clientId), physical sheet (SIGN_OFF_SHEET) and logs SIGN_OFF_SHEET_CREATED with provided comments/names (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10470-10554)
- [ ] Update replaces existing S3 file in place (new signed URL valid 24h) or uploads new asset; only provided text fields updated; logs SIGN_OFF_SHEET_UPDATED with old/new values (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:10557-10778)

## 2. Offers, Time Logs, Tasks, Team Tasks, Scheduler & Logs



### Offers

#### Data model / enums
- [ ] OfferStatus enum is PENDING, ASSIGNED, REJECTED (client rejects/hides), COUNTER, DECLINED (engineer not interested), ROUTED, ACCEPTED, PAID, CANCELLED (engineer cancelled after assignment) (../prisma/schema.prisma:575)
- [ ] Only one Offer may exist per (userId, workOrderId). This is a DB unique constraint `@@unique([userId, workOrderId])` (../prisma/schema.prisma:1981)
- [ ] New Offer defaults: status=PENDING, isCounter=false (../prisma/schema.prisma:1975)
- [ ] OfferHistory has no unique constraint (the old unique on workOrderId+userId+status+isCounter is commented out), so it can hold many rows per offer (../prisma/schema.prisma:2007)
- [ ] Offer ownership: in a company offer, `userId` is the company owner who submitted it and `referredEngineerId` is the engineer who does the work. In a direct or routed offer, `userId` is the engineer and `referredEngineerId` is null (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:133)

#### Create offer (request / route)
- [ ] The engineer who does the work (referredEngineerId ?? userId) must be APPROVED and not deleted, or the call fails with 400 "Engineer is not approved" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:138)
- [ ] An ACCEPTED freelance-company member cannot self-submit a request on a public work order (authUser == userId, no route status, no referredEngineerId). The call fails with 400 "You are linked to a freelance company. Work orders are assigned to you by your company." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:151)
- [ ] The engineer's schedule is checked for conflicts before the offer is created. On conflict the call returns 409 with the TECH message when engineer == auth user, otherwise the CLIENT message (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:164)
- [ ] If the requested work order does not exist, the schedule check fails with 404 "The requested work order does not exist." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:557)
- [ ] If an offer already exists for (userId, workOrderId) with status DECLINED, it is re-used: its status changes to ROUTED (re-route) instead of a new offer being created (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:189)
- [ ] If an existing offer is ROUTED, the call fails with 409 "Already Routed to you, please refresh the page" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:193)
- [ ] If an existing offer has any other non-DECLINED status, the call fails with 409 "You have already submitted an offer for this job." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:195)
- [ ] Offers can only be created on a work order whose status is PUBLISHED and whose engineerId is null. Otherwise the call fails with 403: "Only published work order can be routed" when routing, or "This work order is no longer open for offers." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:205)
- [ ] Re-routing a DECLINED offer sends a ROUTED notification to the engineer and returns early, with no new offer and no billing-rate checks (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:222)
- [ ] A freelance-company owner making a plain request (not a counter) on a work order with no engineerPaymentType (no custom rates) is rejected with 400 "No custom rates are set for this work order. You can only send a counter offer." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:228)
- [ ] Billing-rate checks are skipped when the offer's userId is a freelance-company owner or an ACCEPTED freelance-company member (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:250)
- [ ] For a non-company engineer, a missing hourly, half-day or full-day billing rate fails with 400 "Engineer billing rates are missing." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:256)
- [ ] engineerRate is set by the work order's engineerPaymentType. HOURLY and FTH use the WO engineerHourlyRate, falling back to the engineer's hourly rate. HALF_DAY uses the WO half-day rate, falling back to the engineer's. FULL_DAY uses the WO full-day rate, falling back to the engineer's. FIXED uses the WO engineerFixedAmount (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:264)
- [ ] The offer's minimumHours is the WO engineerMinimumHours when > 0, otherwise 0 (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:286)
- [ ] The offer's currency is the WO engineerCurrency, falling back to the engineer's billing currency (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:288)
- [ ] The offer's hourly, half-day and full-day rates come from the WO engineer rates, each falling back to the engineer's own rate (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:292)
- [ ] For a non-company engineer, the resolved hourly, half-day and full-day rates must all be non-empty (400 "Hourly, Half Day and Full Day rates must not be empty.") and all > 0 (400 "All rates must be greater than 0.") (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:298)
- [ ] A new offer's status is the passed status (for example ROUTED), otherwise PENDING. isCounter defaults to false. fixedAmount is copied from the WO engineerFixedAmount. createdById is the auth user (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:315)
- [ ] Creating an offer writes a work-order log of type OFFER_ROUTED when routed, otherwise OFFER_SENT (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:353)
- [ ] Creating an offer always writes an OfferHistory row that snapshots the rates, status and isCounter (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:357)
- [ ] The offer log metaData includes a snapshot of the submitter's non-company, non-engineer expenses (offerExpenses, offerExpenseOwnerId, offerHistoryId) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2858)
- [ ] A routed offer notifies the engineer (type ROUTED) and pre-creates the WO group chat room for that engineer. The room is seeded with team members plus the company owner and the engineer, and is not duplicated if it already exists (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:484)
- [ ] A requested (non-routed) offer notifies the WO primary and secondary team members with type REQUESTED ("… has sent an offer.") (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:397)
- [ ] Unexpected errors during offer creation return 500 "An error occurred while creating the offer." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:425)

#### Counter offer (create / update)
- [ ] The counter offer's engineer is referredEngineerId ?? authUserId. The offer's userId is always the auth user (the submitter) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:725)
- [ ] BUG candidate: the engineer-approved check in counter offers is not awaited (it is a Promise), so "Engineer is not approved" never fires (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:728)
- [ ] An ACCEPTED freelance-company member cannot self-submit a counter offer (no referredEngineerId). The call fails with 400 "You are linked to a freelance company…" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:741)
- [ ] Counter offers run the same schedule-conflict check (409, TECH message if self, CLIENT message if on behalf) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:755)
- [ ] A counter offer requires a WO with engineerId null, otherwise 409 "This work order is no longer open for offers." There is no PUBLISHED-status check here (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:770)
- [ ] The billing-rate check is skipped if the auth user owns a freelance company or the engineer is an ACCEPTED freelance member. Otherwise missing rates fail with 400 "Engineer billing rates are missing." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:791)
- [ ] Counter expenses: an expense with an id is updated, and an expense without an id is created with isApproved=false. Both are owned by the auth user (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:837)
- [ ] An existing counterable offer is the auth user's offer on this WO with status PENDING or ROUTED (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:867)
- [ ] minimumHours is kept only when >= 1, otherwise 0. additionalHourlyRate is kept only when minimumHours >= 1, otherwise 0 (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:885)
- [ ] Updating an existing counter sets status=PENDING and isCounter=true, overwrites the rates, and sets the currency to dto.currency, then the WO engineerCurrency, then the engineer's currency (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:890)
- [ ] Updating an existing counter writes OfferHistory (isCounter=true) and a work-order log OFFER_UPDATED, and notifies team members with type COUNTER ("has updated a counter offer.") (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:915)
- [ ] With no existing PENDING or ROUTED offer, a new offer is created with status=PENDING and isCounter=true, plus notification COUNTER ("has made a counter offer."), log OFFER_SENT and OfferHistory (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:972)
- [ ] The counter offer flow runs in a single DB transaction. A 409 is re-thrown as a Conflict, and any other error becomes 500 "An error occurred while creating the offer." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:753)

#### Offer listing (client provider tab)
- [ ] Offers list only offers on WOs in the viewer's company, filtered by the requested statuses, and excludes deleted users (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1124)
- [ ] The optional filterByRequiredSkills and filterByRequiredTools flags keep only engineers who have at least one of the WO's required skills or qualification subtypes (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1135)
- [ ] The Pending tab with no sortBy is grouped: plain requests first (rank 0), then counters (1), then ROUTED/ACCEPTED (2). Within a group, offers are ordered by lowest billing rate ascending (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1162)
- [ ] The default sort is updatedAt desc. sortBy=distance sorts by Google distance in memory and then paginates. With no other sort, results are ordered by the engineer's minimum billing rate ascending (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1173)
- [ ] For company offers, the listing shows the referredEngineer as `user` and the company owner as `createdBy` (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1199)

#### Assign work order to engineer
- [ ] Assigning checks for a schedule conflict first and fails with 409 SCHEDULE_CONFLICT_CLIENT_MESSAGE. The outer catch re-maps any non-400 error to 500 (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1297)
- [ ] If the auth user is not found or is deleted, assignment fails with "User Not found" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1302)
- [ ] A WO can have only one ASSIGNED or PAID offer. If one exists, the call fails with 400 "Work order already assigned or completed" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1312)
- [ ] The offer is either the one given by offerId or the engineer's PENDING or ACCEPTED offer (as userId or referredEngineerId). If there is none, the call fails with 400 "Offer does not exist or already assigned" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1330)
- [ ] The engineer who does the work is offer.referredEngineerId ?? offer.userId (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1345)
- [ ] On assignment, the engineer must hold every WO qualification subtype whose type is "Equipment" or "Certification". Otherwise the call fails with 400 "Missing qualifications: …" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:586)
- [ ] The engineer's required Equipment and Certification qualifications must all be verified. Otherwise the call fails with 406 "The following qualifications are not verified: …" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:639)
- [ ] The WO must belong to the auth user's company and must not already have an engineer. Otherwise the call fails with 400 "Work Order does not exist or already assigned" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1349)
- [ ] Assignment sets offer status=ASSIGNED and writes OfferHistory, inside a transaction (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1367)
- [ ] On assignment, any previous WorkOrderSnapshot for this engineer on this WO is deleted (for repeated assign/unassign) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1396)
- [ ] On assignment, a WorkOrderSnapshot is taken for every user with a ROUTED, ACCEPTED or PENDING offer on the WO (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1414)
- [ ] assignedFreelanceCompanyId is set only for genuine company offers (referredEngineerId set). The company is resolved from createdById ?? userId as owner. Direct offers get null (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1438)
- [ ] Assignment sets WO status=ASSIGNED, subStatus=NOT_CONFIRMED, engineerId=engineer and assignedFreelanceCompanyId. The update is guarded by engineerId=null (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1448)
- [ ] Assignment writes a work-order log OFFER_ASSIGN whose metaData holds an immutable offerTimeline (all OfferHistory rows), offerFinalExpenses, offerExpenseOwnerId, offerReferredEngineerId and offerParticipantIds (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1468)
- [ ] If the offer was ACCEPTED (a routed offer accepted by the engineer), team members get an ACCEPT notification ("has accepted.") (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1553)
- [ ] The assigned engineer gets WORK_ORDER_ASSIGNED. The freelance-company owner also gets it when the WO has assignedFreelanceCompanyId and the owner is not the engineer (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1579)
- [ ] BUG candidate: for a non-ACCEPTED offer, the WORK_ORDER_ASSIGNED recipient is the `userId` argument, not the resolved engineerUserId (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1599)
- [ ] The customer (WO client's customer users) is notified with WORK_ORDER_ASSIGNED "An engineer has been assigned to your work order." on a best-effort basis. The performer is excluded (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1650)
- [ ] After assignment, a group chat room (client company + engineer) is created or ensured. The freelance-company owner and the engineer are upserted as participants, and new rooms are seeded with WO team members plus the company owner (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1659)

#### Accept routed offer (engineer)
- [ ] Accepting a routed offer sets status=ACCEPTED and optionally sets referredEngineerId (a company owner accepting on behalf of a member) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1772)
- [ ] Accepting a routed offer writes OfferHistory and log OFFER_ACCEPTED, and notifies team members with ACCEPT ("has accepted the routed offer."). The WO status is not changed (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1780)
- [ ] BUG candidate: acceptRoutedOffer does not check the current offer status (for example ROUTED) or that the offer exists before updating. The not-found check runs after the update (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1831)
- [ ] Any failure accepting a routed offer returns 500 "Failed to accept offer" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1853)

#### Accept counter offer (client)
- [ ] Accepting a counter fails with 400 "Offer does not exist." when the offer is missing (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1873)
- [ ] Accepting a counter approves all of the offer userId's unapproved expenses on the WO (isApproved=true) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1887)
- [ ] Accepting a counter then assigns the WO to referredEngineerId ?? userId with that offerId. All assignment rules apply (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1900)

#### Update offer status (client) / reject
- [ ] Updating status fails with 400 "Offer does not exist." when there is no offer for offerId or (userId, workOrderId) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1932)
- [ ] Setting an offer's status to PENDING through updateWorkOrderStatus triggers an unassign (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1936)
- [ ] Any other status is written directly to the offer, with log OFFER_REJECTED when status=REJECTED, otherwise OTHER, plus OfferHistory. There is no status-transition validation (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:1941)

#### Unassign
- [ ] Unassign resets the WO to engineerId=null, assignedFreelanceCompanyId=null, status=PUBLISHED, subStatus=null (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2085)
- [ ] Unassign sets all of that user's expenses on the WO back to isApproved=false (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2097)
- [ ] Unassign sets the offer back to PENDING and writes log OFFER_UNASSIGNED and OfferHistory (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2107)
- [ ] Unassign deletes all WorkOrderSnapshots of the WO, then retakes a snapshot for the unassigned user after about 100ms (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2156)
- [ ] The unassigned engineer gets a WORK_ORDER_UNASSIGNED notification, and the WO is removed from their realtime list (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2165)

#### Decline (engineer not interested)
- [ ] An engineer declines their own offer, looked up by offerId or by userId/referredEngineerId on the WO. A missing offer fails with 400 "Offer does not exist." (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2006)
- [ ] Declining sets the offer status to DECLINED whatever the passed `status` is, and writes log OFFER_DECLINED, OfferHistory and a WO snapshot for the engineer (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2024)
- [ ] A DECLINED offer can later be re-routed by the client, which reuses the same offer row and sets it to ROUTED (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:428)

#### Withdraw (engineer)
- [ ] Only offers in PENDING, COUNTER or REJECTED status can be withdrawn. Otherwise the call fails with 404 "Offer doesn't exists or either Assigned to you" (the outer catch rewrites every error to 404 "Something went wrong with withdraw request, Try later") (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2192)
- [ ] Withdrawal requires a matching OfferHistory row, otherwise it fails with "Offer History doesn't exists" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2225)
- [ ] Withdrawing hard-deletes all of the user's OfferHistory on the WO, all of the user's expenses on the WO, and the Offer itself (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2237)
- [ ] Withdrawing writes log OFFER_WITHDRAWN with a snapshot of the pre-withdrawal expenses, and notifies team members with WITHDRAW (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2303)
- [ ] On withdrawal, the engineer's WO chat room is deleted if it has no messages (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2356)

#### Remove routed offer (unroute)
- [ ] Only offers in ROUTED status can be removed/unrouted. Otherwise the call fails with 409 "WO Accepted" (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2752)
- [ ] Unrouting deletes all of the user's OfferHistory on the WO (when a ROUTED history row exists), deletes the offer, and writes log OFFER_REJECTED (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2774)
- [ ] Unrouting removes the WO from the engineer's list and deletes their chat room if it has no messages (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2823)
- [ ] An expense can only be deleted by its owner (where id AND userId) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2849)

#### Offer detail / current vs counter
- [ ] The current offer comes from the WO engineer rates by payment type. HOURLY and FTH give hourly + minimumHours + additionalHourlyRate. HALF_DAY gives halfDay + hourly. FULL_DAY gives fullDay + hourly. FIXED gives fixedAmount. The engineer's rates are the fallback (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2459)
- [ ] Offer expenses are scoped to the company owner when companyOwnerId differs from userId, otherwise to userId. Client-added engineer expenses (isEngineerExpense=true) are merged in (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2513)
- [ ] The offer total is: HOURLY/FTH = hourlyRate × minimumHours, HALF_DAY = halfDayRate, FULL_DAY = fullDayRate, FIXED = fixedAmount. totalWorkOrderCost = that total + the sum of expenses (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2660)
- [ ] When the offer is a non-counter PENDING or is ACCEPTED, `current` shows the offer's own values instead of the WO defaults (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:2727)
- [ ] isEngineerQualificationsVerified is false when any of the engineer's required Equipment or Certification qualifications they hold is unverified. Missing qualifications are not counted (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:653)

### Time Logs & Check-in

- [ ] Check-in/out timestamps are truncated to the minute (seconds and ms set to 0) (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:38)
- [ ] Check-in requires a WO with an engineer assigned (engineerId not null). Otherwise the call fails with 404 "Work order not found" (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:41)
- [ ] The check-in time window is skipped when the WO status is INCOMPLETE or INPROGRESS (re-check-in) (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:112)
- [ ] Check-in is allowed from 5 minutes before to 4 hours after the scheduled start, in the WO timezone (utils/helpers/isCheckInAllowed.ts:90)
- [ ] Checking in more than 5 minutes early fails with 400 "Too early to check in. Current time: …" (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:134)
- [ ] Checking in more than 4 hours after the scheduled start fails with 400 "Check-in window expired. You can only check in within 4 hours of the scheduled time." (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:138)
- [ ] An allowed check-in marks the CHECK_IN completed task as completed, with text = check-in ISO time and userId (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:121)
- [ ] Check-in, check-out and custom time logs require latitude and longitude, otherwise 400 "Location is required, Please enable location" (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:561)
- [ ] GPS distance to the WO address is computed with PostGIS ST_DistanceSphere in km and only recorded (checkInDistance / checkOutDistance). No distance threshold blocks check-in or check-out (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:567)
- [ ] Check-in creates a new TimeLog (checkInTime, lat/long, timezone, distance, checkInLoggedById) (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:155)
- [ ] Check-in resets the WO's CHECK_OUT completed task (isCompleted=false, text=null, userId=null) (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:167)
- [ ] Check-in sets the WO to status=INPROGRESS, subStatus=CHECK_IN, and writes log ENGINEER_CHECKED_IN (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:179)
- [ ] Check-in notifies WO team members plus the freelance-company owner (WORK_ORDER_CHECK_IN) and the customer ("The engineer has checked in.") (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:218)
- [ ] The check-in transaction timeout is 15000 ms (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:215)
- [ ] Check-out uses the latest TimeLog on the WO that has a check-in. Without a checkInTime the call fails with 400 "Check-in has not been recorded for this task." (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:262)
- [ ] Check-out requires the WO to have an engineer and be in status INPROGRESS. Otherwise the call fails with 404 "Work order not found" (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:282)
- [ ] Check-out requires the completed task (404 "Completed task not found.") and its parent task (404 "Parent task not found.") to exist (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:333)
- [ ] Check-out time must be >= check-in time, otherwise 400 "Cannot check out. Check-out time must be after check-in time." (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:353)
- [ ] Check-out completes the CHECK_OUT task and sets the WO to status=INPROGRESS, subStatus=CHECK_OUT (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:354)
- [ ] Check-out records the location, distance, checkOutTime, totalHours (fractional hours from check-in) and type=ON_CHECKOUT on the TimeLog. It writes log ENGINEER_CHECKED_OUT (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:388)
- [ ] Check-out notifies team members plus the freelance-company owner (WORK_ORDER_CHECK_OUT) and the customer (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:431)
- [ ] Editing a time log fails with 404 when the log is missing, and with 400 "Check-out time cannot be before check-in time" when the new check-out is before check-in (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:472)
- [ ] Editing a time log recalculates totalHours when both times are present and writes log TIMELOG_UPDATED with the old and new values. There is no role or status restriction in the service (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:490)
- [ ] totalHours = checkOut − checkIn in fractional hours (dayjs diff 'hour', true). Both times are required (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:544)
- [ ] A custom (manual) time log with the same workOrderId, checkInTime and checkOutTime as an existing one is rejected with 400 "This time log already exists." (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:607)
- [ ] A custom time log uses the one given location for both check-in and check-out, has type=CUSTOM, records the logger as both check-in and check-out user, and writes log TIMELOG_ADDED. The WO status is unchanged (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:632)
- [ ] BUG candidate: a custom time log does not validate that checkOut >= checkIn, so negative totalHours are possible (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:628)
- [ ] Time log listing returns only logs with totalHours not null (completed pairs) (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:694)
- [ ] TimeLogType enum is CUSTOM and ON_CHECKOUT. checkInTime and checkIn lat/long are required fields (../prisma/schema.prisma:1542)

### Schedule Conflicts

- [ ] An HOURLY work order blocks the engineer for a flat 8 hours from the scheduled start. End time and minimum hours are ignored (utils/helpers/scheduleConflict.ts:5)
- [ ] HALF_DAY, FULL_DAY and other non-HOURLY types block until the orderEndTime (or start + duration) plus a 1-hour grace buffer (utils/helpers/scheduleConflict.ts:7)
- [ ] Duration without an end time: HALF_DAY = 4h, FULL_DAY = 8h, otherwise max(1, the engineer's minimum hours) (utils/helpers/scheduleConflict.ts:49)
- [ ] The engineer's minimum hours come from their offer on the WO (as userId or referredEngineerId) when non-zero, otherwise from the WO engineerMinimumHours (utils/helpers/scheduleConflict.ts:64)
- [ ] Start/end are built as UTC from the date and time strings. No timezone conversion is applied (utils/helpers/scheduleConflict.ts:20)
- [ ] Only the engineer's non-deleted WOs in ASSIGNED or INPROGRESS are checked, and the WO never conflicts with itself (utils/helpers/scheduleConflict.ts:118)
- [ ] A conflict exists when the requested start falls in [start, busyUntil), or the requested end falls in (start, busyUntil], or the request fully covers the window, or the start times are exactly equal (utils/helpers/scheduleConflict.ts:139)
- [ ] Client conflict message: "Schedule Conflict: The selected engineer is already assigned to another work order during this time." Tech message: "Schedule Conflict: This work order overlaps with an existing assignment." (utils/variables.ts:29)
- [ ] The schedule-conflict check runs on create offer, counter offer and assignment (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:552)

### Tasks

- [ ] TaskType enum is CALL, COLLECT_SIGNATURE, COMPLETE_CUSTOM_TASK, DOWNLOAD_FILE, START_TIME, CHECK_IN, CHECK_OUT, CLOSE_NOTES, ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE, MARK_COMPLETE (../prisma/schema.prisma:469)
- [ ] Each Task has at most one CompletedTask (unique taskId) (../prisma/schema.prisma:2068)
- [ ] Task-update routing: START_TIME (only if not completed) goes to startTime. CHECK_IN (not completed) is a no-op in this service (handled by time-logs). CLOSE_NOTES goes to closeNotes. ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE and MARK_COMPLETE go to their handlers. Everything else is generic completion (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:49)
- [ ] Updating an already-completed START_TIME or CHECK_IN task falls through to the generic completion handler (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:61)
- [ ] START_TIME completion sets the WO subStatus to READY_TO_GO, writes log WORK_ORDER_STATUS_UPDATED, and notifies team, freelance-company owner and customer with WORK_ORDER_READY_TO_GO. The 24-hour-before-start restriction is disabled (commented out) (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:164)
- [ ] ON_THE_WAY completion sets WO subStatus=ON_THE_WAY, writes log WORK_ORDER_STATUS_UPDATED, and notifies team, freelance-company owner and customer (WORK_ORDER_ON_THE_WAY). There is no time restriction (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:342)
- [ ] RUNNING_LATE completion sets WO subStatus=RUNNING_LATE, stores the reason text on Task.reason, writes a log, and notifies team, freelance-company owner and customer (WORK_ORDER_RUNNING_LATE) (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:444)
- [ ] CLOSE_NOTES appends {text, createdAt} to a JSON array in CompletedTask.text (multiple notes allowed) and marks it completed. The WO status is not changed (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:263)
- [ ] MARK_COMPLETE stores isCompleted from the DTO, calls markWorkOrderComplete, stores the reason, and notifies team (WORK_ORDER_WORK_DONE) and customer (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:543)
- [ ] markWorkOrderComplete only applies to WOs in INPROGRESS or INCOMPLETE. It sets status=WORKDONE, subStatus=null and the calculated hoursWorked / engineerHoursWorked (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8012)
- [ ] A FREELANCER cannot mark a WO complete while any COMPLETE_CUSTOM_TASK task is untouched or incomplete (400 listing the titles). Clients may bypass this (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:8025)
- [ ] Generic task completion sets isCompleted from the DTO with text = the current ISO time, notifies team members (SYSTEM), and emails only emailsToNotify entries that pass the regex validation, and only when completed (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:91)
- [ ] Task time restrictions (START_TIME within 24h, ON_THE_WAY/RUNNING_LATE/MARK_COMPLETE after start) are all commented out and not enforced (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:162)
- [ ] WorkOrderSubStatus under ASSIGNED: NOT_CONFIRMED, CONFIRMED, NOT_READY, READY_TO_GO, ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE, CANCELLED. Under INPROGRESS: CHECK_IN, CHECK_OUT, REVIEWED, PO_PENDING, CANCELLED_PAYABLE (../prisma/schema.prisma:558)

### Team Tasks

- [ ] TeamTask defaults: status=TODO, priority=MEDIUM, isDeleted=false. referenceId is unique (../prisma/schema.prisma:1016)
- [ ] TeamTaskStatusType is TODO, INRPOCESS (typo), INPROCESS, COMPLETED, ON_HOLD. TeamTaskPriority is LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, URGENT (../prisma/schema.prisma:489)
- [ ] A user can be assigned to a team task only once (unique taskId+userId). Creation uses skipDuplicates (../prisma/schema.prisma:1051)
- [ ] Creating a team task requires a teamId, can optionally link a workOrderId, and records createdBy = the auth user (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:150)
- [ ] Creating a team task with assignees creates the assignments (assignedById = creator) and notifies the assignees ("You have been assigned to task …") (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:163)
- [ ] Every team task creation writes a TASK_CREATED log with title, priority, dates and assignee ids. Attachments are uploaded after the transaction (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:186)
- [ ] Fetching a task by referenceId excludes deleted tasks and returns 404 "Task not found" (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:269)
- [ ] Task board default scope: a company owner sees all teams of the company, and other users see only the teams they belong to. Users with no teams get an empty result (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:377)
- [ ] A non-owner filtering by teamIds they do not belong to gets 403 "You are not authorized to perform this action" (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:391)
- [ ] Task board lists only non-deleted tasks in the user's company, sorted by updatedAt desc, with a default page size of 10 (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:400)
- [ ] Updating a task diffs the assignees: removed users get an UNASSIGNED log and notification, and added users get an ASSIGNED log and notification (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:521)
- [ ] BUG candidate: an update without assigneeIds treats newAssigneeIds as [], so all existing assignees are removed (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:522)
- [ ] Updating a task logs each changed field: status, priority, title, description, teamId, workOrderId, dueDate, startDate (each with its own TeamTaskLogAction) (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:641)
- [ ] Sending an empty startDate, dueDate or workOrderId on update clears the field (null / disconnect) (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:499)
- [ ] Update and status change do not check team membership or ownership in the service (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:474)
- [ ] A status change writes a STATUS_CHANGED log with the old and new status (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:689)
- [ ] Deleting an attachment requires membership of the task's team (403 otherwise). The file is removed from S3, the asset is soft-deleted, and a removal log is written (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:732)
- [ ] A comment must be non-blank (400 "Comment is required"). Adding one writes COMMENT_ADDED and notifies the assignees and previous commenters, excluding the author (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:756)
- [ ] Only the author can edit a comment (lookup by createdById). An unchanged comment is a no-op. A changed comment writes COMMENT_UPDATED with the old and new text (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:869)
- [ ] BUG candidate: updateComment reads existing.comment before the null check, so a non-author gets a 500 TypeError instead of 403 or 404 (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:878)
- [ ] Only the author can delete a comment (403 "You can only delete your own comment"). The comment is soft-deleted and a COMMENT_DELETED log is written (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:926)
- [ ] Only the task creator can delete a task (otherwise 404 "Task not found"). The task is soft-deleted (isDeleted=true) (modules/team-tasks-management/team-tasks-management.service.ts:961)

### Scheduler/Cron

- [ ] An hourly cron sends start reminders to the engineer for non-deleted WOs in status ASSIGNED with subStatus READY_TO_GO or CONFIRMED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.scheduler.ts:29)
- [ ] WOs missing orderStartDate, orderStartTime or timeZone are skipped by the reminder crons (modules/work-orders/work-orders.scheduler.ts:45)
- [ ] The start is computed in the WO timezone and converted to UTC. A reminder is sent when the start is 23.5 to 24.5 hours away (24h reminder) or 11.5 to 12.5 hours away (12h reminder) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.scheduler.ts:62)
- [ ] READY_TO_GO reminder text is "WO-x: title starts at DD/MM/YYYY, hh:mm a." CONFIRMED adds "Please mark your work order 'Ready to Go'…". Type OTHER, title "Reminder", sender id 1 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.scheduler.ts:73)
- [ ] A per-minute cron sends check-in reminders for ASSIGNED WOs in pre-check-in sub-statuses (NOT_CONFIRMED, CONFIRMED, NOT_READY, READY_TO_GO, ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.scheduler.ts:13)
- [ ] Check-in reminders fire at 5 minutes before the start (window opens), at the start, and 10 minutes after the start (rounded minutes 5, 0, -10). Type WORK_ORDER_CHECK_IN, title "Check-In Reminder" (modules/work-orders/work-orders.scheduler.ts:130)

### Work Order Logs

- [ ] Offer events write logs: OFFER_SENT (request/counter create), OFFER_ROUTED (route/re-route), OFFER_UPDATED (counter update), OFFER_ACCEPTED, OFFER_ASSIGN, OFFER_UNASSIGNED, OFFER_REJECTED (reject/unroute), OFFER_DECLINED, OFFER_WITHDRAWN, OTHER (other status) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:353)
- [ ] Time-log events write logs: ENGINEER_CHECKED_IN, ENGINEER_CHECKED_OUT, TIMELOG_UPDATED, TIMELOG_ADDED. These are persisted only when metaData (changes) is non-empty (modules/time-logs/time-logs.service.ts:211)
- [ ] Task sub-status changes (READY_TO_GO, ON_THE_WAY, RUNNING_LATE) write WORK_ORDER_STATUS_UPDATED logs only when metaData is non-empty (modules/tasks/tasks.service.ts:202)
- [ ] Offer logs are always persisted, even with null metaData (no changes) (modules/offers/offers.service.ts:384)
- [ ] Log metaData.changes holds only changed fields. null, undefined and '' count as equal, and ISO date strings with the same instant count as equal (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-log.factory.ts:328)
- [ ] Update logs strip non-persisted fields: templateId, teamName, secondaryTeamName, 'undefined', 'null' (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-log.factory.ts:6)
- [ ] Expense changes are diffed by id (added/deleted/updated sub-fields). workOrderServices and qualificationSubTypes are compared as whole sorted sets. customerFiles logs only additions (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-log.factory.ts:186)
- [ ] Change labels resolve ids to names (client, user, project, category, vendor, address or "Remote", team, services, qualifications grouped by type). Changes whose from and to labels resolve equal are dropped (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-log.factory.ts:376)
- [ ] The history tab hides OFFER_SENT, WORK_ORDER_NOTE_ADDED, WORK_ORDER_NOTE_UPDATED, OFFER_ROUTED, OFFER_REJECTED, OFFER_ACCEPTED, OFFER_UPDATED, OFFER_DECLINED and OFFER_WITHDRAWN (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:21)
- [ ] Logs are company-scoped (WO.companyId = the viewer's companyId), sorted by createdAt desc then id desc, with a page size capped at 100 (default 20) (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:64)
- [ ] BUG candidate: the history count query is not company-scoped, unlike the list query (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:98)
- [ ] The WORK_ORDER_UPDATED event label is derived from the changed sections ("Schedule, Team Updated"). OTHER logs made only of feedback fields are labelled feedback-to-engineer (CLIENT/CUSTOMER) or feedback-to-client (FREELANCER) (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:411)
- [ ] OFFER_ASSIGN log details rebuild the offer timeline from the stored snapshot (with a fallback to OfferHistory), add the latest withdrawal and "Offer Created", and diff the expenses per step (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:265)
- [ ] Offer timeline labels: ROUTED is "Routed to Engineer", ACCEPTED is "Accepted Routed Offer", ASSIGNED is "Assigned". PENDING created by someone other than the user is "Unassigned", otherwise "Counter Offer" or "Requested" (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:439)
- [ ] Log user role label: a FREELANCER who owns a company is "Freelance Company", otherwise "Engineer" (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:925)
- [ ] Log details show check-in/out times in the WO timezone as MM/DD/YYYY at h:mm A, and distances to 2 decimals with "km" (modules/work-order-logs/work-order-logs.service.ts:759)

## 3. Invoices, Payments, Bank Details, Companies, Clients & Org Structure


### Invoices — Creation eligibility
- [ ] Only a non-deleted user whose role is CLIENT can create an invoice (customer or engineer invoice); anyone else gets "Unauthorized" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:91-111)
- [ ] Customer invoice creation (POST /invoices) requires CUSTOMER_INVOICE isWrite permission; engineer invoice creation (POST /invoices/engineer) requires ENGINEER_INVOICE isWrite (modules/invoices/invoices.controller.ts:35-60)
- [ ] Every workOrderId on create must belong to the creator's company and not be deleted, otherwise 400 "Invalid request" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:113-123)
- [ ] `workOrderIds` must be a non-empty array of integers; `paymentTerms` int, `currency` CurrencyType, `branchId` int, `bankAccountId` string, `role` UserType are required; `taxType`, `taxPercentage`, `clientId`, `engineerId` optional (modules/invoices/dto/create-invoice.dto.ts:15-51)
- [ ] A work order already linked to a live (isDelete=false) invoice of the SAME role returns 409 "Work order {ref} is already added in Invoice #{id}. Please remove it first." (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:125-141)
- [ ] The same work order MAY be on one CLIENT invoice and one FREELANCER invoice simultaneously (duplicate check is scoped by role) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:127-134)
- [ ] createInvoice does NOT check work-order status (APPROVED/CANCELLED_PAYABLE), currency match, or client/engineer ownership — only addInvoiceWorkOrders enforces those (gap) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:113-141)
- [ ] Client invoices can bill work orders whose customerStatus is APPROVED or CANCELLED_PAYABLE (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:52-55)
- [ ] Engineer (FREELANCER) invoices can bill only work orders whose status is APPROVED (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:56)
- [ ] On create, linked work orders move to customerStatus=INVOICED (CLIENT invoice) or status=INVOICED (FREELANCER invoice) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:207-219)
- [ ] Create writes an InvoiceWorkOrder join row per work order carrying the invoice role (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:198-205)
- [ ] Invoice is stamped with generatedById = creator and companyId = creator's company; engineerId defaults to null if not provided (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:175-180)
- [ ] Create writes a status history row with newStatus GENERATED (oldStatus = GENERATED, the DB default) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:189-196)
- [ ] Creation runs in a single DB transaction — any failure rolls back invoice, join rows and work-order status changes (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:89)

### Invoices — Numbering & due dates
- [ ] Invoice number is displayed as `INV-#{invoice.id}` (DB autoincrement id; no separate sequence) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1250)
- [ ] Stored dueDate = creation time + paymentTerms days (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:181)
- [ ] PDF/email dueDate is recomputed as TODAY + paymentTerms days (not the stored dueDate) — so the date on the PDF drifts depending on when it is downloaded/sent (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1256, 2329)
- [ ] New-invoice email uses the stored dueDate formatted "DD MMM, YYYY" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2106)
- [ ] Invoice generated date on PDF = createdAt formatted "DD MMM, YYYY" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1251)

### Invoices — Totals / tax math
- [ ] subTotal = sum of payment.clientGrandTotal (CLIENT invoice) or payment.engineerGrandTotal (FREELANCER invoice) across linked work orders; null treated as 0 (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:336-340)
- [ ] taxAmount = subTotal × taxPercentage / 100 (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:360)
- [ ] grandTotal = subTotal + taxAmount (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:361)
- [ ] amountPercentage is always 0 and therefore totalAmount is always 0 (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:334, 359)
- [ ] taxPercentage stored as 0 when not supplied (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:166)
- [ ] companyTotalExpenseCost = sum of payment.companyTotalExpenseCost only for FREELANCER invoices; always 0 for CLIENT invoices (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:344-350)
- [ ] companyCurrency = first non-null payment.companyCurrency for FREELANCER invoices; null for CLIENT invoices (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:354-357)
- [ ] Totals are never rounded when stored; formatting to two decimals happens only on display/PDF (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1258-1263)
- [ ] updateInvoice recomputes totals from live join rows with the invoice's existing taxPercentage and records a status-history row with newStatus UPDATED (invoice.status itself is unchanged) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:268-319)
- [ ] updateInvoice fails with 400 "Invalid request" if any linked work order has been deleted (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:257-266)

### Invoices — Status flow
- [ ] InvoiceStatus values: GENERATED (DB default), VERIFIED, UNPAID, OVERDUE, PAID, UPDATED — UPDATED is only written to history, never to invoice.status (prisma/schema.prisma:25-32; modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:312-319)
- [ ] Status changes go through updateInvoiceStatus; only VERIFIED, UNPAID and PAID in the body have effect — any other status is a silent no-op (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1501-1524)
- [ ] There is NO transition guard: any current status can be moved to VERIFIED/UNPAID/PAID (e.g., GENERATED→PAID, PAID→UNPAID) (gap) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1501-1524)
- [ ] Every status change writes invoiceStatusHistory {oldStatus, newStatus, changedById} (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2033-2057)
- [ ] VERIFIED = released to customer; triggers in-app notification + "New Invoice Generated" email to the customer (CLIENT invoices only) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1501-1512)
- [ ] UNPAID = "send": requires toEmail unless skipEmail is true/'true', else 400 "Invalid Email" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2216-2220)
- [ ] ccEmails accepts a comma-separated string, split and trimmed, each must be a valid email (modules/invoices/dto/update-invoice.dto.ts:23-31)
- [ ] PAID requires a proof file, else 400 "Invoice Proof is required" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2455-2457; dto/update-invoice.dto.ts:42-44)
- [ ] Marking PAID uploads the proof, takes a snapshot of each linked work order, sets work orders customerStatus=PAID (CLIENT) or status=PAID (FREELANCER), all in one transaction (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2458-2520)
- [ ] Marking a FREELANCER invoice PAID moves the engineer's ASSIGNED offers on those work orders (referredEngineerId = engineer, or referredEngineerId null and userId = engineer) to OfferStatus PAID (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2491-2512)
- [ ] After PAID commits, customer users get an in-app notice "Your invoice of {CUR} {total} has been marked as paid." (CLIENT invoices only) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2523-2528)
- [ ] Separate permissions per action: ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE / ACTION_VERIFY_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_SEND_*, paid endpoints — each isWrite (modules/invoices/invoices.controller.ts:249-379)

### Invoices — Overdue
- [ ] A cron job runs every day at midnight and marks invoices with dueDate < now and status in GENERATED, VERIFIED or UNPAID as OVERDUE (modules/invoices/invoice.scheduler.ts:15-47)
- [ ] The overdue sweep does not filter out soft-deleted invoices (isDelete not checked) (gap) (modules/invoices/invoice.scheduler.ts:20-35)
- [ ] Overdue transitions are recorded in status history with changedById = 1 (system user) (modules/invoices/invoice.scheduler.ts:17, 49-56)
- [ ] Only invoices that were VERIFIED or UNPAID (already released) trigger a customer overdue notice; GENERATED-to-OVERDUE ones are silent (modules/invoices/invoice.scheduler.ts:66-73)
- [ ] Overdue notice text: "Your invoice of {CUR} {grandTotal} is overdue. It was due on {DD MMM, YYYY}." — CLIENT-role, non-deleted invoices only (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2120-2143)
- [ ] PAID and OVERDUE invoices are never re-marked by the sweep (modules/invoices/invoice.scheduler.ts:23-29)

### Invoices — Customer visibility (CUSTOMER role)
- [ ] CUSTOMER users never see GENERATED invoices (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:73, 475-478)
- [ ] CUSTOMER-visible statuses are VERIFIED, UNPAID, OVERDUE, PAID (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:403-408)
- [ ] A customer requesting the GENERATED tab receives VERIFIED invoices (remap) — but the extra AND notIn GENERATED guard still applies (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:419-421)
- [ ] A customer's status filter with no allowed values falls back to all four visible statuses (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:426)
- [ ] CUSTOMER users see only CLIENT-role invoices (never engineer invoices) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:469)
- [ ] CUSTOMER scope = their own client; if that client isParentClient, also all its child clients (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:375-393)
- [ ] CUSTOMER with no linked customerId / client gets an empty list (total 0) and empty status counts (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:466-468, 604-606)
- [ ] Customer status counts always include keys VERIFIED, UNPAID, OVERDUE, PAID (0 when empty), keyed by real status (no remap) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:634-644)
- [ ] A customer opening an invoice (details or its work orders) outside their client scope or not in a visible status gets 404 (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:684-693, 1041-1050)
- [ ] A customer acting on an invoice (status update) outside their scope gets 403 "You can only act on invoices for your own clients"; on an unreleased (GENERATED/UPDATED) invoice gets 404 (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1487-1499)
- [ ] A customer approving (UNPAID) sends a confirmation "Invoice Approval Confirmation – INV-#{id}" (template invoice-approved) to toEmail and a hand-off "Customer Invoice Approved – INV-#{id}" (template invoice-approved-client) to the branch email (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2382-2408)
- [ ] Customer-user recipients of invoice notices = non-deleted CUSTOMER users of the invoice's client or its parent client, whose customer record is not deleted; deleted client → nobody (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2178-2207)
- [ ] New-invoice email is skipped when no signed-up customer user exists for the client (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2076-2082)
- [ ] New-invoice email goes to client.clientAccount.email, falling back to client.email; skipped if neither exists (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2092-2096)
- [ ] Invoice emails are sent from accounts@techdomain.com.au as "Tech Domain Accounts" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2110-2111, 2436-2437)
- [ ] In-app invoice notification: type SYSTEM, title `INV-#{id}`, url `/invoices/edit/{id}?role=CLIENT` (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2163-2170)

### Invoices — Listing, filters & search
- [ ] Invoice list/counts/details are always scoped to the requester's companyId and isDelete=false; user without companyId → Unauthorized (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:436-446)
- [ ] Pagination defaults page=1, limit=10; ordered by createdAt desc; totalPages = ceil(total/limit) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:440-442, 553, 562)
- [ ] Filter reviewedBy matches invoices with a status-history VERIFIED entry by that user; sendBy → UNPAID entry; paidBy → PAID entry; generatedBy → generatedById (utils/helpers/invoiceFilters.ts:41-85)
- [ ] Invalid status/currency/taxType filter values are silently ignored (no filter applied) (utils/helpers/invoiceFilters.ts:20-39)
- [ ] Date range filters are inclusive; end date extends to 23:59:59.999 of that day (utils/helpers/invoiceFilters.ts:3-18)
- [ ] Search matches invoice id (if numeric), comment (case-insensitive contains), client name, or engineer where every space-separated term matches first or last name (utils/helpers/invoiceFilters.ts:98-136)
- [ ] `customer` filter maps to clientId; `engineer` filter maps to engineerId; non-numeric id filter is ignored (utils/helpers/invoiceFilters.ts:87-96, 138-146)
- [ ] Linked work-order count in the list excludes removed (isDelete) join rows (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:523-531)

### Invoices — Adding / removing work orders
- [ ] Available-for-invoice picker lists company work orders that are not deleted, not templates, belong to the invoice's client (CLIENT: customerStatus APPROVED/CANCELLED_PAYABLE) or engineer (FREELANCER: status APPROVED), and are not on any live invoice of the same role; sorted by orderStartDate desc (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1576-1636)
- [ ] Cannot add work orders to a PAID invoice → 409 "This invoice has been paid and can no longer be changed." (all other statuses allow additions) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1684-1689)
- [ ] Duplicate ids in the add payload are de-duplicated (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1693)
- [ ] Added work order must be in an invoiceable status for the invoice type, else 400 "Work order {ref} is not ready to be invoiced..." (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1716-1730)
- [ ] Added work order's currency (clientCurrency / engineerCurrency) must equal invoice currency when set, else 400 "...is priced in X and cannot be added to a Y invoice." (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1736-1745)
- [ ] Added work order must belong to the invoice's client (CLIENT) or engineer (FREELANCER), else 400 "belongs to a different client/engineer" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1750-1764)
- [ ] Adding a work order already on this invoice → 409 "already on this invoice"; on another live same-role invoice → 409 "already added in Invoice #X" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1768-1783)
- [ ] Re-adding a previously removed work order revives the soft-deleted join row instead of inserting a new one (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1791-1810)
- [ ] After add: work orders set to INVOICED (customerStatus or status by role); totals recomputed across all live rows; history row UPDATED recorded; invoice status unchanged (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1813-1872)
- [ ] Removing a work order not live on the invoice → 404 "Work Order not found" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1910-1930)
- [ ] Removing requires at least one work-order snapshot, else 404 "Work order History not found" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1932-1943)
- [ ] Removal from a CLIENT invoice restores customerStatus from the latest snapshot; from a FREELANCER invoice sets status APPROVED (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1952-1962)
