- [ ] Removal from a FREELANCER invoice resets the engineer's offers on that work order to ASSIGNED (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1964-1975)
- [ ] Removal recomputes subTotal/tax/grandTotal/companyTotalExpenseCost/companyCurrency over remaining rows (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1977-2022)
- [ ] Removing a work order is allowed even when the invoice is PAID (no status guard) (gap) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1881-1908)
- [ ] Removing the last work order leaves an invoice with 0 totals (not auto-deleted) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1977-2016)

### Invoices — Deletion
- [ ] Deleting an invoice soft-deletes it (isDelete=true) and all its join rows, in any status incl. PAID (no status guard) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1344-1355)
- [ ] Deleting a CLIENT invoice resets linked work orders' customerStatus to APPROVED; a FREELANCER invoice resets status to APPROVED (even ones that were CANCELLED_PAYABLE or PAID) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1357-1371)
- [ ] Adding a note/comment is company-scoped and overwrites the single `comment` field (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1530-1563)

### Invoices — Line items & expenses shown on invoices
- [ ] CLIENT invoice line expenses = approved, non-company, non-engineer expenses added by a CLIENT user (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:735-756)
- [ ] FREELANCER invoice line expenses = approved non-company expenses that are either (isEngineerExpense=true added by CLIENT) or (isEngineerExpense=false added by FREELANCER) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:757-789)
- [ ] Company expenses (isCompanyExpense=true) are never itemised on any invoice; they only roll into FREELANCER invoice companyTotalExpenseCost (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:748, 769, 344-350)
- [ ] Expense label = "{amount} {currency}/{description if OTHER with description, else lowercase expenseType}" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:58-66)
- [ ] Purchase orders (numbers + files) are attached to work orders only on CLIENT invoices (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:854-855)
- [ ] Work-order lines on invoice detail require the viewer role CLIENT or CUSTOMER, else Unauthorized (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:669-671)

### Invoices — PDF & email rendering
- [ ] PDF rendered from dist/templates/invoice.hbs at width 350mm with background, filename invoice.pdf (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1270-1284)
- [ ] Payment type labels: HOURLY "Hourly", HALF_DAY "Half Day", FULL_DAY "Full Day", FTH "fth", FIXED "Fixed", CUSTOM_HOURS "Fixed Hours" (PER_DEVICE/BLENDED have no label) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1153-1160)
- [ ] Minimum-hours column shows payment type label for FULL_DAY/HALF_DAY/FIXED, otherwise formatted minimumHours (or 0) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1176-1183)
- [ ] Out-of-hours (OOH) column shown only on CLIENT invoices with at least one work order having nonBusinessHours > 0 (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1165-1171)
- [ ] Compensation rate text: "{amount} {cur}/{type}" + ", {rate} {cur}/Additional hour" when additional hours exist + "& {nbh} {cur}/OOH" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1174-1191)
- [ ] CLIENT invoice bills to client (name, address line 1, phone, email) from the branch (with isVat/isGst flags from branch.taxType) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1212-1224)
- [ ] FREELANCER invoice bills to the branch (full address) from the engineer (name, phone, email, ABN, personal address) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1225-1246)
- [ ] Bank account on invoice detail/PDF is decrypted before display (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1054, 1267)
- [ ] PDF generation failure → 502 "Cannot generate pdf at the moment" (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1393-1395)
- [ ] Send email: CLIENT invoice uses template invoice-email, subject "Invoice"; FREELANCER uses invoice-email-engineer, subject "System-Generated Invoice for Completed Work"; PDF always attached (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2343-2356)
- [ ] Each recipient gets a separate email; same address de-duplicated (first wording wins); CC only on the first email (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2413-2439)
- [ ] With skipEmail, invoice still transitions to UNPAID without sending mail (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2418-2447)

### Payments — Persistence (payment.service.ts)
- [ ] There is exactly one Payment row per work order (upsert keyed on unique workOrderId) (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:16-23; prisma/schema.prisma:1536)
- [ ] updateEngineerPayment / updateCustomerPayment are no-ops when the work order has no Payment row yet (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:36-38, 96-98)
- [ ] Recomputing engineer payment overwrites paymentTerms and all engineer* fields (type, hours, amount, currency, additional rate, min hours, additional hours, work-order/expense/labour cost, grand total) (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:43-60)
- [ ] Recomputing customer payment overwrites all client* fields; clientAdditionalHours defaults to 0 when missing (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:103-121)
- [ ] After an engineer payment recompute, the linked live FREELANCER invoice (first one) totals are recomputed via updateInvoice (called even if no invoice id — would fail on null) (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:80-84)
- [ ] After a customer payment recompute, the linked live CLIENT invoice totals are recomputed only if such an invoice exists (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:141-147)
- [ ] updatePaymentStatus always sets status PENDING regardless of the requested status, and stores transactionId (transactionId is unique) (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:153-160; prisma/schema.prisma:1493)
- [ ] Client amount stored per type: FULL_DAY→fullDayRate, HALF_DAY→halfDayRate, HOURLY→hourlyRate, FIXED→fixedAmount, FTH→fthRate, CUSTOM_HOURS→customHoursRate (and clientMinimumHours = customHours for CUSTOM_HOURS) (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:169-191)
- [ ] Engineer amount stored per type: FULL_DAY, HALF_DAY, HOURLY, FIXED only — FTH/CUSTOM_HOURS/PER_DEVICE/BLENDED engineer amount is null (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:193-204)
- [ ] PER_DEVICE and BLENDED have no amount mapping for client or engineer (amount null) (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:169-204)
- [ ] approvedById on payment payload = acting user (modules/payment/payment.service.ts:234)
- [ ] PaymentType enum: HOURLY, FULL_DAY, HALF_DAY, FIXED, FTH (first two hours), CUSTOM_HOURS, PER_DEVICE, BLENDED (prisma/schema.prisma:397-406)

### Payments — Calculation rules (work-orders.service.ts)
- [ ] Service fee percentage is fixed on a work order at creation from env SERVICE_FEE (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:665)
- [ ] Service fee = totalWorkOrderCost × int(serviceFeePercentage) / 100 (percentage truncated to integer via parseInt); if a penalty exists, fee = penalty × pct / 100 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7340-7350)
- [ ] Penalty is currently always 0 (all branches of getPenaltyIfCanceled return 0) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7352-7375)
- [ ] Client grand total = totalWorkOrderCost + serviceFee (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7396-7397)
- [ ] Engineer grand total = totalWorkOrderCost − serviceFee (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7394-7395, 7738-7745)
- [ ] If work order is CANCELLED and cancelled by CLIENT, grand total = totalWorkOrderCost + penalty + serviceFee (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7389-7393)
- [ ] Engineer totalWorkOrderCost and grandTotal are 0 when the work order status is CANCELLED (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7762-7775)
- [ ] Unsupported payment type (PER_DEVICE, BLENDED; and CUSTOM_HOURS for engineer) throws "Invalid payment type" during calculation (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7954-8001)
- [ ] Engineer FTH is calculated with the HOURLY formula (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7964-7966)
- [ ] Full day = 8 hours; half day = 4 hours; FTH base = 2 hours (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9234-9235, 9319, 9403, 9445)
- [ ] Client hours worked = sum of time-log hours, truncated to 3 decimals; rounded UP to next whole hour when the fractional part ≥ 15 minutes (0.25 h) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:111, 9017-9046)
- [ ] Engineer hours worked = sum of time-log hours truncated to 3 decimals (no 15-minute round-up) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9048-9065)
- [ ] Client HOURLY: labour = hourlyRate × minimumHours + additionalHours × additionalRate + nonBusinessHours × nonBusinessHourRate (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9178-9181)
- [ ] Client HOURLY: additionalHours = 0 if stored additionalHours is null (edited from timesheet), stored value if non-zero, else max(hoursWorked, minimumHours) − minimumHours (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9157-9166)
- [ ] Client HOURLY: additional hourly rate falls back to hourlyRate when additionalHourlyRate is not set (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9168-9172)
- [ ] Client HALF_DAY: labour = halfDayRate + additionalHours × hourlyRate + OOH cost; additional hours = hours beyond 4 (unless overridden) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9275-9296)
- [ ] Client FULL_DAY: labour = fullDayRate + additionalHours × hourlyRate + OOH cost; additional hours = hours beyond 8 (unless overridden), formatted to 2 decimals (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9442-9478)
- [ ] Client FTH: labour = fthRate + additionalHours × hourlyRate + OOH cost; additional hours = hours beyond 2 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9316-9336)
- [ ] Client CUSTOM_HOURS: labour = customHoursRate + additionalHours × hourlyRate + OOH cost; additional hours = hours beyond customHours (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9358-9378)
- [ ] Client FIXED: labour = fixedAmount (no additional/OOH) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9199-9212)
- [ ] totalHoursApproved = base hours (min/4/8/2/custom) + nonBusinessHours + additionalHours (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9185, 9303, 9345, 9387, 9471)
- [ ] Client expense total excludes engineer expenses (isEngineerExpense) and company expenses (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7465, 9067-9077)
- [ ] totalWorkOrderCost = totalLabourCost + totalExpenseCost (all types) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9145, 9190, 9209)
- [ ] Engineer HOURLY/FTH: billable hours = logged hours if above engineerMinimumHours, else minimumHours if >0, else 1 hour when minimum is 0 or nothing logged (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9092-9099)
- [ ] Engineer HOURLY: additionalHours = hoursWorked − minimumHours only when minimum > 0; additional rate defaults to 0 (not hourly rate) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9101-9111)
- [ ] Engineer HOURLY labour = rate × minimumHours + additional + OOH when minimum > 0, else rate × hoursWorked + OOH (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9125-9135)
- [ ] Engineer HALF_DAY/FULL_DAY: labour = engineer half/full-day rate + additionalHours × engineerHourlyRate + engineer OOH cost; thresholds 4 / 8 hours (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9231-9254, 9400-9422)
- [ ] Engineer FIXED: labour = engineerFixedAmount (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:9215-9228)
- [ ] Engineer expense total includes all of the engineer's own non-company expenses plus client-added engineer expenses (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7529-7572, 9079-9087)
- [ ] Engineer rate precedence: engineer's offer rate → work-order engineer rate → engineer profile billing rate (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7674-7715)
- [ ] Engineer FIXED amount precedence: offer fixedAmount → work-order engineerFixedAmount (no profile fallback) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7711-7715)
- [ ] Freelance-company owners get no profile billing-rate fallback (rates null) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7654-7663)
- [ ] Engineer currency precedence: offer.engineerCurrency → workOrder.engineerCurrency → engineer profile currency (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7728-7729)
- [ ] When a CLIENT views payment, the engineer side is computed for the assigned engineer (workOrder.engineerId); otherwise for the viewer (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7652)
- [ ] CLIENT viewers additionally receive company expenses and companyTotalExpenseCost (sum of amounts) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7800-7811)
- [ ] CUSTOMER viewers receive only clientRates (no engineer rates) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7812-7819)
- [ ] FREELANCER viewers get expense attachments stripped (null) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7621-7627, 7792-7794)
- [ ] companyTotalExpenseCost on Payment = sum of isCompanyExpense expenses; synced via updateMany (no-op if no Payment) (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7630-7649)
- [ ] Response includes "Cancellation fee applied due to work order cancellation." when penalty > 0 (modules/work-orders/work-orders.service.ts:7502-7504)

### Bank details — Encryption & validation
- [ ] accountNumber, iban, swiftCode, routingNumber, bsb, institutionNumber and transitNumber are stored encrypted with AES-256-GCM (format `iv:tag:ciphertext`, 12-byte random IV) (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:51-66; utils/helpers/encrypt-decrypt.ts:3-27)
- [ ] sortCode, bankCode, bankName, bankAddress, accountHolderName, currency, accountType are stored in plaintext (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:51-69)
- [ ] App refuses to start unless BANK_DATA_KEY is a 64-char hex string (utils/helpers/encrypt-decrypt.ts:8-12)
- [ ] Encrypting the same value twice yields different ciphertext (random IV) (utils/helpers/encrypt-decrypt.ts:17)
- [ ] All API responses return bank fields decrypted (create, update, list, by-branch, invoice detail/PDF) (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:90, 207, 271, 336, 394-405)
- [ ] Required on create: accountHolderName, bankName, accountNumber (strings), accountType (SAVINGS/CHECKING/CURRENT/BUSINESS/SALARY/FIXED_DEPOSIT/OTHER), currency (CurrencyType); others optional; update makes all optional (modules/bank-details/dto/create-bank-detail.dto.ts:4-63; dto/update-bank-detail.dto.ts:4)
- [ ] CLIENT "only company owner can add bank details" check is ineffective (promise not awaited, always truthy) — any CLIENT user can add bank details (gap) (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:35-45)
- [ ] ADMIN creating bank details creates them for `userId` from the body; other roles for themselves; companyId always = creator's companyId (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:47-49, 67-68)
- [ ] Non-admin users can update/delete/list only their own bank accounts; ADMIN can update any, and list/delete for a specified userId (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:115-118, 236, 362)
- [ ] Updating sortCode writes it into bankCode (sortCode itself never updated) (bug) (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:142-143)
- [ ] Setting an optional encrypted field to empty string on update clears it to null (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:158-182)
- [ ] Bank list: default page 1, limit 20, ordered createdAt desc; search (case-insensitive) matches decrypted accountNumber, iban, accountHolderName or bankName, applied in memory after decryption (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:215, 244, 272-283)
- [ ] Bank accounts by branch are scoped to requester's companyId and not deleted (any company user can see them) (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:308-314)
- [ ] Deleting a bank account is a soft delete (isDeleted=true); missing → 404 "this account doesn't exist" (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:364-386)
- [ ] Soft-deleting a bank account does not detach it from existing invoices (invoice still shows it) (modules/bank-details/bank-details.service.ts:379-386; modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:964-982)

### Freelance companies (engineer-owned companies)
- [ ] Only a FREELANCER who owns a company (ownedCompany not null) can create workers, view workers, route or assign work orders to members; others get 403 (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:93-95, 242-244, 367-369, 462-464)
- [ ] An owner cannot add themselves as a worker (email compared case-insensitively) → 400 "You cannot add yourself as a worker" (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:97-99)
- [ ] Worker email must not belong to any non-deleted user (case-insensitive) → 409 "An account with this email already exists." (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:101-108)
- [ ] Worker first/last names letters and spaces only; password min 8 chars; email lower-cased/trimmed; phone required (modules/freelanceCompany/dto/create-linked-engineer.dto.ts)
- [ ] Created worker: role FREELANCER, companyId = owner's company, isFirstLogin=true, referralSource "Freelance Company", email stored lowercase, password bcrypt-hashed (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:113-132)
- [ ] Worker gets an "Engineer" designation with permissions JOBS (read/write/update, no delete) and USERS (read only) (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:144-179)
- [ ] Worker is linked as FreelanceCompanyMember with status ACCEPTED immediately (no invite flow) (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:181-189)
- [ ] Worker receives "Welcome to Tech Domain" email (template welcome-company-user) containing their plaintext password and a login link with role=FREELANCER; supportEmail = owner's email (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:195-209)
- [ ] Worker creation (user, address, designation, permissions, membership) is atomic (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:112-192)
- [ ] Owner's worker list shows only ACCEPTED, non-deleted members; approvedOnly restricts to APPROVED users; default page 1, limit 20, sort createdAt desc (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:222-287)
- [ ] When a workOrderId is passed, worker list is restricted to members whose personal address country equals the work order's country (case-insensitive) (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:261-274)
- [ ] Worker status sort order (asc): ACTIVATED, APPROVED, SUBMITTED, PENDING, UNAPPROVE, DEACTIVATED, unknown last (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:47-60)
- [ ] Route/assign requires work order assigned to the owner's company (assignedFreelanceCompanyId), status ASSIGNED, not deleted → else 404 (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:373-384, 468-492)
- [ ] Route/assign requires target member to be ACCEPTED in the owner's company → else 404 "Member not found in your company" (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:386-397, 494-505)
- [ ] Route/assign requires the owner to hold an ASSIGNED offer on the work order → else 400 "No assigned offer found for this work order" (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:399-405, 507-513)
- [ ] Cannot route if the member already has a ROUTED/ACCEPTED offer → 400; cannot assign if member has ROUTED/ACCEPTED/ASSIGNED offer → 400 (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:407-417, 515-525)
- [ ] Assigning a member is blocked when the member has a schedule conflict (only checked if work order has start date and start time) (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:527-534)
- [ ] Routing sets engineerId = member and subStatus NOT_CONFIRMED and creates a ROUTED offer; assigning sets subStatus CONFIRMED and creates an ASSIGNED offer (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:419-441, 536-558)
- [ ] Member offer copies the owner's offer rates (hourly, half/full-day, fixed, minimumHours, engineerRate, engineerCurrency) with createdById = owner (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:425-439, 542-556)
- [ ] Member is notified (type ROUTED, title "WO-{ref}: {title}") on route ("routed to you") and assign ("assigned to you") (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:443-451, 560-568)
- [ ] Admin freelance-company list = companies whose owner has role FREELANCER; search by company name; newest first; member count counts ACCEPTED only (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:573-629)
- [ ] Vendor directory lists only companies whose FREELANCER owner is APPROVED and not deleted; a purely numeric single-term search matches company id, otherwise every term must match name or owner email (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:639-664)
- [ ] Vendor engineerCount counts ACCEPTED members who are APPROVED, not deleted FREELANCERs (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:714-728)
- [ ] Vendor jobs counts = work orders by owner + members in APPROVED/INVOICED/PAID, not deleted; workedWith = count within viewer's company > 0 (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:739-784; utils/helpers/completedWorkOrderStatuses.ts:3-22)
- [ ] Vendor owner phone/email are masked (first 6 chars + ".....") for non-customers and omitted entirely for CUSTOMER viewers; address line masked to first 2 words (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:764-776; utils/helpers/helpers.ts:1-28)
- [ ] Vendor address = owner's personal address if any of line1/city/country set, else company address (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:766-771)
- [ ] Company member list (admin/customer view) includes the owner as a synthetic first row (id "owner-{id}", isOwner) when owner not deleted, matches search, and excludeOwner is false; totalCount includes it (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:864-970)
- [ ] With approvedOnly, a company whose owner is deleted or not APPROVED → 404 "Freelance company not found" (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:864-871)
- [ ] CUSTOMER viewers get member/owner email, phone, addresses stripped and no identity documents; others see IDENTITY_PROOF documents with signed URLs (modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:810-814, 941-951)

### Companies (client companies) & company users
- [ ] Only a company owner (user with ownedCompany) can create users under a company → else 403 "Only company owners can create users under a company" (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:62-64)
- [ ] Creating a company user with an email of an existing non-deleted verified user → 409 "User already exists."; unverified → 403 "User already exists with this email, but not verified" (email match is case-sensitive here) (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:66-82)
- [ ] New company users get role CLIENT, the owner's company, createdBy = owner, the chosen designation, isFirstLogin=true, bcrypt-hashed password (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:84-113)
- [ ] New company user's personal address is created with type USER, isRemote=false, saveForFuture=false (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:115-136)
- [ ] New company user receives "Welcome" email (template welcome-company-user) with plaintext password and a role-scoped login link; supportEmail = owner email (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:206-215)
- [ ] A user may update themselves; updating another user requires being a company owner, and the target must be in the updater's company → else 403 (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:230-246)
- [ ] Updating a company user without an address creates one (type USER) and links it (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:260-296)
- [ ] Company user list includes only non-deleted CLIENT/CUSTOMER users with customerId null (i.e. excludes customer-portal users) in the company; excludes the requester unless `filter` is set; newest first (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:356-365, 423)
- [ ] Company user list with explicit `id` query lists any company's users (no ownership check) (gap) (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:336-338)
- [ ] Company list (admin) totalPages = ceil(total/limit); freelanceEngineerCount = ACCEPTED members only when owner is FREELANCER, else 0 (owner not counted) (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:563-583)
- [ ] Company list counts only non-deleted projects; work-order status breakdown uses non-deleted work orders (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:505-534)
- [ ] Deactivate allowed for company owner, ADMIN, or self; non-admins may only deactivate CLIENT/CUSTOMER users of their own company → else 403 (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:617-641)
- [ ] Deactivating a user who owns a (non-deleted) customer/client account deactivates the owner and all its CUSTOMER users and ends all their active sessions (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:643-677)
- [ ] Deactivating the company owner deactivates every CLIENT, CUSTOMER and FREELANCER user in the company (sessions NOT ended) (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:684-703)
- [ ] Deactivating a regular user sets status DEACTIVATED and ends active sessions (optionally only for a given deviceId) (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:705-724)
- [ ] Activation is allowed only for company owners or ADMIN (not self) (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:738-745)
- [ ] Activated user status = APPROVED if verified, else PENDING (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:763)
- [ ] Reactivating a customer-account owner reactivates only the owner (not its users), sends "Your Tech Domain Account Has Been Reactivated" email and, if APPROVED, a "Welcome back" notification (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:643-644, 778-806)
- [ ] Activating a regular user to APPROVED sends a PROFILE_APPROVED notification ("Welcome Aboard") and "Tech Domain - Profile Approved" email; PENDING activation sends nothing (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:808-824)
- [ ] Only the company owner (for their own company) or ADMIN can update a company → else 403 "Only the company owner can update this company" (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:926-929)
- [ ] Company update uploads documents by field: companyLogo→COMPANY_LOGO, registrationCertificate→REGISTRATION_CERTIFICATE, vatCertificate→VAT_GST_CERTIFICATE, publicLiabilityInsurance→PUBLIC_LIABILITY_INSURANCE, workerCompensationInsurance→WORKER_COMPENSATION_INSURANCE (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:940-962)
- [ ] Company without an address gets one created (type COMPANY_ADDRESS) on update (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:997-1018)
- [ ] Company details group non-deleted digital assets by category, newest first, with signed URLs (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:883-905)
- [ ] Submitting a company profile requires owning a company; sets owner status SUBMITTED and profileNeedsResubmit=false; notifies the first ADMIN ("🏢 Company Profile Submitted", url /admin/review/companies) (modules/companies/companies.service.ts:1041-1074)

### Talent pools
- [ ] Only CLIENT users can create, list or delete talent pools; otherwise "Unauthorized" (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:21-34, 86-99, 259-273)
- [ ] Talent pool names are unique per company, case-insensitive → 409 "Talent pool with this name already exists for the company" (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:36-51)
- [ ] Renaming a talent pool does NOT re-check name uniqueness or company ownership (gap) (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:66-78)
- [ ] Talent pools are listed only for the requester's company; search matches pool name or creator where every term matches first/last name; default page 1 limit 20, newest first; includes member count (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:101-135)
- [ ] Adding users requires the pool to belong to the adder's company → else 404 "Talent Pool not found" (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:158-163)
- [ ] A user can be in a given pool only once (@@unique userId+talentPoolId); duplicates are reported per user as "Already in pool" without failing the batch (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:165-196; prisma/schema.prisma:933)
- [ ] Removing a user from a pool is a hard delete and does not check the remover's company (gap) (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:200-216)
- [ ] Pools a user belongs to are only reported for the requester's own company (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:240-255)
- [ ] Deleting a talent pool is a hard delete, restricted to pools in the CLIENT's company (modules/talent-pool/talent-pool.service.ts:275-290)

### Clients (customers) — creation & validation
- [ ] Only a non-deleted user with a companyId can create/delete clients or link/unlink them ("Unauthorized." 404 otherwise) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1723-1734)
- [ ] Client name + email combination must be unique among non-deleted clients of the same company → 400 "Client with same name and email already exists" (exact, case-sensitive match) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:81-100, 140)
- [ ] A branchId, if given, must belong to the creator's company → "Branch not found" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:142-151)
- [ ] Non-parent clients require both a service delivery team (teamId) → 400 "Service delivery team is required" and an operations team (secondaryTeamId) → 400 "Operations team is required"; parent clients do not (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:153-162)
- [ ] Primary and secondary teams must exist in the creator's company and must be different → 400 "Secondary team must be different from the primary team" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:164-186)
- [ ] A parentClientId on create must reference a non-deleted client in the same company flagged isParentClient → 400 "Parent client not found or is not a parent client" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:189-200)
- [ ] isParentClient defaults to false; client address created as type CLIENT_CUSTOMER, locationType COMMERCIAL (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:214, 221-232)
- [ ] Every new client gets a ClientAccount (accounts person/email/phone, paymentTerms, taxType, taxPercentage) — one account per client (unique clientId → "Client Account already exists.") (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:238-250, 1255-1286)
- [ ] If the primary team has an email, it becomes the teamEmail of the parent client (when parentClientId given) or of the new client (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:252-257)
- [ ] Creating a client account for a client in another company → 403 "You are not allowed to add accounts for this client" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1264-1266)
- [ ] Client logo and registration certificate uploads are optional and linked after creation (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:259-284)

### Clients — listing & visibility
- [ ] Client list is scoped to clients created by users of the requester's company, non-deleted, newest first (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:336-340, 361)
- [ ] CUSTOMER users see only their own client and its child clients (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:331-334)
- [ ] Client counters count work orders in PUBLISHED, ASSIGNED, WORKDONE, APPROVED, PAID (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:380-397)
- [ ] A parent client's project/work-order/status counts are the SUM of its non-deleted child clients' counts (not its own) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:409-505)
- [ ] isSelfSignup = client.createdById equals client.ownerId (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:495)
- [ ] Count filters (projectsCount, publishedCount, etc.) are applied in memory after pagination, so totalCount may not match filtered rows (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:520-562)
- [ ] getClientData by id has no company-scope check (any authenticated caller with access to the route can read any client) (gap) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:565-594)
- [ ] Child-client list requires the parent to be a non-deleted isParentClient in the requester's company → 404 "Parent client not found" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1294-1303)
- [ ] Child clients display the parent's logo when the parent has one (overrides their own logo) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1407-1411)
- [ ] Clients available to link = same company, not a parent, not already linked, not deleted; default page 1, limit 20 (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1419-1450)

### Clients — parent/child linking rules
- [ ] Linking requires the parent to be a non-deleted isParentClient in the user's company → 404 (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1505-1513)
- [ ] All child ids must exist, be non-deleted and in the same company → 404 "One or more clients not found" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1515-1521)
- [ ] A parent client cannot be linked as a child → 400 "Client \"X\" is a parent client and cannot be linked as a child" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1524-1527)
- [ ] A client already linked to a parent cannot be re-linked → 400 "Client \"X\" is already linked to a parent" (unlink first) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1528-1529)
- [ ] Hierarchy is exactly two levels (parent → children; no grandchildren) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1523-1530)
- [ ] Unlinking a client not linked → 400 "Client is not linked to any parent" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1547)
- [ ] Unlinking a child clears it as any customer's defaultEntityId (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1549-1553)

### Clients — updates
- [ ] ADMIN may edit any company's client; others only clients of their own company → 403 "You are not allowed to update this client" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:659-675)
- [ ] Only fields sent are written (partial updates per profile card) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:748, 839-864)
- [ ] Duplicate name+email check on update runs only when name or email changes (skipped for self/admin customer-profile edits) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:777-782, 1602, 1630)
- [ ] Team requirement on update is enforced only for non-parent clients and only when teams are changed (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:784-799)
- [ ] Sending empty secondaryTeamId disconnects the operations team (then fails for non-parent clients with "Operations team is required") (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:786-799, 855-860)
- [ ] Changing the primary team copies that team's email to teamEmail on the parent client (or the client itself if no parent) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:801-812, 877-885)
- [ ] For a self-signup customer with a default entity, entity-only fields (managerName, registrationNumber, taxRegistrationNumber, accountsPerson/Email/Phone, paymentTerms, taxType, taxPercentage) are written to the default entity; both saves are atomic (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:37-47, 680-709)
- [ ] Registration certificate uploaded from a customer goes to its default entity (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:711-725)
- [ ] Blank accounts values clear the field (null); non-numeric paymentTerms/taxPercentage become null (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:58-70)
- [ ] ClientAccount update: blank taxType → null; blank/non-numeric taxPercentage → null (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1172-1179)
- [ ] A CUSTOMER can edit only the client they own (user.customerId with ownerId = user) → 403 otherwise (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1567-1587)
- [ ] Admin customer-profile edit applies only to clients that have an owner account (ownerId not null) → 404 "Customer not found" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1615-1623)
- [ ] Submitting a customer profile sets the CUSTOMER owner status SUBMITTED, profileNeedsResubmit=false, notifies an ADMIN ("🧾 Customer Profile Submitted", url /admin/review/customers) and emails the internal customer-notifications mailbox (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1640-1721)

### Clients — customer portal accounts & deletion
- [ ] Only company owners can create a customer-portal user for a client → 403 (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:902-904)
- [ ] Customer portal user email/name/phone default to the client's email/clientName/phoneNumber when not provided (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:920-923)
- [ ] Customer portal user email must not already exist (verified → 409, unverified → 403) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:925-941)
- [ ] Customer portal user gets a random password (8 random bytes, base64), role CUSTOMER, a new "Customer" designation, isFirstLogin=true, and becomes the client's owner (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:952-994)
- [ ] Customer owner permissions: USERS full, CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS full, CLIENT_PROJECT read, CLIENT_CUSTOMER read, WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS read, INVOICE read, CUSTOMER_INVOICE read, ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE read+write, CUSTOMER_USERS full (no invoice VERIFY permission) (utils/helpers/customerPermissions.ts:3-80)
- [ ] Customer portal user's personal address is copied from the client address (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:996-1017)
- [ ] Customer portal user receives a Welcome email with the generated password (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1032-1041)
- [ ] Deleting a client (same company only) soft-deletes the client, its ClientAccount and all users with customerId = client; deletes the logo from S3; unlinks its child clients (parentClientId=null) and clears it as a defaultEntity (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1052-1097)
- [ ] Deleting a client's associated account soft-deletes the owner user and clears client.ownerId; no owner → 404 "No associated account found for this client" (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:1099-1124)

### Customer users (customer portal sub-users)
- [ ] Only a CUSTOMER-role user can manage customer users → 403 "Customer users are only managed from a customer account" (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:43-45)
- [ ] The CUSTOMER must be linked to a non-deleted client in their company → 403 "This account is not linked to a customer entity" (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:47-58)
- [ ] Only the client's owner (client.ownerId = user) can create/activate/deactivate/remove customer users → 403 "Only the customer account owner can manage users" (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:60-62)
- [ ] Owner cannot add themselves (case-insensitive email) → 400 "You cannot add yourself as a user" (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:70-72)
- [ ] New customer user email must not exist among non-deleted users (case-insensitive) → 409 "An account with this email already exists." (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:74-81)
- [ ] New customer user: role CUSTOMER, same companyId and customerId as owner, email lower-cased, random 8-byte base64 password (bcrypt-hashed), isFirstLogin=true, referralSource "Customer Portal", new "Customer" designation (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:83-115)
- [ ] Customer sub-user permissions: USERS full, CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS full, CLIENT_PROJECT read, CLIENT_CUSTOMER read, WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS read, INVOICE read, CUSTOMER_INVOICE read, ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE read+write — NO CUSTOMER_USERS module (unlike the owner) (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:117-180)
- [ ] Sub-user's personal address copied from client address if the client has one (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:182-205)
- [ ] Sub-user receives "Welcome to Tech Domain" email with the generated password; login link has no role param (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:210-222)
- [ ] A customer owner's user list excludes the owner; ADMIN or a non-CUSTOMER user of the servicing company may list another customer's users (owner included, flagged isOwner) (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:235-279, 312)
- [ ] Another customer (or a different company) listing a customer's users → 403; unknown/deleted customer → 404 (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:256-270)
- [ ] Customer-user list: default page 1, limit 20, sort by id/name/status/createdAt (default createdAt desc) (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:281-306)
- [ ] Bulk activate/deactivate requires at least one id → 400 "Select at least one user"; owner cannot change own status → 400 (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:376-384)
- [ ] All target ids must be non-deleted CUSTOMER users of the owner's customer → 404 "Some users were not found under your customer account" (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:386-398)
- [ ] Deactivation skips already-DEACTIVATED users; if none left → 400 "The selected users are already deactivated"; deactivation ends active sessions (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:400-420)
- [ ] Activation only affects DEACTIVATED users (else 400 "Only a deactivated account can be activated"); verified → APPROVED, unverified → PENDING (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:423-455)
- [ ] Removing a customer user: owner cannot remove self → 400; target must be under the owner's customer → 404 (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:474-493)
- [ ] Removal writes a DeletedAccount record (reason trimmed, deletedById = owner), soft-deletes and anonymises the user (email `deleted-{id}@deleted.invalid`, phone and password emptied) and ends sessions, atomically (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:495-520)
- [ ] Bulk remove de-duplicates ids and processes each through single removal (stops on first failure, earlier removals persist) (modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:460-472)

### Projects
- [ ] Projects require a non-deleted company user; creating requires the client to be non-deleted and in the same company → 404 "Client not found" (modules/projects/projects.service.ts:36-46, 279-290)
- [ ] Project stores creator (userId), companyId and isPoRequired flag (modules/projects/projects.service.ts:48-60)
- [ ] Project list is company-scoped, non-deleted, optional clientId filter, case-insensitive title search, newest first; unpaginated when no limit (modules/projects/projects.service.ts:107-128)
- [ ] Viewing/updating/deleting a project outside the user's company or deleted → 404 "Project not found" / 403 "You are not allowed to update this project" (modules/projects/projects.service.ts:153-170, 199-205, 255-260)
- [ ] Project update does not verify the new clientId belongs to the company (gap); errors in update are swallowed (returns undefined) (modules/projects/projects.service.ts:218-250)
- [ ] Project delete is a soft delete; the contract file is removed from S3 and unlinked (modules/projects/projects.service.ts:262-272)
- [ ] Project contract uploaded on create uses the client-asset uploader, on update the project-asset uploader (inconsistent category) (modules/projects/projects.service.ts:73-78, 232-237)

### Branches
- [ ] Only CLIENT-role, non-deleted users can create, list, update or delete branches (modules/branches/branches.service.ts:13-27, 72-86, 150-163, 235-248)
- [ ] A branch is always created under the company OWNED by the creator — a non-owner CLIENT fails (company null) (modules/branches/branches.service.ts:28-43)
- [ ] Branch address is created with type BRANCH_ADDRESS; branch carries taxType (GST/VAT/OTHERS) and taxNumber used on invoices (modules/branches/branches.service.ts:44-56; modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:1220-1224)
- [ ] Branch list is company-scoped, non-deleted; search on name or email (case-insensitive); unpaginated when no limit (modules/branches/branches.service.ts:88-112)
- [ ] Updating a branch requires it to belong to the user's company → 404 "Branch not found" (deleted branches can still be updated) (modules/branches/branches.service.ts:165-180)
- [ ] Deleting a branch is a soft delete scoped to company and non-deleted (Prisma throws if not found) (modules/branches/branches.service.ts:250-262)

### Departments
- [ ] Only the company owner can create/update/delete departments → 403 "Only the company owner can manage departments" (modules/departments/departments.service.ts:47-58)
- [ ] Department names are unique per company, case-insensitive (409 "A department with this name already exists"); DB unique (companyId, name) is a backstop (modules/departments/departments.service.ts:18-31, 162-169; prisma/schema.prisma:1013)
- [ ] All teamIds assigned to a department must be non-deleted teams of the same company → 400 "One or more selected teams are invalid" (modules/departments/departments.service.ts:34-44)
- [ ] Saving a department's teamIds attaches listed teams and detaches previously attached teams not listed (a team belongs to at most one department) (modules/departments/departments.service.ts:71-94)
- [ ] Department list/detail are company-scoped; users without a company get an empty list (modules/departments/departments.service.ts:171-220)
- [ ] Deleting a department is a hard delete that first detaches all its teams, atomically (modules/departments/departments.service.ts:222-241)

### Teams
- [ ] Creating a team requires the user to belong to a company; optional departmentId must be in the same company → 400 "Selected department is invalid" (modules/teams/teams.service.ts:17-44)
- [ ] Team members are added via TeamMember rows (unique teamMemberId+teamId); member ids are not validated to belong to the company (gap) (modules/teams/teams.service.ts:56-66; prisma/schema.prisma:976)
- [ ] Updating a team has no company-ownership check (any caller with the permission can edit any team by id) (gap) (modules/teams/teams.service.ts:71-83)
- [ ] Update: departmentId number moves the team, null detaches it; members array replaces the whole member list (modules/teams/teams.service.ts:87-114)
- [ ] Team list is company-scoped, non-deleted, optional department filter, name search, newest first (modules/teams/teams.service.ts:140-165)
- [ ] Team members lookup by team ids is limited to members in the requester's company; with no teamIds returns all company users; de-duplicated by id (modules/teams/teams.service.ts:205-275)
- [ ] Deleting a team is a hard delete of its members then the team, with no company check (gap) (modules/teams/teams.service.ts:281-285)
- [ ] Non-parent clients must have a service-delivery team and a different operations team (see Clients) (modules/clients/clients.service.ts:153-186)

### Categories, vendors, sub-categories, qualifications, locations
- [ ] Categories/vendors/sub-categories lists exclude soft-deleted rows; categories sorted by name asc, vendors by name asc, sub-categories by subCategory asc (modules/categories/categories.service.ts:91-212)
- [ ] List pagination applies only when limit > 0; page defaults to 1; without limit all rows are returned and limit = total (utils/helpers/pagination.ts:1-15; modules/categories/categories.service.ts:114-119)
- [ ] Vendor search matches vendor name or category name; sub-category search matches sub-category, vendor name or category name (case-insensitive) (modules/categories/categories.service.ts:127-141, 170-188)
- [ ] Sub-categories belong to vendors (vendorId), vendors to categories (categoryId): hierarchy Category → Vendor → SubCategory (modules/categories/categories.service.ts:51-69, 214-249)
- [ ] Bulk create uses skipDuplicates, but Category/Vendor/SubCategory/QualificationType have no unique constraints, so duplicates are NOT skipped (gap) (modules/categories/categories.service.ts:38-69; modules/qualifications/qualifications.service.ts:28-53)
- [ ] Qualification type names must be unique case-insensitively on single create → 409 "Qualification type already exists" (bulk create does not check) (modules/qualifications/qualifications.service.ts:10-39)
- [ ] Qualification list default page 1, limit 20, sorted by name asc (modules/qualifications/qualifications.service.ts:55-75)
- [ ] Saved locations = the user's own non-deleted addresses with saveForFuture=true (modules/locations/locations.service.ts:17-33)
- [ ] A user can delete (soft) only their own saved location → "Location not found or access denied" (modules/locations/locations.service.ts:38-51)
- [ ] Location search and place details are delegated to Google autocomplete/place details (modules/locations/locations.service.ts:35-37, 53-55)

### Digital assets (files, documents, signed URLs)
- [ ] Every upload requires a file → 400 "File is required" (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:363, 407, 449, 510, 567, 627, 683, 719, 1366, 1446, 1769, 1803)
- [ ] Profile pictures are resized to 500×500 JPEG quality 75; uploading a new one soft-deletes the previous (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:358-400)
- [ ] A user holds at most one live document per category via uploadUserDigitalAsset (previous one soft-deleted) (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:402-446)
- [ ] Client logo, client registration certificate, project contract, purchase-order file and expense file uploads REPLACE the existing asset in place (same row updated, old S3 object deleted) (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:448-620, 1358-1516)
- [ ] Company documents (per category) replace all previous live assets of that category: old S3 objects deleted, old rows soft-deleted, new row created (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:621-673)
- [ ] S3 key layout: `{userId}/profile`, `{userId}/documents`, `{userId}/clients/{clientId}`, `{userId}/projects/{projectId}`, `{userId}/companies/{companyId}`, `{userId}/workOrders/{id}/customerFiles|deliverables|messages|purchaseOrder/{po}|expenses/{expenseId}`, `{userId}/invoices/{invoiceId}`, `teamTasks/{taskId}/{userId}` (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:384, 429, 470, 588, 641, 1277, 1389, 1468, 1525, 1566, 1620, 1815)
- [ ] Signed URLs are regenerated on every read with expiry AWS_S3_OBJECT_EXPIRY_SECONDS; the cached url/urlExpiration in DB is refreshed only when expired (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:15, 1676-1707)
- [ ] An asset without a path yields a null URL (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1679)
- [ ] Users may delete only their own assets (soft delete) — "Digital asset not found" otherwise (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:803-816)
- [ ] Exception: WORK_ORDER_CUSTOMER_FILE assets on a work order can be deleted by any CLIENT of the work order's company, or by a CUSTOMER who created the work order or whose client scope (own client + children if parent) owns it (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:752-801)
- [ ] Uploading/deleting work-order customer files writes a work-order log (CUSTOMER_FILE_UPLOADED / CUSTOMER_FILE_DELETED) with unchanged statuses (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:832-840, 1301-1307, 1319-1356)
- [ ] Deliverable deletion is restricted to the uploader (optionally by category) (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:845-869)
- [ ] ID documents are OCR-checked: text shorter than 10 chars fails; valid only if it contains one of the multilingual "front" keywords (name, DOB, passport, etc.) (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1206-1218)
- [ ] OCR preprocessing: image resized to width 1000, grayscale, sharpened, normalised; PDFs converted first page only at 300 DPI, 1000×1300 (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1220-1263)
- [ ] Multiple ID documents upload as IDENTITY_PROOF within a single transaction (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1167-1204)
- [ ] Background-verification WORKING_RIGHTS URL is generated from the EDUCATION_CERTIFICATE asset (bug) (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:942-947)
- [ ] Invoice payment proof is stored as WORK_ORDER_INVOICE_PROOF linked to the invoice; download returns the first live proof (no ordering) or nulls when none (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1797-1854)
- [ ] Only the first file of the PAID upload is stored as proof (file[0]) (modules/invoices/invoices.service.ts:2459-2464)
- [ ] Purchase-order files list newest first (by createdAt desc) per work order (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1064-1123)
- [ ] A user's work-order documents exclude deleted assets and assets of deleted work orders, newest first (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1864-1901)
- [ ] Resume deletion soft-deletes the user's live RESUME → 400 "No resume found for this user" if none (modules/digital-assets/digital-assets.service.ts:1148-1160)

### Schema enums & unique constraints (data-level rules)
- [ ] InvoiceStatus: GENERATED (default), VERIFIED, UNPAID, OVERDUE, PAID, UPDATED (prisma/schema.prisma:25-32, 1738)
- [ ] TaxType: GST, VAT, OTHERS (prisma/schema.prisma:34-38)
- [ ] AccountType: SAVINGS, CHECKING, CURRENT, BUSINESS, SALARY, FIXED_DEPOSIT, OTHER (prisma/schema.prisma:429-437)
- [ ] ExpenseType: TRAVEL, TOOLS, MATERIAL, ALL, OTHER (prisma/schema.prisma:439-445)
- [ ] OfferStatus: PENDING, ASSIGNED, REJECTED, COUNTER, DECLINED, ROUTED, ACCEPTED, PAID, CANCELLED (prisma/schema.prisma:575-585)
- [ ] Invoice money fields (taxAmount, subTotal, grandTotal, totalAmount) are Float; taxPercentage nullable Float; companyTotalExpenseCost defaults 0 (prisma/schema.prisma:1739-1747)
- [ ] InvoiceWorkOrder has NO unique (invoiceId, workOrderId) — duplicate prevention is application-level only (prisma/schema.prisma:1774-1786)
- [ ] One Payment per work order (Payment.workOrderId unique); Payment.transactionId unique (prisma/schema.prisma:1493, 1536)
- [ ] One company per owner (Company.ownerId unique) (prisma/schema.prisma:835)
- [ ] One ClientAccount per client (ClientAccount.clientId unique) (prisma/schema.prisma:2101)
- [ ] A user can own at most one client (Client.ownerId unique); a client can be the default entity of at most one customer (Client.defaultEntityId unique) (prisma/schema.prisma:2127, 2157)
- [ ] A user can be a member of a given freelance company once (FreelanceCompanyMember userId+companyId unique) (prisma/schema.prisma:948)
- [ ] One offer per user per work order (Offer userId+workOrderId unique) — routing/assigning a member who already has any offer (e.g. DECLINED) on the work order fails at DB level (prisma/schema.prisma:1981; modules/freelanceCompany/freelanceCompany.service.ts:425, 542)
- [ ] Department name unique per company; TeamMember (teamMemberId, teamId) unique; TalentPoolUser (userId, talentPoolId) unique (prisma/schema.prisma:1013, 976, 933)
- [ ] Work order referenceId is a unique DB sequence (workorder_ref_seq) (prisma/schema.prisma:1803)
- [ ] User email has NO DB unique constraint — uniqueness among non-deleted users is enforced only in services (prisma/schema.prisma:1113; modules/customer-users/customer-users.service.ts:74-81)

## 4. Auth, Users, Permissions & Admin


### Auth: Password login (local strategy)
- [ ] Login uses `email` as the username field (modules/auth/local.strategy.ts:9)
- [ ] Email lookup on login is trimmed and case-insensitive (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:104)
- [ ] Unknown email returns 404 "Invalid email or password" (same message as wrong password) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:110)
- [ ] Soft-deleted user (`isDeleted=true`) returns 404 "User not found" on login (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:113)
- [ ] Wrong password (bcrypt compare fails) returns 404 "Invalid email or password" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:117)
- [ ] Successful credential validation attaches role permissions and strips `password` from the returned user (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:333)
- [ ] Login fails with 502 "Please clear the app from background and Try again." if request has no `deviceInfo.deviceType` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:133)
- [ ] User with status `DEACTIVATED` gets 403 with `deactivated: true` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:139)
- [ ] Deactivated customer sub-user (has `customer` and `customer.ownerId !== user.id`) gets the "deactivated by your organisation's administrator" message and `isCustomerUser: true`; others get "Your account is not active. Please contact support" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:140)
- [ ] Unverified user (`isVerified === false`) triggers an OTP resend to their email and gets 307 "Please verify email" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:152)
- [ ] Deactivation check runs before email-verification check (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:139)
- [ ] Each login creates a new active `Session` storing device info (ip, deviceType, brand, model, os, osVersion, platform, appVersion, deviceId, fcmToken, browserName) and `lastActivityAt=now` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:183)
- [ ] JWT payload = `{ sub: userId, sessionId, permissions: user.role.permissions }` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:202)
- [ ] Refresh token signed with `JWT_REFRESH_SECRET`, lifetime from `JWT_REFRESH_EXPIRES_IN` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:204)
- [ ] Refresh token stored only as a bcrypt hash (10 rounds) on the session (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:209)
- [ ] Session `expiresAt` = now + 7 days on login (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:217)
- [ ] Access token signed with `JWT_SECRET`, lifetime from `JWT_ACCESS_EXPIRES_IN` (comment states access token expires in 5 minutes) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:221; modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:53)
- [ ] Login response user includes `profilePicture`, `teams`, `isCompanyOwner` (owns a company), `isCompanyMember` (FreelanceCompanyMember ACCEPTED), `hasPendingCompanyInvite` (FreelanceCompanyMember PENDING), `isCustomerOwner` (owns a non-deleted Client) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:226)
- [ ] `GET /auth/session` returns the same enriched shape as login (permissions + flags) for the JWT user; 404 if user missing (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:349)
- [ ] Signup without a social ticket creates the user and responds "Verification email sent" (OTP verification required) (modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:282)

### Auth: JWT validation / sessions
- [ ] JWT read from `Authorization: Bearer`, expiration enforced, verified with `JWT_SECRET` (modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:11)
- [ ] Every authenticated request requires an `isActive=true` session matching `sessionId` + `sub`, else 401 "Session invalid or logged out" (modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:17)
- [ ] Session past `expiresAt` → 401 "Session expired" (modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:33)
- [ ] Missing user → 401 "User not found"; `isDeleted` → 401 "User deleted"; status `DEACTIVATED` → 401 "User deactivated" (immediate lockout of existing tokens) (modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:47)
- [ ] `req.user` = `{ id, sessionId, permissions }` where permissions come from the token payload (snapshot at login) (modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:58)

### Auth: Refresh / logout
- [ ] Refresh requires non-empty `oldRefreshToken` else 400 "Refresh token is required" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:243)
- [ ] Refresh token verified with `JWT_REFRESH_SECRET`; invalid/expired → 401 "Invalid or expired refresh token" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:252)
- [ ] Refresh requires an active session for (sessionId, sub) whose stored hash matches the presented token, else 401 "Refresh token does not match any active session" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:266)
- [ ] Refresh rotates the refresh token (new hash stored), resets session `expiresAt` to now + 7 days and updates `lastActivityAt` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:284)
- [ ] Refreshed permissions are copied from the old token payload (not recalculated) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:260)
- [ ] BUG/CHECK: expired-session check reads `matchedSession?.expiresAt` on a boolean, so it never fires (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:276)
- [ ] BUG/CHECK: new access token on refresh is signed with `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET` while JwtStrategy verifies with `JWT_SECRET` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:300; modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:13)
- [ ] Logout verifies refresh token, requires matching active session hash, then sets session `isActive=false` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:308)
- [ ] Logout with non-matching token → 503 "Something went wrong" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:320)

### Auth: Passwords
- [ ] Passwords hashed with bcrypt using `SALT` env rounds on change/reset/create-new (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:558)
- [ ] Change password requires correct old password else 400 "Wrong password" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:552)
- [ ] Change password: `newPassword` minimum 8 chars (no complexity regex) (modules/auth/dto/change-password.dto.ts:10)
- [ ] Reset password: `newPassword` must be 8–20 chars with a lowercase, uppercase, digit and a symbol from `!@#$%^&*` (modules/auth/dto/reset-password.dto.ts:11)
- [ ] Forgot password looks up non-deleted user by trimmed, case-insensitive email; unknown email returns `sent:false` "This is not a registered email" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:505; modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:302)
- [ ] Reset token = nanoid(64), expires 15 minutes after issue (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:512)
- [ ] Reset link is `${FRONTEND_BASE_URL}/resetPassword?token=...&role=<UserType>` (role appended with `&` when URL already has `?`) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:513; utils/helpers/withRoleParam.ts:12)
- [ ] Reset email subject "Tech Domain - Reset Password", template `reset-password` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:524)
- [ ] Unknown reset token → 300 "Invalid link"; expired (expiresAt <= now) → 404 "Expired link" (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:568; utils/helpers/dateTimeUtility.ts:12)
- [ ] Reset password sets `isFirstLogin=false` (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:584)
- [ ] Reset token is not deleted/invalidated after use (reusable until expiry) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:562)
- [ ] Reset from iOS mobile returns "Please return to the mobile app and log in to continue." (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:586)
- [ ] Create-new-password (authenticated, first-login flow) sets password and `isFirstLogin=false`, no old password required (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:594)

### Auth: Social login (Google / Apple via Firebase, Microsoft)
- [ ] Supported social providers: `google`, `apple` (Firebase sign-in methods `google.com`, `apple.com`); others → 401 "Sign-in method ... is not supported" (modules/auth/social/firebase-token.service.ts:5)
- [ ] Firebase token required and verified (signature, issuer, audience, expiry); invalid → 401 "Invalid or expired token" (modules/auth/social/firebase-token.service.ts:31)
- [ ] Identity keyed on provider subject id (not Firebase uid); missing → 401 (modules/auth/social/firebase-token.service.ts:62)
- [ ] Firebase display name split on first whitespace into first/last name (modules/auth/social/firebase-token.service.ts:70)
- [ ] Google `emailVerified` defaults to false unless profile or raw claim says verified (modules/auth/google.strategy.ts:33)
- [ ] Social profile with no email → REJECT `NO_EMAIL` (modules/auth/social/social-identity.service.ts:26)
- [ ] Resolution order: existing (provider, providerUserId) link first → LOGIN even if email changed (modules/auth/social/social-identity.service.ts:34)
- [ ] No link but user with same email (trimmed, case-insensitive, deleted users included) → LINK only if provider verified the email, else REJECT `UNVERIFIED_EMAIL` (modules/auth/social/social-identity.service.ts:56)
- [ ] No link and no user → SIGNUP (modules/auth/social/social-identity.service.ts:60)
- [ ] Deleted user → REJECT `ACCOUNT_DELETED`; `DEACTIVATED` → REJECT `ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED` (both for LOGIN and LINK) (modules/auth/social/social-identity.service.ts:120)
- [ ] LINK outcome records the SocialAccount before tokens are issued (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:437)
- [ ] Provider email changes never overwrite `User.email` (only SocialAccount.email) (modules/auth/social/social-identity.service.ts:112)
- [ ] OAuth `state` JWT expires in 10m, signed with `JWT_SECRET + ":oauth-state"`; invalid/missing state defaults to intent `login` (modules/auth/social/social-flow.service.ts:41)
- [ ] OAuth role query accepted only as `TECH` or `CLIENT`; intent `signup` only when query `intent=signup` (modules/auth/social/google-oauth.guard.ts:23)
- [ ] Signup ticket JWT expires in 15m, signed with `JWT_SECRET + ":signup-ticket"`; invalid → 400 "This signup link has expired. Please sign in again." (modules/auth/social/social-flow.service.ts:71)
- [ ] Web Google callback: SIGNUP with login intent redirects to `/login?socialError=NO_ACCOUNT` (+ masked email) rather than signup (modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:73)
- [ ] Web Google callback: SIGNUP with signup intent redirects to `/clientSignup`, `/techSignup`, or `/getStarted` (no role) with ticket (modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:90)
- [ ] Web Google success redirects to `/socialCallback?accessToken&refreshToken`; errors → `/login?socialError=PROVIDER_ERROR` (modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:63)
- [ ] Mobile social login returns `status:signup` with `reason: NO_ACCOUNT`, a ticket and prefill (email, emailMasked, names); rejects → 403 with reason (modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:119)
- [ ] Social signup completion: email taken from ticket (never body), `emailVerified=true`, no OTP step (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:465; modules/auth/auth.controller.ts:269)
- [ ] Social signup completion when account already exists/linked → 409 "This account already exists. Please sign in." (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:473)
- [ ] Social-signup users get a random 96-hex-char placeholder password (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:480)
- [ ] Microsoft login requires an existing non-deleted user with matching email (case-insensitive) else 404 "No user linked" (no auto signup) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:411)
- [ ] BUG/CHECK: Microsoft login passes a raw user row to login() without attached permissions/role relations (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:417)

### Auth: Default permissions auto-provisioned on login (attachPermissions)
- [ ] Permissions are attached per role: ADMIN, FREELANCER, CLIENT, CUSTOMER; MODERATOR gets an empty permission list (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:333)
- [ ] ADMIN with no designation: auto-create designation "Admin" ("auto generated on login") with full R/W/U/D on ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT, USERS, COMPANIES, MANAGE, WORK_EXPERIENCE, QUALIFICATIONS and assign it (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:610)
- [ ] ADMIN with a designation: uses existing designation permissions unchanged (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:702)
- [ ] FREELANCER with no designation: auto-create designation "Engineer" with full R/W/U/D on JOBS and USERS (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:706)
- [ ] CLIENT company owner with no designation: auto-create "Client" designation with full R/W/U/D on COMPANIES, WORK_ORDERS, ACTION_APPROVE_WORK_ORDER, ACTION_VERIFY_WORK_ORDER, ACTION_REVIEW_WORK_ORDER, ACTION_CANCEL_WORK_ORDER, USERS, CLIENT_CUSTOMER, CLIENT_PROJECT, JOBS, CLIENT_BANK_ACCOUNTS, COMPANY_BRANCHES, INVOICE, CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_SEND_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_PAID_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_PAID_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ENGINEER_INVOICE, WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS, WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL, ENGINEER_SKILLS_SET, USER_PERMISSIONS (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:777)
- [ ] CLIENT owner default WORK_FORCE_COVERAGE_MAP is read-only (isRead only) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:989)
- [ ] CLIENT non-owner with no designation: "Client" designation created with ZERO permissions (non-owner defaults commented out) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1014)
- [ ] CLIENT owner with existing designation: on every login, any module in `ClientPermissionModules` missing from the designation is added with full R/W/U/D (existing rows untouched) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1090)
- [ ] CLIENT non-owner with designation: permissions returned unchanged (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1133)
- [ ] CUSTOMER owner (company.ownerId === user.id) with no designation: auto-create "Customer" designation with USERS (full), CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS (full R/W/U/D), CLIENT_PROJECT (read), CLIENT_CUSTOMER (read), WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS (read) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1148)
- [ ] CUSTOMER non-owner with no designation: "Customer" designation created with no permissions (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1147)
- [ ] BUG/CHECK: giveClientPermissions/giveCustomerPermissions read `user.company.ownerId` — throws if user has no company (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:777)
- [ ] CUSTOMER with existing designation: required rows INVOICE (read), CUSTOMER_INVOICE (read), ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE (read+write), CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS (R/W/U/D) are created if missing or have missing flags topped up (never downgraded) on login (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1252)
- [ ] CUSTOMER who owns a non-deleted Client entity additionally requires CUSTOMER_USERS (R/W/U/D) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1273)
- [ ] CUSTOMER logins revoke (delete) ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE always, and CUSTOMER_USERS when they do not own a Client entity (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:1286)

### OTP
- [ ] OTP is 6 numeric digits (Math.random based) (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:20; utils/helpers/codeGenerator.ts:1)
- [ ] OTP default use case is `SIGNUP` (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:19)
- [ ] OTP expires 5 minutes after creation (getExpiry default) (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:25; utils/helpers/dateTimeUtility.ts:6)
- [ ] No resend limit / cooldown and no attempt counter: each resend creates a new OTP row; older OTPs are not invalidated (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:33)
- [ ] Resend looks up non-deleted user by trimmed, lower-cased, case-insensitive email; unknown → 404 "User not found" (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:34)
- [ ] OTP email subject " Tech Domain - Verify OTP", template `verify-otp` (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:54)
- [ ] OTP is looked up by code + useCase only (not scoped to user in the lookup); not found → 404 "Invalid OTP" (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:133)
- [ ] BUG/CHECK: `findFirst` by code across all users — a colliding code for another user yields "User not found" (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:133)
- [ ] Expired OTP (expiresAt <= now) → 400 "Expired OTP" (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:146)
- [ ] OTP must belong to the user whose email (trimmed, case-insensitive) is submitted, else 404 "User not found" (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:152)
- [ ] OTP is not consumed/deleted after successful verification (reusable within 5 min) (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:130)
- [ ] Verify request requires valid `email` and non-empty `code` (modules/otps/dto/verify-otp.dto.ts:4)
- [ ] Non-ACCOUNT_DELETE verification sets `isVerified=true` (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:208)
- [ ] On verification, CLIENT or CUSTOMER users with a `createdById` (created by someone else) become `APPROVED`; all others keep their current status (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:209)
- [ ] Successful SIGNUP verification auto-logs-in: creates active session (no device info), 7-day expiresAt, returns accessToken + refreshToken (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:216)
- [ ] OTP-login token permissions come from Permission rows with `userId` = user (not designation) (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:190)
- [ ] ACCOUNT_DELETE use case does not mark verified or create a session; returns a single `token` signed with default JWT options (no sessionId) (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:254)
- [ ] Verification response includes `isCustomerOwner` (owns non-deleted Client) (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:258)
- [ ] After SIGNUP verification, Tech Domain gets a customer registration notice only for CUSTOMER role users who self-signed up (ownedClient.createdById === ownedClient.ownerId) (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:77, 107, 114)
- [ ] Registration notice `accountStatus` = "Active" if status APPROVED else "Pending Approval"; `registeredAt` = user.createdAt (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:124)
- [ ] Registration notice email is sent after (outside) the verification transaction (modules/otps/otps.service.ts:75)

### Users: Signup / account creation
- [ ] Signup first/last name must match `^[A-Za-z\s]+$` (letters and spaces only) (modules/users/dto/create-user.dto.ts:46)
- [ ] Signup email must be valid and is lower-cased by the DTO (modules/users/dto/create-user.dto.ts:58)
- [ ] Signup phone number is required (modules/users/dto/create-user.dto.ts:63)
- [ ] Signup password required unless a social `ticket` is present; must be 8–20 chars with lower, upper, digit, and one of `!@#$%^&*` (modules/users/dto/create-user.dto.ts:75)
- [ ] Duplicate email check is case-insensitive and includes deleted users (modules/users/users.service.ts:231)
- [ ] Signup with email of a deleted account → 403 "Account deleted. Please contact support." (modules/users/users.service.ts:241)
- [ ] Signup with email of a verified user → 409 "User already exists." (modules/users/users.service.ts:244)
- [ ] Signup with email of an unverified user → OTP resent and 403 "User already exists with this email, OTP code sent" (modules/users/users.service.ts:246)
- [ ] NOTE: all create() errors are re-thrown as HTTP 500 with the original message (status codes above are lost) (modules/users/users.service.ts:412)
- [ ] Social signups are created `isVerified=true`; email signups `isVerified=false` (modules/users/users.service.ts:228)
- [ ] Every new user is created with the FREELANCER role first, email lower-cased, bcrypt(`SALT`) password (modules/users/users.service.ts:2486)
- [ ] Every new user gets a USER-type Address from the signup payload, linked as `personalAddressId` (modules/users/users.service.ts:2509)
- [ ] Signup branch precedence: `customerCompanyName` → customer account; else `name` → client company; else FREELANCER engineer setup (modules/users/users.service.ts:257)
- [ ] Email signups get a 6-digit OTP emailed ("Tech Domain - Verify OTP"); social signups skip OTP (modules/users/users.service.ts:391)

#### Customer self-signup
- [ ] Customer signup requires a configured CUSTOMER role else 500 "Customer role is not configured" (modules/users/users.service.ts:2259)
- [ ] Customer signup places the account under the default Tech Domain company (`TECHDOMAIN_COMPANY_ID`) when set (modules/users/users.service.ts:2263)
- [ ] Customer signup creates designation "Customer", a parent Client (`isParentClient=true`, owner = user) and a child entity Client (`isParentClient=false`) linked to the parent; parent `defaultEntityId` = child (modules/users/users.service.ts:2267)
- [ ] Customer company email defaults to the user's email when `companyEmail` omitted (modules/users/users.service.ts:265)
- [ ] Customer signup sets user role CUSTOMER, `customerId` = parent client, assigned designation = Customer (modules/users/users.service.ts:2308)
- [ ] Customer owner default permissions: USERS (R/W/U/D), CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS (R/W/U/D), CLIENT_PROJECT (R), CLIENT_CUSTOMER (R), WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS (R), INVOICE (R), CUSTOMER_INVOICE (R), ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE (R+W), CUSTOMER_USERS (R/W/U/D); no ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE (utils/helpers/customerPermissions.ts:3)
- [ ] Social customer signup immediately sends the customer registration notice with status "Pending Approval" (modules/users/users.service.ts:274)

#### Client company signup
- [ ] Client signup (`name` present) creates a company owned by the user, designation "Company Owner", switches role to CLIENT and sets companyId (modules/users/users.service.ts:2330)
- [ ] Client company owner gets full R/W/U/D on every module in `ClientPermissionModules` (modules/users/users.service.ts:2346)

#### Engineer (FREELANCER) signup
- [ ] Engineer with `freelanceCompanyName` creates a company they own with noOfEmployees/revenue/frequency/urgency = "N/A" and keeps FREELANCER role (modules/users/users.service.ts:299)
- [ ] Engineer without a company is assigned the default `TECHDOMAIN_COMPANY_ID` company (if configured) (modules/users/users.service.ts:310)
- [ ] Engineer signup creates designation "Engineer" with JOBS (R/W/U, no delete) and USERS (read only) (modules/users/users.service.ts:316)
- [ ] Engineer freelance-company owner additionally gets FREELANCE_COMPANY_WORKERS (R/W/U/D) (modules/users/users.service.ts:354)

### Users: Account deletion
- [ ] FREELANCER may only delete their own account (else 403) (modules/users/users.service.ts:425)
- [ ] CLIENT may delete an account only if they own a company or it is their own (else 403) (modules/users/users.service.ts:428)
- [ ] ADMIN (and other roles) not restricted by deleteAccount ownership checks (modules/users/users.service.ts:425)
- [ ] Target must exist and not already be deleted (404) (modules/users/users.service.ts:439)
- [ ] Deletion reason required unless one is already stored (400 "Reason is required") (modules/users/users.service.ts:445)
- [ ] Stored deletion reason is prefixed "admin: ", "client: " or "engineer: " by the deleter's role (modules/users/users.service.ts:448)
- [ ] FREELANCER self-deletion is two-step: without OTP an ACCOUNT_DELETE OTP is emailed; with OTP it must verify (modules/users/users.service.ts:459)
- [ ] Deleted user marked `isDeleted=true` (modules/users/users.service.ts:480)
- [ ] Deleted FREELANCER: personal digital assets (PROFILE_PICTURE, RESUME, CERTIFICATE, IDENTITY_PROOF, EDUCATION_CERTIFICATE, WORKING_RIGHTS, INSURANCE_DOC, SIGNATURE, USER_QUALIFICATION) are removed from S3 and DB (modules/users/users.service.ts:498)
- [ ] Deleted FREELANCER: sessions, OTPs, reset tokens, education, employment, languages, bank accounts, skills, qualifications, chat read statuses and talent-pool memberships are hard-deleted (modules/users/users.service.ts:530)
- [ ] Deleted FREELANCER: a `DeletedAccount` audit row (userId, email, role, deletedById, reason) is created (modules/users/users.service.ts:543)
- [ ] Deleted FREELANCER: personal address soft-deleted and PII fields nulled (modules/users/users.service.ts:562)
- [ ] Deleted FREELANCER row kept as ghost: email → `deleted-<id>@deleted.invalid`, phone '' , password '', nationality null, personalAddressId null; name retained (modules/users/users.service.ts:586)
- [ ] Deleting a CLIENT also soft-deletes non-deleted CLIENT/CUSTOMER users in the same company whose `createdById` = deleted user, and emails them (modules/users/users.service.ts:598)
- [ ] Deleting a CLIENT who was not created by anyone (company owner, `createdById === null`) soft-deletes all the company's work orders (modules/users/users.service.ts:616)
- [ ] Deletion emails "Tech Domain - Account Deletion" (template `delete-account`) to the deleted user and each cascaded user (modules/users/users.service.ts:625)

### Users: Profile read / visibility
- [ ] `findOne` excludes deleted users (modules/users/users.service.ts:652)
- [ ] Manual email verification: lower-cased trimmed exact match; 404 if not found, 400 "Email is already verified" if verified (modules/users/users.service.ts:674)
- [ ] Viewing (non-self, non-admin, non-direct) an ACCEPTED freelance-company member returns the company OWNER's profile instead, unless the viewer is that owner (modules/users/users.service.ts:725)
- [ ] Customer entity details on a profile are shown only to the entity owner; non-owner customer users get `customer: null` (modules/users/users.service.ts:868)
- [ ] Company assets come from ownedCompany when it differs from assigned company (freelance company owners) (modules/users/users.service.ts:884)
- [ ] A FREELANCER may view another FREELANCER's profile only if both share the same freelance company (owned or assigned), else 403 "You are not allowed to view this profile." (modules/users/users.service.ts:914)
- [ ] Profile `jobsCompleted` counts offers where the engineer is the referred engineer, or userId with no referred engineer, on non-deleted work orders in APPROVED/INVOICED/PAID status (scoped to ownedCompany if any) (modules/users/users.service.ts:944; utils/helpers/completedWorkOrderStatuses.ts:3; utils/helpers/engineerOfferFilter.ts:7)
- [ ] Profile `numberOfClients` = distinct CLIENT-role creators of work orders assigned to the engineer (plus ACCEPTED, non-deleted company members if owner) (modules/users/users.service.ts:988)
- [ ] CUSTOMER viewers never receive an engineer's resume (modules/users/users.service.ts:1017)
- [ ] Profile completion: complete if status APPROVED; otherwise requires personal address longitude, latitude, `isVerified`, and ≥1 identity document; `missingFields` lists missing ones (utils/helpers/userProfileCheck.ts:3)
- [ ] Profile flags: `isCompanyOwner`, `isCompanyMember` (ACCEPTED), `isFreelanceMember` (FREELANCER with companyId and no owned company), `hasPendingCompanyInvite` (PENDING) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1064)
