- [ ] `createdAt` and `lastActive` (latest session lastActivityAt) are added to response on self-view (modules/users/users.service.ts:1084)

### Users: Jobs completed list
- [ ] CLIENT viewers only see completed jobs within their own company (modules/users/users.service.ts:1114)
- [ ] Jobs-completed search matches title (case-insensitive) or exact work order id when numeric (modules/users/users.service.ts:1125)
- [ ] Engineer who owns a company: completed jobs include ACCEPTED, non-deleted members (modules/users/users.service.ts:1153)
- [ ] Jobs completed sorted by work order start date desc; paginated only when both page and limit given (modules/users/users.service.ts:1121, 1255)
- [ ] Job distance uses referred engineer's address, falling back to offer user's address; Haversine km (modules/users/users.service.ts:1265; utils/helpers/getGoogleDistanceKm.ts:42)
- [ ] Job rating = average Feedback rating of the work order rounded to 1 decimal, null if none (modules/users/users.service.ts:1276)

### Users: Profile / address / billing updates
- [ ] Address update upserts the user's USER-type address and links `personalAddressId` if missing (modules/users/users.service.ts:1314)
- [ ] Profile update: user must exist and not be deleted (404) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1383)
- [ ] Profile email change rejected 409 "Email is already in use." if another non-deleted user has it (exact match) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1392)
- [ ] Profile update may change only firstName, lastName, email, phoneNumber, australianBusinessNumber, gstPercentage, isForeignContractor (modules/users/users.service.ts:1403)
- [ ] Billing rates: only ADMIN (any user) or a FREELANCER for themselves may upsert, else 403 "You may only update your own billing rates." (modules/users/users.service.ts:1450)
- [ ] FREELANCER may edit own billing rates only while status is PENDING or UNAPPROVE, else 404 "You are not allowed to update rates." (modules/users/users.service.ts:1459)
- [ ] Billing rates must include all of HOURLY, HALF_DAY, FULL_DAY, else 400 "For currency X, missing Hourly, Half Day, Full Day rates" (modules/users/users.service.ts:1479)
- [ ] Billing rates: one row per payment type per user (existing updated with rate+currency, new created) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1493)
- [ ] Billing rates array must be non-empty with valid PaymentType and CurrencyType enums (modules/users/dto/create-billing-rate.dto.ts:13)

### Users: Engineer discovery (findNearbyUsers)
- [ ] Modes: `nearby` (default), `workedWith`, `talentPool`; page default 1, limit default 10 (modules/users/users.service.ts:1577)
- [ ] Work order must exist with a companyId else 400 "Invalid work order or company" (modules/users/users.service.ts:1626)
- [ ] Only APPROVED, non-deleted FREELANCER users within a lat/lon bounding box of the radius are candidates (modules/users/users.service.ts:1657)
- [ ] Final distance filter: Haversine distance must be ≤ radius km (non-null) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1783)
- [ ] Optional `filterByRequiredSkills=true` requires ≥1 skill in the work order's services (modules/users/users.service.ts:1667)
- [ ] Optional `filterByRequiredTools=true` requires ≥1 qualification in the work order's qualification subtypes (modules/users/users.service.ts:1677)
- [ ] Search splits on whitespace; each term must match first or last name (case-insensitive) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1685)
- [ ] `nearby` excludes engineers with ANY offer on the work order (modules/users/users.service.ts:1698)
- [ ] `workedWith` requires the engineer to have a non-deleted work order for this company and no PENDING/ROUTED offer on this work order (modules/users/users.service.ts:1702)
- [ ] `talentPool` requires membership in ≥1 of the company's talent pools and no ROUTED/PENDING offer on the work order (modules/users/users.service.ts:1776)
- [ ] `talentPoolId` filter keeps only members of that pool (modules/users/users.service.ts:1774)
- [ ] Ranking = averageRating × 0.7 + (1 − cancellationRate) × 0.3, rounded to 2 decimals (modules/users/users.service.ts:1824)
- [ ] `workedWith` flag true when workedWithJobsCount > 0 (modules/users/users.service.ts:1842)
- [ ] BUG/CHECK: `talentPoolJobsCount`/`talentPool` use the company-wide talent pool user count, not the engineer's (modules/users/users.service.ts:1843)
- [ ] nearby/workedWith: engineers in a PENDING or ACCEPTED freelance company are grouped into one "freelanceCompany" row (id = owner, closest member distance) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1861)
- [ ] Company groups are dropped when the owner is not APPROVED or is deleted (modules/users/users.service.ts:1891)
- [ ] Company groups are dropped when the owner already has an offer on the work order (workedWith: only PENDING/ROUTED offers) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1904)
- [ ] Company owner is removed from individual rows (appears as the company row) (modules/users/users.service.ts:1959)
- [ ] workedWith company rows roll up workedWith/marketplace job counts of members + owner (modules/users/users.service.ts:1929)
- [ ] talentPool: owners of a company shown as "freelanceCompany" with engineerCount = ACCEPTED, APPROVED, non-deleted members (modules/users/users.service.ts:1966)
- [ ] Sort by `personalAddress` sorts by distance asc/desc; sortBy `id` orders by id (modules/users/users.service.ts:2008, 1720)

### Users: Worked-with engineers / rate comparison
- [ ] Worked-with list requires numeric companyId (400 "Company ID is required"/"Invalid Company ID") (modules/users/users.service.ts:2031)
- [ ] Worked-with list: APPROVED non-deleted engineers with a non-deleted company work order and no freelance company membership, plus owners of freelance companies assigned company work orders; optional status/customerStatus/email filters; de-duplicated, sorted by full name (modules/users/users.service.ts:2040)

- [ ] Only ADMIN, CLIENT, FREELANCER may add skills (else 403) (modules/users/users.service.ts:2639)

### Users: Work summary, reliability and ratings
- [ ] Profile stats for a freelance company owner aggregate the owner + ACCEPTED non-deleted members (modules/users/users.service.ts:3110)
- [ ] Work summary groups completed (APPROVED/INVOICED/PAID, non-deleted) jobs by category; jobs without a category are skipped; missing vendor shown "N/A"; lastCompletedJob = latest updatedAt per subcategory (modules/users/users.service.ts:3047)
- [ ] Reliability is aggregated across company only on profile view (`aggregate=true`); listings/offers use per-user numbers (modules/users/users.service.ts:3157)
- [ ] Reliability can be scoped to the viewer's company work orders (`companyId`) (modules/users/users.service.ts:3154)
- [ ] Timeliness uses the FIRST check-in per work order (TimeLog `checkInLoggedById`, falling back to completed CHECK_IN task text timestamp when no TimeLog) (modules/users/users.service.ts:3496, 3511)
- [ ] Timeliness counts only non-CANCELLED, non-deleted work orders (utils/helpers/completedWorkOrderStatuses.ts:9)
- [ ] Check-in is late if > 10 minutes after scheduled start (orderStartDate + orderStartTime in work order timeZone) (modules/users/users.service.ts:3472, 3452)
- [ ] Work orders with no resolvable scheduled start are never counted late (modules/users/users.service.ts:3552)
- [ ] timelinessRate = round((checkedIn − late) / checkedIn × 100), 0 when no check-ins (modules/users/users.service.ts:3557)
- [ ] Cancellation numerator = CancelWorkOrder rows with `canceledBy = FREELANCER` by the engineer (modules/users/users.service.ts:3583)
- [ ] Cancellation denominator = engineer's work orders in ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, WORKDONE, INCOMPLETE, VERIFIED, APPROVED, INVOICED, PAID, DISPUTED, plus CANCELLED ones the engineer cancelled; client cancellations excluded (modules/users/users.service.ts:3571)
- [ ] cancellationRate = round(cancelled / assigned × 100), 0 when none assigned (modules/users/users.service.ts:3609)
- [ ] Bulk reliability must yield identical numbers to per-user reliability (bulk CHECK_IN task fallback only when completer is the assigned engineer) (modules/users/users.service.ts:3174, 3313)
- [ ] averageRating = average of all Feedback `givenToUserId` (0 if none); totalFeedbacks and privateFeedbackCount (`isPrivate=true`) counted (modules/users/users.service.ts:3403)
- [ ] completedJobs = marketplaceJobsCount = all completed (APPROVED/INVOICED/PAID) work orders of the engineer, unscoped by company (modules/users/users.service.ts:3436)
- [ ] workedWithJobsCount = completed work orders of the engineer within the viewer's company (modules/users/users.service.ts:3419)
- [ ] Job activity: requested = PENDING non-counter + PENDING counter offer histories in the viewer's company; routed/assigned from offer history statuses; completed = PAID offers; marketplaceCompleted = PAID offer histories in any company (modules/users/users.service.ts:3638)

### Permissions: model, guard, decorator
- [ ] Permission modules (Prisma ModuleType): WORK_ORDERS, CUSTOMER_WORK_ORDERS, CLIENT_WORK_ORDERS, ACTION_APPROVE/VERIFY/REVIEW/CANCEL_WORK_ORDER, COMPANIES, ADMIN_NEW_USERS_MANAGEMENT, USER_PERMISSIONS, WORK_EXPERIENCE, QUALIFICATIONS, USERS, JOBS, MANAGE, CLIENT_CUSTOMER, CLIENT_PROJECT, INVOICE, CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY/SEND/PAID_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY/SEND/PAID_ENGINEER_INVOICE, COMPANY_BRANCHES, CLIENT_BANK_ACCOUNTS, WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS, WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL, WORK_FORCE_COVERAGE_MAP, ENGINEER_SKILLS_SET, TASK_BOARD, FREELANCE_COMPANY_WORKERS, CUSTOMER_USERS (../prisma/schema.prisma:341)
- [ ] Each permission row carries four flags: isRead, isWrite, isUpdate, isDelete (modules/permissions/permissions.service.ts:36)
- [ ] `ClientPermissionModules` (client-owner module set) = COMPANIES, WORK_ORDERS, CUSTOMER_WORK_ORDERS, ACTION_APPROVE_WORK_ORDER, ACTION_VERIFY_WORK_ORDER, ACTION_REVIEW_WORK_ORDER, ACTION_CANCEL_WORK_ORDER, USERS, CLIENT_CUSTOMER, CLIENT_PROJECT, JOBS, CLIENT_BANK_ACCOUNTS, COMPANY_BRANCHES, INVOICE, CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_VERIFY_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_SEND_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ACTION_SEND_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_PAID_ENGINEER_INVOICE, ACTION_PAID_CUSTOMER_INVOICE, ENGINEER_INVOICE, USER_PERMISSIONS, WORK_FORCE_COVERAGE_MAP, ENGINEER_SKILLS_SET, TASK_BOARD (WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS / WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL excluded) (utils/enums.ts:247)
- [ ] `@RequiredPermissions(...)` stores `{module, access}` requirements on the handler (common/decorators/permissions.decorator.ts:4)
- [ ] PermissionsGuard denies (returns false → 403) any handler without `@RequiredPermissions` metadata (common/guards/permissions.guard.ts:14)
- [ ] PermissionsGuard passes if the user holds ANY one of the listed requirements (OR semantics): same module and `permission[access] === true` (common/guards/permissions.guard.ts:31)
- [ ] PermissionsGuard checks `request.user.permissions` = permissions snapshotted into the JWT at login (changes need re-login/refresh) (common/guards/permissions.guard.ts:21; modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:61)
- [ ] ProfileCompletionGuard: 403 "Please complete your profile to access this feature. Missing: <fields>" when profile incomplete (applied on an offers endpoint) (common/guards/profile-completion.guard.ts:26; modules/offers/offers.controller.ts:67)
- [ ] ProfileCompletionGuard: missing user id → 403 "Unauthorized" (common/guards/profile-completion.guard.ts:21)

### Permissions: service
- [ ] BUG/CHECK: getPermissionsForUser's ADMIN check is not awaited (Promise always truthy) so any authenticated user can read/seed anyone's permissions (modules/permissions/permissions.service.ts:13)

### Admin: guards on admin endpoints

### Admin: engineer approval lifecycle
- [ ] User profile statuses: PENDING, SUBMITTED, APPROVED, UNAPPROVE, DEACTIVATED, ACTIVATED (../prisma/schema.prisma:587)
- [ ] Approve profile: requester must be ADMIN else 403 "Access denied"; sets target status APPROVED (no role/status precondition on target) (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:49)
- [ ] Approval sends PROFILE_APPROVED notification "🎉 Welcome Aboard, <firstName>!" linking `/jobs` (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:62)
- [ ] Approval email "Tech Domain - Profile Approved": template `profile-approved-company-engineer` (with company name) if engineer is an ACCEPTED freelance-company member, else `profile-approved` (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:71)
- [ ] Invite engineer: ADMIN only (403); valid email required; email "Tech Domain - Invitation" with link `${FRONTEND_BASE_URL}/techSignup?role=FREELANCER` (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:97; modules/admin/dto/invite-engineer.dto.ts:4)
- [ ] Activate engineer: ADMIN only ("Only admins can activate engineers"); target must be FREELANCER (403 "Engineer not found"); sets APPROVED, sends notification + `profile-approved` email (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:380)
- [ ] Deactivate engineer: ADMIN only; target must be FREELANCER; sets status DEACTIVATED (no notification/email) (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:437)
- [ ] Deactivated users are blocked at login (403) and on every JWT-authenticated request (401) (modules/auth/auth.service.ts:139; modules/auth/jwt.strategy.ts:55)
- [ ] Missing-docs request: target must be a FREELANCER; status becomes UNAPPROVE unless currently PENDING (PENDING stays PENDING) (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:321)
- [ ] Missing-docs request increments `reminderCount` by 1 every time (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:331)
- [ ] Missing-docs `emailTemplate` values: CUSTOM_EMAIL, ID, RATES, SKILL_SET, EQUIPMENT, EMPLOYMENT, LANGUAGE, EDUCATION, PROFILE_PICTURE, IDENTITY_PROOF, WORKING_RIGHTS, ADDRESS; `emailContent` required when CUSTOM_EMAIL included (modules/admin/dto/send-missing-doc-email.dto.ts:3, 26)
- [ ] CUSTOM_EMAIL sends "Important: Action Required" (`custom-email`) with the content; otherwise "Tech Domain - Missing Documents" listing docs (underscores → spaces, lower-case) with link `${FRONTEND_BASE_URL}/completeYourProfile?role=FREELANCER` (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:336)
- [ ] Skill verify/unverify and equipment (qualification) verify/unverify allowed only for ADMIN or CLIENT roles (403) and target must exist (404) (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:482, 519)

### Admin: new users listing
- [ ] Admin user listing excludes the requesting admin, unverified users and deleted users (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:161)
- [ ] Optional filters: role, `excludeCompanyMembers=true` (no ACCEPTED membership), `excludeCompanyOwners=true` (no owned company) (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:165)

- [ ] Customer listing: non-deleted clients that have an owner account, or children of `parentClientId` if given (modules/admin/admin.service.ts:698)

### Cross-cutting: request handling / constants
- [ ] Device info: mobile apps identified by `x-device-id` header (device type/brand/model/os/version/platform/app version/fcm token headers); web parsed from User-Agent with platform "web" (common/middleware/device.middleware.ts:14)
- [ ] Web device type: Android/iOS → mobile; Windows/Mac/Linux → desktop; else unknown (common/middleware/device.middleware.ts:36)
- [ ] Client IP taken from first `x-forwarded-for` entry, else socket address (common/middleware/device.middleware.ts:9)
- [ ] All HTTP request bodies are stripped of control chars U+0000–U+0008, U+000B, U+000C, U+000E–U+001F (tab/LF/CR kept) (common/interceptors/sanitize-input.interceptor.ts:17; utils/helpers/stripControlChars.ts:11)
- [ ] Prisma unique-constraint errors (P2002) map to 409 "Username or email already exists" (common/filters/http-exception.filter.ts:32)
- [ ] Unknown non-HTTP exceptions return 500 "Internal server error" (common/filters/http-exception.filter.ts:43)
- [ ] Apple private-relay emails (`@privaterelay.appleid.com`) are displayed as 12 asterisks (utils/variables.ts:34)
- [ ] Social rejection messages per reason (NO_ACCOUNT, NO_EMAIL, UNVERIFIED_EMAIL, ACCOUNT_DELETED, ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED) (utils/variables.ts:40)
- [ ] Customer notification mailbox = `CUSTOMER_NOTIFICATIONS_EMAIL` env or default customer.notifications@techdomain.com.au (utils/variables.ts:26)
- [ ] FULL_DAY_HOURS = 8; HALF_DAY_HOURS = 4 (utils/variables.ts:18)
- [ ] Work order status order: DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, PUBLISHED, ROUTED, ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, INCOMPLETE, CANCELLED, WORKDONE, APPROVED, PAID (utils/variables.ts:4)
- [ ] Schedule conflict messages for client vs tech (utils/variables.ts:29)
- [ ] Check-in statuses: TOO_EARLY, ALLOWED, TOO_LATE (utils/enums.ts:278)

## 5. Feedback, Ratings, Analytics, Dashboards, Chat & Notifications


### Feedback — Client/Customer rating the engineer (type FREELANCER)

- [ ] Client→engineer feedback can only be given when the work order status is one of WORKDONE, APPROVED, VERIFIED, INVOICED, DISPUTED, PAID; otherwise "Work order not found" error (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:44-82)
- [ ] Only a user whose role is CLIENT or CUSTOMER AND who belongs to the work order's company may rate the engineer; otherwise 401 Unauthorized (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:85-93)
- [ ] A CLIENT rater must be a team member of the WO's primary team (teamId) or secondary team (secondaryTeamId); otherwise 403 "Only the work order's service delivery or operations team can give or update this feedback." (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:95-110)
- [ ] CUSTOMER raters are NOT subject to the team-membership check (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:95)
- [ ] Only ONE FREELANCER-type feedback exists per work order per rater role within the company: a second CLIENT (any CLIENT of that company) updates the existing one instead of creating a new one; same for CUSTOMER (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:112-121)
- [ ] A CLIENT feedback and a CUSTOMER feedback can coexist on the same WO (lookup is keyed by the rater's role) (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:116)
- [ ] On update, the original givenByUserId is kept (the updater does not become the author); rating, comment, isPrivate and givenToUserId (=current WO engineerId) are overwritten (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:122-131)
- [ ] Feedback is always addressed to the WO's current engineerId (givenToUserId) (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:128,140)
- [ ] "mostLiked" options are replaced (delete-all then insert) ONLY when the array is provided; omitted array leaves existing options untouched; empty array clears them (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:145-158)
- [ ] "problemFaced" options follow the same replace-only-if-provided rule; duplicate options are skipped (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:161-174)
- [ ] Engineer is notified of client feedback (WORK_ORDER_FEEDBACK) only when WO status is APPROVED, INVOICED, DISPUTED or PAID (not WORKDONE/VERIFIED) (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:36-41,176-178)
- [ ] Client-feedback notification recipients = WO engineer + owner of the assigned freelance company (vendor), deduplicated; nothing sent if no recipients (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:229-235)
- [ ] Client-feedback notification title "WO-{referenceId}: {title}", content "{creator first last} has left feedback for you." (falls back to "The client"), url /workOrder/feedback/{id} (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:237-249)
- [ ] notifyEngineerOfClientFeedback re-sends the deferred client-feedback notification (sender = original author) if a FREELANCER feedback exists for the WO; no-op otherwise (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:252-275)
- [ ] Every create/update of client feedback writes a WorkOrderLog of type OTHER with old vs new {rating, comment, isPrivate}; status fields unchanged; log only persisted if metaData present (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:180-213)

### Feedback — Engineer rating the client (type CLIENT)

- [ ] Engineer→client feedback allowed only when WO status is APPROVED, INVOICED or DISPUTED (not PAID, not WORKDONE/VERIFIED) and the caller is the WO's engineer; else 404 "Work order not found" (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:278-306)
- [ ] One feedback per (workOrderId, givenByUserId) — engineer feedback is upserted (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:320-344)
- [ ] Engineer feedback is addressed to the WO creator (createdById), not the company (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:335,341)
- [ ] Liked/problem options replaced only when provided (same semantics as client flow) (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:346-374)
- [ ] Engineer feedback always notifies (no status gate) the WO creator + assigned freelance company owner (deduped) with WORK_ORDER_FEEDBACK "{engineer first last} has left feedback for you." (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:377-391)
- [ ] Engineer feedback create/update writes an OTHER-type WorkOrderLog with old/new rating, comment, isPrivate (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:393-426)

### Feedback — Listing, formatting and marketplace rating

- [ ] Profile feedback list for a freelance-company owner (vendor) aggregates owner + all ACCEPTED, non-deleted company members (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:700-718)
- [ ] Vendor profile lists only public (isPrivate=false) FREELANCER-type feedback received by the aggregated ids (engineer's own feedback for the client excluded) (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:446-451)
- [ ] Individual profile lists public feedback both given and received by the user (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:452-455)
- [ ] Private feedback (isPrivate=true) is never shown in profile feedback lists (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:450,454)
- [ ] Profile feedback is grouped per work order into {feedbackGiven, feedbackReceived}; "given" = authored by any aggregated id, otherwise "received" (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:634-679)
- [ ] Each grouped feedback entry includes distance between the rated user's personal address and the WO address (Haversine calculateDistance), null if no personal address (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:649-657)
- [ ] Feedback list ordered by createdAt desc (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:458-460)
- [ ] findOne returns only the feedback the caller authored for that WO (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:514-543)
- [ ] Feedback author companyName shown only for CLIENT/CUSTOMER authors: owned company name, else company name, else null (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:596-600)
- [ ] Liked/problem option enum values are mapped to display texts via FEEDBACKS_LIKEMOST_TEXTS / FEEDBACKS_PROBLEM_TEXTS; unknown values dropped (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:602-609)
- [ ] getFormattedFeedback: for CLIENT viewer, received = CLIENT-type, given = FREELANCER-type; for FREELANCER viewer, the reverse; other roles get null; returns null if no feedbacks (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:545-594)
- [ ] Marketplace rating = average of ALL feedback ratings given to the user (both types, private included), plus count (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:725-733)
- [ ] Marketplace rating aggregates over company owner + accepted engineers on profile view (aggregate=true); listings pass aggregate=false for per-user rating (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:720-723)
- [ ] Liked-option counts exclude feedback authored by the aggregated ids themselves (no self-rating inflation) (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:735-772)
- [ ] problemOptions counts are computed but NOT returned by marketplace rating (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:783-788)

### Analytics — Shared definitions and scoping

- [ ] All company analytics are scoped to the caller's companyId; unknown caller → 404 "User not found" (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:98-105)
- [ ] "Completed" status set = WORKDONE, APPROVED, VERIFIED, INVOICED, PAID (DISPUTED and INCOMPLETE are NOT completed) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:41-47)
- [ ] "Cancelled" set = CANCELLED only (CANCELLED_PAYABLE status is not counted as cancelled) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:51-54)
- [ ] Base WO filter for every company metric: companyId, isDeleted=false, status NOT IN (DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, CANCELLED); cards that need CANCELLED/DRAFT override the status clause explicitly (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:58-62,146-160)
- [ ] Optional client filter: selecting a PARENT client expands to parent + all non-deleted child clients; non-parent → just that client; client not found in company → [clientId] as given (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:127-143)
- [ ] Optional team filter restricts by WO primary teamId only (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:157)
- [ ] Date range on work orders filters by orderStartDate using date-only bounds: gte start day (UTC), lt (end day + 1) — end date inclusive (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:74-97; modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:107-117)
- [ ] Date range on DateTime columns (createdAt/updatedAt) uses gte start-of-UTC-day and lt start of next UTC day after end (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:102-115)
- [ ] Filter options: clients dropdown lists only non-deleted PARENT clients sorted by name; teams dropdown lists non-deleted teams whose name does NOT contain "-" sorted by name (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:162-179)
- [ ] Percent change = (curr−prev)/prev×100 rounded to 1dp; if prev=0 → 100 when curr>0 else 0 (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:324-325)

### Analytics — KPI cards (getKpiMetrics)

- [ ] Month/week windows are UTC calendar month/week (dayjs startOf('week') = Sunday); only the "Today" card uses a local zone (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:184-199)
- [ ] "Today" zone = caller-supplied IANA tz if valid, else APP_TIMEZONE env, else Australia/Sydney (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:27-38,187)
- [ ] Total Work Orders = count of base WOs with orderStartDate in current UTC month; previousValue = previous month (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:229-234,350-355)
- [ ] Completed = base WOs in Completed statuses with orderStartDate this month vs last month (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:235-248)
- [ ] Cancelled = status CANCELLED with orderStartDate this month vs last month (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:249-262)
- [ ] Cancelled.customerCancelled = this month's CANCELLED WOs with customerSubStatus=CANCELLED (the client-actioned/payable slice) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:268-275,373)
- [ ] weekSummary = base WOs this UTC week vs last week with changePercent (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:276-281,344-348)
- [ ] Assigned card = status ASSIGNED with orderStartDate in current month (not "now" overall) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:289-295,365-368)
- [ ] In Progress card = status INPROGRESS with orderStartDate in current month (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:296-302)
- [ ] Published count is computed but NOT returned in the KPI response (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:282-288,343-381)
- [ ] Today = WOs with orderStartDate on today's local date and status ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, any Completed status, or INCOMPLETE (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:303-316)
- [ ] Sparklines = 12 weekly buckets (oldest→newest) starting 11 weeks before current week start, bucket [weekStart, nextWeekStart) using date-only string compare, excluding DRAFT/PENDING_APPROVAL/CANCELLED (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:208,318-341)
- [ ] Cancelled sparkline is always zeros because sparkline rows exclude CANCELLED (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:318-339,374)

### Analytics — Trends (getTrends)

- [ ] Weekly with date range: week buckets from startOf(week of start) to end date (last bucket clipped to end), label "DD MMM"; compared to same buckets shifted −1 year (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:414-467)
- [ ] Weekly default: last 13 weeks (current week inclusive) vs same 13 weeks one year earlier; series named by year, current #0F40AE, previous #F9A825 (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:470-537)
- [ ] Monthly with date range: one bucket per calendar month from start month to end month, label "MMM YY", compared to same months previous year (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:541-597)
- [ ] Monthly default: Jan–Dec of previous year and current year (current year counted up to end of current month) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:599-645)
- [ ] Yearly: last 5 calendar years (current−4 .. current), date range ignored, single "Work Orders" series (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:648-670)
- [ ] Trend bucketing uses parsed timestamps of orderStartDate (not string compare) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:399-411)

### Analytics — Annual target

- [ ] Annual target is a fixed constant of 5000 work orders (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:22)
- [ ] Achieved = WOs (company, not deleted, client/team filter) with orderStartDate in target year and customerStatus IN (CANCELLED_PAYABLE, APPROVED, INVOICED, PAID) — status filter dropped so CANCELLED-status rows count (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:681-706)
- [ ] Target year defaults to current UTC year; lastYearAchieved uses the prior year (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:677,699-705)
- [ ] percentage = achieved/5000×100 rounded 1dp, capped at 100; remaining = max(5000−achieved, 0) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:708,718)
- [ ] expectedByNow = round(5000 × monthsElapsed/12), monthsElapsed = current month number for current year else 12; onTrack = achieved ≥ expectedByNow (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:709-720)

### Analytics — Status breakdown, monthly, teams, top engineers

- [ ] Status breakdown counts per status within date range: Drafted (DRAFT), Published, Assigned, In-Progress, Work Done, In-Complete (INCOMPLETE), Verified, Approved, Invoiced, Paid, Cancelled (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:741-831)
- [ ] "Paid" in status breakdown counts WOs with status PAID WITHOUT the isDeleted filter (deleted WOs included) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:788-796)
- [ ] stats card returns published, assigned, workDone, approved, paid (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:818)
- [ ] yearlyBreakdown = base WO counts per calendar year for last 5 years (ignores date range) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:803-835)
- [ ] Monthly bar chart = base WO counts per month for selected year (default current) and previous year (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:840-872)
- [ ] Team distribution pie: only teams without "-" in the name; teams with zero WOs omitted; WOs with no/other team grouped as "N/A"; team filter not applied (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:875-924)
- [ ] Top engineers ranked by total base WOs assigned (engineerId not null) in range, desc, default limit 10; client/team filters not applied (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:927-938)
- [ ] Top engineer completionRate = round(completed/totalAssigned×100) where completed = Completed-status WOs in range (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:946-955,988)
- [ ] Top engineer avgRating = avg of FREELANCER-type feedback with rating > 0 (all-time, 1dp), null if none; ratingCount returned (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:957-966,1003-1004)
- [ ] Unknown engineer name shown as "Unknown"; initials = first letters of first two name words (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:987-999)

### Analytics — Breakdown / advanced / geography

- [ ] Day-of-week breakdown counts base WOs in range by weekday of orderStartDate (Sun..Sat), invalid dates skipped (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1032-1049)
- [ ] Top categories = top 8 categories by base WO count in range (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1039-1046)
- [ ] Advanced completedCount = base WOs in range with Completed statuses (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1078-1081,1180)
- [ ] Counter vs requested = count of offers with status ASSIGNED or PAID on completed, non-deleted company WOs in range, split by isCounter (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1092-1111)
- [ ] Top cities = top 8 trimmed non-empty address cities by base WO count (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1114-1122)
- [ ] Monthly completion rate: always full current year (ignores date range and team filter), rate = round(completed/total×100) per month (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1082-1091,1139-1157)
- [ ] WO size buckets by grandTotal: falsy/0 = Unpriced, <200 Micro, <1000 Small, <5000 Medium, <20000 Large, else Enterprise; empty buckets omitted (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1160-1177)
- [ ] Work orders by country: base WOs in range grouped by trimmed address.country desc; missing country counted as "N/A" appended last (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1193-1229)
- [ ] Client yearly trends: one line per non-deleted parent client (children rolled up, standalone clients excluded) with counts for startYear and endYear only (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1235-1307)
- [ ] Client monthly race: one series per parent client (optionally one clientId), children rolled up, 12 monthly counts for the year; parents with all-zero data omitted (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1310-1427)

### Analytics — Engineer dashboard

- [ ] Engineer scope: a freelance company owner aggregates self + ACCEPTED company members; anyone else sees only self (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1437-1449)
- [ ] Engineer totalJobs = WOs (not deleted) assigned to scope with status in ASSIGNED, INPROGRESS, WORKDONE, INCOMPLETE, APPROVED, INVOICED, PAID, DISPUTED, CANCELLED in range (VERIFIED/PUBLISHED excluded) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1503-1521)
- [ ] Engineer completed = Completed statuses in range; cancelled = CANCELLED in range; ongoing = ASSIGNED+INPROGRESS in range (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1522-1542)
- [ ] totalHoursWorked = sum of non-null timeLog.totalHours for scope WOs in range, rounded to 1dp (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1551-1559,1656-1659)
- [ ] Engineer sparkline: 12 weekly buckets of non-DRAFT WOs by weeks-ago of orderStartDate (older than 11 weeks clamp into oldest bucket); completed sparkline = each bucket −1 floored at 0 (synthetic) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1543-1550,1593-1599,1652-1653)
- [ ] Performance-score metrics (completion, cancellation, acceptance, timeliness, totalJobs) use ALL-TIME data when a date range is given (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1601-1638)
- [ ] All-time totalJobs = non-DRAFT WOs of scope (includes PUBLISHED/VERIFIED etc.), unlike ranged totalJobs (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1616-1618)
- [ ] completionRate = round(completed/total×100) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1640-1641)
- [ ] cancellationRate = round(engineerCancels/(total + engineerCancels)×100), engineerCancels = cancelWorkOrder rows by scope users with canceledBy=FREELANCER (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1584-1590,1642-1645)
- [ ] acceptanceRate = round(offers with status ASSIGNED or PAID / all offers by scope ×100) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1482-1489)
- [ ] timelinessRate = % of time logs whose checkInTime is ≤15 minutes after scheduled orderStartDate+orderStartTime (treated as UTC); logs with missing/invalid schedule skipped in numerator but count in denominator (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1459-1480)
- [ ] idleRate = round(weeks with zero jobs among last 12 / 12 ×100) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1647-1648)
- [ ] Engineer avgRating = avg FREELANCER-type feedback rating >0 received by scope (all-time), 2dp, 0 if none (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1575-1583,1665)
- [ ] Engineer invoice stats: FREELANCER-role, non-deleted invoices of scope engineers in createdAt range; upcoming = GENERATED or OVERDUE, paid = PAID; totals on grandTotal rounded 2dp (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:2305-2339)

### Analytics — Finance (invoices, FX, profit)

- [ ] All finance amounts converted to USD using open.er-api.com USD rates, cached 1 hour; on failure keep stale cache or fall back to {USD:1} (unconverted) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1675-1701)
- [ ] toUsd = amount / rate[currency] rounded 2dp; USD or unknown currency returns amount unchanged (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1704-1708)
- [ ] Finance KPIs: role=FREELANCER → expense measured on grandTotal; role=CLIENT → revenue measured on pre-tax subTotal (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1880-1885)
- [ ] Invoices considered: company, isDelete=false, matching role, createdAt within [start 00:00 local, end+1 day) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1751-1771)
- [ ] Client scope on invoices: CLIENT invoices by invoice.clientId; FREELANCER invoices by any non-deleted linked work order's clientId (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1716-1724)
- [ ] companyTotalExpenseCost (converted using companyCurrency, fallback invoice currency) is ADDED to every invoice's amount (both roles) and reported separately as `extra` (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1874-1891)
- [ ] Per-status KPI cards: GENERATED, VERIFIED, UNPAID, OVERDUE, PAID each with amountUsd, count, monthly sparkline, extra (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1934-1939,2012-2016)
- [ ] Outstanding = total − PAID (amount, count and company-expense extra) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1947-1950,2017-2021)
- [ ] Sparkline months: from startDate (or earliest invoice, or 5 months ago) to endDate (or now), capped at 24 months (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1835-1856)
- [ ] Client breakdown pie rolled up to parent client (CLIENT invoices via clientId; FREELANCER via first linked WO's client), sorted desc, zero omitted; unmatched amounts in "Other" slice (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1902-1917,1955-1974)
- [ ] Team breakdown: invoice's client's teamId; no team → "Unassigned"; sorted desc (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:1919-1929,1978-1997)
- [ ] Expense yearly trend (FREELANCER): stacked monthly columns "Engineer Expense" (grandTotal) and "Company Expense" (companyTotalExpenseCost) for calendar year by invoice createdAt (UTC) (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:2066-2107)
- [ ] Revenue yearly trend (CLIENT): one line per parent client (subTotal, USD), date range ignored, zero-lines omitted (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:2110-2204)
- [ ] Profit trend per month = revenue (CLIENT subTotal) − expense (FREELANCER grandTotal + companyTotalExpenseCost), USD, 2dp (modules/analytics/analytics.service.ts:2212-2302)

### Customer Dashboard

- [ ] Customer dashboard is available only to non-deleted users with role CUSTOMER; anyone else → 403 "Customer dashboard is only available to customer accounts" (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:107-117)
- [ ] CUSTOMER without companyId or customerId → 403 "This account is not linked to a customer entity" (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:119-121)
- [ ] The linked client (customerId) must be non-deleted and belong to the user's company, else 403 (cross-company guard) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:123-136)
- [ ] Authorized entities: parent client → itself + non-deleted children; child/standalone client → only itself (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:138-148)
- [ ] Display entities (filter list, per-entity bars): parent account → children only (parent not listed); child account → only itself (siblings never disclosed) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:146,166-184)
- [ ] Work raised directly against the parent still counts in totals for a parent account (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:84-91,143-145)
- [ ] entityId filter outside the authorized set silently yields an empty dashboard (no error, no existence leak) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:156-159,192-196)
- [ ] Default date window = start of previous month through end of next month (UTC) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:581-587)
- [ ] Date window is clamped to at most 24 months before the end month (start moved forward) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:68-69,589-590)
- [ ] End date is inclusive (end-of-day + 1ms exclusive bound) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:582-584,596-598)
- [ ] Work-order metrics: company, clientId in scope, not deleted, orderStartDate in [startDay, endDayExclusive) by date string, status ≠ DRAFT (CANCELLED included) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:54,198-207)
- [ ] Completed = WORKDONE, VERIFIED, APPROVED, INVOICED, PAID (same as client analytics) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:11-17)
- [ ] Pending approval = PENDING_APPROVAL; Published = PUBLISHED; Assigned = ASSIGNED; In progress = INPROGRESS (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:23-42)
- [ ] Cancelled (customer dashboard) = CANCELLED + CANCELLED_PAYABLE (differs from client analytics) (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:44-47)
- [ ] Pending = max(total − completed − pendingApproval − published − assigned − inProgress − cancelled, 0) — catch-all for other open stages, KPIs disjoint (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:295-308)
- [ ] completionRate = completed/total ×100 rounded to 1dp, 0 when total=0 (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:325-327)
- [ ] Monthly trends bucket by first 7 chars (YYYY-MM) of orderStartDate string; every month in range emitted (zeros for gaps), max 24 months; each month carries same KPI split incl. pending (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:359-471)
- [ ] Status distribution grouped by status, sorted by count desc (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:330-332)
- [ ] Entity performance: only display entities (parent never gets its own bar), total + completed per entity, sorted by total desc (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:336-344)
- [ ] Invoice metrics include only CLIENT-role, non-deleted invoices of scoped clients with createdAt in [start,end), excluding GENERATED status (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:63,209-218)
- [ ] Invoice summary grouped per currency (never summed across currencies): invoiceCount, billed=sum grandTotal, paid=PAID sum, outstanding=UNPAID+OVERDUE sum; 2dp; sorted by billed desc (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:66,525-564)
- [ ] Invoice status distribution = counts per status summed across currencies, sorted desc (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:513-523)
- [ ] Country breakdown: trimmed address country (empty/missing → "N/A"), sorted desc, limited to 100 rows (modules/customer-dashboard/customer-dashboard.service.ts:76,483-510)

### Workforce (engineer directory) — access, visibility, metrics

- [ ] Engineer map locations: any existing user may fetch; only APPROVED, non-deleted FREELANCERs with non-null personal-address lat/lng are returned (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:51-86)
- [ ] Engineer map image clusters engineers into ~3° grid cells (count-weighted mean position); single-engineer cells drawn tiny #4F7FEF, clusters mid #0F40AE (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:38-39,103-151)
- [ ] Map image URL kept ≤8000 chars by dropping single markers first (clusters prioritized) (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:155-164)
- [ ] Map image is cached in-memory for 10 minutes; missing GOOGLE_API_KEY → 500; Google error/JSON response → 500 (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:89-91,123-125,169-180)
- [ ] Workforce listing is allowed only for CLIENT or CUSTOMER users; others → 403 "You are not authorized" (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:185-200)
- [ ] Pagination defaults page=1, limit=10 (non-positive values replaced); ordered by user createdAt desc (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:203-205,256)
- [ ] Listing includes only APPROVED, non-deleted FREELANCER users (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:214-217)
- [ ] Without freelanceCompanyId, freelance-company owners (users owning a company) are excluded from the workforce list (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:228-230)
- [ ] With freelanceCompanyId>0, list only ACCEPTED members of that company whose owner is APPROVED and not deleted (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:209-227)
- [ ] CUSTOMER viewers cannot use talentPool, workedWith, backgroundCheck or jobsCompleted filters (ignored) (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:206-207,241-247)
- [ ] CUSTOMER viewers do not receive phoneNumber, email, sessions, freelanceCompany, talent pools, lastActive, job counts, cancellation/timeliness rates, backgroundChecked, workedWith (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:409-433)
- [ ] CLIENT viewers receive masked phone, masked email; addressLine1 is masked for all viewers (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:409-414)
- [ ] workedWithJobsCount = engineer's WOs in APPROVED/INVOICED/PAID, not deleted, in the viewer's company; marketplaceJobsCount = same across all companies (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:309-319; utils/helpers/completedWorkOrderStatuses.ts:3-22)
- [ ] workedWith flag = workedWithJobsCount > 0 (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:431)
- [ ] Workforce rating = per-user marketplace rating (aggregate=false), default 0 (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:337-339,416)
- [ ] Reliability (cancellationRate, timelinessRate) computed per user relative to the viewer's company (CLIENT only) (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:332-336,428-429)
- [ ] backgroundChecked = user has ≥1 qualification of type "Background check" (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:303-305,430)
- [ ] Talent pools shown are only those belonging to the viewer's company (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:321-326)
- [ ] Freelance company shown = earliest ACCEPTED membership's company (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:285-292,404)
- [ ] lastActive (main list) = most recent session's lastActivityAt (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:272-278,425)
- [ ] jobsCompleted filter is applied in-memory after fetching ALL matches: keep users whose (workedWith ? workedWithJobsCount : marketplaceJobsCount) equals jobsCompleted exactly; total and pagination recomputed (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:436-443; utils/helpers/workforcefilters.ts:21-31)
- [ ] Talent-pool workforce listing: CLIENT only (403 otherwise); APPROVED non-deleted FREELANCERs in the given talent pool (freelance company owners included) (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:457-488)
- [ ] Talent-pool listing: a freelance company owner row is displayed with company name, and job counts/reliability/rating aggregate owner + ACCEPTED members (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:578-596,621)
- [ ] Talent-pool listing: company owner with no personal addressLine1/city/country shows the company address instead (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:613-618)
- [ ] Talent-pool listing: lastActive = latest session createdAt (not lastActivityAt) (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:529-535,629)
- [ ] Talent-pool listing returns talentPool name (null if missing) (modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:652-659)

### Workforce filters (utils/helpers)

- [ ] talentPool filter: user is in the given talent pool id (utils/helpers/workforcefilters.ts:3-12)
- [ ] workedWith=true → engineer has ≥1 WO (any status) in viewer's company; false → none; ignored if undefined or no companyId (utils/helpers/workforcefilters.ts:14-20)
- [ ] equipment / certifications filter: user has a qualification with the given qualificationSubTypeId; 0/NaN ignored (utils/helpers/workforcefilters.ts:33-53)
- [ ] backgroundCheck=true → has a "Background check" qualification; false → has none; undefined ignored (utils/helpers/workforcefilters.ts:55-83)
- [ ] skillSet filter: user has skill with given id (utils/helpers/workforcefilters.ts:85-95)
- [ ] search: single all-digit token → exact user id match; otherwise every whitespace token must match firstName, lastName or email (case-insensitive contains); '+' decoded as space (utils/helpers/adminFilters.ts:25-30,36-54)
- [ ] fullName filter: ANY token matching firstName or lastName (OR across tokens, case-insensitive) (utils/helpers/adminFilters.ts:81-93)
- [ ] country / city filters match any of the user's addresses case-insensitively (trimmed); when both are given the city filter overwrites the country filter (same `addresses` key) (utils/helpers/adminFilters.ts:124-144; modules/workforce/workforce.service.ts:238-239)

### Work order filters (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts)

- [ ] mergeFilters ANDs only non-empty filters; returns {} if none (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:10-13)
- [ ] radiusFilter is a bounding box: ±radiusMeters/111000 degrees on both latitude and longitude (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:15-27)
- [ ] category/vendor/subcategory filter: exact categoryId, vendorId, and any service in subCategoryIds (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:29-55)
- [ ] clients/teams/client/project/team filters are exact-match or IN list; empty input ignored (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:161-201)
- [ ] currency filter applied only when value is a valid CurrencyType (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:203-211)
- [ ] engineerCurrency filter: WO has an ASSIGNED/PAID offer in that currency OR WO.engineerCurrency equals it (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:213-233)
- [ ] provider filter: engineerId = id OR assigned freelance company owner = id (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:235-243)
- [ ] country / city filters: case-insensitive exact match on WO address (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:245-263)
- [ ] workOrderId filter matches referenceId (not internal id) (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:273-279)
- [ ] rate filter: any of fixedAmount, hourlyRate, halfDayRate, fullDayRate within [min,max] (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:285-301)
- [ ] WO search: integer input → referenceId equals; otherwise title contains (case-insensitive) (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:303-312)
- [ ] requestCount filter: WOs (non-deleted) whose count of offers from non-deleted users is within [min default 0, max default MAX_SAFE_INTEGER] (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:314-341)
- [ ] cancelled filter: WO has a cancel record matching canceledBy (valid UserType) and/or cancellationReason (valid enum); invalid values ignored (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:343-363)
- [ ] status filter uses customerStatus for customer viewers, status otherwise; single or IN list (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:365-375)
- [ ] Date-range filter on orderStartDate/orderEndDate string columns compares only the YYYY-MM-DD prefix (UTC), end inclusive; invalid dates ignored (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:57-97)

### Task board filters (utils/helpers/taskBoardFilters.ts)

- [ ] teams filter: teamId IN list (utils/helpers/taskBoardFilters.ts:3-10)
- [ ] status and priority filters applied only for valid TeamTaskStatusType / TeamTaskPriority values; invalid ignored (utils/helpers/taskBoardFilters.ts:12-37)
- [ ] workOrder filter accepts one or many numeric ids; non-numeric dropped (utils/helpers/taskBoardFilters.ts:39-51)
- [ ] assignees filter: task has an assignment for any given user id (utils/helpers/taskBoardFilters.ts:53-66)
- [ ] createdBy filter: createdById IN numeric ids (utils/helpers/taskBoardFilters.ts:67-77)
- [ ] task search: title contains (case-insensitive) OR, if numeric, referenceId equals the number as string; blank → no filter (utils/helpers/taskBoardFilters.ts:79-102)

### Chat — Room creation and join authorization

- [ ] One engineer room per (workOrderId, companyId, engineerId); one customer room per (workOrderId, companyId, userId=customer) with engineerId null; room name "Group Chat for Work Order {id}" (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:303-349)
- [ ] When the target (engineerId param) is a CUSTOMER, the customer↔team room is opened/created instead of an engineer room; WO not found → 403 (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:72-84,100-105)
- [ ] A FREELANCER can never create or join a customer↔team room (unless they are the target themselves) → 403 (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:86-98)
- [ ] Customer room participants = customer + members of WO primary & secondary teams (non-deleted teams) + client company owner; all ensured present even if room existed (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:368-420; utils/helpers/getTeamMemberstoNotify.ts:3-29)
- [ ] A team member opening the customer room is upserted as a participant (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:107-114)
- [ ] Engineer room: WO must exist in the given companyId, else 403 "Work order not found" (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:118-136)
- [ ] The CUSTOMER who created the WO is blocked from opening engineer rooms on it → 403 (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:143-146)
- [ ] Engineer room may be opened only by: WO team member (primary/secondary) or client company owner, assigned freelance company owner, assigned engineer, ACCEPTED member of the assigned freelance company, the target engineer with an offer in ROUTED/ACCEPTED/PENDING (as userId or referredEngineerId), or the engineer who already owns an existing room on this WO; otherwise 403 (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:151-224)
- [ ] Active-offer access only authorizes an engineer to open their OWN room (authUserId must equal target engineerId) (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:178-196)
- [ ] Engineer room initial participants = WO team members + company owner + the engineer; the assigned freelance company owner is added only if the engineer is an ACCEPTED member of that company (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:226-243)
- [ ] Opening an existing room adds the opener as participant if missing and marks messages as read for them (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:319-335)
- [ ] Socket joinGroupChat: on auth failure emits joinError; on success joins socket room, resets opener's unreadCount to 0, marks read statuses read, marks MESSAGE notifications for that room read, emits unreadCountUpdated 0 (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:32-100)
- [ ] Socket joinGroupChat trusts the payload's clientId as the acting user (no socket-level JWT identity check) (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:43-50,64)
- [ ] Customer room back-fill (from sidebar GET) only for non-DRAFT WOs created by a CUSTOMER in the given company, and only when the caller is the customer or a WO team member/company owner (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:432-474)

### Chat — Read access, sidebar listing and send authorization

- [ ] Room read access requires a ChatRoomUser row AND one of: user is room engineer, room customer (userId), WO company owner, member of WO primary/secondary (non-deleted) team, assigned freelance company owner, or has ever sent a message in the room (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:882-936)
- [ ] Past senders keep read access to their history after the relationship ends, but cannot send (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:902-906)
- [ ] Sidebar list: non-integer ids → empty list (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:661-667)
- [ ] Sidebar list: a regular FREELANCER (not a company owner) gets an empty list (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:683-693)
- [ ] Sidebar list: freelance company owner sees rooms only when their company is the WO's assigned freelance company — own room + rooms of ACCEPTED members (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:695-723)
- [ ] Sidebar list: freelance company owner sees the assigned member engineer as an entry (chatRoomId null, unread 0) even before a room exists (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:714-716,851-877)
- [ ] Sidebar list: CLIENT/other roles see only rooms of the WO they participate in (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:725-767)
- [ ] Sidebar entries sorted by most recent message desc (no message = oldest); each carries caller's unreadCount, isCustomerChat flag, isDeletedUser (deleted engineer or SYSTEM_USER_ID) (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:772-849)
- [ ] Send allowed for: room customer (userId), assigned WO engineer or room engineer, client company owner, assigned freelance company owner, member of WO primary/secondary non-deleted team, ACCEPTED member of assigned freelance company, or engineer with ROUTED/ACCEPTED/PENDING offer (userId or referredEngineerId) (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:944-1020)
- [ ] Socket send authorization is cached per socket for 5 minutes after a successful join/check for the same user and room; otherwise re-checked; failure emits sendError "You are not authorized to send messages in this chat." (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:24,211-234)

### Chat — Messages, idempotency, edit/delete, reactions

- [ ] Sending with a clientMessageId already used by the same sender returns the original message (no new row, no broadcast, no unread increment, no notification) (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:546-549; modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:253)
- [ ] Concurrent duplicate clientMessageId inserts resolve via unique (senderId, clientMessageId): loser returns winner's row (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:565-578)
- [ ] New message increments unreadCount by 1 for every room participant except the sender (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:590,1250-1258)
- [ ] Message attachments are linked from digitalAssetIds (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:579-586)
- [ ] Only the original sender may edit a message; edit sets isEdited=true and editedAt; broadcast messageEdited (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:476-483; modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:102-124)
- [ ] Only the original sender may delete a message; delete is a soft delete (isDeleted=true); broadcast messageDeleted (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:485-492; modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:126-138)
- [ ] Edit/delete sender identity comes from the socket payload senderId (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:104-113,128-133)
- [ ] Reactions toggle: same (message, user, emoji) removes it, otherwise adds; full reaction list broadcast; requires a conversationId (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:494-512; modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:140-171)
- [ ] Web message history paginated by message id: default 10, max 100, cursor = oldest id already held, returned oldest-first with nextCursor/hasMore (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1022-1131)
- [ ] Mobile conversation history ordered by id only; beforeId → newest rows below cursor; afterId → oldest rows above (catch-up); both narrow the range; always returned chronologically (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1142-1204)

### Chat — Delivery, unread counts and notifications on send

- [ ] A participant counts as "viewing" if their socket's reported viewingConversationId equals the room; sockets that never report fall back to socket-room membership (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:257-279,565-574)
- [ ] FREELANCER participants receive message fan-out only if they are the room engineer, the assigned WO engineer, or the assigned freelance company owner; all non-FREELANCER participants receive it (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:325-351)
- [ ] The assigned freelance company owner is auto-added to the room when the engineer is an ACCEPTED member of their company (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:305-366)
- [ ] The client company owner is always auto-added as participant and included in fan-out (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:368-377)
- [ ] Viewing (non-sender) participants get a read status (delivered+read) and a messageReadByUser broadcast; no notification (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:422-448)
- [ ] Non-viewing non-sender participants get an unread read-status row, unreadCountUpdated with their count, and a MESSAGE notification (title = WO title, url /workOrder/messages/{woId}?engineerId={roomEngineer or payload engineer}) (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:450-494)
- [ ] Sender and viewing participants have unreadCount reset to 0 and receive unreadCountUpdated 0 (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:495-517)
- [ ] Client company owner receives a MESSAGE notification even when actively viewing (if not sender) (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:519-538)
- [ ] Message notification content is prefixed with a reply quote (sender first name + first 60 plain-text chars) when replying to a non-deleted message (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:379-388)
- [ ] Failure processing one participant does not stop others (per-participant try/catch) (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:404-545)
- [ ] Mark-as-read: flips isRead on messages not sent by the reader, resets reader's unreadCount to 0 (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1260-1283)
- [ ] Mark-as-delivered on connect: all undelivered read statuses of the engineer's rooms set isDelivered=true (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1306-1343)

### Chat — Room cleanup

- [ ] Leaving a room deletes it if it has zero messages, unless protected; broadcasts chatRoomDeleted (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:576-605; modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1424-1436)
- [ ] Protected from empty-room cleanup: the assigned engineer's room, the assigned freelance company owner's room, and the customer↔team room (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1383-1419)
- [ ] Bulk cleanup deletes every non-protected room with zero messages (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1439-1450)
- [ ] Unread-client-room cleanup: on WOs in ASSIGNED..PAID, delete rooms where every message is unread and sent by CLIENT users and orderStartDate is >15 days ago; assigned engineer's room and customer room excluded (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1454-1502)
- [ ] Room deletion removes participants, read statuses and the room in one transaction (messages cascade) (modules/chat/chat.service.ts:1349-1363)

### Chat — Mobile inbox

- [ ] Inbox lists only accessible rooms (participant + live relationship) that have at least one message, ordered by latest message createdAt desc then id desc (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:60-99)
- [ ] Inbox page size default 20, clamped 1..50; opaque base64url cursor "{ISO}|{id}"; malformed cursor → first page (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:11-12,49-54,566-586)
- [ ] totalUnreadCount = sum of caller's unreadCount across accessible rooms; unreadConversationCount = rooms with unreadCount > 0 (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:115-134)
- [ ] Opening a conversation requires read access, else 403 "You are not allowed to view this conversation"; marks it read by default (markAsRead≠false) (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:141-149)
- [ ] Conversation history default limit 0 = whole conversation; previousCursor only on full history page; nextCursor only on full afterId (backfill) page (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:13,151-173)
- [ ] Mobile send: room must exist (404) and caller must pass send authorization (403); workOrderId/engineerId taken from the room, not the client; same gateway side-effects as web (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:184-217)
- [ ] Mark conversation read: resets unread, flips message isRead, marks MESSAGE notifications for that room read, broadcasts read receipts (broadcast failure never fails the request) (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:220-246)
- [ ] Mark all read: only accessible rooms with unread>0 — flips messages isRead, zeros unreadCount, marks unread MESSAGE notifications for those rooms read, emits unreadCountUpdated (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:248-298)
- [ ] Inbox counterpart: room engineer if not the caller, else room customer if not the caller, else the client company (name/logo) (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:450-467)
- [ ] Avatar fallback: user profile picture, else their company's logo (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:419-426)
- [ ] Preview: "You: …" for caller's own message, "{sender first name}: …" otherwise; clipped to 200 chars with "…"; deleted → "This message was deleted"; voice → "🎤 Voice message"; location → "📍 Location"; empty text with files → "📎 Attachment"; HTML stripped (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:14,523-549)
- [ ] participantCount excludes the caller; full participants list only when a single conversation is opened (modules/chat/mobile-inbox.service.ts:485-494)

### Notifications — Rules

- [ ] sendNotification never notifies the sender (senderId removed from recipients); no other dedupe — one row per recipient per call (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:588-599)
- [ ] Every notification is persisted, then emitted on socket room user_{id} as event "notification" (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:602-621; modules/notifications/notifications.gateway.ts:104-110)
- [ ] FCM push is sent only when the recipient is offline (no subscribed socket) AND has a deviceToken; body is HTML-stripped content; payload includes current total unread count (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:623-651)
- [ ] A user is "online" while at least one socket has sent subscribeUser for them; removed when their last socket disconnects (modules/notifications/notifications.gateway.ts:92-102,112-128)
- [ ] Notifying an online user also marks their undelivered chat read-statuses as delivered (CLIENT: all rooms of their company; others: rooms where they are engineer) (modules/notifications/notifications.gateway.ts:29-90,104-108)
- [ ] Engineers who are ACCEPTED members of a freelance company only see notifications with no WO or on WOs that have an assigned freelance company (direct-client WO notifications hidden) (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:82-95)
- [ ] Message notification list is grouped per chat room (latest room first), paginated by chatRoomId cursor, default 10 rooms (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:269-297)
- [ ] CLIENT who is neither company owner nor in any team gets no message notifications (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:242-248)
- [ ] CLIENT company owner sees all their message notifications; other team-member users see only those on WOs whose primary teamId is one of their teams (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:257-261,355-360)
- [ ] Linked-company filter is not applied to CLIENT message notifications (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:264-267,361-363)
- [ ] Message unreadNotificationCount = number of distinct chat rooms with ≥1 unread MESSAGE notification (in-scope) (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:365-378,182-212)
- [ ] Message notification sender avatar falls back to sender company's logo (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:398-422)
- [ ] Other (non-MESSAGE) notifications ordered newest first, paginated by notification id cursor (default 10); unread count = all unread non-MESSAGE in scope (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:438-506)
- [ ] markAsRead only updates a notification owned by the caller (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:53-57)
- [ ] "Mark all as read" affects only non-MESSAGE notifications; message notifications have their own mark-all and per-chat-room mark-read (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:521-568)
- [ ] WO published notification: skipped when WO address has no latitude/longitude (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:659-660)
- [ ] WO published recipients = FREELANCERs NOT in any ACCEPTED freelance company whose personal address is within 50 km (Haversine) + owners of freelance companies having any ACCEPTED member within 50 km; deduped (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:655-723)
- [ ] WO published recipients are not filtered by user approval status or isDeleted (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:663-682)
- [ ] WO published content "New work order available in {city, state, country | displayName}. Request now to get assigned." (fallback "in your area"), title "WO-{ref}: {title}", url /workOrder/details/{id}, sender = WO creator (modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:725-739)
- [ ] Feedback notifications: see Feedback sections (WORK_ORDER_FEEDBACK) (modules/feedback/feedback.service.ts:241-249,383-391)
- [ ] Chat message notifications: see Chat delivery section (MESSAGE) (modules/chat/chat.gateway.ts:485-537)

### Geo, Google places and distance

- [ ] Distance is great-circle (Haversine) with Earth radius 6371 km, returned in kilometres (utils/helpers/calculateDistance.ts:1-23)
- [ ] getGoogleDistanceKm currently returns Haversine straight-line km (Google Directions driving route call is disabled); on error also Haversine (utils/helpers/getGoogleDistanceKm.ts:33-61)
- [ ] (Disabled) route selection rule: shortest duration, tie → shortest distance (utils/helpers/getGoogleDistanceKm.ts:5-31)
- [ ] Work-order radius search uses a lat/lng bounding box of radiusMeters/111000 degrees (not a true circle, no longitude cos-correction) (utils/helpers/workOrderFilters.ts:15-27)
- [ ] geo.service and google.service contain no distance calculation (distance lives in utils helpers) (modules/geo/geo.service.ts:45-263; modules/google/google.service.ts:10-204)
- [ ] Countries list all rows sorted by name; country search is case-insensitive contains, paginated (modules/geo/geo.service.ts:61-86)
- [ ] States by country / cities by state / cities by country: 404 if parent country/state id not found; sorted by name; cities paginated (modules/geo/geo.service.ts:89-135,244-262)
- [ ] Pagination meta: totalPages = ceil(total/limit) (0 if limit 0), hasNextPage = page < totalPages (modules/geo/geo.service.ts:49-58)
- [ ] Country by code matches ISO2 or ISO3 case-insensitively (trimmed, upper-cased); not found → 404 (modules/geo/geo.service.ts:165-175)
- [ ] City names by country name: case-insensitive exact country name; unknown country → [] (not 404); duplicate city names collapsed (modules/geo/geo.service.ts:184-202)
- [ ] Paginated city names by country name: default page 1, limit 20, optional q contains filter, distinct names, hasNextPage via limit+1 fetch; unknown country → empty page (modules/geo/geo.service.ts:210-242)
- [ ] Location search uses the FIRST Google findPlaceFromText candidate; none → error; all failures surface as 500 "Failed to fetch location data." (modules/google/google.service.ts:23-47,99-102)
- [ ] Address parsing from Google components uses the component's first type: postal_code→zipCode, country→country, locality/sublocality→city, administrative_area_level_1→state, administrative_area_level_2→area (modules/google/google.service.ts:62-73,162-173)
- [ ] Every resolved location is enriched with Google Time Zone (timeZoneId, timeZoneName) for its lat/lng at the current timestamp (modules/google/google.service.ts:75-96,175-196)
- [ ] Autocomplete: empty/null query → []; returns description + place_id only; Google calls time out after 1s (modules/google/google.service.ts:104-130)
- [ ] Place details by placeId: missing result → error; failures → 500 "Failed to fetch place details." (modules/google/google.service.ts:132-203)

# Appendix — Known issues found while building this checklist

Highlights only; each is detailed (with file:line) in the section named. Verify before fixing.

## Security

- [ ] **Malicious code in backend git history** — `.vscode/tasks.json` ran a disguised JS payload (`public/fonts/fa-solid-500.woff2`) on folder open; merged via PR #589 (2026-09-11), removed in `a1b28595` (2026-09-15). Rotate backend secrets; check machines that opened the repo in that window.
- [ ] WebSocket gateways accept `userId`/`role` from the client with no JWT check; chat `sendMessage`/`editMessage`/`deleteMessage`/`toggleReaction` trust client-sent sender ids (Notifications/Real-time; API).
- [ ] Recipients' FCM device tokens are sent to browsers over the socket (Notifications/Real-time).
- [ ] Unauthenticated or unowned REST routes: `POST /workOrderLogs`, `PATCH /education/:id`, `PATCH /employment/:id`, `POST/DELETE /companies`, `PATCH /admin/permissions`, `GET /users/workedWith`; `PATCH /workOrders/:id/subStatus` and `POST /offers/status` have no ownership check (API).
- [ ] Missing `await` on authorisation checks: `GET /permissions/user/:userId` admin check, bank-details owner check (API; Business Rules).
- [ ] Refresh-token expiry never enforced; refresh signs with `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET` (not defined) while login uses `JWT_SECRET`; permissions frozen in the JWT (Business Rules).
- [ ] ~20 unsanitised `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` sinks with tokens in `localStorage`; no size/type limits on chat attachments (Frontend sections; Notifications/Real-time).

## Backend behaviour

- [ ] Cancellation penalty always computes to 0 (Business Rules).
- [ ] `PER_DEVICE` and `BLENDED` payment types throw "Invalid payment type" (Business Rules).
- [ ] GPS distance at check-in is recorded but never enforced (Business Rules).
- [ ] `WORK_ORDER_PAID`, `WORK_ORDER_REMINDER`, `FREELANCE_COMPANY_INVITE` notifications are never created; two email templates unused (Notifications).
- [ ] Self-signed-up clients lack `WORK_FORCE_ENGINEERS` / `WORK_FORCE_TALENT_POOL` permissions (Notifications/Roles).
- [ ] Socket room naming mismatch (`user_<id>` vs `user:<id>`); `messageDelivered` sent to wrong room; NotificationGateway registered twice (Real-time).

## Frontend behaviour

- [ ] Notifications read `conversationId` but backend sends `chatRoomId` → mark-read sends `NaN` (Notifications/Real-time).
- [ ] Chat list loses "You:"/sender prefix after live messages; "Seen by" shows "undefined undefined"; mark-all-read doesn't sync other tabs (Work Order Details — Chat).
- [ ] Buttons with no handler: Find Job Declined/Accept/Withdraw/Export/Cancel Assignment; Details "Copy", "Save as Template", "Tags", "Print", "Report problem"; Providers "Route", "Export", "Invite to Recruitment"; "Help & Support"; "Invite Workforce" for approved users.
- [ ] Silent fake success: user-permissions save and client-account delete never call the API; bank-account errors show as success toasts; task update fails silently.
- [ ] Company bank-account delete calls `bankDetails/undefined/undefined`; Send Invoice puts Cc emails under `toEmail`; invoice Tax column always blank.
- [ ] "Work between hours" / "Work anytime" schedule types show no fields; five statuses have no badge; Mark Complete custom-task check not wired.
- [ ] No confirmation on work-order row delete or task delete; admin Company Users "Reset Filters" throws.
- [ ] Logout leaves the user signed in if `POST auth/logout` fails; Get Started "CLIENT" card routes to `/customerSignup`; `/requestDemo` renders technician signup.
